`ARCHITECTURE.md`:

```md
# ACF Architecture

## 1. Placement and trust boundaries

| Code | Location | Replicates to client? |
|------|----------|-----------------------|
| Framework, detectors, evidence, configuration | `ServerScriptService.ACF` | **No** |
| Constants safe to expose (`VERSION`) | `ReplicatedStorage.ACFShared` | Yes |
| Client telemetry (Phase 3) | `StarterPlayerScripts` | Yes, untrusted |

Thresholds, enforcement policy and evidence never leave the server. The shared folder holds
only values an exploiter can read without harm.

## 2. Subsystems

```
                      ┌────────────────────────── Framework ───────────────────────────┐
 Environment ───────▶ │ Configuration ─▶ Logger ─▶ EventBus ◀─▶ Scheduler              │
 (Players, Heartbeat, │        │                     ▲    ▲         ▲                    │
  Clock, GenerateId,  │        ▼                     │    │         │                    │
  LogSink)            │  ModuleLoader ──context──▶ [Detection modules]                  │
                      │                              │ context:Report()                  │
                      │  PlayerRegistry ◀────────────┤                                   │
                      │        │                     ▼                                   │
                      │        └──────────────▶ DetectionManager ─▶ EvidenceManager      │
                      └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

| Module | File | Responsibility |
|--------|------|----------------|
| Framework | `Core/Framework.luau` | Builds services, owns the framework state machine, builds module contexts |
| Configuration | `Core/Configuration.luau`, `Core/DefaultConfig.luau` | Schema validation, defaults, deep-frozen values, late module sections |
| Logger | `Core/Logger.luau` | Structured, levelled, rate-limited logging with a ring buffer |
| EventBus | `Core/EventBus.luau` | Synchronous, error-isolated pub/sub with strict topics |
| Scheduler | `Core/Scheduler.luau` | One Heartbeat connection, interval tasks, per-task CPU stats, failure disabling |
| ModuleLoader | `Core/ModuleLoader.luau` | Definition validation, dependency order, lifecycle state machine |
| PlayerRegistry | `Core/PlayerRegistry.luau` | Sessions, character generations, per-session state and cleanup |
| Cleanup | `Core/Cleanup.luau` | Reverse-order resource release, error-tolerant |
| RateLimiter | `Core/RateLimiter.luau` | Keyed token bucket with bounded keys |
| DetectionManager | `Detection/DetectionManager.luau` | Validates, rate limits, sanitises and normalises detection reports |
| EvidenceManager | `Intelligence/EvidenceManager.luau` | Bounded per-user evidence, duplicate merging, expiry |
| RobloxEnvironment | `Platform/RobloxEnvironment.luau` | The only place that reads Roblox services |
| BuiltIns | `Detection/BuiltIns.luau` | Factories for built-in modules, registered at `Initialize` |
| RemoteSecurity | `Detection/RemoteSecurity/RemoteSecurity.luau` | Protected remotes, request pipeline, suspicion tracking |
| Validators | `Detection/RemoteSecurity/Validators.luau` | Bounded, injection-safe argument schemas |
| Policies | `Detection/RemoteSecurity/Policies.luau` | Permission / ownership checks |
| Movement | `Detection/Movement/Movement.luau` | Character sampling, exemptions, allowances, reporting |
| MovementDetectors | `Detection/Movement/MovementDetectors.luau` | Pure detectors: speed, teleport, flight, air jump, acceleration, noclip |
| Physics | `Detection/Physics/Physics.luau` | Fling, extreme velocity, velocity mismatch (consumes `Movement.Sample`) |
| Telemetry | `Detection/Telemetry/Telemetry.luau` | Optional client heartbeat (weak signal) |
| Combat | `Detection/Combat/Combat.luau` | Weapon state, two-stage shot/hit validation, providers |
| CombatRules | `Detection/Combat/CombatRules.luau` | Pure: definitions, damage, projectile geometry |
| Behavioral | `Detection/Behavioral/Behavioral.luau` | Signals, actions and combat statistics per player; scheduling and reporting |
| BehavioralDetectors | `Detection/Behavioral/BehavioralDetectors.luau` | Pure: robust baselines, CUSUM signals, Wilson-bounded ratios, timing regularity |
| Statistics | `Intelligence/Statistics.luau` | Pure: EWMA, CUSUM, z-score with floors, Wilson bound, decay |
| RiskEngine | `Intelligence/RiskEngine.luau` | Core service: per-user risk from every detection, bands, history, persistence queue |
| RiskModel | `Intelligence/RiskModel.luau` | Pure: decaying saturating keys, correlation groups, noisy-OR confidence, bands, explanations |
| RobloxStore | `Platform/RobloxStore.luau` | DataStore adapter (`Environment.Store`), non-yielding, pcall-wrapped |
| EnforcementManager | `Enforcement/EnforcementManager.luau` | Core service: levels, restrictions, correction permission, cases, removals, ban records, overrides |
| EnforcementPolicy | `Enforcement/EnforcementPolicy.luau` | Pure: band and history to a level, corroboration rule, automatic versus approval |
| BanRecords | `Enforcement/BanRecords.luau` | Pure: stored-record validation, expiry, escalation, messages |
| AuditLog | `Enforcement/AuditLog.luau` | Bounded, summarising audit log published on `ACF.EnforcementAudit` |
| RobloxEnforcer | `Platform/RobloxEnforcer.luau` | `Environment.Enforcer`: Kick, Players:BanAsync / UnbanAsync |
| IntegrationAPI | `API/IntegrationAPI.luau` | Game-facing rule definitions: combat calls, behavioural signals and actions |
| ViolationTracker | `Intelligence/ViolationTracker.luau` | Shared decaying violation score: the "never on one sample" rule |
| RobloxPhysics | `Platform/RobloxPhysics.luau` | Real character probe and ray casts |
| Protocol | `Shared/Protocol.luau` (replicated) | Remote names and intervals for the client script |
| ClientTelemetry | `Client/ClientTelemetry.client.luau` | Optional heartbeat sender (untrusted) |
| PublicAPI | `API/PublicAPI.luau` | Process-wide singleton facade |

## 3. Lifecycles

**Framework:** `Created → Initialized → Running ⇄ Stopped → Destroyed`.
`Initialize` is atomic: if configuration or a queued module is invalid, the framework stays
`Created` and the call can be retried.

**Modules:**

```
Registered ─▶ Initializing ─▶ Initialized ─▶ Starting ─▶ Running ─▶ Stopping ─▶ Stopped
     │              │                            │                      │          │
     ├▶ Disabled    └▶ Failed                    └▶ Failed              └▶ Failed  └▶ Starting (restart)
     └▶ Blocked (missing / disabled / failed dependency)
any initialized state ─▶ Destroying ─▶ Destroyed
```

Illegal transitions raise immediately because they indicate a framework bug. Every
transition is published on `ACF.ModuleStateChanged`.

**Resource scoping:** resources acquired through the context (`Events:Subscribe`,
`Scheduler:Every/After`, `Cleanup:Add`) during `Initialize` are released at `Destroy`.
Resources acquired during `Start` are released at `Stop`. A module can therefore be stopped
and restarted without leaking or duplicating subscriptions.

**Start order:** modules start first, then the PlayerRegistry. Modules therefore observe
`PlayerSessionStarted` for every player, including players already in the server.
On `Stop`, sessions end first, so modules receive `PlayerSessionEnded` while still running.

## 4. Detection data flow

1. A module calls `context:Report(target, { Kind, Severity, Confidence, Details })`.
2. The context binds the module's `Name` and `Category`, so modules cannot report under
   another identity.
3. `DetectionManager` checks, in order: the module is `Running`; the input is valid
   (Severity 0–100, Confidence 0–1, finite); the target resolves to an **active** session.
   A forged table or stale Session does not resolve.
4. A per-(session, detector) token bucket limits flooding.
5. Details are sanitised:
   - string keys only, bounded key count and string length, nesting depth ≤ 2;
   - Instances are stored as their full names, so destroyed parts aren't kept alive;
   - functions and threads are dropped.
6. A frozen `DetectionEvent` is recorded by `EvidenceManager` and published on
   `ACF.DetectionReported` together with the resulting record.

**Severity vs Confidence.** Severity is the risk weight of an observation at full certainty
(the `w` in the Phase 6 model). Confidence is how sure the detector is (`c`).
Phase 6 combines them as `R = min(100, Σ wᵢ·cᵢ·e^(−λ·tᵢ))`. Phase 1 stores both but does not
score.

## 5. Evidence model

- Keyed by **UserId**, not session. Leaving and rejoining the same server does not erase
  recent evidence. Each record keeps the SessionId it was first seen in.
- Repeated identical `(Detector, Kind)` detections within `DuplicateWindowSeconds` merge
  into one record: Count increments, and Severity/Confidence keep the maximum. A flood of
  one signal costs O(1) memory.
- Per-user cap (`MaxRecordsPerPlayer`, least-recently-seen eviction) and time-based expiry
  (`RetentionSeconds`, pruned by a scheduler task).
- Cross-server persistence is deferred to Phase 6, where it is justified by risk scoring.

## 6. Error isolation

| Fault | Effect |
|-------|--------|
| Invalid configuration / module definition | Raises at boot with every problem listed |
| Module lifecycle method throws | Module → `Failed`; dependents → `Blocked`; others unaffected |
| `Critical = true` module fails to start | `Start` raises; already-started modules are stopped |
| Event handler throws | Logged; after N consecutive failures the handler is disconnected |
| Scheduled task throws | Logged; after N consecutive failures the task is disabled |
| Log sink throws | Counted; logging continues |
| Cleanup task throws | Reported; the remaining tasks still run |

## 7. Design decisions

- **Environment injection instead of globals.** Core code never calls `game:GetService`
  for platform services (`Framework` reads only the shared `Constants` module). This makes
  every subsystem testable offline with fakes. The fakes stand in for Roblox only; ACF
  logic under test is always the real code.
- **Synchronous event bus.** Deterministic ordering, simple reasoning and testability.
  The trade-off is that handlers must not yield.
- **Central scheduler instead of per-detector Heartbeat.** One connection, measurable cost
  per task, and one place to disable a broken task.
- **`script.Parent` requires, not require-by-string.** These work in every Roblox Studio
  version. The Lune harness reproduces them via a virtual DataModel built from the same Rojo
  project file.
- **Evidence ≠ risk ≠ enforcement.** Each concern is a separate subsystem (Phase 1, 6
  and 7), as the security model requires.

## 8. Remote security (Phase 2)

Built-in modules are ordinary modules. They are registered by the Framework at
`Initialize`, use the same context and lifecycle as user modules, and can be disabled
through `Modules`. RemoteSecurity sits in front of the game's handlers:

```
client ──▶ RemoteEvent.OnServerEvent ──▶ RemoteSecurity.admit()
                                           ├─ reject ──▶ stats, decaying suspicion score ──▶ (sustained) context:Report
                                           └─ accept ──▶ pcall(Handler, player, validatedArgs...)
```

- **Fail closed.** While stopped, no request reaches a handler and RemoteFunctions return
  `RejectResponse`.
- **Per-player state** (rate limiter, cooldown, transaction ids, suspicion) lives in
  `session:GetState("RemoteSecurity.State")`, so it is bounded by the number of protected
  remotes and freed when the player leaves.
- **Suspicion.** It is a leaky integrator rather than a per-request verdict, because
  invalid requests also come from lag, version skew and game bugs. Details are in
  docs/REMOTE_SECURITY.md.

## 9. Movement and physics (Phase 3)

```
Scheduler (0.2 s) ─▶ Movement.tick ─▶ Environment.Physics:Sample(character)   [server-observed]
                                       ├─ exemptions (seated, dead, generation change)
                                       ├─ MovementDetectors.evaluate ─▶ violations ─▶ ViolationTracker ─▶ context:Report
                                       └─ publish "Movement.Sample" ─▶ Physics ─▶ violations ─▶ ViolationTracker ─▶ context:Report
```

- **Pure detectors.** `MovementDetectors` takes plain-number samples and returns weighted
  violations. All Roblox access is in `Platform/RobloxPhysics`, so every detector is tested
  against scripted legitimate and malicious traces.
- **Module dependency in practice.** `Physics` declares `Dependencies = { "Movement" }` and
  subscribes to the `Movement.Sample` topic. If Movement is disabled or fails, Physics is
  Blocked, not half-running.
- **One violation model.** Movement, Physics, RemoteSecurity and Telemetry all score through
  `ViolationTracker`. The "do not act on a single event" rule therefore lives in one
  audited place.

## 10. Combat (Phase 4)

```
client claim ─▶ ProtectEvent (types, sizes, transport rate) ─▶ game handler
                                                                  └─▶ ACF:ValidateAttack(player, weapon, claim)
                                                                        ├─ stage 1: shot legitimacy ─▶ commit ammo / cooldown
                                                                        ├─ stage 2: hit legitimacy (Physics:Describe, Physics:Cast)
                                                                        └─▶ verdict + server-computed damage ─▶ game applies it
```

- **Verdicts, not side effects.** ACF never applies damage or edits inventories. The game
  applies `result.Damage`. That keeps ACF genre-agnostic, and it means ACF bugs cannot
  corrupt game state.
- **Providers are the integration seam.** Inventory, team and damage rules are
  game-specific, so they are injectable functions with safe defaults. They fail closed.
- **Two stages.** Separating "did the shot happen" (it consumes ammo and fire-rate budget)
  from "did it hit" stops a cheater from retrying invalid hits for free.

## 11. Behavioural intelligence (Phase 5)

```
game server code ── ACF:RecordSignal / RecordAction ──┐
Combat ── "Combat.Shot" topic (validated shots) ──────┼─▶ Behavioral (per-session state)
                                                      │     ├─ every 10 s: close signal buckets,
                                                      │     │  median baseline, CUSUM
                                                      │     ├─ every 10 s: Wilson-bounded accuracy
                                                      │     │  and invalid-action ratios
                                                      │     └─ per action: timing windows
                                                      └─▶ ViolationTracker ─▶ DetectionManager
```

- **Only trusted inputs.** Signals and actions come from the game's server code. Shots
  come from Combat after validation. The client contributes nothing directly.
- **Robust to the cheater being measured.**
  - The signal baseline is the median player, not the mean.
  - Accuracy compares a player with the server *minus that player's own shots*.
- **Statistical and practical significance.** Every statistical report needs:
  - evidence over several samples (CUSUM run, Wilson bound, full timing windows);
  - an effect large enough to matter (`MinRatio`, accuracy margins and floors).
- **Optional coupling.** Behavioral subscribes to `Combat.Shot` only if the topic exists
  (`context.Events:Has`). Disabling Combat leaves Behavioral running, not Blocked.
- **Pure core.** `Statistics` and `BehavioralDetectors` have no clock or Roblox access.
  `Behavioral.luau` only owns state, timing and reporting, like Movement.

## 12. Risk (Phase 6)

```
any module ─▶ DetectionManager ─▶ EvidenceManager (records: what happened)
                     │
                     └─ ACF.DetectionReported ─▶ RiskEngine (score: how worried to be)
                                                   ├─ per (Detector, Kind): decaying, saturating score
                                                   ├─ correlation groups ─▶ R = min(100, Σ G + History)
                                                   ├─ ACF.RiskChanged on band change ─▶ (Phase 7 enforcement)
                                                   └─ session end ≥ 40 ─▶ batched Store writes
```

- **Core service, not a module.** Risk must see every module's detections, custom
  ones included, and must not be disabled by a module failure. It subscribes to the bus
  for the framework's whole life and is gated by Start/Stop.
- **Evidence and risk are separate.** EvidenceManager keeps *what happened*, with
  details. RiskEngine keeps *how much it matters now*. Enforcement (Phase 7) will be a
  third subsystem consuming `RiskChanged`, so detection, punishment and evidence stay
  separate.
- **Storage is asynchronous and fails safe.** `Environment.Store` uses callbacks; the
  Roblox adapter spawns and pcalls. Retries and batching live in RiskEngine, where the
  tests reach them. A failed load means no history, never higher risk.

## 13. Enforcement (Phase 7)

```
RiskEngine ── ACF.RiskChanged / DetectionReported ──▶ EnforcementManager
                                                        ├─ EnforcementPolicy (pure): level, tags, proposal
                                                        ├─ levels 2-4: answers context.Enforcement questions
                                                        │     RemoteSecurity (spec.Restrict), Combat, Movement (ShouldCorrect)
                                                        ├─ levels 5-7: Environment.Enforcer (Kick, BanAsync) or a Case
                                                        ├─ ban records ⇄ Environment.Store (read-modify-write, retries)
                                                        └─ AuditLog ─▶ ACF.EnforcementAudit
```

- **Separation.** Modules detect, and ask yes/no questions through the read-only
  `context.Enforcement` facade. Intelligence scores. Only EnforcementManager decides or
  acts, and only through `Environment.Enforcer`.
- **Subscription order.** EnforcementManager is created after the RiskEngine, so for a
  detection the risk is already updated when enforcement re-evaluates. The bus calls
  handlers in subscription order.
- **Fail safe in both directions.**
  - A storage failure never delays a ban: the ban applies first, then the write follows.
  - Statistical evidence never removes anyone automatically.
  - Permanent bans need a human by default.
  - Shadow mode applies nothing automatic.

## 14. Planned additions

| Phase | Additions |
|-------|-----------|
| 6 | `RiskEngine`, `ConfidenceEngine`, correlation, persistent risk via `DataStoreAdapter` |
| 7 | `EnforcementManager`, `ActionPolicy`, `Quarantine`, `BanManager`, `AuditLogger` |

```

`CHANGELOG.md`:

```md
# Changelog

All notable changes are documented here. ACF uses [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/).
Before 1.0.0, minor versions may contain breaking changes; each one is listed.

## [1.0.0] — 2026-11-20 — Phase 8: Testing and Production Release

Released on the canary channel as `1.0.0-rc.1`. Stable follows the canary criteria in
RELEASING.md.

### Added
- `tests/Specs/Simulation.luau`: a deterministic population simulator.
  - **Honest players:** walking, sprinting, jumps, lag spikes, jitter, respawns, server
    teleports, shooting, earning patterns, human clicking, shop remote.
  - **Nine cheat types:** speed, teleport, fly, noclip, aimbot, rapid fire, coin dupe,
    autoclicker, remote spam.
- `tests/lune/evaluate.luau` / `tools/evaluate.sh`. They measure:
  - detection accuracy and time to detect;
  - honest false positives;
  - live enforcement outcomes;
  - CPU per tick at 10/50/100 players;
  - a 2-hour churn soak with a faulty module, counting ACF's per-user structures;
  - logging cost.

  The results are in `docs/EVALUATION.md`.
- `Integration/Population` (CI population run) and `Integration/Latency` (honest players
  at 0.02–0.9 s ping).
- Release channels: `Constants.CHANNEL`, `ACF.Channel`, `GetStatus().Channel`;
  `tools/release.sh canary|stable`; `RELEASING.md`; `examples/CanaryBootstrap.server.luau`.
- Enforcement:
  - review cases for players restricted longer than
    `Cases.ReviewAfterRestrictedSeconds` (10 min);
  - departed players forgotten after `ForgetAfterSeconds` (1 h);
  - `GetStatus().Enforcement.Users` / `Records`.

### Fixed (found by Phase 8 testing)
- **Teleport and speed bypass (found in the live Studio run).** The lag time credit was
  uncapped, so standing still for a minute licensed a ~1,200-stud jump. It is now
  capped at `Movement.MaxTimeCreditSeconds` (3 s).
- **False positive (found by the simulation).** A lag spike in mid-air looked like
  hovering (`Flight`). Frozen airborne samples that still report velocity are now
  treated as a replication stall, with credit capped at 3 s per airborne period.
- **Memory growth (found by the soak).**
  - A forgotten player's decaying risk re-created enforcement state.
  - Review cases opened as risk *decayed* through Investigation, and for departed
    players; they now open only on a rising band for a present player.
  - Open cases pinned departed users.
  - Departed risk users waited for every kind to decay below 0.05; they are now
    forgotten below total risk 1.

### Changed (calibration from the evaluation)
These are behaviour changes; see docs/EVALUATION.md for the before and after.
- `Risk.RepeatCap` 2 → 3: sustained severe evidence can now reach correction and review.
- Movement `Severity.Speed` 20 → 25.
- Behavioral `Severity.SignalAnomaly` 20 → 30 and `Accuracy`/`Headshots` 25 → 30;
  `Violation.ConfidenceScale` 6 → 3. Statistical evidence still never removes anyone
  automatically.

### Verified
- 420/420 tests under Lune and in Roblox Studio's VM (Studio 0.748).
- Evaluation, 10 seeds, 50 honest player-hours:
  - honest players: 0 with evidence, 0 restricted, 0 would-be removals;
  - every cheat type caught in every run.
  - CPU: ~1 ms per tick at 50 players (Lune).
  - The soak's per-user structures are bounded.
- Live Studio, Play mode, with the real `RobloxEnvironment`:
  - **Movement correction.** A client-side 150-stud teleport was pulled back within
    0.25–0.28 s, three times out of three. The correction is visible on the client.
  - **Latency.** Honest walking and jumping for 45 s at a measured 278 ms and 790 ms
    NetworkPing (Network Simulator) produced no evidence. A teleport at 790 ms was still
    detected.
  - **DataStore failure.** In an unpublished place every call fails. Risk continues
    without history; enforcement retries 5×, then audits `PersistenceFailed`. Nothing
    crashes.
  - **Kicks.** `Player:Kick` fires `PlayerRemoving` after 1.1 s and is audited with the
    staff name.
  - **Ban API.** `Players:BanAsync` returns success instantly *and does nothing* in an
    unpublished place (PlaceId 0), so real bans can only be verified in a published game.

### Not verified (open for the canary period)
- Several real clients in one server (multi-client network load). Studio's
  single-client Play was the limit here.
- `Players:BanAsync` and DataStore persistence in a published experience.

## [0.7.0] — 2026-11-20 — Phase 7: Hybrid Enforcement

**Behaviour change:** ACF now acts on risk. With the defaults (Hybrid, AutoKick on,
AutoBan off):

- it restricts tagged operations at risk ≥ 40;
- it corrects movement at ≥ 60;
- it kicks at ≥ 80 only on corroborated, non-statistical evidence.

Use `Enforcement.ShadowMode = true` first. Documented in docs/ENFORCEMENT.md.

### Added
- `Enforcement/EnforcementManager`, a core service separate from detection and risk:
  - **Levels 0–7:** logging, monitoring, rejection, correction, temporary restriction,
    kick, temporary ban, permanent ban.
  - **Restrictions** by risk-group tags:
    - `ProtectEvent/ProtectFunction` spec `Restrict = "<tag>" | true`, rejection reason
      `restricted`, not scored as suspicion;
    - Combat refuses shots from Combat/Aim-restricted players (`Reason = "Restricted"`).
  - **Movement correction** at level 3, rate-limited and distance-capped, back to the
    last grounded clean position. The correction is authorised, so it isn't scored.
  - **Corroboration rule** for automatic removal. Statistical evidence alone never
    removes anyone automatically.
  - **Escalation:** kick → temporary ban (24 h, doubling, capped) → permanent-ban
    proposal. Permanent bans need approval by default. A removed player is not removed
    again until new evidence arrives.
  - **Cases:** a staff approval workflow (`GetCases`, `ApproveCase`, `RejectCase`,
    `OnEnforcementCase`), review cases at the Investigation band, and expiry.
  - **Staff actions:** `Kick`, `Ban` (offline too), `Unban`, and overrides (`Exempt` or a
    level, with expiry). Every one requires `By`.
  - **Ban records** persisted per user: active ban, expiry, history and appeal
    references (`ACF-XXXXXXXX`).
    - Enforced on join.
    - Roblox `Players:BanAsync` as well, by default.
    - Immediate effect even when storage fails.
    - Retries, and a flush at shutdown.
  - **Audit log:** every decision and staff action. Bounded, repeats summarised,
    published on `ACF.EnforcementAudit`.
  - **Shadow mode:** everything decided and audited, nothing automatic applied.
- `EnforcementPolicy` and `BanRecords` (pure), `AuditLog`.
- Platform:
  - `RobloxEnforcer` (Kick, BanAsync, UnbanAsync);
  - `Environment.Enforcer`;
  - `PhysicsService.Correct`.
- Module context: `context.Enforcement` (`IsRestricted`, `ShouldCorrect`, `NoteCorrected`).
- Topics `ACF.EnforcementAudit` and `ACF.EnforcementCase`.
- Tests: `Unit/EnforcementPolicy` (with BanRecords) and `Integration/Enforcement` (21).
  Fakes for the enforcer, physics correction and per-scope store counters. 405 tests.

### Fixed during the phase
- The detection that triggered a kick also cleared the "wait for new evidence" guard, so
  a kicked player would have been kicked again on rejoin. Only evidence after the
  removal now counts.
- Ban records for offline players were written only on the next tick; they are now
  written as soon as the record has loaded.
- Bans no longer wait for the stored record to load before removing the player.

### Verified (offline)
- Seven mutation checks are each caught: statistical corroboration, unapproved permanent
  bans, shadow mode, the new-evidence guard, the join ban check, exempt overrides and
  the temporary-restriction window.
- The false-positive suites (MovementLegit, CombatLegit, BehavioralLegit) are unchanged
  and clean.
- No measurable benchmark regression on remote, combat or movement paths.
- Not yet run in Studio. `BanAsync`, `Kick` and DataStore records need a live check.

## [0.6.0] — 2026-11-20 — Phase 6: Risk Scoring and Evidence

### Added
- `Intelligence/RiskEngine`, a core service: one risk score (0–100) per player from every
  accepted detection, built-in or custom. Documented in docs/RISK.md.
  - Confidence-weighted, exponentially decaying evidence per (Detector, Kind). The
    first detection adds severity × confidence, matching
    `R = Σ wᵢ·cᵢ·e^(−λtᵢ)`.
  - Duplicate suppression: repeats within `RepeatSuppressSeconds` are not added, and
    later repeats saturate at `RepeatCap` × severity × confidence.
  - Correlation groups (`Risk.Groups`): related kinds count once plus 25% of the rest.
  - Bands Normal / Increased / Restrict / Investigation / EnforcementReview
    (20/40/60/80), with a new `ACF.RiskChanged` topic on every band change.
  - Noisy-OR confidence across independent groups.
  - Session risk per UserId: rejoining does not reset it.
  - Opt-in persistent risk (`Risk.Persistence`):
    - saved only for sessions ending at ≥ 40;
    - seeds later sessions as weighted, decayed and capped "History", which on its own
      reaches at most Increased;
    - batched writes with retries, flushed on Stop and at server shutdown.
  - Evidence history, plain-text explanations, and the `ResetRisk` staff override.
- `Intelligence/RiskModel`: the pure mathematics.
- `Platform/RobloxStore`: a DataStore adapter.
- `Environment.Store` and `Environment.OnShutdown`; RobloxEnvironment wires
  `BindToClose`.
- API:
  - `ACF:GetRisk`, `ACF:GetRiskHistory`, `ACF:ResetRisk`, `ACF:OnRiskChanged`;
  - `GetStatus().Risk`.
- Tests: `Unit/RiskModel`, `Integration/Risk` and `Integration/RiskPersistence` (37 new);
  fake Store with latency and failure injection; risk benchmarks. 372 tests in total.

### Verified (offline)
- The R formula holds to four decimal places.
- A burst of 20 duplicates counts once.
- 20 minutes of one repeated cheat stays in Restrict.
- Correlated detections give 37.5, where independent ones give 60.
- Persistence:
  - load failure, corrupt records and late loads are handled safely;
  - failed writes are retried, then given up;
  - writes are batched;
  - shutdown flushes everything.
- Eight mutation checks are each caught: correlation, saturation, suppression,
  confidence weighting, the stale-load guard, the history cap, the save threshold and
  save retries.
- Not yet run in Studio. The real DataStore needs API access enabled.

## [0.5.0] — 2026-11-19 — Phase 5: Behavioural Intelligence

### Added
- Built-in `Behavioral` module (on by default), documented in docs/BEHAVIORAL.md:
  - **Signals** (`ACF:DefineSignal`, `ACF:RecordSignal`): per-player resource and
    progression rates. A robust median-player population baseline, or a game-defined
    one, feeds a clamped CUSUM. `SignalAnomaly` needs both a CUSUM alarm and ≥ 3× the
    baseline. `MaxPerSecond` adds a hard limit (`SignalRateLimit`).
  - **Accuracy and headshots** from Combat's validated shots: Wilson lower bounds
    against the server's own ratio for that weapon, excluding the player, with
    absolute floors (`AccuracyAnomaly`, `HeadshotAnomaly`).
  - **Actions** (`ACF:RecordAction`): machine-regular timing (`RegularTiming`) and
    sustained invalid actions (`RepeatedInvalidActions`).
  - `ACF:GetBehaviorProfile(player)` and `ACF:GetBehaviorStats()`.
- `Intelligence/Statistics`: pure EWMA, CUSUM, floored z-scores, Wilson bound, decay.
- Combat publishes `Combat.Shot` for every accepted shot and first projectile hit.
- Module context `Events:Has(topic)`, for optional coupling between modules.
- Tests:
  - `Unit/Statistics` and `Unit/BehavioralDetectors`;
  - `Integration/BehavioralLegit`: 10 legitimate scenarios and the false-positive guard;
  - `ExploitSimulation/Behavioral`: 15 tests;
  - behavioural benchmarks.
  - 334 tests in total.

### Changed
- `Modules.Economy` is no longer a reserved future module. Resource-gain detection is
  Behavioral signals; configuring `Economy` now logs "Configured module is not
  registered". **Breaking** only for configs that set `Modules.Economy`, which was
  never functional.

### Verified (offline)
- No behavioural evidence for:
  - steady, lumpy, bursty, 2×-gamepass, AFK and late-joining earners;
  - a server-wide double-coins event;
  - shooters up to 80% accuracy and 55% headshots over 9,000 validated shots;
  - human clicking (CV 4–15%);
  - occasional invalid purchases.
- Reported:
  - 10× (within 60 s), 4×, colluding and from-join currency exploits;
  - 99%-accuracy aimbot, and head-lock;
  - 1 s and 0.5 s autoclickers;
  - invalid-action floods.
- Mutation checks confirm the suites aren't vacuous. Breaking any one of these fails
  tests: the median, the z clamp, the Wilson bound, the MinRatio guard, idle-bucket
  counting, the timing check.
- Not yet run in Studio.

### Added (since 0.4.0, before Phase 5)
- `RobloxPhysics.create({ GetCharacters = fn })`: choose which characters raycasts ignore.
  Needed when ACF tracks server-controlled characters (bots, NPCs).

### Verified
- NPC load test in Studio:
  - 50 real characters at 1.43 ms per sampling tick;
  - 5,100 combat validations at ~37 µs each;
  - flat memory over 60 s;
  - 0 honest bots flagged, and both cheating bots flagged.
- Morphs: four character replacements across R15/R6/scaled rigs with no false detections,
  and detection still working afterwards.

### Fixed
- Hits on head-worn accessories (hats, hair, face accessories) now count as headshots.
  This was found during the Studio verification session.

### Verified
- In Studio, live: R6 and R15 rigs, seats and vehicles, a moving platform (GroundSpeed),
  ladders, terrain water and accessory hits. See TESTING.md.

## [0.4.0] — 2026-11-17 — Phase 4: Combat Integrity

### Added
- Built-in `Combat` module (on by default; passive until weapons are defined):
  server-authoritative shot and hit validation with server-computed damage.
- Weapon kinds Hitscan, Projectile, Melee and Ability, with:
  - falloff and headshot multipliers;
  - fire rate with burst, and cooldowns;
  - magazines, reserve ammo and server-timed reloads;
  - projectile speed, gravity, lifetime and pierce.
- Checks:
  - ownership, shooter alive, ammo, reloading, cooldown, origin and fire rate;
  - hit-point mismatch, self hit, dead target, range, friendly fire and line of sight;
  - projectile unknown/duplicate shot, expiry, arrival timing and trajectory (searched
    along the real arc).
- `API/IntegrationAPI`, assigned explicitly onto the public API (so `--!strict` games see the types):
  - `DefineWeapon`, `DefineAbility` and `SetCombatProvider` (Inventory, CanDamage,
    DamageCalculator);
  - `ValidateAttack`, `FireProjectile` and `ValidateProjectileHit`;
  - `Reload`, `GetAmmo`, `SetAmmo` and `GetCombatStats`;
  - `ACF.CombatClaim`.
- `Environment.Physics:Describe(part)` plus its Roblox implementation.
- `examples/CombatWiring.server.luau`.
- Combat benchmarks in `tools/bench.sh`.
- 42 new tests (278 total): a 16-scenario legitimate-combat suite and a combat exploit suite.
- `tools/verify.sh`: a single pre-commit gate (tests, type check, build).

### Fixed (follow-up to the initial 0.4.0 commit)
- The combat methods were attached to `ACF` dynamically, so the type checker could not
  see them (`ACF:DefineWeapon` was a type error in strict-mode games). They are now
  assigned explicitly.

## [0.3.0] — 2026-11-13 — Phase 3: Movement & Physics

### Added
- Built-in `Movement` module (on by default): server-observed sampling and detectors for
  speed, teleport, flight, infinite jump, acceleration and noclip (crossed / embedded).
- Built-in `Physics` module (on by default, depends on Movement): fling, extreme velocity,
  velocity mismatch (tp-walk).
- Built-in `Telemetry` module (off by default) with `Shared/Protocol` and the client script
  `StarterPlayerScripts.ACFTelemetry`.
- Handling for respawn, seats/vehicles, death, climbing, swimming, falling, moving
  platforms, lag freezes (time credit) and ping.
- `ACF:AuthorizeTeleport(player, seconds?)` and
  `ACF:AllowMovement(player, kind, seconds, options?)`.
- `Environment.Physics` (Sample, Cast) and `Environment.Remotes`, with Roblox
  implementations in `Platform/RobloxPhysics.luau` and `RobloxEnvironment`; exposed to
  modules as `context.Platform`.
- `Intelligence/ViolationTracker`: the shared decaying violation score.
- Movement tick benchmark in `tools/bench.sh`.
- 62 new tests (236 total), including a 19-scenario legitimate-play false-positive suite.

### Changed
- RemoteSecurity's suspicion scoring now uses ViolationTracker (behaviour unchanged).
- The Rojo projects map the telemetry client script into StarterPlayerScripts.
- The test fakes provide a physics world (boxes, Roblox ray semantics) and remote provider.

## [0.2.0] — 2026-11-13 — Phase 2: Remote Security

### Added
- Built-in `RemoteSecurity` module (enabled by default, `Category = "Remote"`):
  `ACF:ProtectEvent`, `ACF:ProtectFunction`, `ACF:FindUnprotectedRemotes`, `ACF:GetRemoteStats`.
- Request pipeline: session → rate limit → argument validation → character → cooldown →
  permission → ownership → idempotency → handler. Fails closed while stopped.
- `ACF.Validators`: number, integer, string, boolean, literal, enum, optional, union,
  custom, array, struct, map, instance, vector3, cframe. Secure defaults, bounded
  traversal, no attacker text in errors.
- `ACF.Permissions` (userIds, groupRank, all, any) and `ACF.Ownership`
  (descendantOfCharacter, descendantOf, attribute).
- Suspicion model: decaying weighted score per (player, remote, reason) that reports to
  evidence only on sustained abuse.
- `Framework:GetModule(name)` and `Framework:GetModuleState(name)`.
- Built-in module registry (`Detection/BuiltIns.luau`).
- `tools/bench.sh` hot-path benchmarks.
- 57 new tests (174 total).

### Changed
- Built-in modules are registered automatically at `Initialize`, so `RemoteSecurity` now
  appears in `GetStatus().Modules`.
- Test fakes create real Instances/Vector3/CFrame when run in Studio.

## [0.1.0] — 2026-11-13 — Phase 1: Core Foundation

### Added
- `Framework` orchestrator with the `Created → Initialized → Running ⇄ Stopped → Destroyed`
  lifecycle and atomic, retryable `Initialize`.
- `ModuleLoader`: definition validation, dependency resolution (missing, disabled and
  cyclic dependencies), explicit lifecycle state machine, `Critical` modules, error
  isolation with dependent blocking.
- `Configuration`: schema constructors (`Number`, `Boolean`, `String`, `Enum`, `Table`,
  `Map`, `Open`), all-errors validation with typo suggestions, deep-frozen values,
  per-module sections validated at registration.
- `Logger`: levels, scopes, structured fields, per-message rate limiting with suppressed
  counts, ring buffer.
- `EventBus`: strict topics, error isolation, auto-disconnect of failing handlers, dispatch
  depth limit.
- `Scheduler`: central Heartbeat-driven interval and one-shot tasks, per-task CPU stats,
  stall skipping, automatic disabling of failing tasks.
- `PlayerRegistry`: sessions with per-join SessionIds, character generations, per-session
  state and cleanup.
- `DetectionManager`: report validation, identity binding, per-detector rate limiting,
  detail sanitisation, frozen `DetectionEvent`s.
- `EvidenceManager`: per-UserId bounded evidence, duplicate merging, expiry, summaries.
- `PublicAPI` singleton (`ACF:Initialize/RegisterModule/Start/Stop/...`).
- Example module `LeaderstatRateDetector` and bootstrap script.
- 117 tests (unit, integration, exploit simulation), runnable under Lune and in Studio.
- Tooling: `tools/test.sh`, `tools/check.sh` (luau-lsp strict analysis), `tools/build.sh`,
  `tools/bootstrap-tools.sh`.

`DEVELOPMENT.md`:

```md
# Developing ACF

## Toolchain

All tools live in `.bin/` (git-ignored). `tools/bootstrap-tools.sh` downloads pinned versions:

| Tool | Version | Use |
|------|---------|-----|
| Rojo | 7.7.0 | Sync to Studio (`rojo serve`, port 34877 / tests 34878), build place files, sourcemaps |
| Lune | 0.10.5 | Runs the test suite outside Roblox |
| luau-lsp | 1.70.1 | `analyze` = strict type checking against Roblox definitions |

Scripts are POSIX shell (Git Bash on Windows).

## Repository layout

```
src/Server/            → ServerScriptService.ACF (init.luau returns the PublicAPI)
  Types.luau             shared server types
  Core/                  Framework, ModuleLoader, Configuration, Logger, EventBus,
                         Scheduler, PlayerRegistry, Cleanup, RateLimiter, Topics, DefaultConfig
  Detection/             DetectionManager, BuiltIns registry
    RemoteSecurity/      RemoteSecurity module, Validators, Policies
    Movement/            Movement module + pure MovementDetectors
    Physics/             Physics module
    Combat/              Combat module + pure CombatRules
    Behavioral/          Behavioral module + pure BehavioralDetectors
    Telemetry/           optional heartbeat module
  Intelligence/          EvidenceManager, ViolationTracker, Statistics, RiskEngine, RiskModel
  Enforcement/           EnforcementManager, pure EnforcementPolicy and BanRecords, AuditLog
  Platform/              RobloxEnvironment, RobloxPhysics, RobloxStore, RobloxEnforcer (the only Roblox-service code)
  API/                   PublicAPI, IntegrationAPI (game-defined rules)
src/Shared/            → ReplicatedStorage.ACFShared: Constants, Protocol (client-visible; keep it harmless)
src/Client/            → StarterPlayerScripts.ACFTelemetry (optional heartbeat; untrusted)
examples/              → ServerStorage.ACFExamples in the test place
tests/Specs/           → ServerStorage.ACFTests (portable specs + TestKit, Fakes, Runner)
tests/lune/            Lune-only harness (virtual DataModel)
tools/                 test / check / bench / build / bootstrap scripts; verify = pre-commit gate;
                       evaluate = Phase 8 evaluation; release = canary/stable builds (RELEASING.md)
docs/                  API, module authoring, configuration, troubleshooting
```

## Conventions

- `--!strict` in every source file. `tools/check.sh` must report no errors.
- Require with `script.Parent...`, never by string path. This keeps the source compatible
  with every Studio version and with the Lune harness.
- Never call `game:GetService` for platform services outside `Platform/`. Take them from the
  `Environment` instead.
- Raise on developer error (bad config, bad definitions, wrong state). Isolate and log runtime
  faults; never let them escape into the framework.
- Log messages are constant strings. Variable data goes in fields, because rate limiting
  keys on scope + message.
- Every per-player or per-key structure must have an explicit bound and a release path.
- New behaviour ships with tests, including at least one adversarial case where relevant.
- Never log or embed attacker-supplied strings in messages; describe types and lengths.
- Guard Debug logging on hot paths with `Logger:IsEnabled("Debug")`.
- Detectors emit weighted violations into a `ViolationTracker`; never call
  `context:Report` straight from a single observation.
- Keep detector maths pure (plain numbers in, violations out). Roblox access belongs in
  `Platform/`.
- Update CHANGELOG.md, ROADMAP.md and the relevant docs in the same change.

## Before every commit

Run `tools/verify.sh` and commit only if it prints `VERIFY OK`. It stops at the first
failing step, so a failed type check can never slip through a command chain.

## Workflow per phase

1. Inspect the current code and docs. 2. Plan. 3. Implement. 4. `tools/test.sh`.
5. `tools/check.sh`. 6. Review for architectural problems. 7. Fix. 8. Update docs.
9. Summarise completed and remaining work in ROADMAP.md.

## Versioning

- SemVer; `src/Shared/Constants.luau` `VERSION` is the single source of truth.
- Each phase bumps the minor version until 1.0.0.
- Release channels (dev / canary / stable) are introduced in Phase 8.

```

`README.md`:

```md
# ACF — Anti-Cheat Framework for Roblox

ACF is a modular, server-authoritative anti-cheat framework for Roblox experiences. It gives
detection modules a common lifecycle, a validated configuration system, a player session
registry, an event bus, a central scheduler and a bounded evidence store, so each detector
only has to implement its own detection logic.

> **Status: v1.0.0 release candidate. All 8 phases are complete.** 420 tests pass offline
> and inside Roblox Studio's VM. A population evaluation (docs/EVALUATION.md) covering
> 50 honest player-hours found no false positives, and every cheat type was caught.
> Live Studio checks covered movement correction against a client-side teleport,
> 790 ms network latency, kicks, and DataStore failure.
>
> **Ships on the canary channel.** Stable 1.0.0 is promoted after a canary period in
> real servers ([RELEASING.md](RELEASING.md)), because multi-client load and the live
> Roblox ban API can only be verified in a published game.
>
> **ACF now enforces.** With the defaults it restricts suspicious players, corrects
> movement, and kicks automatically only on corroborated, non-statistical evidence.
> Bans need `AutoBan` or staff approval; permanent bans always need approval. **Start
> with `Enforcement = { ShadowMode = true }`** and read [docs/ENFORCEMENT.md](docs/ENFORCEMENT.md).

**Principle: never trust the client.** ACF runs entirely on the server. Nothing in
`ServerScriptService.ACF` replicates to clients.

## Installation

**With Rojo:** add this repository's `src/` to your project, mapping:

| Path            | DataModel location                 |
|-----------------|------------------------------------|
| `src/Server`    | `ServerScriptService.ACF`          |
| `src/Shared`    | `ReplicatedStorage.ACFShared`      |
| `src/Client/ClientTelemetry.client.luau` | `StarterPlayer.StarterPlayerScripts.ACFTelemetry` (optional; only used with `Modules.Telemetry = true`) |

`default.project.json` shows the mapping (serve port 34877).

**Without Rojo:** run `tools/build.sh` and open `build/ACF.rbxl`. Copy `ServerScriptService.ACF`
and `ReplicatedStorage.ACFShared` into your place.

## Quick start

```lua
-- ServerScriptService/ACFBootstrap (Script)
local ACF = require(game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF)

ACF:Initialize({
    LogLevel = "Info",
    Enforcement = { Mode = "Hybrid", AutoKick = true, AutoBan = false, ShadowMode = true },
    ModuleSettings = {
        LeaderstatRate = { StatName = "Coins", MaxGainPerSecond = 50 },
    },
})

ACF:RegisterModule(require(path.to.LeaderstatRateDetector).new(ACF.Schema))

local V = ACF.Validators
ACF:ProtectEvent(game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.BuyItem, {
    Arguments = { V.string({ MaxLength = 32 }), V.integer({ Min = 1, Max = 99 }) },
    RateLimit = { Burst = 5, PerSecond = 1 },
    Handler = function(player, itemId, quantity) --[[ validated ]] end,
})

ACF:OnDetection(function(payload)
    print(payload.Event.Detector, payload.Event.Kind, payload.Event.UserId)
end)

ACF:Start()
```

Invalid configuration fails at `Initialize` and lists every problem, with suggestions for typos:

```
ACF configuration is invalid:
  - Enforcment: unknown configuration key (did you mean 'Enforcement'?)
  - Evidence.MaxRecordsPerPlayer: 0 is below the minimum of 1
```
