`docs/ENFORCEMENT.md`:

```md
# Hybrid Enforcement (v1.0.0)

Enforcement is the third subsystem, separate from the other two:

| Subsystem | Answers | Lives in |
|-----------|---------|----------|
| Detection | What happened? | Modules |
| Evidence and risk | How worried should we be? | `Intelligence/` |
| Enforcement | What do we do about it? | `Enforcement/` |

Enforcement reads risk bands. It never detects anything, and modules never punish
anyone: they only ask enforcement questions such as "is this player restricted for
Economy?".

> **Before going live, run in shadow mode.** Set
> `Enforcement = { ShadowMode = true }` and ACF makes every decision and writes every
> audit entry, but applies nothing. Watch `ACF:GetAuditLog()` and the cases on real
> servers, tune severities and bands, then turn shadow mode off.

## Levels

| Level | Name | Effect | Comes from |
|-------|------|--------|------------|
| 0 | Logging | None | Band `Normal` |
| 1 | Monitoring | None (visible in `GetEnforcement`) | Band `Increased` |
| 2 | Rejection | Operations tagged with the player's risk groups are refused | Band `Restrict` |
| 3 | Correction | As 2; Movement-tagged players are rubber-banded | Band `Investigation`, which also opens a review case |
| 4 | Restriction | *Every* tagged operation refused for `TemporaryRestrictionSeconds` (600) | Band `EnforcementReview` |
| 5 | Kick | Removed, with an appeal reference | `EnforcementReview`: automatic if corroborated, otherwise a case |
| 6 | Temporary ban | Banned for 24 h, doubling per earlier temporary ban (capped at 720 h) | After `KicksBeforeTempBan` (2) kicks in 7 days |
| 7 | Permanent ban | Banned | After `TempBansBeforePermanentProposal` (2) temporary bans. **Always needs approval by default.** |

Levels 0–4 are automatic in `Hybrid` and `Manual` mode, and they follow the band both
up and down. Level 4 lasts at least its window once entered.

### When does ACF remove a player on its own?

All of these must hold:

1. Mode is `Hybrid`, and the switch for that level is on:
   - `AutoKick` (default on) for a kick;
   - `AutoBan` (default **off**) for a temporary ban;
   - `AutoBan` with `Bans.RequireApprovalForPermanentBan = false` for a permanent ban.
2. The player is in the `EnforcementReview` band (risk ≥ 80).
3. The evidence is **corroborated**:
   - confidence ≥ 0.9, **and**
   - either one non-statistical group ≥ 40, or ≥ 2 groups at ≥ 15 of which at least one
     is non-statistical.

**Statistical evidence alone (`Aim`, `Economy`, `Automation`) never removes anyone
automatically.** Otherwise the decision becomes a case for staff (tested).

After a removal, ACF waits for **new** evidence before removing the player again.
Rejoining with leftover risk only re-applies level 4. A kicked player who keeps
cheating escalates: kick → kick → temporary-ban proposal (tested).

## Wiring it into your game

**Remotes.** Mark operations that should stop for suspicious players:

```lua
ACF:ProtectEvent(Remotes.Trade, {
    Arguments = { V.string({ MaxLength = 32 }) },
    Restrict = "Economy",   -- refused while the player is restricted for Economy
    Handler = doTrade,
})
ACF:ProtectEvent(Remotes.RedeemCode, { Arguments = { ... }, Restrict = true, Handler = ... }) -- any restriction
```

Refusals are reported to the handler as rejection reason `restricted`. They are **not**
counted as suspicious behaviour, since they are ACF's own decision.

**Combat.** Players restricted for `Combat` or `Aim` cannot deal damage: `ValidateAttack`
returns `Reason = "Restricted"`. No code is needed.

**Movement correction.** At level 3 and above, a `Movement`-tagged player is moved back
to their last grounded position without violations when they speed, teleport, fly or
noclip.

- It happens at most once per `Correction.CooldownSeconds` (1 s).
- It only applies within `MaxDistance` (200 studs).
- It never applies across a respawn.
- The correction is authorised, so it is not itself scored as a teleport.

**Your own code:**

```lua
if ACF:IsRestricted(player, "Economy", "Shop.Buy") then return end
```

**Staff notifications.** Cases need people. Forward them however your team works:

```lua
ACF:OnEnforcementCase(function(case)
    if case.Status == "Open" then
        notifyStaff(case.CaseId, case.UserId, case.ProposedLevel, case.Explanation)
    end
end)
```

## Cases: the approval workflow

Cases open in three situations:

- **Action:** a removal is proposed at `EnforcementReview` without being allowed to run
  automatically.
- **Review, on Investigation:** the band *rises* into `Investigation` while the player is
  in the server. Decaying through the band, or having left, opens nothing.
- **Review, on long restriction:** the player has been restricted (level 2+) for
  `Cases.ReviewAfterRestrictedSeconds` (10 min). Restriction neutralises a cheat but also
  stops new evidence, so without this the restricted cheater never reached staff. It
  also guarantees a human look at anyone restricted for long, whatever the reason.
  Added in Phase 8.

| Field | Meaning |
|-------|---------|
| `CaseId` / `AppealRef` | e.g. `ACF-3F9A21C0` |
| `Type` | `Action` (approve a proposed level 5–7) or `Review` (investigate; staff pick the level) |
| `Status` | `Open`, `Approved`, `Rejected`, `Expired` (after 72 h), or `Shadow` (shadow mode, cannot be approved) |
| `Reason`, `Explanation`, `Risk`, `Band` | Why, captured when the case opened |
| `DecidedBy`, `DecidedAt`, `Note`, `ExecutedLevel` | Filled in by the decision |

```lua
for _, case in ACF:GetCases({ Status = "Open" }) do ... end
ACF:ApproveCase(caseId, { By = "Mod_Alice", Note = "watched replay" })     -- proposed level
ACF:ApproveCase(caseId, { By = "Mod_Alice", Level = 6, Hours = 48 })        -- choose the action
ACF:RejectCase(caseId,  { By = "Mod_Bob", Note = "legit, known pro" })
```

Cases live in the server that opened them. Use `OnEnforcementCase` to keep them
elsewhere, for example a Discord webhook or your own DataStore.

## Staff actions and overrides

Every staff action needs `By`, the person acting. It is written to the audit log, and to
the ban record where relevant.

```lua
local appeal = ACF:Kick(player, { By = "Mod", Reason = "..." })
local appeal = ACF:Ban(userId, { By = "Admin", Reason = "...", Hours = 72 }) -- omit Hours: permanent
ACF:Unban(userId, { By = "Admin", Reason = "appeal ACF-3F9A21C0 upheld" })
ACF:SetEnforcementOverride(player, { Exempt = true, By = "Admin", Reason = "dev testing", Seconds = 3600 })
ACF:SetEnforcementOverride(player, { Level = 2, By = "Mod", Reason = "watch" })   -- 0-4 only
ACF:ClearEnforcementOverride(player, { By = "Admin" })
```

- **Bans work for offline players** (by UserId).
- **Staff actions run even in shadow mode.** Shadow mode only governs automatic
  decisions.
- **Exempt players** are never restricted, corrected or removed automatically.

## Bans, expiry, history and appeals

Each user has one record in the DataStore `ACF_Bans_v1`, at key `Bans_<UserId>`:

```lua
{ Version = 1, UserId = 123,
  Active = { Level = 6, AppealRef = "ACF-…", Reason = "...", By = "...", ApprovedBy = "...",
             IssuedAt = unix, ExpiresAt = unix | nil },
  History = { { Type = "Kick" | "TemporaryBan" | "PermanentBan" | "Unban" | "Expired",
                At, AppealRef, Reason, By, ExpiresAt } } }            -- last 20
```

- **Enforced on join.** Every join loads the record. An active ban kicks the player with
  the ban message and the appeal reference. An expired ban moves into history as
  `Expired`.
- **Roblox ban API.** By default bans also call `Players:BanAsync`, universe-wide, with
  the duration. If that call fails, it is logged, and ACF's own record still enforces the
  ban on join (tested). Set `Bans.UseRobloxBanApi = false` where the API is unavailable.
- **Immediate effect.** A ban removes the player at once, even when the DataStore is
  failing. The record write follows, retried up to `MaxAttempts`. If it can never load
  the old record, it writes a fresh one and audits `PersistenceFailed` (tested).
- **Shutdown.** Pending writes are flushed at shutdown, using `BindToClose`.
- **Concurrency.** Records use read-modify-write. Two servers banning the *same* user
  at the *same* moment could lose one history entry; the ban itself still applies.
- **Lookups.** `ACF:LoadEnforcementRecord(userId, callback)` returns the ban and history
  for an appeal lookup.

## Audit log

Every decision and staff action produces an entry:

```
LevelChanged  Rejected  Corrected  Kicked  Banned  Unbanned  BanEnforced  BanExpired
CaseOpened  CaseUpdated  CaseApproved  CaseRejected  CaseExpired
OverrideSet  OverrideCleared  ShadowAction  PersistenceFailed
```

- **Fields.** Entries carry `UserId`, `By`, `Reason`, `AppealRef`, `CaseId`, `Level`,
  `Band`, `Risk`, `Tags` and `Shadow`, as relevant.
- **Repeats.** Repeated rejections or corrections of one user are summarised: one entry
  per minute, with `Count`.
- **Memory.** The last 500 entries are kept in memory (`ACF:GetAuditLog({ UserId?, Type?,
  Since? })`).
- **Keeping it.** Every entry is also published on `ACF.EnforcementAudit`. To keep the log
  permanently, subscribe with `ACF:OnEnforcementAudit(handler)` and store it yourself.

## Modes

| Mode | Levels 0–4 | Kick / bans |
|------|------------|-------------|
| `Hybrid` (default) | Automatic | Automatic only when corroborated and switched on; otherwise cases |
| `Manual` | Automatic | Always cases |
| `Off` | Level 0 only | Staff actions only |

`ShadowMode = true` works with any mode: everything is decided and audited, and nothing
automatic is applied.

## API

| Method | Notes |
|--------|-------|
| `ACF:GetEnforcement(player)` | `{ Level, LevelName, Tags, RestrictedFor, Shadow, Override, OpenCase, Ban, History }` |
| `ACF:IsRestricted(player, tag, operation?)` | `tag` is a risk group, or `true` for any restriction |
| `ACF:Kick`, `ACF:Ban`, `ACF:Unban` | Return the appeal reference (Kick, Ban) |
| `ACF:SetEnforcementOverride`, `ACF:ClearEnforcementOverride` | As above |
| `ACF:GetCases(filter?)`, `ACF:ApproveCase`, `ACF:RejectCase` | Cases |
| `ACF:GetAuditLog(filter?)` | Audit entries |
| `ACF:OnEnforcementCase(handler)`, `ACF:OnEnforcementAudit(handler)` | Subscriptions |
| `ACF:LoadEnforcementRecord(player, callback)` | Stored ban and history |
| `ACF:GetStatus().Enforcement` | Kicks, bans, rejections, corrections, open cases, levels, pending writes |

## Configuration (`Enforcement`)

| Key | Default |
|-----|---------|
| Mode | `Hybrid` |
| AutoKick | true |
| AutoBan | false |
| ShadowMode | false |
| BandLevels | Normal 0, Increased 1, Restrict 2, Investigation 3, EnforcementReview 4 |
| TagMinScore | 15 |
| StatisticalGroups | Aim, Economy, Automation |
| Corroboration.MinConfidence | 0.9 |
| Corroboration.MinIndependentGroups | 2 |
| Corroboration.HardEvidenceMinScore | 40 |
| TemporaryRestrictionSeconds | 600 |
| Correction.Enabled | true |
| Correction.CooldownSeconds | 1 |
| Correction.MaxDistance | 200 |
| Bans.DataStoreName | `ACF_Bans_v1` |
| Bans.CheckOnJoin | true |
| Bans.UseRobloxBanApi | true |
| Bans.TempBanHours | 24 |
| Bans.EscalationFactor | 2 |
| Bans.MaxTempBanHours | 720 |
| Bans.KicksBeforeTempBan | 2 |
| Bans.TempBansBeforePermanentProposal | 2 |
| Bans.RepeatWindowDays | 7 |
| Bans.RequireApprovalForPermanentBan | **true** |
| Bans.MaxHistory | 20 |
| Bans.FlushIntervalSeconds | 5 |
| Bans.MaxAttempts | 5 |
| Cases.MaxOpen | 200 |
| Cases.ExpiryHours | 72 |
| Cases.ReviewAfterRestrictedSeconds | 600 |
| ForgetAfterSeconds | 3600 |
| Audit.MaxEntries | 500 |
| Audit.RepeatIntervalSeconds | 60 |
| Messages.Kick | Contains `{appeal}` |
| Messages.TempBan | Contains `{until}` and `{appeal}` |
| Messages.PermanentBan | Contains `{appeal}` |

## Limits

- **Correction is server-authoritative but coarse.** It moves the character back; it does
  not replay physics. A fast cheater can still gain ground between corrections, but loses
  it on the next one.
- **Restrictions stop at your code.** They only cover tagged remotes, Combat and your own
  `IsRestricted` checks. Untagged remotes are not restricted.
- **Per-server data.** Cases and the in-memory audit log are per server. Ban records
  persist.
- **Memory.** Departed players are forgotten after `ForgetAfterSeconds` (1 h) unless
  something is pending: a write, an override, or a ban record when there is no Store.
  Their record reloads if they return. Measured in a 2-hour churn soak: steady at the
  present players plus the last hour's departures (docs/EVALUATION.md).

```

## Memory and stability soak

120 simulated minutes, 45 players at a time, 2 leaving and 2 joining every minute
(285 distinct players in total). A faulty module throws from a scheduled task
and from an event handler.

Heap is sampled with `collectgarbage("count")` (Lune cannot force a collection), so
individual samples include uncollected garbage; look at the trend, not one value.

`docs/MODULES.md`:

```md
# Writing ACF Detection Modules

A module is a table with a name and four lifecycle methods. Register a **fresh table per
framework instance**: the table is the module instance.

```lua
local function new(Schema)
    local module = {
        Name = "SpeedSampler",          -- identifier, unique, max 48 chars
        Version = "1.0.0",              -- optional
        Category = "Movement",          -- Movement | Physics | Remote | Combat | Economy | Behavioral | Integrity | Custom
        Dependencies = {},              -- optional: names of modules that must run first
        Critical = false,               -- optional: if true, ACF:Start() fails when this module cannot start
        EnabledByDefault = true,        -- optional: Modules = { SpeedSampler = false } overrides
        ConfigSchema = Schema.Table({   -- optional: validated ModuleSettings.SpeedSampler
            MaxSpeed = Schema.Number(32, { Min = 0 }),
        }),
    }
    local context

    function module:Initialize(ctx)  -- one-time setup; no players yet
        context = ctx
    end

    function module:Start()          -- subscribe, schedule; released automatically at Stop
        context.Scheduler:Every("Sample", 0.5, function(now, dt)
            for _, session in context.Players:GetSessions() do
                -- observe server-side state, then maybe:
                -- context:Report(session, { Kind = "SpeedExceeded", Severity = 20, Confidence = 0.6, Details = {...} })
            end
        end)
    end

    function module:Stop() end       -- release anything not acquired through the context
    function module:Destroy() end    -- final teardown

    return module
end
```

## The context

| Member | Description |
|--------|-------------|
| `context.Name` | The module's name |
| `context.Config` | Frozen, validated `ModuleSettings.<Name>` (defaults applied) |
| `context:GetConfig(path)` | Read any global configuration value |
| `context.Logger` | Logger scoped to the module: `:Debug/:Info/:Warn/:Error(message, fields?)`. Keep messages constant; variable data goes in fields. |
| `context.Clock` | `Now()` monotonic seconds, `Wall()` Unix seconds |
| `context.Events:Subscribe(topic, fn)` | Auto-released (see scoping) |
| `context.Events:Declare(suffix)` | Registers and returns the topic `"<Name>.<suffix>"` |
| `context.Events:Publish(topic, payload)` | Only the module's own topics; `ACF.*` topics are reserved |
| `context.Events:Has(topic)` | Whether a topic exists. Use it to subscribe to an optional module's topic, e.g. `Combat.Shot`. |
| `context.Scheduler:Every(name, interval, fn, phase?)` / `:After(name, delay, fn)` | `fn(now, dt)`; auto-released |
| `context.Cleanup:Add(item)` | Function, thread, connection, Instance, or anything with `Disconnect`/`Destroy` |
| `context.Players:GetSession(target)` / `:GetSessions()` | Active sessions |
| `context:Report(target, input)` | Returns `(true, eventId)` or `(false, reason)`; never throws |
| `context:GetModule(name)` | A declared dependency's module table |

**Scoping:** whatever you acquire through the context during `Initialize` is released at
`Destroy`. Whatever you acquire during `Start` is released at `Stop`.

**Per-player state:** `session:GetState("MyModule.Key", factory)` is freed automatically
when the session ends. Prefix keys with your module name. Use `session.Cleanup:Add(...)` for
per-player connections.

## Reporting guidelines

- **Severity (0–100):** how much this observation should weigh at full certainty. Reserve
  high values for observations that legitimate play cannot produce.
- **Confidence (0–1):** how certain you are. A single unusual sample should rarely exceed
  ~0.5. Raise confidence with repeated or corroborated evidence, not with a larger Severity.
- **Kind:** stable machine-readable identifier (≤ 64 chars). Identical `Kind`s merge within
  the duplicate window. Use distinct Kinds for distinct phenomena.
- **Details:** the explanation, i.e. what was measured, the limit, and the time window.
  Sanitised and size-limited; Instances become their full names.
- Detecting is not punishing. Modules never kick or ban; enforcement is a separate
  subsystem (Phase 7).
- Use server-observed state. Client-provided data is a hint at best.

## Worked example

`examples/LeaderstatRateDetector.luau` is a complete module with configuration, scheduling,
per-session state and explained reports. Its tests are in
`tests/Specs/Integration/ExampleDetector.spec.luau`.

```

`docs/MOVEMENT.md`:

```md
# Movement, Physics and Telemetry (v0.3.0)

Three built-in modules:

| Module | Default | Category | What it checks |
|--------|---------|----------|----------------|
| `Movement` | on | Movement | Speed, teleport, flight, infinite jump, acceleration, noclip |
| `Physics` | on (needs Movement) | Physics | Fling, extreme velocity, velocity mismatch (tp-walk) |
| `Telemetry` | **off** | Integrity | Client heartbeat presence and sequence |

All detection is **server-observed**. Every 0.2 s ACF samples each character's root part
and humanoid on the server:

- position and velocity;
- server-side WalkSpeed, JumpPower and JumpHeight;
- distance to the ground (water counts as ground);
- humanoid state, seat, health and ping.

The client is never asked where it is.

**Never on a single sample.** Each detector produces *weighted violations* that feed a
decaying per-player score (`ViolationTracker`). ACF reports only when the score reaches
the threshold. One odd position update cannot reach it.

## Detectors

| Detector | Signal | Weight | Reported Kind |
|----------|--------|--------|---------------|
| Speed | Horizontal distance over a 0.5–2 s window > allowed speed × window (+ ping, + slack) | 1–4, by excess | `SpeedExceeded` |
| Teleport | Displacement between distinct positions larger than the allowed speed covers in the elapsed time (+ ping, + 10 studs), or a rise above jump apex + platform allowance | 4 | `TeleportDetected` |
| Flight | Airborne longer than a full jump arc (+1 s) while falling slower than 4 studs/s | 1 per sample | `Flight` |
| InfiniteJump | Rising ≥ 3 studs mid-air after descending, more than `MaxAirJumps` times | 2.5 | `AirJump` |
| Acceleration | Change in reported horizontal velocity > 600 studs/s² | 1 | `AbnormalAcceleration` |
| Noclip | Segment between samples crosses solid geometry ("Crossed"), or ends inside it ("Embedded") | 2.5 | `PassedThroughGeometry` |
| Fling | Root angular speed > 60 rad/s | 2 | `FlingDetected` |
| ExtremeVelocity | Horizontal > 300 or upward > 250 studs/s (downward ignored) | 2 | `ExtremeVelocity` |
| VelocityMismatch | Observed speed > 2.5 × reported velocity + 8, near or above the allowed speed | 1 | `VelocityMismatch` |

With defaults (threshold 5, half-life 10 s), the following get reported:

| Behaviour | Reported after |
|-----------|----------------|
| Sustained 2× speed | ~1–2 s |
| Two unauthorized teleports | ~10 s apart |
| Hovering | ~1 s past the jump arc |
| Air jumps | Two extra air jumps |
| Noclip | Two walls |

A single teleport, or a single wall crossed, is recorded as a violation but not reported.

**Allowed speed** = `max(server WalkSpeed, MinAllowedSpeed) × SpeedTolerance × multiplier +
platform speed × SpeedTolerance`. Games that change WalkSpeed on the server need no
configuration: client-side WalkSpeed changes never replicate, so the server value is the
authorized one.

## Legitimate movement handled

| Situation | Handling |
|-----------|----------|
| Respawn / character replacement | All movement state resets when `CharacterGeneration` changes |
| Seats and vehicles | Detectors pause while seated; history resets on leaving the seat |
| Death / ragdoll | Paused while dead |
| Climbing, swimming | Exempt from flight and air-jump checks |
| Falling | Speed is horizontal; downward motion never violates; long falls pass the flight check (they fall fast) |
| Moving platforms, trains, conveyors | Speed of the part underfoot is added to the allowed speed |
| Lag / packet bunching | Ping compensation (≤ 0.5 s) plus *time credit*: an unchanged position keeps its old timestamp, so a catch-up after a freeze is measured over the real elapsed time, up to `MaxTimeCreditSeconds` (3 s). The cap closes a bypass found in the Phase 8 Studio run: standing still for a minute used to license a 1,200-stud jump. |
| Lag spike mid-jump | The server keeps the last position *and* the last, non-zero velocity of a stalled client. Airborne samples that don't move while reporting velocity ≥ `StallMinVelocity` are not counted as airtime, up to `MaxStallCreditSeconds` (3 s) per airborne period. A hover cheat reports ~0 velocity and gets no credit. Found by the Phase 8 population simulation. |
| Jumping | Flight allows a full jump arc from the server JumpPower/JumpHeight + 1 s; one rise per airtime is a normal jump |
| Double-jump games | `ModuleSettings.Movement.MaxAirJumps = 1` |
| Server teleports, dashes, jetpacks, knockback | The allowance API below |

These cases are covered by `tests/Specs/Integration/MovementLegit.spec.luau`.

## Allowance API

```lua
-- Right before the server moves a character (PivotTo, spawn points, portals):
ACF:AuthorizeTeleport(player)              -- 1 s window (pass seconds to change)
character:PivotTo(destination)

ACF:AllowMovement(player, "Speed", 2, { Multiplier = 3 })  -- dash / speed boost
ACF:AllowMovement(player, "Flight", 10)    -- jetpack, glider
ACF:AllowMovement(player, "Impulse", 1)    -- knockback, explosion, launch pad
ACF:AllowMovement(player, "Noclip", 5)     -- scripted phasing
ACF:AllowMovement(player, "All", 3)        -- cutscenes, admin tools
```

- **Teleport and Impulse** re-anchor history at the current position, so the displacement
  is never judged.
- **Speed** keeps covering the speed window for `MaxWindowSeconds` after it expires. That
  stops the window that still contains the fast samples from being flagged.

## Configuration

`ModuleSettings.Movement` (defaults):

| Key | Default | Key | Default |
|-----|---------|-----|---------|
| SampleIntervalSeconds | 0.2 | AirborneClearance | 1.5 |
| MinAllowedSpeed | 8 | MaxAirtimeExtraSeconds | 1 |
| SpeedTolerance | 1.25 | MinFallStudsPerSecond | 4 |
| SpeedSlackStuds | 4 | MaxAirJumps | 0 |
| MinWindowSeconds / MaxWindowSeconds | 0.5 / 2 | AirJumpRiseStuds | 3 |
| MaxPingCompensationSeconds | 0.5 | MaxHorizontalAcceleration | 600 |
| TeleportSlackStuds / TeleportMinDistance | 10 / 20 | NoclipMinDistance / NoclipMinThickness | 1 / 0.5 |
| VerticalSlackStuds / MaxVerticalPlatformSpeed | 8 / 25 | MaxTimeCreditSeconds | 3 |
| MaxStallCreditSeconds | 3 | StallMinVelocity | 1 |

Each module also has these tables:

- `Detectors.<Name>` (booleans): turn individual detectors on or off.
- `Severity.<Name>`: Speed 25, Teleport 30, Flight 30, InfiniteJump 25, Acceleration 10, Noclip 30.
  Speed was raised from 20 in Phase 8 so a sustained speed hack reaches correction.
- `Violation`: `HalfLifeSeconds` 10, `ReportThreshold` 5, `ReportIntervalSeconds` 10, `ConfidenceScale` 8.

`ModuleSettings.Physics` has the same tables, plus:

| Key | Default |
|-----|---------|
| MaxAngularSpeed | 60 |
| MaxHorizontalSpeed | 300 |
| MaxUpwardSpeed | 250 |
| MismatchRatio | 2.5 |
| MismatchSlack | 8 |
| MismatchMinFractionOfAllowed | 0.8 |
| Severity | Fling 35, ExtremeVelocity 25, VelocityMismatch 10 |
| Violation.ReportThreshold | 4 |

## Telemetry (optional, weak signal)

```lua
ACF:Initialize({ Modules = { Telemetry = true } })
```

The client script `StarterPlayerScripts.ACFTelemetry` (included in the Rojo projects) sends
`(sequence, fps)` every 5 s to `ReplicatedStorage.ACFRemotes.Telemetry`. That remote is
created by the server and protected by RemoteSecurity.

| Kind | Meaning | Defaults |
|------|---------|----------|
| `TelemetryMissing` | No heartbeat for 20 s, after a 30 s grace period | Severity 5, confidence ≤ 0.5, needs minutes of absence to report |
| `TelemetrySequence` | Sequence repeated or went backwards | Severity 10 |

**Be clear about what this proves.** An exploiter can delete the script or fire the remote
themselves. A *present* heartbeat proves nothing; only absence or malformation is weak
evidence. Lag and packet loss also cause absence, which is why the weight and confidence
are capped low. Never enforce on telemetry alone.

## Platform and testing notes

`Platform/RobloxPhysics.luau` samples via `HumanoidRootPart`, the first `Humanoid`, and
`workspace:Raycast`, excluding all player characters. Water counts as ground; R6 leg height
is accounted for. Under Lune the suite uses a fake world of boxes with Roblox ray semantics:
a ray starting inside a part does not hit it, which the Embedded noclip mode relies on.

The real probe is verified in Studio (TESTING.md): R6/R15/scaled rigs, seats, ladders,
water, moving platforms, morphs, and 50 characters at once.

**Server-controlled characters.** If ACF tracks bots or NPCs (via a custom players adapter),
pass their models so a character's own limbs never block its ground ray:
`RobloxPhysics.create({ GetCharacters = function() return myCharacters end })`.

Performance (Lune, `tools/bench.sh`): about 0.56 ms per tick for 50 players, including two
noclip ray casts per moving player. At 5 Hz that is roughly 0.3% of one core.

```

`docs/REMOTE_SECURITY.md`:

```md
# Remote Security (v0.2.0)

`RemoteSecurity` is a built-in module, enabled by default. It validates every request to the
RemoteEvents and RemoteFunctions you protect, before your handler runs. It also reports
**sustained** abuse as evidence. A single bad request is never reported.

## Protecting a remote

```lua
local ACF = require(game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF)
local V = ACF.Validators
local remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes

ACF:Initialize({})

ACF:ProtectEvent(remotes.BuyItem, {
    Arguments = { V.string({ MaxLength = 32 }), V.integer({ Min = 1, Max = 99 }) },
    RateLimit = { Burst = 5, PerSecond = 1 },
    Cooldown = 0.25,
    Handler = function(player, itemId, quantity)
        -- itemId is a string of at most 32 valid UTF-8 bytes; quantity an integer in 1..99
    end,
})

ACF:ProtectFunction(remotes.GetPrice, {
    Arguments = { V.string({ MaxLength = 32 }) },
    RejectResponse = nil,           -- returned for rejected calls and handler errors
    Handler = function(player, itemId)
        return Prices[itemId]
    end,
})

ACF:Start()
```

Use `Handler` instead of connecting `OnServerEvent` or setting `OnServerInvoke` yourself.
Protections may be created before or after `Start`. Each call returns
`{ Name, Unprotect() }`.

## Spec fields

| Field | Required | Description |
|-------|----------|-------------|
| `Handler` | yes | `function(player, ...validatedArgs)`. Errors are logged, never blamed on the player. |
| `Arguments` | yes | Array of validators, one per argument (`{}` for none). Extra arguments are rejected. Missing ones arrive as `nil`, so use `V.optional`. |
| `Name` | no | Stats and evidence name (default: the remote's Name). Must be unique. |
| `RateLimit` | no | `{ Burst, PerSecond }` token bucket per player (default: `ModuleSettings.RemoteSecurity.DefaultRateLimit`, 10 / 5 per second). |
| `Cooldown` | no | Minimum seconds between *accepted* requests per player. |
| `Permission` | no | `function(player, ...args) -> (allowed, reason?)`. Errors deny (fail closed). |
| `Ownership` | no | Same shape. Use for "does this player own the object referenced?" |
| `Idempotency` | no | `{ Argument = index, WindowSeconds = 60, MaxEntries = 64 }`. Rejects a repeated transaction id (string/number argument) within the window. Memory per player is capped. |
| `RequireCharacter` | no | Reject while the player has no character. |
| `SuspicionWeights` | no | Per-remote overrides of the suspicion weights below (e.g. `{ InvalidArguments = 0 }`). |
| `RejectResponse` | no | RemoteFunctions only. |

Unknown fields raise an error, so typos are caught.

## Validators (`ACF.Validators`)

| Validator | Default limits / notes |
|-----------|------------------------|
| `number({ Min, Max, Integer })`, `integer({ Min, Max })` | NaN and ±infinity always rejected |
| `string({ MinLength, MaxLength, Pattern, AllowInvalidUtf8 })` | MaxLength **256**, valid UTF-8 required |
| `boolean()`, `literal(value)`, `enum({ ... })` | |
| `optional(v)`, `union(v1, v2, ...)`, `custom(name, fn)` | `custom` errors count as rejection |
| `array(v, { MinLength, MaxLength })` | MaxLength **64**; sequential keys only, no holes |
| `struct({ Field = v }, { AllowExtra })` | Unknown keys rejected unless AllowExtra |
| `map(keyV, valueV, { MaxEntries })` | MaxEntries **32** |
| `instance(className?, { DescendantOf })` | `DescendantOf` = Instance or `function(value) -> Instance?` |
| `vector3({ MaxMagnitude })`, `cframe()` | Non-finite components rejected |

Tables are walked with an entry budget. An oversized table is rejected after at most
`limit + 1` entries, so a 100,000-element table costs about the same as a small one.
Error messages contain only schema paths and value *types*, never attacker-supplied
text, so logs cannot be injected or bloated.

## Policies (`ACF.Permissions`, `ACF.Ownership`)

```lua
Permission = ACF.Permissions.groupRank(GROUP_ID, 200)                  -- fails closed on lookup errors
Permission = ACF.Permissions.userIds({ 1234, 5678 })
Permission = ACF.Permissions.all(checkA, checkB)   -- also .any
Ownership  = ACF.Ownership.descendantOfCharacter(1)                    -- argument 1 inside player's character
Ownership  = ACF.Ownership.descendantOf(1, function(player) return Plots[player] end)
Ownership  = ACF.Ownership.attribute(1, "OwnerUserId")                -- or an ancestor's attribute
```

## Request pipeline

| # | Check | Rejection reason | Suspicion weight (default) |
|---|-------|------------------|----------------------------|
| 1 | Player has an active session | `no_session` | — |
| 2 | Rate limit | `rate_limited` | 1 |
| 3 | Argument count and validators | `invalid_arguments` | 2 |
| 4 | Character present (if required) | `no_character` | — |
| 5 | Cooldown | `cooldown` | 0.5 |
| 6 | Permission | `permission_denied` | 3 |
| 7 | Ownership | `ownership_denied` | 3 |
| 8 | Transaction id not seen | `duplicate_transaction` | 0.5 |
| 9 | Handler runs (pcall) | — (logged) | — |

**Fail closed:** while ACF is stopped, events are ignored and functions return
`RejectResponse`. Clients are never left yielding.

## Suspicion model

**Input:** rejections per (player, remote, reason).

**Model:** a leaky integrator with half-life `H`:
`score ← score · 0.5^(Δt / H) + w_reason`.

**Output:** when `score ≥ ReportThreshold`, and the last report for that pair is at least
`ReportIntervalSeconds` old, ACF reports a detection with:

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| Detector | `RemoteSecurity` |
| Category | `Remote` |
| Kind | `RemoteInvalidArguments`, `RemoteRateLimitExceeded`, `RemotePermissionDenied`, `RemoteOwnershipViolation`, `RemoteCooldownViolation` or `RemoteDuplicateTransaction` |
| Severity | the configured severity for that reason |
| Confidence | `min(0.95, 1 − e^(−score / ConfidenceScale))` |
| Details | Remote, Reason, Score, Threshold, HalfLifeSeconds, LastProblem |

**Behaviour with defaults** (H = 30 s, threshold 6):

| Traffic | Result |
|---------|--------|
| One malformed request (score 2) | Never reported |
| Occasional mistakes spaced over minutes | Decay away; never reported |
| Laggy client delivering a short burst (a few rate-limited requests) | Not reported |
| Three malformed requests in quick succession | Reported |
| Two ownership violations | Reported |
| Sustained flooding | Reported once per interval; evidence merges duplicates |

These behaviours are covered by `tests/Specs/ExploitSimulation/Remotes.spec.luau`.

## Configuration (`ModuleSettings.RemoteSecurity`)

| Key | Default |
|-----|---------|
| `DefaultRateLimit.Burst` / `.PerSecond` | 10 / 5 |
| `Suspicion.HalfLifeSeconds` | 30 |
| `Suspicion.ReportThreshold` | 6 |
| `Suspicion.ReportIntervalSeconds` | 10 |
| `Suspicion.ConfidenceScale` | 10 |
| `Suspicion.Weights.{RateLimited, InvalidArguments, Cooldown, PermissionDenied, OwnershipDenied, Duplicate}` | 1, 2, 0.5, 3, 3, 0.5 |
| `Suspicion.Severity.{same keys}` | 10, 15, 5, 20, 25, 5 |

Disable the module with `Modules = { RemoteSecurity = false }`. `ProtectEvent` then raises.

## Auditing

```lua
for _, remote in ACF:FindUnprotectedRemotes(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")) do
    warn("Unprotected remote:", remote:GetFullName())
end
print(ACF:GetRemoteStats())   -- { [name] = { Kind, Accepted, Rejected = { [reason] = n }, HandlerErrors, Reports } }
```

## Performance (Lune, indicative)

`tools/bench.sh`:

| Case | Cost |
|------|------|
| Accepted request, 2 scalar arguments | ~3 µs |
| Accepted request, array of 32 structs | ~60 µs |
| Invalid argument rejection | ~4 µs |
| Rate-limited flood rejection | ~2 µs |
| 100,000-element table rejection | ~11 µs |

Re-measure on a Roblox server in Phase 8.

`docs/RISK.md`:

```md
# Risk Scoring and Evidence (v1.0.0)

Every module reports detections; the **RiskEngine** turns them into one number per player:

- **Risk:** 0–100.
- **Band:** a response band (below).
- **Confidence:** how independently corroborated the evidence is.
- **Explanation:** a plain-text breakdown of what drove the score.

It is a core service, not a module, so it sees detections from built-in and custom
modules alike. It **never punishes anyone**. Phase 7 enforcement acts on its bands.

```lua
local report = ACF:GetRisk(player)
print(report.Risk, report.Band, report.Confidence)
print(report.Explanation)
--[[
Risk 62.7 (Investigation)
  Movement 36.8: SpeedExceeded x2 (Movement, sev 20, conf 1, 30) + TeleportDetected x2 (Movement, sev 30, conf 0.6, 27)
  Aim 25.9: AccuracyAnomaly x2 (Behavioral, sev 25, conf 0.74, 25.9)
]]

ACF:OnRiskChanged(function(change)
    -- change.UserId, change.Band, change.PreviousBand, change.Risk, change.Explanation
end)
```

## Bands

| Risk | Band | Intended response (Phase 7) |
|------|------|-----------------------------|
| 0–19 | `Normal` | Normal monitoring |
| 20–39 | `Increased` | Increased monitoring |
| 40–59 | `Restrict` | Restrict suspicious operations |
| 60–79 | `Investigation` | Investigation |
| 80–100 | `EnforcementReview` | Enforcement review |

These are starting values, not tuned production thresholds. Change them with
`Risk.Bands`; each lower bound must be greater than the previous one, or `Initialize`
fails. `ACF.RiskChanged` fires whenever a player's band changes, both up and down.

## Model

### Per kind

Each (Detector, Kind) pair, for example `Movement.TeleportDetected`, has its own score:

```
decay:  S ← S · 0.5^(Δt / H)                        λ = ln 2 / H, H = Risk.HalfLifeSeconds (600)
add:    S ← S + w·c · max(0, 1 − S / (w·c·RepeatCap))
```

`w` is the detection's severity and `c` its confidence.

- **One detection adds exactly w·c.** With different kinds this is the brief's formula,
  `R = Σ wᵢ·cᵢ·e^(−λtᵢ)`; the tests check it to four decimal places.
- **Confidence weighting.** A detection at confidence 0.5 counts half as much as one at
  1.0.
- **Duplicate suppression, two layers:**
  - Repeats of the same kind within `RepeatSuppressSeconds` (5) are not added at all;
    they are counted as `Suppressed`.
  - Later repeats have diminishing returns and approach w·c·`RepeatCap` (3×).
    - A low-severity kind can never climb far on its own: severity 15 tops out near 40.
    - A *sustained*, severe kind can. Twenty minutes of Flight (severity 30, confidence
      0.9) approaches 81, which is EnforcementReview. This was calibrated in Phase 8: with
      2×, flying, teleporting and speed cheaters were never corrected or reviewed
      (docs/EVALUATION.md).
- **Evidence expiration.** Everything decays. Kinds below 0.05 are pruned, and players
  who left are forgotten once their risk falls below 1.

### Correlation: avoiding double counting

One cheat often trips several related detectors. A teleport, for example, trips speed,
teleport and velocity-mismatch checks. Kinds are therefore grouped, and within a group
the strongest kind counts fully while the rest count at `SecondaryWeight` (0.25):

```
G = max(Sₖ) + SecondaryWeight · (Σ Sₖ − max(Sₖ))
R = min(100, Σ G + History)
```

Two related detections at 30 give 37.5, not 60; two independent ones give 60 (tested).

Default groups (`Risk.Groups`, matched by `Detector.Kind`, then Detector, then Category):

| Group | Members |
|-------|---------|
| `Movement` | Movement and Physics |
| `Aim` | `AccuracyAnomaly`, `HeadshotAnomaly` |
| `Economy` | `SignalAnomaly`, `SignalRateLimit` |
| `Automation` | `RegularTiming`, `RepeatedInvalidActions` |
| Anything else | Its own category: `Combat`, `Remote`, `Integrity`, `Custom`, … |

Your entries merge over the defaults:

```lua
ACF:Initialize({ Risk = { Groups = { ["MyDetector.Wallhack"] = "Aim" } } })
```

### Confidence

`Confidence` = 1 − Π(1 − c_g), a noisy-OR over groups of each group's strongest
confidence. Groups are treated as independent evidence, which is exactly why correlated
kinds are grouped first.

## Session and persistent risk

**Session risk** is kept per UserId for the life of the server. Leaving and rejoining does
not reset it (tested); it decays like any evidence.

**Persistent risk** is off by default; turn it on with
`Risk = { Persistence = { Enabled = true } }`. It applies only where it is justified:

- **Saving.** Only a session that ends at risk ≥ `MinRiskToSave` (40) is saved. Clean
  sessions cause no writes.
- **Seeding.** On the next join, the saved risk seeds the session as a `History`
  contribution:

  ```
  History = min(Cap, saved × Weight × 0.5^(hours since / HalfLifeHours))
                  35         0.5              24 h
  ```

  History alone can never leave `Increased`. A week later it adds nothing (tested).
- **Batching.** Writes are queued and flushed every `FlushIntervalSeconds` (30), at most
  `MaxWritesPerFlush` (10) at a time. They are also flushed on `ACF:Stop()` and at
  server shutdown (`BindToClose`, waiting up to 25 s for writes in flight).
- **Failure handling:**
  - A failed write is retried up to `MaxAttempts` (3) and then logged as an Error.
  - A failed or corrupt load means *no history*; it never raises risk.
  - A load that finishes after the player has left is discarded.
- **Record.** Stored at key `Risk_<UserId>` in DataStore `ACF_Risk_v1`:

  ```lua
  {
      Version = 1, Risk = 47.5, WallTime = 1763600000,
      Sessions = { { WallTime, Risk, Peak, Band, Top = { { Group, Score, Kind } } } }, -- last 5
      ResetReason = "..."?,
  }
  ```

  It holds no personal data beyond the UserId in the key.

In Studio, persistence needs *Game Settings → Security → Enable Studio Access to API
Services*. Without it every load fails, which is harmless and logged.

## History and explanations

`ACF:GetRiskHistory(player)` returns up to `MaxHistory` (50) entries, oldest first:

| `Type` | Meaning |
|--------|---------|
| `Detection` | A detection that changed risk, with `Detector`, `Kind`, `Group`, `Contribution` and the resulting `Risk`/`Band` |
| `Band` | A band change, `Note = "Restrict -> Investigation"` |
| `History` | Risk loaded from an earlier session |
| `Reset` | A staff reset, with the reason |

Full detection details stay in the evidence store (`ACF:GetEvidence`). Risk links to
them by Detector and Kind.

`ACF:ResetRisk(player, { Reason = "...", Persistent = true })` is the staff override. It
clears session risk and, with `Persistent`, writes a Risk-0 record that keeps the
session list for audit. It is logged, and recorded in the player's history.

## API

| Method | Returns |
|--------|---------|
| `ACF:GetRisk(player)` | `{ UserId, Risk, Band, Confidence, Persistent, Peak, Groups = { { Group, Score, Confidence, Kinds } }, Explanation }` |
| `ACF:GetRiskHistory(player)` | History entries, as above |
| `ACF:ResetRisk(player, options?)` | — |
| `ACF:OnRiskChanged(handler)` | Subscription; payload as in `ACF.Topics.RiskChanged` |
| `ACF:GetStatus().Risk` | Tracked and elevated users, detections, suppressed, band changes, persistence counters |

`player` may be a Player, Session or UserId; departed players are fine.

## Configuration (`Risk`)

| Key | Default |
|-----|---------|
| Enabled | true |
| HalfLifeSeconds | 600 |
| RepeatCap | 3 |
| RepeatSuppressSeconds | 5 |
| SecondaryWeight | 0.25 |
| RecomputeIntervalSeconds | 5 |
| MaxKeysPerUser | 64 |
| MaxHistory | 50 |
| Bands | Increased 20, Restrict 40, Investigation 60, EnforcementReview 80 |
| Groups | See above (merged) |
| Persistence.Enabled | false |
| Persistence.DataStoreName | `ACF_Risk_v1` |
| Persistence.MinRiskToSave | 40 |
| Persistence.Weight | 0.5 |
| Persistence.HalfLifeHours | 24 |
| Persistence.Cap | 35 |
| Persistence.FlushIntervalSeconds | 30 |
| Persistence.MaxWritesPerFlush | 10 |
| Persistence.MaxAttempts | 3 |
| Persistence.MaxSessions | 5 |

## Performance

Measured with `tools/bench.sh` under Lune:

| Operation | Cost |
|-----------|------|
| Detection → evidence → risk update (whole pipeline) | ~8 µs |
| Periodic recompute, 50 users × 6 kinds, every 5 s | ~0.26 ms |

Memory per user is bounded by `MaxKeysPerUser` (weakest kind evicted) and `MaxHistory`.

## What the numbers mean

Risk aggregates evidence; it is not a probability of cheating. Severities are set per
detector, confidences per detection, and both are judgement calls. Before acting
automatically, watch real servers, using `ShadowMode` when Phase 7 lands, and tune the
bands.

```

`docs/TROUBLESHOOTING.md`:

```md
# Troubleshooting

**"ACF configuration is invalid"** — Read the list: every bad key is reported with its full
path and, for typos, a suggestion. Fix and call `ACF:Initialize` again (the failed call
changed nothing).

**"ModuleSettings.X: no registered module with a ConfigSchema has this name"** — A
`ModuleSettings` key does not match the `Name` of any registered module that has a
`ConfigSchema`. Check spelling, or check that `RegisterModule` ran before `Start`.

**"Configured module is not available in this ACF version"** — You enabled a built-in
whose phase has not shipped. The warning names the phase.

**"Configured module is not registered" for `Economy`** — There is no Economy module.
Resource-gain detection is Behavioral signals: `ACF:DefineSignal` and `ACF:RecordSignal`
([BEHAVIORAL.md](BEHAVIORAL.md)).

**A protected remote stopped working for one player** — The player is restricted
(enforcement level 2 or higher) and the remote has `Restrict` set. Check
`ACF:GetEnforcement(player)` (`Tags`, `Level`) and the `Rejected` entries in
`ACF:GetAuditLog({ UserId = ... })`. For staff or testers, use
`ACF:SetEnforcementOverride(player, { Exempt = true, By = ... })`.

**Bans don't apply in Studio** — `Players:BanAsync` and DataStores need *Enable Studio
Access to API Services*. Without them the Roblox ban call fails (logged), and records only
last for the server. Set `Enforcement.Bans.UseRobloxBanApi = false` to silence the
former.

**I want to see what ACF would do without it acting** — Use
`Enforcement = { ShadowMode = true }`. Decisions appear in the audit log with
`Shadow = true`, and cases with status `Shadow`.

**`RegularTiming` reported for an honest player** — Something calls `ACF:RecordAction` on a
timer (auto-collect, regeneration, a tick reward) instead of on a player-initiated
request. Only record actions the player starts.

**`SignalAnomaly` reported for gamepass owners** — Their multiplier exceeds `MinRatio`
(3×). Record the amount before multipliers, or raise
`ModuleSettings.Behavioral.Signals.MinRatio`.

**A module shows `Failed` in `ACF:GetStatus().Modules`** — The `Error` field contains the
lifecycle method and the error message. Modules that depend on it show `Blocked`.

**My event handler stopped firing** — Handlers that throw repeatedly are disconnected. Look
for "Event handler disconnected after repeated failures" in `ACF:GetRecentLogs()`.

**A scheduled task stopped running** — Same mechanism: "Scheduled task disabled after
repeated failures". `ACF:GetStatus().Scheduler[<Module>.<task>]` shows its failure counts.

**`context:Report` returns `false, "rate_limited"`** — The detector is reporting faster than
`Detection.ReportsPerSecond` / `ReportBurst` for that player. Report state changes, not
every sample.

**`context:Report` returns `false, "no_session"`** — The target is not an active player, the
Session object belongs to an ended session, or the value is not a real Player.

**My remote handler never runs** — Check `ACF:GetRemoteStats()[name].Rejected`.
`invalid_arguments` usually means the validators don't match what your client sends.
Remember that trailing optional arguments need `V.optional`. If nothing is counted at
all, ACF may not be running (protected remotes fail closed).

**"ACF: call ACF:Initialize before protecting remotes"** — Call `ProtectEvent` /
`ProtectFunction` after `ACF:Initialize` (before or after `Start`).

**A RemoteFunction returns nil for valid requests** — The handler probably errored. ACF
returns `RejectResponse` and logs "Remote handler errored".

**Players get SpeedExceeded / TeleportDetected from a game mechanic** — Grant the
mechanic an allowance:

- `ACF:AuthorizeTeleport(player)` before `PivotTo`;
- `ACF:AllowMovement(player, "Speed", seconds, { Multiplier = n })` for boosts;
- `"Impulse"` for knockback;
- `"Flight"` for jetpacks.

Raising WalkSpeed on the server needs nothing. See docs/MOVEMENT.md.

**Flight reports in a game with gliders or low gravity** — Use `AllowMovement(..., "Flight")`
while gliding, or raise `ModuleSettings.Movement.MaxAirtimeExtraSeconds` / lower
`MinFallStudsPerSecond`.

**`Movement` shows Failed with "requires Environment.Physics"** — You built a custom
Environment without a physics service. Use `RobloxEnvironment` (the default) or supply one.

**Telemetry reports everyone as missing** — The client script is not installed
(`StarterPlayerScripts.ACFTelemetry`). Install it, or leave `Modules.Telemetry` off.

**Every shot returns `NotOwned`** — The default Inventory provider looks for a Tool
named after the weapon id in the character or Backpack. If your weapons aren't Tools,
set `ACF:SetCombatProvider("Inventory", function(player, weaponId) ... end)`.

**Shots return `InvalidClaim`** — Hitscan needs `Origin`; Projectile needs `Origin`,
`Direction` and a unique `ShotId`; Melee needs `Target`. Values must be Vector3/Instance,
not tables. Validate remote arguments with `ACF.CombatClaim`.

**Legitimate hits refused as `NoLineOfSight`** — Line of sight is cast against collidable
world geometry. Make decorative parts `CanCollide = false`, or set
`RequireLineOfSight = false` on that weapon.

**`OriginMismatch` for guns with long barrels or third-person cameras** — The claimed
origin must be within `ModuleSettings.Combat.OriginToleranceStuds` (default 8) of the
character root. Send the muzzle position, or raise the tolerance.

**"ACF can only run on the server"** — ACF was required from a LocalScript. It belongs in
ServerScriptService.

**Log lines end with "(+N suppressed)"** — Rate limiting collapsed N identical messages.
Increase `Logging.RateLimitBurst` while debugging if needed.

```

`examples/Bootstrap.server.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Example bootstrap. Copy into ServerScriptService as a Script in your own game and
	adjust. (Inside the test place this sits in ServerStorage, where it does not run.)
]]

local ServerScriptService = game:GetService("ServerScriptService")
local ServerStorage = game:GetService("ServerStorage")

local ACF = require(ServerScriptService:WaitForChild("ACF"))
local LeaderstatRateDetector = require(ServerStorage:WaitForChild("ACFExamples"):WaitForChild("LeaderstatRateDetector"))

ACF:Initialize({
	LogLevel = "Info",
	Enforcement = {
		Mode = "Hybrid",
		AutoKick = true,
		AutoBan = false,
		ShadowMode = true, -- observe only while tuning thresholds
	},
	ModuleSettings = {
		LeaderstatRate = { StatName = "Coins", MaxGainPerSecond = 50 },
	},
})

ACF:RegisterModule(LeaderstatRateDetector.new(ACF.Schema))

-- Protect your game's remotes. Rojo users typically keep them in ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.
local V = ACF.Validators
local remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
if remotes then
	local buyItem = remotes:FindFirstChild("BuyItem")
	if buyItem and buyItem:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
		ACF:ProtectEvent(buyItem, {
			Arguments = { V.string({ MaxLength = 32 }), V.integer({ Min = 1, Max = 99 }) },
			RateLimit = { Burst = 5, PerSecond = 1 },
			Handler = function(player: Player, itemId: string, quantity: number)
				print(`{player.Name} buys {quantity}x {itemId}`)
			end,
		})
	end
	for _, remote in ACF:FindUnprotectedRemotes(remotes) do
		warn(`[ACF] Unprotected remote: {remote:GetFullName()}`)
	end
end

ACF:OnDetection(function(payload)
	local event = payload.Event
	print(`[ACF] {event.Detector}/{event.Kind} user={event.UserId} severity={event.Severity} confidence={event.Confidence}`)
end)

ACF:Start()

```

`examples/CanaryBootstrap.server.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Example: rolling out a canary build of ACF (see RELEASING.md).

	Roblox runs one copy of your code in every server, so a canary is the new build
	published everywhere with its enforcement held back:

	  1. Publish the canary build with ENFORCE_PERCENT = 0. Every server runs the new
	     detection, risk and enforcement logic in shadow mode: decisions are made and
	     audited, nothing automatic is applied.
	  2. Review the audit log (ACF:OnEnforcementAudit, shipped to your own storage) and
	     the evaluation criteria in RELEASING.md for a few days.
	  3. Raise ENFORCE_PERCENT step by step (10, 50, 100). Servers are chosen by a hash of
	     game.JobId, so a server keeps its choice for its whole life.
	  4. Promote the same commit to stable with tools/release.sh stable.

	Staff actions (Kick, Ban, case approvals) work in shadow mode too.
]]

local ServerScriptService = game:GetService("ServerScriptService")

-- Share of servers that enforce automatically (0-100).
local ENFORCE_PERCENT = 0

-- Stable 0-99 bucket for this server.
local function serverBucket(jobId: string): number
	local hash = 0
	for index = 1, #jobId do
		hash = (hash * 31 + string.byte(jobId, index)) % 1000003
	end
	return hash % 100
end

local enforcing = serverBucket(game.JobId) < ENFORCE_PERCENT

local ACF = require(ServerScriptService:WaitForChild("ACF"))
ACF:Initialize({
	Enforcement = {
		ShadowMode = not enforcing,
	},
})

ACF:OnEnforcementAudit(function(entry)
	-- Ship to your own storage (DataStore queue, MessagingService, an HTTP log sink...).
	-- Tag entries with the build so canary and stable decisions can be compared.
	local _ = entry
end)

ACF:Start()
print(`[ACF] {ACF.Version} ({ACF.Channel}) started; automatic enforcement {if enforcing then "ON" else "shadow"}`)

```

`examples/CombatWiring.server.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Example: wiring a gun through ACF so the server authorizes every hit.

	Client (LocalScript) sends a claim:
		Fire:FireServer("Rifle", { Origin = muzzle, Target = hitPart, HitPosition = hitPoint })
		Reload:FireServer("Rifle")

	Server (this script) validates the remote arguments with RemoteSecurity, then asks
	Combat whether the shot and hit are legitimate and how much damage to apply.
	(Inside the test place this sits in ServerStorage, where it does not run.)
]]

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local ServerScriptService = game:GetService("ServerScriptService")

local ACF = require(ServerScriptService:WaitForChild("ACF"))
local V = ACF.Validators

ACF:Initialize({})

ACF:DefineWeapon("Rifle", {
	Kind = "Hitscan",
	Damage = 25,
	HeadshotMultiplier = 2,
	FireRate = 10,
	MaxRange = 400,
	Falloff = { Start = 150, End = 400, MinMultiplier = 0.6 },
	Ammo = { Magazine = 30, Reserve = 120, ReloadSeconds = 2 },
})

-- Teams: no damage to teammates (this is also the default behaviour).
ACF:SetCombatProvider("CanDamage", function(attacker: Player, target): boolean
	local victim: Player? = target.Player
	if victim == nil or attacker.Team == nil then
		return true
	end
	return victim.Team ~= attacker.Team
end)

local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")

ACF:ProtectEvent(remotes:WaitForChild("Fire"), {
	Arguments = { V.string({ MaxLength = 32 }), ACF.CombatClaim },
	-- Transport-level limit, generous; the weapon's own FireRate is enforced by Combat.
	RateLimit = { Burst = 20, PerSecond = 20 },
	Handler = function(player: Player, weaponId: string, claim)
		local result = ACF:ValidateAttack(player, weaponId, claim)
		if result.Allowed and result.Target and result.Target.Humanoid and result.Damage > 0 then
			result.Target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(result.Damage)
		end
	end,
})

ACF:ProtectEvent(remotes:WaitForChild("Reload"), {
	Arguments = { V.string({ MaxLength = 32 }) },
	RateLimit = { Burst = 3, PerSecond = 1 },
	Handler = function(player: Player, weaponId: string)
		ACF:Reload(player, weaponId)
	end,
})

ACF:Start()

```

`examples/LeaderstatRateDetector.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Example custom detector: flags implausibly fast growth of a leaderstat.

	Input:  a numeric leaderstat (e.g. leaderstats.Coins), sampled on the server every
	        SampleIntervalSeconds through the ACF scheduler. Clients cannot fake this
	        value: leaderstats are server-owned.
	Model:  gain rate = (value - previous value) / elapsed seconds. A sample whose rate
	        exceeds MaxGainPerSecond is a violation. Spending (negative change) and
	        respawns are ignored.
	Output: a detection with Kind "StatGainRateExceeded". Confidence scales with how far
	        the rate exceeds the limit, capped at 0.9: one sample is never treated as
	        certain.
	Explain: Details carry the observed rate, the limit, the delta and the window.

	This is deliberately simple. Phase 5 adds statistical baselines (EMA, CUSUM) for
	games whose legitimate income is bursty. Use this file as a template for writing
	ACF modules, not as a finished economy detector.

	Usage:
		local ACF = require(ServerScriptService.ACF)
		local Detector = require(path.to.LeaderstatRateDetector)
		ACF:Initialize({
			ModuleSettings = {
				LeaderstatRate = { StatName = "Coins", MaxGainPerSecond = 50 },
			},
		})
		ACF:RegisterModule(Detector.new(ACF.Schema))
		ACF:Start()
]]

local LeaderstatRateDetector = {}

type Sample = { value: number, time: number }

-- `Schema` is ACF.Schema (Configuration.Schema). Passing it in keeps this example
-- free of hard-coded paths into the ACF package.
function LeaderstatRateDetector.new(Schema: any)
	local module = {
		Name = "LeaderstatRate",
		Version = "1.0.0",
		Category = "Economy",
		ConfigSchema = Schema.Table({
			StatName = Schema.String("Coins", { MaxLength = 64 }),
			MaxGainPerSecond = Schema.Number(100, { Min = 0 }),
			SampleIntervalSeconds = Schema.Number(5, { Min = 0.5, Max = 300 }),
			Severity = Schema.Number(15, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
		}),
	}

	local context: any = nil

	local function readStat(player: any): number?
		local stats = player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
		local stat = stats and stats:FindFirstChild(context.Config.StatName)
		local value = stat and stat.Value
		return if type(value) == "number" then value else nil
	end

	local function sample(now: number)
		local config = context.Config
		for _, session in context.Players:GetSessions() do
			local value = readStat(session.Player)
			local previous: Sample? = session:GetState("LeaderstatRate.Last")
			if value == nil then
				session:ClearState("LeaderstatRate.Last")
				continue
			end
			if previous then
				local elapsed = now - previous.time
				local delta = value - previous.value
				if elapsed > 0 and delta > 0 then
					local rate = delta / elapsed
					if rate > config.MaxGainPerSecond then
						local excess = if config.MaxGainPerSecond > 0 then rate / config.MaxGainPerSecond else math.huge
						context:Report(session, {
							Kind = "StatGainRateExceeded",
							Severity = config.Severity,
							Confidence = math.min(0.9, 0.5 + 0.1 * (excess - 1)),
							Details = {
								Stat = config.StatName,
								Rate = rate,
								Limit = config.MaxGainPerSecond,
								Delta = delta,
								WindowSeconds = elapsed,
							},
						})
					end
				end
			end
			local last = session:GetState("LeaderstatRate.Last", function()
				return {}
			end)
			last.value = value
			last.time = now
		end
	end

	function module:Initialize(ctx)
		context = ctx
	end

	function module:Start()
		context.Scheduler:Every("Sample", context.Config.SampleIntervalSeconds, sample)
	end

	function module:Stop()
		-- The scheduled task is released automatically (acquired during Start).
		for _, session in context.Players:GetSessions() do
			session:ClearState("LeaderstatRate.Last")
		end
	end

	function module:Destroy()
		context = nil
	end

	return module
end

return LeaderstatRateDetector

```

`src/Client/ClientTelemetry.client.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	ACF client telemetry (optional). Sends a heartbeat with a sequence number and the
	measured frame rate every TELEMETRY_INTERVAL_SECONDS.

	This script is NOT a security boundary: an exploiter can delete it or fire the remote
	themselves. The server treats it as a weak hint (see docs/MOVEMENT.md, Telemetry).
	If the server has Telemetry disabled, the remote never appears and the script exits.
]]

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Protocol = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("ACFShared"):WaitForChild("Protocol"))

local folder = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild(Protocol.REMOTES_FOLDER, 30)
if folder == nil then
	return
end
local remote = folder:WaitForChild(Protocol.TELEMETRY_REMOTE, 30)
if remote == nil or not remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
	return
end
local telemetry = remote :: RemoteEvent

local frames = 0
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	frames += 1
end)

local sequence = 0
while true do
	local started = os.clock()
	task.wait(Protocol.TELEMETRY_INTERVAL_SECONDS)
	local elapsed = math.max(os.clock() - started, 1e-3)
	sequence += 1
	local fps = math.clamp(math.floor(frames / elapsed + 0.5), 0, Protocol.MAX_FPS)
	frames = 0
	telemetry:FireServer(sequence, fps)
end

```

`src/Server/API/IntegrationAPI.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Integration API: how a game defines its own rules for ACF to enforce (combat
	weapons and providers; behavioural signals and actions). PublicAPI
	assigns these functions onto the ACF table explicitly, so they are visible to the
	type checker in games that use --!strict. See docs/COMBAT.md.

		ACF:DefineWeapon("Rifle", { Kind = "Hitscan", Damage = 25, FireRate = 10, MaxRange = 400,
			HeadshotMultiplier = 2, Ammo = { Magazine = 30, Reserve = 120, ReloadSeconds = 2 } })
		ACF:SetCombatProvider("CanDamage", function(attacker, target) ... end)

		local result = ACF:ValidateAttack(player, "Rifle", claim)
		if result.Allowed and result.Target and result.Target.Humanoid then
			result.Target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(result.Damage)
		end
]]

local IntegrationAPI = {}

-- Builds the integration methods bound to `getFramework` (returns the singleton).
-- Each function takes the ACF table as its first argument (method-call syntax).
function IntegrationAPI.create(getFramework: () -> any)
	local function combat(requireRunning: boolean): any
		local framework = getFramework()
		if framework:GetState() == "Created" then
			error("ACF: call ACF:Initialize before using the combat API", 3)
		end
		local state = framework:GetModuleState("Combat")
		if state == "Disabled" or framework:GetConfig("Modules").Combat == false then
			error("ACF: the Combat module is disabled (Modules.Combat = false)", 3)
		end
		if requireRunning and state ~= "Running" then
			error("ACF: the Combat module is not running (call ACF:Start)", 3)
		end
		return framework:GetModule("Combat")
	end

	local function behavioral(requireRunning: boolean): any
		local framework = getFramework()
		if framework:GetState() == "Created" then
			error("ACF: call ACF:Initialize before using the behavioural API", 3)
		end
		local state = framework:GetModuleState("Behavioral")
		if state == "Disabled" or framework:GetConfig("Modules").Behavioral == false then
			error("ACF: the Behavioral module is disabled (Modules.Behavioral = false)", 3)
		end
		if requireRunning and state ~= "Running" then
			error("ACF: the Behavioral module is not running (call ACF:Start)", 3)
		end
		return framework:GetModule("Behavioral")
	end

	local api = {}

	-- Declares a weapon or ability. Allowed any time after Initialize; ids are unique.
	function api.DefineWeapon(_self: any, id: string, definition: { [string]: any })
		combat(false):Define(id, definition)
	end

	-- Abilities are weapons with Kind = "Ability"; this sets it for you.
	function api.DefineAbility(_self: any, id: string, definition: { [string]: any })
		local copy = table.clone(definition)
		copy.Kind = "Ability"
		combat(false):Define(id, copy)
	end

	-- "Inventory" | "CanDamage" | "DamageCalculator"; nil restores the default.
	function api.SetCombatProvider(_self: any, name: string, fn: ((...any) -> any)?)
		combat(false):SetProvider(name, fn)
	end

	function api.ValidateAttack(_self: any, player: Player, weaponId: string, claim: { [string]: any }): { [string]: any }
		return combat(true):ValidateAttack(player, weaponId, claim)
	end

	function api.FireProjectile(_self: any, player: Player, weaponId: string, claim: { [string]: any }): { [string]: any }
		return combat(true):FireProjectile(player, weaponId, claim)
	end

	function api.ValidateProjectileHit(_self: any, player: Player, shotId: string, claim: { [string]: any }): { [string]: any }
		return combat(true):ValidateProjectileHit(player, shotId, claim)
	end

	function api.Reload(_self: any, player: Player, weaponId: string): (boolean, string?)
		return combat(true):Reload(player, weaponId)
	end

	function api.GetAmmo(_self: any, player: Player, weaponId: string): (number?, number?, boolean)
		return combat(true):GetAmmo(player, weaponId)
	end

	function api.SetAmmo(_self: any, player: Player, weaponId: string, magazine: number, reserve: number?): boolean
		return combat(true):SetAmmo(player, weaponId, magazine, reserve)
	end

	function api.GetCombatStats(_self: any): { [string]: any }
		return combat(false):GetStats()
	end

	-- Behavioural signals and actions (docs/BEHAVIORAL.md) ---------------------------

	-- Declares a per-player rate to watch, e.g. "Coins". options: { MaxPerSecond?,
	-- Baseline? = { Mean, StdDev } (per second), MinStdDev?, Statistical? }.
	function api.DefineSignal(_self: any, name: string, options: { [string]: any }?)
		behavioral(false):DefineSignal(name, options)
	end

	-- Adds `amount` to a player's signal. Call it where the server grants the resource.
	function api.RecordSignal(_self: any, player: Player, name: string, amount: number): boolean
		return behavioral(true):RecordSignal(player, name, amount)
	end

	-- Records one game action; valid = false for an action the game's client never sends.
	function api.RecordAction(_self: any, player: Player, action: string, valid: boolean?): boolean
		return behavioral(true):RecordAction(player, action, valid)
	end

	function api.GetBehaviorProfile(_self: any, player: Player): { [string]: any }?
		return behavioral(false):GetProfile(player)
	end

	function api.GetBehaviorStats(_self: any): { [string]: any }
		return behavioral(false):GetStats()
	end

	return api
end

return IntegrationAPI

```

		local ACF = require(game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF)

local Configuration = require(script.Parent.Parent.Core.Configuration)
