`src/Server/Core/Cleanup.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Resource cleanup bag.

	Collects things that must be released later and releases them in reverse order of
	acquisition. Accepted tasks:
	  - functions (called)
	  - threads (cancelled with task.cancel)
	  - RBXScriptConnections and tables with a Disconnect method
	  - Instances and tables with a Destroy method

	A failing task never prevents the remaining tasks from running; failures are passed
	to the optional onError callback and counted in Clean()'s return value.
]]

local Cleanup = {}
Cleanup.__index = Cleanup

export type Cleanup = typeof(setmetatable(
	{} :: {
		_tasks: { any },
		_onError: ((message: string) -> ())?,
	},
	Cleanup
))

local function isValidTask(item: any): boolean
	local kind = typeof(item)
	if kind == "function" or kind == "thread" or kind == "Instance" or kind == "RBXScriptConnection" then
		return true
	end
	if kind == "table" then
		return type(item.Disconnect) == "function" or type(item.Destroy) == "function"
	end
	return false
end

local function runTask(item: any)
	local kind = typeof(item)
	if kind == "function" then
		item()
	elseif kind == "thread" then
		if coroutine.status(item) ~= "dead" then
			task.cancel(item)
		end
	elseif kind == "RBXScriptConnection" then
		item:Disconnect()
	elseif kind == "Instance" then
		item:Destroy()
	elseif type(item.Disconnect) == "function" then
		item:Disconnect()
	else
		item:Destroy()
	end
end

function Cleanup.new(onError: ((message: string) -> ())?): Cleanup
	return setmetatable({ _tasks = {}, _onError = onError }, Cleanup)
end

-- Adds a task and returns it unchanged, so acquisition can be written inline:
--   local conn = bag:Add(signal:Connect(fn))
function Cleanup.Add(self: Cleanup, item: any): any
	if not isValidTask(item) then
		error(`Cleanup:Add received an unsupported value of type '{typeof(item)}'`, 2)
	end
	table.insert(self._tasks, item)
	return item
end

-- Removes a task without running it. Returns true if it was present.
function Cleanup.Remove(self: Cleanup, item: any): boolean
	local index = table.find(self._tasks, item)
	if index then
		table.remove(self._tasks, index)
		return true
	end
	return false
end

-- Runs all tasks in reverse order. Tasks added while cleaning are also run.
-- Returns the number of tasks that raised an error.
function Cleanup.Clean(self: Cleanup): number
	local failures = 0
	local tasks = self._tasks
	while #tasks > 0 do
		local item = table.remove(tasks)
		local ok: boolean, err: any = pcall(runTask, item)
		if not ok then
			failures += 1
			local onError = self._onError
			if onError then
				pcall(onError, tostring(err))
			end
		end
	end
	return failures
end

function Cleanup.Count(self: Cleanup): number
	return #self._tasks
end

-- Alias so a Cleanup can itself be added to another Cleanup.
function Cleanup.Destroy(self: Cleanup)
	self:Clean()
end

return Cleanup

```

`src/Server/Core/Configuration.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Schema-validated configuration.

	The full configuration is described by a schema built from Configuration.Schema
	constructors. Load() resolves user overrides against the schema and collects every
	problem it finds (unknown keys with "did you mean" suggestions, wrong types, values
	out of range, invalid enum values) instead of stopping at the first one. Resolved
	values are deep-frozen: nothing can change configuration after load.

	"Open" nodes (used for ModuleSettings) accept arbitrary sub-keys at load time. Each
	sub-key is validated later, when the module that owns it registers its schema via
	Extend(). Keys never claimed by a module are reported by UnclaimedKeys(), so a typo
	in a module name still fails loudly at Start.
]]

export type SchemaKind = "number" | "boolean" | "string" | "enum" | "table" | "map" | "open"

export type SchemaNode = {
	Kind: SchemaKind,
	Default: any,
	Description: string?,
	Min: number?,
	Max: number?,
	Integer: boolean?,
	MaxLength: number?,
	Values: { string }?,
	Fields: { [string]: SchemaNode }?,
	ValueSchema: SchemaNode?,
}

local MAX_DEPTH = 16

local function deepClone(value: any, depth: number?): any
	local level = depth or 0
	if type(value) ~= "table" then
		return value
	end
	if level > MAX_DEPTH then
		error("Configuration: table nesting is too deep (cyclic table?)", 0)
	end
	local copy = {}
	for k, v in value do
		copy[k] = deepClone(v, level + 1)
	end
	return copy
end

local function deepFreeze(value: any): any
	if type(value) == "table" and not table.isfrozen(value) then
		for _, v in value do
			deepFreeze(v)
		end
		table.freeze(value)
	end
	return value
end

local function isFiniteNumber(value: any): boolean
	return type(value) == "number" and value == value and value ~= math.huge and value ~= -math.huge
end

local function levenshtein(a: string, b: string): number
	local la, lb = #a, #b
	if la == 0 then
		return lb
	end
	if lb == 0 then
		return la
	end
	local prev = table.create(lb + 1, 0)
	for j = 0, lb do
		prev[j + 1] = j
	end
	for i = 1, la do
		local cur = table.create(lb + 1, 0)
		cur[1] = i
		local ca = string.byte(a, i)
		for j = 1, lb do
			local cost = if ca == string.byte(b, j) then 0 else 1
			cur[j + 1] = math.min(prev[j + 1] + 1, cur[j] + 1, prev[j] + cost)
		end
		prev = cur
	end
	return prev[lb + 1]
end

local function suggest(input: string, candidates: { string }): string?
	local lower = string.lower(input)
	local best, bestDistance = nil, math.huge
	for _, candidate in candidates do
		if string.lower(candidate) == lower then
			return candidate
		end
		local distance = levenshtein(lower, string.lower(candidate))
		if distance < bestDistance then
			best, bestDistance = candidate, distance
		end
	end
	if best and bestDistance <= math.max(1, math.floor(#input / 4)) then
		return best
	end
	return nil
end

local function describe(value: any): string
	if type(value) == "string" then
		return `'{value}'`
	end
	return `{typeof(value)} {tostring(value)}`
end

-- Schema constructors ----------------------------------------------------------------

local Schema = {}

local function check(condition: boolean, message: string)
	if not condition then
		error("Configuration schema: " .. message, 3)
	end
end

function Schema.Number(
	default: number,
	options: { Min: number?, Max: number?, Integer: boolean?, Description: string? }?
): SchemaNode
	local opts: { Min: number?, Max: number?, Integer: boolean?, Description: string? } = options or {}
	check(isFiniteNumber(default), "Number default must be a finite number")
	check(opts.Min == nil or default >= opts.Min, "Number default is below Min")
	check(opts.Max == nil or default <= opts.Max, "Number default is above Max")
	check(not opts.Integer or default == math.floor(default), "Number default must be an integer")
	return table.freeze({
		Kind = "number",
		Default = default,
		Min = opts.Min,
		Max = opts.Max,
		Integer = opts.Integer,
		Description = opts.Description,
	}) :: any
end

function Schema.Boolean(default: boolean, description: string?): SchemaNode
	check(type(default) == "boolean", "Boolean default must be a boolean")
	return table.freeze({ Kind = "boolean", Default = default, Description = description }) :: any
end

function Schema.String(default: string, options: { MaxLength: number?, Description: string? }?): SchemaNode
	local opts: { MaxLength: number?, Description: string? } = options or {}
	check(type(default) == "string", "String default must be a string")
	return table.freeze({
		Kind = "string",
		Default = default,
		MaxLength = opts.MaxLength,
		Description = opts.Description,
	}) :: any
end

function Schema.Enum(values: { string }, default: string, description: string?): SchemaNode
	check(type(values) == "table" and #values > 0, "Enum needs at least one value")
	check(table.find(values, default) ~= nil, "Enum default must be one of its values")
	return table.freeze({
		Kind = "enum",
		Values = table.freeze(table.clone(values)),
		Default = default,
		Description = description,
	}) :: any
end

function Schema.Table(fields: { [string]: SchemaNode }, description: string?): SchemaNode
	check(type(fields) == "table", "Table fields must be a table")
	for key, node in fields do
		check(type(key) == "string", "Table field names must be strings")
		check(type(node) == "table" and type(node.Kind) == "string", `Table field '{key}' is not a schema node`)
	end
	return table.freeze({
		Kind = "table",
		Fields = table.freeze(table.clone(fields)),
		Default = nil,
		Description = description,
	}) :: any
end

-- A string-keyed dictionary whose values all follow valueSchema. User entries are
-- merged over the defaults.
function Schema.Map(valueSchema: SchemaNode, default: { [string]: any }?, description: string?): SchemaNode
	check(type(valueSchema) == "table" and type(valueSchema.Kind) == "string", "Map value schema is not a schema node")
	return table.freeze({
		Kind = "map",
		ValueSchema = valueSchema,
		Default = table.freeze(table.clone(default or {})),
		Description = description,
	}) :: any
end

-- A table whose sub-keys are validated later via Configuration:Extend().
function Schema.Open(description: string?): SchemaNode
	return table.freeze({ Kind = "open", Default = nil, Description = description }) :: any
end

-- Resolution -------------------------------------------------------------------------

local resolve: (node: SchemaNode, value: any, path: string, errors: { string }) -> any

local function defaultOf(node: SchemaNode): any
	if node.Kind == "table" then
		local result = {}
		for key, field in node.Fields :: { [string]: SchemaNode } do
			result[key] = defaultOf(field)
		end
		return result
	elseif node.Kind == "map" then
		return deepClone(node.Default)
	elseif node.Kind == "open" then
		return {}
	end
	return node.Default
end

local function sortedKeys(dict: { [string]: any }): { string }
	local keys = {}
	for key in dict do
		table.insert(keys, key)
	end
	table.sort(keys)
	return keys
end

resolve = function(node: SchemaNode, value: any, path: string, errors: { string }): any
	if value == nil then
		return defaultOf(node)
	end
	local kind = node.Kind
	if kind == "number" then
		if not isFiniteNumber(value) then
			table.insert(errors, `{path}: expected a finite number, got {describe(value)}`)
			return node.Default
		end
		if node.Integer and value ~= math.floor(value) then
			table.insert(errors, `{path}: expected an integer, got {value}`)
			return node.Default
		end
		if node.Min ~= nil and value < node.Min then
			table.insert(errors, `{path}: {value} is below the minimum of {node.Min}`)
			return node.Default
		end
		if node.Max ~= nil and value > node.Max then
			table.insert(errors, `{path}: {value} is above the maximum of {node.Max}`)
			return node.Default
		end
		return value
	elseif kind == "boolean" then
		if type(value) ~= "boolean" then
			table.insert(errors, `{path}: expected a boolean, got {describe(value)}`)
			return node.Default
		end
		return value
	elseif kind == "string" then
		if type(value) ~= "string" then
			table.insert(errors, `{path}: expected a string, got {describe(value)}`)
			return node.Default
		end
		if node.MaxLength and #value > node.MaxLength then
			table.insert(errors, `{path}: string is longer than {node.MaxLength} characters`)
			return node.Default
		end
		return value
	elseif kind == "enum" then
		local values = node.Values :: { string }
		if type(value) ~= "string" or table.find(values, value) == nil then
			local hint = if type(value) == "string" then suggest(value, values) else nil
			local message = `{path}: expected one of {table.concat(values, ", ")}, got {describe(value)}`
			if hint then
				message ..= ` (did you mean '{hint}'?)`
			end
			table.insert(errors, message)
			return node.Default
		end
		return value
	elseif kind == "table" then
		if type(value) ~= "table" then
			table.insert(errors, `{path}: expected a table, got {describe(value)}`)
			return defaultOf(node)
		end
		local fields = node.Fields :: { [string]: SchemaNode }
		local known = sortedKeys(fields)
		for key in value do
			if type(key) ~= "string" then
				table.insert(errors, `{path}: keys must be strings, got {describe(key)}`)
			elseif fields[key] == nil then
				local childPath = if path == "" then key else `{path}.{key}`
				local hint = suggest(key, known)
				local message = `{childPath}: unknown configuration key`
				if hint then
					message ..= ` (did you mean '{hint}'?)`
				end
				table.insert(errors, message)
			end
		end
		local result = {}
		for _, key in known do
			local childPath = if path == "" then key else `{path}.{key}`
			result[key] = resolve(fields[key], value[key], childPath, errors)
		end
		return result
	elseif kind == "map" then
		if type(value) ~= "table" then
			table.insert(errors, `{path}: expected a table, got {describe(value)}`)
			return defaultOf(node)
		end
		local result = defaultOf(node)
		for key, entry in value do
			if type(key) ~= "string" then
				table.insert(errors, `{path}: keys must be strings, got {describe(key)}`)
			else
				result[key] = resolve(node.ValueSchema :: SchemaNode, entry, `{path}.{key}`, errors)
			end
		end
		return result
	elseif kind == "open" then
		if type(value) ~= "table" then
			table.insert(errors, `{path}: expected a table, got {describe(value)}`)
			return {}
		end
		local result = {}
		for key, entry in value do
			if type(key) ~= "string" then
				table.insert(errors, `{path}: keys must be strings, got {describe(key)}`)
			else
				-- Snapshot now; validated when the owning module calls Extend().
				local ok, cloned = pcall(deepClone, entry)
				if ok then
					result[key] = cloned
				else
					table.insert(errors, `{path}.{key}: {cloned}`)
				end
			end
		end
		return result
	end
	error(`Configuration: unknown schema kind '{tostring(kind)}'`)
end

-- Configuration object ---------------------------------------------------------------

local Configuration = {}
Configuration.__index = Configuration
Configuration.Schema = table.freeze(Schema)

export type Configuration = typeof(setmetatable(
	{} :: {
		_schema: SchemaNode,
		_values: { [string]: any }?,
		-- open path -> raw user values awaiting Extend()
		_pending: { [string]: { [string]: any } },
		-- open path -> resolved, frozen sections
		_claimed: { [string]: { [string]: any } },
	},
	Configuration
))

function Configuration.new(schema: SchemaNode): Configuration
	assert(type(schema) == "table" and schema.Kind == "table", "Configuration root schema must be a Table")
	return setmetatable({ _schema = schema, _values = nil, _pending = {}, _claimed = {} }, Configuration)
end

local function findOpenNodes(node: SchemaNode, path: string, out: { string })
	if node.Kind == "open" then
		table.insert(out, path)
	elseif node.Kind == "table" then
		for key, field in node.Fields :: { [string]: SchemaNode } do
			findOpenNodes(field, if path == "" then key else `{path}.{key}`, out)
		end
	end
end

-- Validates overrides against the schema. Returns (true, {}) on success, or
-- (false, errors) without changing any state.
function Configuration.Load(self: Configuration, overrides: { [string]: any }?): (boolean, { string })
	if self._values ~= nil then
		error("Configuration has already been loaded", 2)
	end
	local errors = {}
	if overrides ~= nil and type(overrides) ~= "table" then
		return false, { `configuration must be a table, got {typeof(overrides)}` }
	end
	local resolved = resolve(self._schema, overrides or {}, "", errors)
	if #errors > 0 then
		return false, errors
	end

	local openPaths = {}
	findOpenNodes(self._schema, "", openPaths)
	for _, openPath in openPaths do
		local container = resolved
		local parts = string.split(openPath, ".")
		for i = 1, #parts - 1 do
			container = container[parts[i]]
		end
		local last = parts[#parts]
		self._pending[openPath] = container[last]
		self._claimed[openPath] = {}
		-- Replaced on read by the claimed sections (see Get).
		container[last] = nil
	end

	self._values = deepFreeze(resolved)
	return true, {}
end

function Configuration.IsLoaded(self: Configuration): boolean
	return self._values ~= nil
end

-- Validates the raw value at `openPath.key` against `schema` and makes it readable via
-- Get(). Must be called after Load(). A key may be claimed once.
function Configuration.Extend(self: Configuration, openPath: string, key: string, schema: SchemaNode): (boolean, { string })
	if self._values == nil then
		error("Configuration:Extend called before Load", 2)
	end
	local pending = self._pending[openPath]
	local claimed = self._claimed[openPath]
	if pending == nil or claimed == nil then
		error(`Configuration: '{openPath}' is not an open section`, 2)
	end
	if claimed[key] ~= nil then
		error(`Configuration: '{openPath}.{key}' has already been claimed`, 2)
	end
	if type(schema) ~= "table" or schema.Kind ~= "table" then
		error(`Configuration: schema for '{openPath}.{key}' must be a Schema.Table`, 2)
	end
	local errors = {}
	local value = resolve(schema, pending[key], `{openPath}.{key}`, errors)
	if #errors > 0 then
		return false, errors
	end
	claimed[key] = deepFreeze(value)
	pending[key] = nil
	return true, {}
end

-- Keys supplied under open sections that no module has claimed, as full paths.
function Configuration.UnclaimedKeys(self: Configuration): { string }
	local result = {}
	for openPath, pending in self._pending do
		for key in pending do
			table.insert(result, `{openPath}.{key}`)
		end
	end
	table.sort(result)
	return result
end

-- Reads a dotted path such as "Evidence.MaxRecordsPerPlayer". Returned tables are frozen.
function Configuration.Get(self: Configuration, path: string): any
	local values = self._values
	if values == nil then
		error("Configuration:Get called before Load", 2)
	end
	for openPath, claimed in self._claimed do
		if path == openPath then
			return table.freeze(table.clone(claimed))
		end
		local prefix = openPath .. "."
		if string.sub(path, 1, #prefix) == prefix then
			local rest = string.split(string.sub(path, #prefix + 1), ".")
			local current: any = claimed
			for _, part in rest do
				if type(current) ~= "table" then
					return nil
				end
				current = current[part]
			end
			return current
		end
	end
	local current: any = values
	for _, part in string.split(path, ".") do
		if type(current) ~= "table" then
			return nil
		end
		current = current[part]
	end
	return current
end

return Configuration
```

```luau
return function(): Configuration.SchemaNode
	return S.Table({

				MinRiskToSave = S.Number(40, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),

				Weight = S.Number(0.5, { Min = 0, Max = 1 }),
				HalfLifeHours = S.Number(24, { Min = 0.1, Max = 8760 }),
				Cap = S.Number(35, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
				FlushIntervalSeconds = S.Number(30, { Min = 1, Max = 3600 }),
				MaxWritesPerFlush = S.Number(10, { Min = 1, Max = 100, Integer = true }),
				MaxAttempts = S.Number(3, { Min = 1, Max = 10, Integer = true }),
				-- Session summaries kept in the saved record.
				MaxSessions = S.Number(5, { Min = 1, Max = 50, Integer = true }),

		Scheduler = S.Table({
			MaxConsecutiveFailures = S.Number(5, { Min = 1, Max = 1000, Integer = true }),
		}),

		EventBus = S.Table({
			MaxHandlerFailures = S.Number(10, { Min = 1, Max = 1000, Integer = true }),
			MaxDispatchDepth = S.Number(8, { Min = 1, Max = 64, Integer = true }),
		}),
	})
```

`src/Server/Core/EventBus.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Synchronous, error-isolated publish/subscribe bus.

	- Delivery is synchronous and in subscription order, so behaviour is deterministic
	  and testable. Handlers must not yield; spawn a thread yourself for slow work.
	- Every handler runs in pcall. A failing handler is logged and, after
	  MaxHandlerFailures consecutive failures, disconnected so one broken subscriber
	  cannot flood logs or degrade the server.
	- In strict mode (the framework default) publishing or subscribing to an
	  unregistered topic raises an error, which catches typos at development time.
	- Re-entrant publishes are allowed up to MaxDispatchDepth to stop runaway loops.
]]

local Logger = require(script.Parent.Logger)

type Logger = Logger.Logger

export type Subscription = {
	Connected: boolean,
	Topic: string,
	Owner: string,
	Disconnect: (self: Subscription) -> (),
}

type InternalSubscription = Subscription & {
	_handler: (any) -> (),
	_failures: number,
}

local EventBus = {}
EventBus.__index = EventBus

export type EventBus = typeof(setmetatable(
	{} :: {
		_logger: Logger,
		_strict: boolean,
		_maxFailures: number,
		_maxDepth: number,
		_topics: { [string]: boolean },
		_subscriptions: { [string]: { InternalSubscription } },
		_dirty: { [string]: boolean },
		_depth: number,
		_destroyed: boolean,
		_published: number,
		_handlerErrors: number,
	},
	EventBus
))

export type EventBusOptions = {
	Logger: Logger,
	Strict: boolean?,
	MaxHandlerFailures: number?,
	MaxDispatchDepth: number?,
}

function EventBus.new(options: EventBusOptions): EventBus
	return setmetatable({
		_logger = options.Logger,
		_strict = options.Strict ~= false,
		_maxFailures = options.MaxHandlerFailures or 10,
		_maxDepth = options.MaxDispatchDepth or 8,
		_topics = {},
		_subscriptions = {},
		_dirty = {},
		_depth = 0,
		_destroyed = false,
		_published = 0,
		_handlerErrors = 0,
	}, EventBus)
end

function EventBus.RegisterTopic(self: EventBus, topic: string)
	if type(topic) ~= "string" or #topic == 0 then
		error("EventBus:RegisterTopic expects a non-empty string", 2)
	end
	self._topics[topic] = true
end

function EventBus.HasTopic(self: EventBus, topic: string): boolean
	return self._topics[topic] == true
end

local function checkTopic(self: EventBus, topic: string, level: number)
	if type(topic) ~= "string" then
		error("EventBus: topic must be a string", level + 1)
	end
	if self._strict and not self._topics[topic] then
		error(`EventBus: unknown topic '{topic}'`, level + 1)
	end
end

local function compact(self: EventBus, topic: string)
	local list = self._subscriptions[topic]
	if list then
		local kept = {}
		for _, sub in list do
			if sub.Connected then
				table.insert(kept, sub)
			end
		end
		self._subscriptions[topic] = kept
	end
	self._dirty[topic] = nil
end

function EventBus.Subscribe(self: EventBus, topic: string, handler: (any) -> (), owner: string?): Subscription
	checkTopic(self, topic, 2)
	if type(handler) ~= "function" then
		error("EventBus:Subscribe expects a handler function", 2)
	end
	if self._destroyed then
		error("EventBus:Subscribe called on a destroyed bus", 2)
	end

	local bus = self
	local sub: InternalSubscription
	sub = {
		Connected = true,
		Topic = topic,
		Owner = owner or "anonymous",
		_handler = handler,
		_failures = 0,
		Disconnect = function(_self: Subscription)
			if not sub.Connected then
				return
			end
			sub.Connected = false
			if bus._depth > 0 then
				bus._dirty[topic] = true
			else
				compact(bus, topic)
			end
		end,
	} :: any

	local list = self._subscriptions[topic]
	if list == nil then
		list = {}
		self._subscriptions[topic] = list
	end
	table.insert(list, sub)
	return sub
end

-- Delivers payload to every connected subscriber of topic. Returns the number of
-- handlers that completed without error.
function EventBus.Publish(self: EventBus, topic: string, payload: any): number
	checkTopic(self, topic, 2)
	if self._destroyed then
		return 0
	end
	local list = self._subscriptions[topic]
	if list == nil or #list == 0 then
		self._published += 1
		return 0
	end
	if self._depth >= self._maxDepth then
		self._logger:Error("Dispatch depth exceeded; event dropped", { Topic = topic, Depth = self._depth })
		return 0
	end

	self._published += 1
	self._depth += 1
	local delivered = 0
	-- Snapshot the length: subscribers added during dispatch receive the next event.
	for i = 1, #list do
		local sub = list[i]
		if sub.Connected then
			local ok: boolean, err: any = pcall(sub._handler, payload)
			if ok then
				delivered += 1
				sub._failures = 0
			else
				self._handlerErrors += 1
				sub._failures += 1
				self._logger:Error("Event handler failed", {
					Topic = topic,
					Owner = sub.Owner,
					Error = tostring(err),
					ConsecutiveFailures = sub._failures,
				})
				if sub._failures >= self._maxFailures then
					sub:Disconnect()
					self._logger:Error("Event handler disconnected after repeated failures", {
						Topic = topic,
						Owner = sub.Owner,
					})
				end
			end
		end
	end
	self._depth -= 1

	if self._depth == 0 then
		for dirtyTopic in self._dirty do
			compact(self, dirtyTopic)
		end
	end
	return delivered
end

function EventBus.SubscriberCount(self: EventBus, topic: string): number
	local list = self._subscriptions[topic]
	if list == nil then
		return 0
	end
	local count = 0
	for _, sub in list do
		if sub.Connected then
			count += 1
		end
	end
	return count
end

function EventBus.GetStats(self: EventBus): { Published: number, HandlerErrors: number, Topics: number }
	local topics = 0
	for _ in self._topics do
		topics += 1
	end
	return { Published = self._published, HandlerErrors = self._handlerErrors, Topics = topics }
end

function EventBus.Destroy(self: EventBus)
	for _, list in self._subscriptions do
		for _, sub in list do
			sub.Connected = false
		end
	end
	table.clear(self._subscriptions)
	table.clear(self._dirty)
	self._destroyed = true
end

return EventBus

```

`src/Server/Core/Framework.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	ACF framework orchestrator.

	Owns the core services (configuration, logger, event bus, scheduler, player registry,
	evidence, detection pipeline, module loader) and drives the framework lifecycle:

	  Created --Initialize(config)--> Initialized --Start()--> Running
	  Running --Stop()--> Stopped --Start()--> Running
	  any --Destroy()--> Destroyed

	Error policy:
	  - Developer errors (invalid configuration, invalid module definitions, calls in the
	    wrong state, a Critical module failing to start) raise immediately with a clear
	    message. Failing loudly at boot is safer than running half-configured.
	  - Runtime faults inside modules, event handlers and scheduled tasks are isolated,
	    logged and never propagate into the framework.

	Framework does not touch Roblox globals. All platform access goes through the
	Environment passed to new(); see Platform/RobloxEnvironment.
]]

local Constants = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("ACFShared"):WaitForChild("Constants"))
local Cleanup = require(script.Parent.Cleanup)
local Configuration = require(script.Parent.Configuration)
local DefaultConfig = require(script.Parent.DefaultConfig)
local EventBus = require(script.Parent.EventBus)
local Logger = require(script.Parent.Logger)
local ModuleLoader = require(script.Parent.ModuleLoader)
local PlayerRegistry = require(script.Parent.PlayerRegistry)
local Scheduler = require(script.Parent.Scheduler)
local Topics = require(script.Parent.Topics)
local BuiltIns = require(script.Parent.Parent.Detection.BuiltIns)
local DetectionManager = require(script.Parent.Parent.Detection.DetectionManager)
local EvidenceManager = require(script.Parent.Parent.Intelligence.EvidenceManager)
local RiskEngine = require(script.Parent.Parent.Intelligence.RiskEngine)
local EnforcementManager = require(script.Parent.Parent.Enforcement.EnforcementManager)
local Types = require(script.Parent.Parent.Types)

type FrameworkState = Types.FrameworkState
type ModuleDefinition = Types.ModuleDefinition

local MODULE_SETTINGS = "ModuleSettings"

-- Built-in module names reserved for later phases. Enabling one in configuration
-- before it ships produces a warning rather than silently doing nothing.
-- Built-in modules announced for a later phase (name -> phase). Configuring one of
-- these logs "not available in this ACF version" instead of "not registered".
local PLANNED_BUILTINS: { [string]: string } = table.freeze({})

type Services = {
	Config: Configuration.Configuration,
	Logger: Logger.Logger,
	Bus: EventBus.EventBus,
	Scheduler: Scheduler.Scheduler,
	Registry: PlayerRegistry.PlayerRegistry,
	Evidence: EvidenceManager.EvidenceManager,
	Detections: DetectionManager.DetectionManager,
	-- nil when Risk.Enabled = false.
	Risk: RiskEngine.RiskEngine?,
	Enforcement: EnforcementManager.EnforcementManager,
	Loader: ModuleLoader.ModuleLoader,
}

local Framework = {}
Framework.__index = Framework
Framework.Version = Constants.VERSION
Framework.Channel = Constants.CHANNEL

export type Framework = typeof(setmetatable(
	{} :: {
		_env: Types.Environment,
		_state: FrameworkState,
		_services: Services?,
		_queued: { ModuleDefinition },
		_runCleanup: Cleanup.Cleanup?,
		_resolved: boolean,
	},
	Framework
))

local function validateEnvironment(env: any)
	assert(type(env) == "table", "ACF: Framework.new requires an Environment table")
	assert(env.Players ~= nil, "ACF: Environment.Players is required")
	assert(type(env.Clock) == "table", "ACF: Environment.Clock is required")
	assert(type(env.Clock.Now) == "function", "ACF: Environment.Clock.Now must be a function")
	assert(type(env.Clock.Wall) == "function", "ACF: Environment.Clock.Wall must be a function")
	assert(type(env.GenerateId) == "function", "ACF: Environment.GenerateId must be a function")
end

function Framework.new(environment: Types.Environment): Framework
	validateEnvironment(environment)
	return setmetatable({
		_env = environment,
		_state = "Created" :: FrameworkState,
		_services = nil,
		_queued = {},
		_runCleanup = nil,
		_resolved = false,
	}, Framework)
end

local function setState(self: Framework, to: FrameworkState)
	local from = self._state
	self._state = to
	local services = self._services
	if services and to ~= "Destroyed" then
		services.Bus:Publish(Topics.FrameworkStateChanged, { From = from, To = to })
	end
end

local function requireServices(self: Framework, action: string): Services
	local services = self._services
	if services == nil then
		error(`ACF: {action} requires Initialize() to be called first`, 3)
	end
	return services
end

local function formatErrors(header: string, errors: { string }): string
	return header .. "\n  - " .. table.concat(errors, "\n  - ")
end

-- A Player, Session or UserId -> UserId.
local function resolveTarget(target: any): number?
	if type(target) == "number" then
		return target
	end
	local ok, userId = pcall(function()
		return target.UserId
	end)
	return if ok and type(userId) == "number" then userId else nil
end

-- Module context ---------------------------------------------------------------------

local function makeContext(self: Framework, services: Services, entry: ModuleLoader.ModuleEntry): Types.ModuleContext
	local name = entry.Name
	local config = services.Config
	local moduleConfig = config:Get(`{MODULE_SETTINGS}.{name}`) or table.freeze({})

	local function bag(): Cleanup.Cleanup
		return entry.ActiveCleanup
	end

	local events = table.freeze({
		Subscribe = function(_, topic: string, handler: (any) -> ())
			return bag():Add(services.Bus:Subscribe(topic, handler, name))
		end,
		-- Declares a module-owned topic "<ModuleName>.<suffix>" and returns its full name.
		Declare = function(_, suffix: string): string
			if type(suffix) ~= "string" or not string.match(suffix, "^[%a][%w_]*$") then
				error("Events:Declare expects an identifier suffix", 2)
			end
			local topic = `{name}.{suffix}`
			services.Bus:RegisterTopic(topic)
			return topic
		end,
		-- True when a topic exists (another module may be disabled and not declare it).
		Has = function(_, topic: string): boolean
			return services.Bus:HasTopic(topic)
		end,
		Publish = function(_, topic: string, payload: any): number
			if type(topic) ~= "string" or string.sub(topic, 1, #name + 1) ~= name .. "." then
				error(`module '{name}' may only publish its own topics ('{name}.*'); got '{tostring(topic)}'`, 2)
			end
			return services.Bus:Publish(topic, payload)
		end,
	})

	local scheduler = table.freeze({
		Every = function(_, taskName: string, interval: number, callback, phase: number?)
			return bag():Add(services.Scheduler:Every(`{name}.{taskName}`, interval, callback, phase))
		end,
		After = function(_, taskName: string, delay: number, callback)
			return bag():Add(services.Scheduler:After(`{name}.{taskName}`, delay, callback))
		end,
	})

	local players = table.freeze({
		GetSession = function(_, target: any)
			return services.Registry:GetSession(target)
		end,
		GetSessions = function(_)
			return services.Registry:GetSessions()
		end,
	})

	-- The Cleanup facade routes to the lifetime bag during Initialize and to the run
	-- bag afterwards, matching the facades above.
	local cleanup = table.freeze({
		Add = function(_, item: any)
			return bag():Add(item)
		end,
	})

	-- Read-only questions modules may ask about enforcement; they never decide it.
	local enforcementFacade = table.freeze({
		IsRestricted = function(_, target: any, tag: string | boolean, operation: string?): boolean
			local userId = resolveTarget(target)
			return userId ~= nil and services.Enforcement:IsRestricted(userId, tag, operation or name)
		end,
		ShouldCorrect = function(_, target: any): boolean
			local userId = resolveTarget(target)
			return userId ~= nil and services.Enforcement:ShouldCorrect(userId)
		end,
		NoteCorrected = function(_, target: any, details: { [string]: any })
			local userId = resolveTarget(target)
			if userId ~= nil then
				services.Enforcement:NoteCorrected(userId, details)
			end
		end,
	})

	local context: Types.ModuleContext = table.freeze({
		Name = name,
		Config = moduleConfig,
		Logger = services.Logger:Scope(name),
		Clock = self._env.Clock,
		Cleanup = cleanup,
		Events = events,
		Scheduler = scheduler,
		Players = players,
		Platform = table.freeze({ Physics = self._env.Physics, Remotes = self._env.Remotes }),
		Enforcement = enforcementFacade,
		Report = function(_, target: any, input: Types.DetectionInput): (boolean, string)
			return services.Detections:Report(entry, target, input)
		end,
		GetConfig = function(_, path: string): any
			return config:Get(path)
		end,
		GetModule = function(_, other: string): any
			if not table.find(entry.Dependencies, other) then
				error(`module '{name}' did not declare '{other}' in Dependencies`, 2)
			end
			local dep = services.Loader:Get(other)
			return if dep then dep.Definition else nil
		end,
	}) :: any
	return context
end

-- Lifecycle --------------------------------------------------------------------------

function Framework.Initialize(self: Framework, userConfig: { [string]: any }?)
	if self._state ~= "Created" then
		error(`ACF: Initialize can only be called once (state is {self._state})`, 2)
	end
	local env = self._env

	local config = Configuration.new(DefaultConfig())
	local ok, errors = config:Load(userConfig)
	if not ok then
		error(formatErrors("ACF configuration is invalid:", errors), 2)
	end

	local level = if config:Get("Debug") then "Debug" else config:Get("LogLevel")
	local rootLogger = Logger.new({
		Level = level,
		BufferSize = config:Get("Logging.BufferSize"),
		RateLimitBurst = config:Get("Logging.RateLimitBurst"),
		RateLimitWindowSeconds = config:Get("Logging.RateLimitWindowSeconds"),
		Now = env.Clock.Now,
		Wall = env.Clock.Wall,
		Sink = env.LogSink,
	})

	local bus = EventBus.new({
		Logger = rootLogger:Scope("Core.EventBus"),
		Strict = true,
		MaxHandlerFailures = config:Get("EventBus.MaxHandlerFailures"),
		MaxDispatchDepth = config:Get("EventBus.MaxDispatchDepth"),
	})
	for _, topic: string in Topics :: any do
		bus:RegisterTopic(topic)
	end

	local scheduler = Scheduler.new({
		Now = env.Clock.Now,
		Logger = rootLogger:Scope("Core.Scheduler"),
		MaxConsecutiveFailures = config:Get("Scheduler.MaxConsecutiveFailures"),
	})

	local registry = PlayerRegistry.new({
		Players = env.Players,
		Bus = bus,
		Logger = rootLogger:Scope("Core.Players"),
		Clock = env.Clock,
		GenerateId = env.GenerateId,
	})

	local evidence = EvidenceManager.new({
		Clock = env.Clock,
		Logger = rootLogger:Scope("Intelligence.Evidence"),
		GenerateId = env.GenerateId,
		Settings = config:Get("Evidence"),
	})

	local detections = DetectionManager.new({
		Registry = registry,
		Evidence = evidence,
		Bus = bus,
		Logger = rootLogger:Scope("Detection"),
		Clock = env.Clock,
		GenerateId = env.GenerateId,
		Settings = config:Get("Detection"),
	})

	local risk: RiskEngine.RiskEngine? = nil
	if config:Get("Risk.Enabled") then
		local riskOk, created = pcall(RiskEngine.new, {
			Bus = bus,
			Registry = registry,
			Logger = rootLogger:Scope("Intelligence.Risk"),
			Clock = env.Clock,
			Settings = config:Get("Risk"),
			Store = env.Store,
			OnShutdown = env.OnShutdown,
		})
		if not riskOk then
			bus:Destroy()
			error(tostring(created), 2)
		end
		risk = created
	end

	-- Created after the RiskEngine so its DetectionReported handler runs after risk has
	-- been updated (the bus calls handlers in subscription order).
	local enforcement = EnforcementManager.new({
		Bus = bus,
		Registry = registry,
		Logger = rootLogger:Scope("Enforcement"),
		Clock = env.Clock,
		GenerateId = env.GenerateId,
		Settings = config:Get("Enforcement"),
		Risk = risk,
		Store = env.Store,
		Enforcer = env.Enforcer,
	})

	local services: Services
	local loader = ModuleLoader.new({
		Logger = rootLogger:Scope("Core.Modules"),
		Bus = bus,
		MakeContext = function(entry)
			return makeContext(self, services, entry)
		end,
	})

	services = {
		Config = config,
		Logger = rootLogger,
		Bus = bus,
		Scheduler = scheduler,
		Registry = registry,
		Evidence = evidence,
		Detections = detections,
		Risk = risk,
		Enforcement = enforcement,
		Loader = loader,
	}

	-- Register built-ins, then modules queued before Initialize. Everything is
	-- validated before committing, so a failed Initialize leaves the framework in Created.
	local definitions: { ModuleDefinition } = {}
	for _, factory in BuiltIns do
		table.insert(definitions, factory())
	end
	for _, definition in self._queued do
		table.insert(definitions, definition)
	end
	for _, definition in definitions do
		loader:Register(definition)
		if definition.ConfigSchema then
			local extendOk, extendErrors = config:Extend(MODULE_SETTINGS, definition.Name, definition.ConfigSchema)
			if not extendOk then
				error(formatErrors(`ACF configuration for module '{definition.Name}' is invalid:`, extendErrors), 2)
			end
		end
	end
	table.clear(self._queued)

	self._services = services
	setState(self, "Initialized")
	rootLogger:Info("Framework initialized", { Version = Constants.VERSION, Channel = Constants.CHANNEL })
end

-- Registers a module. Allowed before Initialize (queued) or between Initialize and the
-- first Start. Raises on invalid definitions or invalid module configuration.
function Framework.RegisterModule(self: Framework, definition: ModuleDefinition)
	local state = self._state
	if state == "Created" then
		local problem = ModuleLoader.Validate(definition)
		if problem then
			error("ACF: " .. problem, 2)
		end
		for _, queued in self._queued do
			if queued.Name == definition.Name then
				error(`ACF: a module named '{definition.Name}' is already registered`, 2)
			end
		end
		table.insert(self._queued, definition)
		return
	end
	if state ~= "Initialized" or self._resolved then
		error(`ACF: modules must be registered before the first Start (state is {state})`, 2)
	end
	local services = requireServices(self, "RegisterModule")
	services.Loader:Register(definition)
	if definition.ConfigSchema then
		local ok, errors = services.Config:Extend(MODULE_SETTINGS, definition.Name, definition.ConfigSchema)
		if not ok then
			error(formatErrors(`ACF configuration for module '{definition.Name}' is invalid:`, errors), 2)
		end
	end
end

local function resolveModules(self: Framework, services: Services)
	local config = services.Config
	local unclaimed = config:UnclaimedKeys()
	if #unclaimed > 0 then
		local errors = {}
		for _, path in unclaimed do
			table.insert(errors, `{path}: no registered module with a ConfigSchema has this name`)
		end
		error(formatErrors("ACF configuration is invalid:", errors), 3)
	end

	local enabledMap: { [string]: boolean } = config:Get("Modules")
	for moduleName, enabled in enabledMap do
		if enabled and not services.Loader:Has(moduleName) then
			local phase = PLANNED_BUILTINS[moduleName]
			if phase then
				services.Logger:Warn("Configured module is not available in this ACF version", {
					Module = moduleName,
					PlannedFor = phase,
					Version = Constants.VERSION,
				})
			else
				services.Logger:Warn("Configured module is not registered", { Module = moduleName })
			end
		end
	end

	services.Loader:Resolve(function(entry)
		local configured = enabledMap[entry.Name]
		if configured ~= nil then
			return configured
		end
		return entry.Definition.EnabledByDefault ~= false
	end)
	services.Loader:InitializeAll()
	self._resolved = true
end

-- Starts the framework. Returns a status snapshot. Raises if a Critical module fails.
function Framework.Start(self: Framework): { [string]: any }
	local state = self._state
	if state ~= "Initialized" and state ~= "Stopped" then
		error(`ACF: Start requires state Initialized or Stopped (state is {state})`, 2)
	end
	local services = requireServices(self, "Start")

	if not self._resolved then
		resolveModules(self, services)
	end
	services.Loader:StartAll()

	local criticalFailures = {}
	for name, info in services.Loader:GetStates() do
		if info.Critical and info.State ~= "Running" and info.State ~= "Disabled" then
			table.insert(criticalFailures, `{name} ({info.State}{if info.Error then ": " .. info.Error else ""})`)
		end
	end
	if #criticalFailures > 0 then
		table.sort(criticalFailures)
		services.Loader:StopAll()
		error(formatErrors("ACF: critical modules failed to start:", criticalFailures), 2)
	end

	local runCleanup = Cleanup.new(function(message)
		services.Logger:Error("Framework cleanup task failed", { Error = message })
	end)
	runCleanup:Add(services.Scheduler:Every("ACF.EvidencePrune", services.Config:Get("Evidence.PruneIntervalSeconds"), function()
		services.Evidence:Prune()
	end))
	if services.Risk then
		services.Risk:Start(services.Scheduler, runCleanup)
	end
	services.Enforcement:Start(services.Scheduler, runCleanup, self._env.OnShutdown)
	local heartbeat = self._env.Heartbeat
	if heartbeat ~= nil then
		services.Scheduler:Bind(heartbeat)
		runCleanup:Add(function()
			services.Scheduler:Unbind()
		end)
	end
	self._runCleanup = runCleanup

	setState(self, "Running")
	-- Sessions start after modules are running so modules observe every join,
	-- including players already in the server.
	services.Registry:Start()
	services.Logger:Info("Framework started", {
		Modules = #services.Loader:InState("Running"),
		Players = services.Registry:Count(),
	})
	return self:GetStatus()
end

function Framework.Stop(self: Framework)
	if self._state ~= "Running" then
		return
	end
	local services = requireServices(self, "Stop")
	-- End sessions first so modules receive PlayerSessionEnded while still running.
	services.Registry:Stop()
	if services.Risk then
		-- Sessions have ended (and queued their risk records); write them now.
		services.Risk:Stop()
	end
	services.Enforcement:Stop()
	services.Loader:StopAll()
	local runCleanup = self._runCleanup
	if runCleanup then
		runCleanup:Clean()
		self._runCleanup = nil
	end
	setState(self, "Stopped")
	services.Logger:Info("Framework stopped")
end

function Framework.Destroy(self: Framework)
	if self._state == "Destroyed" then
		return
	end
	self:Stop()
	local services = self._services
	if services then
		services.Loader:DestroyAll()
		services.Scheduler:Destroy()
		services.Evidence:Clear()
		if services.Risk then
			services.Risk:Destroy()
		end
		services.Enforcement:Destroy()
		services.Logger:Info("Framework destroyed")
		services.Bus:Destroy()
	end
	table.clear(self._queued)
	setState(self, "Destroyed")
end

-- Advances the scheduler once. Production code never needs this (Heartbeat drives it);
-- hosts without a Heartbeat signal, such as tests, call it manually.
function Framework.Step(self: Framework)
	if self._state ~= "Running" then
		return
	end
	requireServices(self, "Step").Scheduler:Step()
end

-- Queries ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

function Framework.GetState(self: Framework): FrameworkState
	return self._state
end

function Framework.GetStatus(self: Framework): { [string]: any }
	local services = self._services
	if services == nil then
		return { State = self._state, Version = Constants.VERSION, Channel = Constants.CHANNEL, QueuedModules = #self._queued }
	end
	return {
		State = self._state,
		Version = Constants.VERSION,
		Channel = Constants.CHANNEL,
		Modules = services.Loader:GetStates(),
		ModuleOrder = services.Loader:GetOrder(),
		Players = services.Registry:GetStats(),
		Evidence = services.Evidence:GetStats(),
		Detections = services.Detections:GetStats(),
		Risk = if services.Risk then services.Risk:GetStats() else nil,
		Enforcement = services.Enforcement:GetStats(),
		EventBus = services.Bus:GetStats(),
		Scheduler = services.Scheduler:GetStats(),
		Logging = services.Logger:GetStats(),
	}
end

function Framework.GetSession(self: Framework, target: any): PlayerRegistry.Session?
	return requireServices(self, "GetSession").Registry:GetSession(target)
end

function Framework.GetSessions(self: Framework): { PlayerRegistry.Session }
	return requireServices(self, "GetSessions").Registry:GetSessions()
end

local function resolveUserId(target: any): number?
	if type(target) == "number" then
		return target
	end
	local ok, userId = pcall(function()
		return target.UserId
	end)
	return if ok and type(userId) == "number" then userId else nil
end

-- Evidence for a Player, Session or UserId. Works after the player has left, until the
-- records expire.
function Framework.GetEvidence(self: Framework, target: any, filter: any?): { Types.EvidenceRecord }
	local services = requireServices(self, "GetEvidence")
	local userId = resolveUserId(target)
	if userId == nil then
		error("ACF: GetEvidence expects a Player, Session or UserId", 2)
	end
	return services.Evidence:Get(userId, filter)
end

function Framework.GetEvidenceSummary(self: Framework, target: any): EvidenceManager.EvidenceSummary
	local services = requireServices(self, "GetEvidenceSummary")
	local userId = resolveUserId(target)
	if userId == nil then
		error("ACF: GetEvidenceSummary expects a Player, Session or UserId", 2)
	end
	return services.Evidence:Summary(userId)
end

local function riskFor(self: Framework, action: string, target: any): (RiskEngine.RiskEngine, number)
	local services = requireServices(self, action)
	local userId = resolveUserId(target)
	if userId == nil then
		error(`ACF: {action} expects a Player, Session or UserId`, 3)
	end
	local risk = services.Risk
	if risk == nil then
		error("ACF: risk assessment is disabled (Risk.Enabled = false)", 3)
	end
	return risk, userId
end

-- Current risk for a Player, Session or UserId, with groups and an explanation.
function Framework.GetRisk(self: Framework, target: any): RiskEngine.RiskReport
	local risk, userId = riskFor(self, "GetRisk", target)
	return risk:Get(userId)
end

-- Risk history (detections that changed risk, band changes, loaded history, resets).
function Framework.GetRiskHistory(self: Framework, target: any): { RiskEngine.HistoryEntry }
	local risk, userId = riskFor(self, "GetRiskHistory", target)
	return risk:GetHistory(userId)
end

-- Staff override: clears a user's risk; options = { Persistent = true, Reason = "..." }.
function Framework.ResetRisk(self: Framework, target: any, options: { Persistent: boolean?, Reason: string? }?)
	local risk, userId = riskFor(self, "ResetRisk", target)
	risk:Reset(userId, options)
end

-- Enforcement (Phase 7) ------------------------------------------------------------

local function enforcementFor(self: Framework, action: string, target: any): (EnforcementManager.EnforcementManager, number)
	local services = requireServices(self, action)
	local userId = resolveUserId(target)
	if userId == nil then
		error(`ACF: {action} expects a Player, Session or UserId`, 3)
	end
	return services.Enforcement, userId
end

function Framework.GetEnforcement(self: Framework, target: any): { [string]: any }
	local enforcement, userId = enforcementFor(self, "GetEnforcement", target)
	return enforcement:Get(userId)
end

function Framework.IsRestricted(self: Framework, target: any, tag: string | boolean, operation: string?): boolean
	local enforcement, userId = enforcementFor(self, "IsRestricted", target)
	return enforcement:IsRestricted(userId, tag, operation or "Game")
end

function Framework.Kick(self: Framework, target: any, options: { By: any, Reason: string? }): string
	local enforcement, userId = enforcementFor(self, "Kick", target)
	return enforcement:Kick(userId, options)
end

function Framework.Ban(self: Framework, target: any, options: { By: any, Reason: string?, Hours: number? }): string
	local enforcement, userId = enforcementFor(self, "Ban", target)
	return enforcement:Ban(userId, options)
end

function Framework.Unban(self: Framework, target: any, options: { By: any, Reason: string? })
	local enforcement, userId = enforcementFor(self, "Unban", target)
	enforcement:Unban(userId, options)
end

function Framework.SetEnforcementOverride(self: Framework, target: any, options: { [string]: any })
	local enforcement, userId = enforcementFor(self, "SetEnforcementOverride", target)
	enforcement:SetOverride(userId, options)
end

	enforcement:ClearOverride(userId, options)
end

	enforcement:LoadRecord(userId, callback)
end

function Framework.GetCases(self: Framework, filter: { Status: string?, UserId: number? }?): { EnforcementManager.Case }
	return requireServices(self, "GetCases").Enforcement:GetCases(filter)

-- Subscribes to a core topic (see Core/Topics). Returns a subscription with Disconnect().

	return requireServices(self, "Subscribe").Bus:Subscribe(topic, handler, "External")
end

	return self:Subscribe(Topics.DetectionReported, handler)
end

	local entry = requireServices(self, "GetModule").Loader:Get(name)
	return if entry then entry.Definition else nil
end

	local entry = requireServices(self, "GetModuleState").Loader:Get(name)
	return if entry then entry.State else nil
end

	return requireServices(self, "GetConfig").Config:Get(path)
end
```
