`src/Server/Core/Logger.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Structured, rate-limited logger.

	Every entry has a level, a scope (e.g. "Core.Scheduler" or a module name), a constant
	message and optional structured fields. Put variable data in fields, not in the
	message: rate limiting is keyed by scope + message, so a constant message lets a flood
	of identical events collapse into one entry plus a suppressed count.

	Entries go to a sink (default: print/warn) and into a bounded ring buffer that admin
	tooling and tests can read back with GetRecent().
]]

local Types = require(script.Parent.Parent.Types)
local RateLimiter = require(script.Parent.RateLimiter)

type LogLevel = Types.LogLevel
type LogEntry = Types.LogEntry
type LogFields = Types.LogFields

local LEVEL_ORDER: { [string]: number } = { Debug = 1, Info = 2, Warn = 3, Error = 4 }
local MAX_SUPPRESSED_KEYS = 256

type SharedState = {
	level: number,
	buffer: { LogEntry },
	bufferSize: number,
	nextIndex: number,
	buffered: number,
	limiter: RateLimiter.RateLimiter,
	suppressedByKey: { [string]: number },
	suppressedKeyCount: number,
	sink: (LogEntry) -> (),
	now: () -> number,
	wall: () -> number,
	emitted: number,
	suppressed: number,
	sinkFailures: number,
}

local Logger = {}
Logger.__index = Logger

export type Logger = typeof(setmetatable({} :: { _scope: string, _shared: SharedState }, Logger))

export type LoggerOptions = {
	Level: LogLevel?,
	BufferSize: number?,
	RateLimitBurst: number?,
	RateLimitWindowSeconds: number?,
	Now: () -> number,
	Wall: (() -> number)?,
	Sink: ((LogEntry) -> ())?,
}

local function formatValue(value: any): string
	if type(value) == "string" then
		return string.format("%q", value)
	elseif type(value) == "number" then
		if value == math.floor(value) and math.abs(value) < 1e15 then
			return string.format("%d", value)
		end
		return string.format("%.4g", value)
	end
	return tostring(value)
end

-- Formats an entry as a single line: [ACF][Level][Scope] message key=value ...
function Logger.Format(entry: LogEntry): string
	local parts = { `[ACF][{entry.Level}][{entry.Scope}] {entry.Message}` }
	local fields = entry.Fields
	if fields then
		local keys = {}
		for key in fields do
			table.insert(keys, tostring(key))
		end
		table.sort(keys)
		for _, key in keys do
			table.insert(parts, `{key}={formatValue(fields[key])}`)
		end
	end
	if entry.Suppressed and entry.Suppressed > 0 then
		table.insert(parts, `(+{entry.Suppressed} suppressed)`)
	end
	return table.concat(parts, " ")
end

local function defaultSink(entry: LogEntry)
	local line = Logger.Format(entry)
	if entry.Level == "Warn" or entry.Level == "Error" then
		warn(line)
	else
		print(line)
	end
end

function Logger.new(options: LoggerOptions): Logger
	local level = options.Level or "Info"
	assert(LEVEL_ORDER[level] ~= nil, `Logger: unknown level '{tostring(level)}'`)
	local burst = options.RateLimitBurst or 20
	local window = options.RateLimitWindowSeconds or 10
	local shared: SharedState = {
		level = LEVEL_ORDER[level],
		buffer = {},
		bufferSize = options.BufferSize or 500,
		nextIndex = 1,
		buffered = 0,
		limiter = RateLimiter.new(burst, burst / window, options.Now, 512),
		suppressedByKey = {},
		suppressedKeyCount = 0,
		sink = options.Sink or defaultSink,
		now = options.Now,
		wall = options.Wall or os.time,
		emitted = 0,
		suppressed = 0,
		sinkFailures = 0,
	}
	return setmetatable({ _scope = "Core", _shared = shared }, Logger)
end

-- Returns a logger that shares buffer, level and rate limits but tags entries with a
-- different scope. Scope("X") on the root logger yields scope "X".
function Logger.Scope(self: Logger, name: string): Logger
	return setmetatable({ _scope = name, _shared = self._shared }, Logger)
end

function Logger.GetScope(self: Logger): string
	return self._scope
end

function Logger.SetLevel(self: Logger, level: LogLevel)
	assert(LEVEL_ORDER[level] ~= nil, `Logger: unknown level '{tostring(level)}'`)
	self._shared.level = LEVEL_ORDER[level]
end

function Logger.IsEnabled(self: Logger, level: LogLevel): boolean
	return LEVEL_ORDER[level] >= self._shared.level
end

function Logger.Log(self: Logger, level: LogLevel, message: string, fields: LogFields?)
	local shared = self._shared
	local order = LEVEL_ORDER[level]
	if order == nil or order < shared.level then
		return
	end

	local key = self._scope .. "|" .. message
	if not shared.limiter:Allow(key) then
		shared.suppressed += 1
		local count = shared.suppressedByKey[key]
		if count then
			shared.suppressedByKey[key] = count + 1
		elseif shared.suppressedKeyCount < MAX_SUPPRESSED_KEYS then
			shared.suppressedByKey[key] = 1
			shared.suppressedKeyCount += 1
		end
		return
	end

	local suppressedSince = shared.suppressedByKey[key]
	if suppressedSince then
		shared.suppressedByKey[key] = nil
		shared.suppressedKeyCount -= 1
	end

	local entry: LogEntry = table.freeze({
		Time = shared.now(),
		WallTime = shared.wall(),
		Level = level,
		Scope = self._scope,
		Message = message,
		Fields = if fields then table.freeze(table.clone(fields)) else nil,
		Suppressed = suppressedSince,
	})

	shared.buffer[shared.nextIndex] = entry
	shared.nextIndex = (shared.nextIndex % shared.bufferSize) + 1
	shared.buffered = math.min(shared.buffered + 1, shared.bufferSize)
	shared.emitted += 1

	local ok = pcall(shared.sink, entry)
	if not ok then
		shared.sinkFailures += 1
	end
end

function Logger.Debug(self: Logger, message: string, fields: LogFields?)
	self:Log("Debug", message, fields)
end

function Logger.Info(self: Logger, message: string, fields: LogFields?)
	self:Log("Info", message, fields)
end

function Logger.Warn(self: Logger, message: string, fields: LogFields?)
	self:Log("Warn", message, fields)
end

function Logger.Error(self: Logger, message: string, fields: LogFields?)
	self:Log("Error", message, fields)
end

-- Returns up to `count` most recent entries, oldest first.
function Logger.GetRecent(self: Logger, count: number?): { LogEntry }
	local shared = self._shared
	local n = math.min(count or shared.buffered, shared.buffered)
	local result = table.create(n)
	-- nextIndex points at the slot after the newest entry.
	local start = shared.nextIndex - n
	for i = 0, n - 1 do
		local index = ((start + i - 1) % shared.bufferSize) + 1
		table.insert(result, shared.buffer[index])
	end
	return result
end

function Logger.GetStats(self: Logger): { Emitted: number, Suppressed: number, Buffered: number, SinkFailures: number }
	local shared = self._shared
	return {
		Emitted = shared.emitted,
		Suppressed = shared.suppressed,
		Buffered = shared.buffered,
		SinkFailures = shared.sinkFailures,
	}
end

return Logger

```

`src/Server/Core/ModuleLoader.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Module registration, dependency resolution and lifecycle management.

	Every module goes through an explicit state machine (see TRANSITIONS). Illegal
	transitions raise immediately; they indicate a framework bug, not a module bug.

	Lifecycle:
	  Resolve()       topological sort by Dependencies; disabled, missing and cyclic
	                  dependencies are detected here.
	  InitializeAll() Initialize(context) in dependency order.
	  StartAll()      Start() in dependency order.
	  StopAll()       Stop() in reverse order. Resources added to context.Cleanup or
	                  acquired through context facades during Start are released here.
	  DestroyAll()    Destroy() in reverse order. Resources acquired during Initialize
	                  are released here.

	Error isolation: each lifecycle call runs in pcall. A module that throws becomes
	Failed, its resources are released, and every module that depends on it (directly
	or transitively) becomes Blocked. Other modules keep running.
]]

local Cleanup = require(script.Parent.Cleanup)
local EventBus = require(script.Parent.EventBus)
local Logger = require(script.Parent.Logger)
local Topics = require(script.Parent.Topics)
local Types = require(script.Parent.Parent.Types)

type ModuleState = Types.ModuleState
type ModuleDefinition = Types.ModuleDefinition

local TRANSITIONS: { [string]: { [string]: boolean } } = {
	Registered = { Initializing = true, Disabled = true, Blocked = true, Failed = true },
	Initializing = { Initialized = true, Failed = true },
	Initialized = { Starting = true, Blocked = true, Destroying = true },
	Starting = { Running = true, Failed = true },
	Running = { Stopping = true },
	Stopping = { Stopped = true, Failed = true },
	Stopped = { Starting = true, Blocked = true, Destroying = true },
	Failed = { Destroying = true, Destroyed = true },
	Blocked = { Destroying = true, Destroyed = true, Starting = true },
	Disabled = { Destroyed = true },
	Destroying = { Destroyed = true },
	Destroyed = {},
}

local VALID_CATEGORIES = {
	Movement = true,
	Physics = true,
	Remote = true,
	Combat = true,
	Economy = true,
	Behavioral = true,
	Integrity = true,
	Custom = true,
}

local LIFECYCLE_METHODS = { "Initialize", "Start", "Stop", "Destroy" }

export type ModuleEntry = {
	Name: string,
	Definition: ModuleDefinition,
	Category: Types.DetectionCategory,
	Dependencies: { string },
	Critical: boolean,
	State: ModuleState,
	Error: string?,
	WasInitialized: boolean,
	Order: number,
	Context: any?,
	RunCleanup: Cleanup.Cleanup,
	LifetimeCleanup: Cleanup.Cleanup,
	-- Bag that context facades add to: lifetime during Initialize, run otherwise.
	ActiveCleanup: Cleanup.Cleanup,
}

local ModuleLoader = {}
ModuleLoader.__index = ModuleLoader

export type ModuleLoader = typeof(setmetatable(
	{} :: {
		_logger: Logger.Logger,
		_bus: EventBus.EventBus,
		_makeContext: (entry: ModuleEntry) -> any,
		_entries: { [string]: ModuleEntry },
		_registrationOrder: { ModuleEntry },
		_order: { ModuleEntry }?,
	},
	ModuleLoader
))

export type ModuleLoaderOptions = {
	Logger: Logger.Logger,
	Bus: EventBus.EventBus,
	MakeContext: (entry: ModuleEntry) -> any,
}

function ModuleLoader.new(options: ModuleLoaderOptions): ModuleLoader
	return setmetatable({
		_logger = options.Logger,
		_bus = options.Bus,
		_makeContext = options.MakeContext,
		_entries = {},
		_registrationOrder = {},
		_order = nil,
	}, ModuleLoader)
end

-- Validates a module definition. Returns nil when valid, otherwise an error message.
function ModuleLoader.Validate(definition: any): string?
	if type(definition) ~= "table" then
		return `module definition must be a table, got {typeof(definition)}`
	end
	local name = definition.Name
	if type(name) ~= "string" or not string.match(name, "^[%a][%w_]*$") or #name > 48 then
		return "module Name must be an identifier (letters, digits, underscore; max 48 chars)"
	end
	for _, method in LIFECYCLE_METHODS do
		if type(definition[method]) ~= "function" then
			return `module '{name}' must implement {method}()`
		end
	end
	local deps = definition.Dependencies
	if deps ~= nil then
		if type(deps) ~= "table" then
			return `module '{name}': Dependencies must be an array of module names`
		end
		local seen = {}
		for _, dep in deps do
			if type(dep) ~= "string" then
				return `module '{name}': Dependencies must contain only strings`
			end
			if dep == name then
				return `module '{name}' cannot depend on itself`
			end
			if seen[dep] then
				return `module '{name}' lists dependency '{dep}' twice`
			end
			seen[dep] = true
		end
	end
	if definition.Category ~= nil and not VALID_CATEGORIES[definition.Category] then
		return `module '{name}': unknown Category '{tostring(definition.Category)}'`
	end
	if definition.Version ~= nil and type(definition.Version) ~= "string" then
		return `module '{name}': Version must be a string`
	end
	if definition.Critical ~= nil and type(definition.Critical) ~= "boolean" then
		return `module '{name}': Critical must be a boolean`
	end
	if definition.EnabledByDefault ~= nil and type(definition.EnabledByDefault) ~= "boolean" then
		return `module '{name}': EnabledByDefault must be a boolean`
	end
	local schema = definition.ConfigSchema
	if schema ~= nil and (type(schema) ~= "table" or schema.Kind ~= "table") then
		return `module '{name}': ConfigSchema must be built with Schema.Table`
	end
	return nil
end

local function transition(self: ModuleLoader, entry: ModuleEntry, to: ModuleState, err: string?)
	local from = entry.State
	if not TRANSITIONS[from][to] then
		error(`ModuleLoader: illegal transition {from} -> {to} for module '{entry.Name}'`, 2)
	end
	entry.State = to
	if err ~= nil then
		entry.Error = err
	elseif to ~= "Failed" and to ~= "Blocked" then
		entry.Error = nil
	end
	self._bus:Publish(Topics.ModuleStateChanged, { Name = entry.Name, From = from, To = to, Error = err })
end

function ModuleLoader.Register(self: ModuleLoader, definition: ModuleDefinition): ModuleEntry
	local problem = ModuleLoader.Validate(definition)
	if problem then
		error("ACF: " .. problem, 3)
	end
	if self._order ~= nil then
		error(`ACF: cannot register module '{definition.Name}' after modules have been resolved`, 3)
	end
	if self._entries[definition.Name] then
		error(`ACF: a module named '{definition.Name}' is already registered`, 3)
	end

	local logger = self._logger
	local name = definition.Name
	local function onCleanupError(message: string)
		logger:Error("Module resource cleanup failed", { Module = name, Error = message })
	end
	local lifetime = Cleanup.new(onCleanupError)
	local entry: ModuleEntry = {
		Name = name,
		Definition = definition,
		Category = definition.Category or "Custom",
		Dependencies = table.freeze(table.clone(definition.Dependencies or {})),
		Critical = definition.Critical == true,
		State = "Registered",
		Error = nil,
		WasInitialized = false,
		Order = #self._registrationOrder + 1,
		Context = nil,
		RunCleanup = Cleanup.new(onCleanupError),
		LifetimeCleanup = lifetime,
		ActiveCleanup = lifetime,
	}
	self._entries[name] = entry
	table.insert(self._registrationOrder, entry)
	return entry
end

function ModuleLoader.Has(self: ModuleLoader, name: string): boolean
	return self._entries[name] ~= nil
end

function ModuleLoader.Get(self: ModuleLoader, name: string): ModuleEntry?
	return self._entries[name]
end

-- Decides which modules run and in what order. `isEnabled` maps an entry to whether
-- configuration enables it. Must be called once, before InitializeAll().
function ModuleLoader.Resolve(self: ModuleLoader, isEnabled: (entry: ModuleEntry) -> boolean)
	if self._order ~= nil then
		error("ModuleLoader:Resolve called twice", 2)
	end
	local entries = self._registrationOrder

	for _, entry in entries do
		if not isEnabled(entry) then
			transition(self, entry, "Disabled")
		end
	end

	-- Propagate missing/disabled dependencies until nothing changes.
	local changed = true
	while changed do
		changed = false
		for _, entry in entries do
			if entry.State == "Registered" then
				for _, depName in entry.Dependencies do
					local dep = self._entries[depName]
					local reason: string? = nil
					if dep == nil then
						reason = `missing dependency '{depName}'`
					elseif dep.State == "Disabled" then
						reason = `dependency '{depName}' is disabled`
					elseif dep.State == "Blocked" or dep.State == "Failed" then
						reason = `dependency '{depName}' is unavailable`
					end
					if reason then
						transition(self, entry, "Blocked", reason)
						self._logger:Warn("Module blocked", { Module = entry.Name, Reason = reason })
						changed = true
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	-- Kahn's algorithm over the remaining modules; ties broken by registration order.
	local inDegree: { [string]: number } = {}
	local dependents: { [string]: { ModuleEntry } } = {}
	local pending = {}
	for _, entry in entries do
		if entry.State == "Registered" then
			inDegree[entry.Name] = #entry.Dependencies
			table.insert(pending, entry)
			for _, depName in entry.Dependencies do
				dependents[depName] = dependents[depName] or {}
				table.insert(dependents[depName], entry)
			end
		end
	end

	local order: { ModuleEntry } = {}
	local ready: { ModuleEntry } = {}
	for _, entry in pending do
		if inDegree[entry.Name] == 0 then
			table.insert(ready, entry)
		end
	end
	while #ready > 0 do
		table.sort(ready, function(a, b)
			return a.Order < b.Order
		end)
		local entry = table.remove(ready, 1) :: ModuleEntry
		table.insert(order, entry)
		local waiting = dependents[entry.Name]
		if waiting then
			for _, dependent in waiting do
				inDegree[dependent.Name] -= 1
				if inDegree[dependent.Name] == 0 then
					table.insert(ready, dependent)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if #order < #pending then
		local stuck = {}
		for _, entry in pending do
			if inDegree[entry.Name] > 0 then
				table.insert(stuck, entry.Name)
			end
		end
		table.sort(stuck)
		local reason = "dependency cycle among: " .. table.concat(stuck, ", ")
		for _, entry in pending do
			if inDegree[entry.Name] > 0 then
				transition(self, entry, "Failed", reason)
			end
		end
		self._logger:Error("Module dependency cycle", { Modules = table.concat(stuck, ", ") })
	end

	self._order = order
end

local function dependenciesIn(self: ModuleLoader, entry: ModuleEntry, states: { [string]: boolean }): string?
	for _, depName in entry.Dependencies do
		local dep = self._entries[depName]
		if dep == nil or not states[dep.State] then
			return depName
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function runLifecycle(entry: ModuleEntry, method: string, ...: any): (boolean, string?)
	local definition = entry.Definition
	local ok, err = pcall(definition[method], definition, ...)
	if ok then
		return true, nil
	end
	return false, tostring(err)
end

local INITIALIZED_OK = { Initialized = true }
local RUNNING_OK = { Running = true }

function ModuleLoader.InitializeAll(self: ModuleLoader)
	local order = self._order
	if order == nil then
		error("ModuleLoader:InitializeAll called before Resolve", 2)
	end
	for _, entry in order do
		if entry.State == "Registered" then
			local badDep = dependenciesIn(self, entry, INITIALIZED_OK)
			if badDep then
				transition(self, entry, "Blocked", `dependency '{badDep}' failed to initialize`)
			else
				transition(self, entry, "Initializing")
				entry.Context = self._makeContext(entry)
				entry.ActiveCleanup = entry.LifetimeCleanup
				entry.WasInitialized = true
				local ok, err = runLifecycle(entry, "Initialize", entry.Context)
				entry.ActiveCleanup = entry.RunCleanup
				if ok then
					transition(self, entry, "Initialized")
				else
					entry.LifetimeCleanup:Clean()
					transition(self, entry, "Failed", `Initialize: {err}`)
					self._logger:Error("Module failed to initialize", { Module = entry.Name, Error = err })
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

function ModuleLoader.StartAll(self: ModuleLoader)
	local order = self._order
	if order == nil then
		error("ModuleLoader:StartAll called before Resolve", 2)
	end
	for _, entry in order do
		local state = entry.State
		local startable = state == "Initialized"
			or state == "Stopped"
			or (state == "Blocked" and entry.WasInitialized)
		if startable then
			local badDep = dependenciesIn(self, entry, RUNNING_OK)
			if badDep then
				if state ~= "Blocked" then
					transition(self, entry, "Blocked", `dependency '{badDep}' is not running`)
				end
			else
				transition(self, entry, "Starting")
				entry.ActiveCleanup = entry.RunCleanup
				local ok, err = runLifecycle(entry, "Start")
				if ok then
					transition(self, entry, "Running")
				else
					entry.RunCleanup:Clean()
					transition(self, entry, "Failed", `Start: {err}`)
					self._logger:Error("Module failed to start", { Module = entry.Name, Error = err })
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

function ModuleLoader.StopAll(self: ModuleLoader)
	local order = self._order
	if order == nil then
		return
	end
	for i = #order, 1, -1 do
		local entry = order[i]
		if entry.State == "Running" then
			transition(self, entry, "Stopping")
			local ok, err = runLifecycle(entry, "Stop")
			entry.RunCleanup:Clean()
			if ok then
				transition(self, entry, "Stopped")
			else
				transition(self, entry, "Failed", `Stop: {err}`)
				self._logger:Error("Module failed to stop cleanly", { Module = entry.Name, Error = err })
			end
		end
	end
end

function ModuleLoader.DestroyAll(self: ModuleLoader)
	self:StopAll()
	local entries = self._registrationOrder
	local order: { ModuleEntry } = self._order or {}
	-- Destroy resolved modules in reverse dependency order, then everything else.
	local sequence: { ModuleEntry } = {}
	for i = #order, 1, -1 do
		table.insert(sequence, order[i])
	end
	for i = #entries, 1, -1 do
		if not table.find(sequence, entries[i]) then
			table.insert(sequence, entries[i])
		end
	end

	for _, entry in sequence do
		local state = entry.State
		if state == "Destroyed" then
			continue
		end
		if entry.WasInitialized then
			transition(self, entry, "Destroying")
			local ok, err = runLifecycle(entry, "Destroy")
			entry.RunCleanup:Clean()
			entry.LifetimeCleanup:Clean()
			if not ok then
				self._logger:Error("Module failed to destroy cleanly", { Module = entry.Name, Error = err })
			end
			transition(self, entry, "Destroyed", if ok then nil else `Destroy: {err}`)
		elseif state == "Registered" then
			-- Never resolved (framework destroyed before Start). Nothing to release.
			entry.State = "Destroyed"
		else
			transition(self, entry, "Destroyed")
		end
	end
end

-- Modules in resolved order that are currently in `state`.
function ModuleLoader.InState(self: ModuleLoader, state: ModuleState): { ModuleEntry }
	local result = {}
	for _, entry in self._registrationOrder do
		if entry.State == state then
			table.insert(result, entry)
		end
	end
	return result
end

function ModuleLoader.GetStates(self: ModuleLoader): { [string]: { State: ModuleState, Error: string?, Critical: boolean } }
	local result = {}
	for name, entry in self._entries do
		result[name] = table.freeze({ State = entry.State, Error = entry.Error, Critical = entry.Critical })
	end
	return result
end

function ModuleLoader.GetOrder(self: ModuleLoader): { string }
	local names = {}
	local order = self._order
	if order then
		for _, entry in order do
			table.insert(names, entry.Name)
		end
	end
	return names
end

return ModuleLoader

```

`src/Server/Core/PlayerRegistry.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Player session registry.

	Creates one Session per player per join and ends it when the player leaves. A rejoin
	creates a new session with a new SessionId. Sessions track the current character and
	a CharacterGeneration counter that increments on every spawn. Later phases use that
	counter to discard movement samples that straddle a respawn.

	Each session owns a Cleanup bag that is cleaned when the session ends, so modules can
	attach per-player connections without tracking them themselves.

	Published topics: PlayerSessionStarted, PlayerSessionEnded, CharacterSpawned,
	CharacterRemoving. PlayerSessionEnded is published before the session's Cleanup runs,
	so subscribers can still read session state.
]]

local Cleanup = require(script.Parent.Cleanup)
local EventBus = require(script.Parent.EventBus)
local Logger = require(script.Parent.Logger)
local Topics = require(script.Parent.Topics)
local Types = require(script.Parent.Parent.Types)

-- Session ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

local Session = {}
Session.__index = Session

export type Session = typeof(setmetatable(
	{} :: {
		SessionId: string,
		UserId: number,
		Name: string,
		Player: Types.PlayerLike,
		JoinedAt: number,
		JoinedWallTime: number,
		Character: any?,
		CharacterGeneration: number,
		Active: boolean,
		Cleanup: Cleanup.Cleanup,
		_state: { [string]: any },
	},
	Session
))

-- Per-session scratch state keyed by owner (normally a module name). Created on first
-- access by `factory`. Freed automatically when the session ends.
function Session.GetState(self: Session, key: string, factory: (() -> any)?): any
	local value = self._state[key]
	if value == nil and factory then
		value = factory()
		self._state[key] = value
	end
	return value
end

function Session.ClearState(self: Session, key: string)
	self._state[key] = nil
end

-- Registry ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local PlayerRegistry = {}
PlayerRegistry.__index = PlayerRegistry

export type PlayerRegistry = typeof(setmetatable(
	{} :: {
		_players: Types.PlayersLike,
		_bus: EventBus.EventBus,
		_logger: Logger.Logger,
		_clock: Types.Clock,
		_generateId: () -> string,
		_byUserId: { [number]: Session },
		_connections: Cleanup.Cleanup,
		_running: boolean,
		_started: number,
		_ended: number,
	},
	PlayerRegistry
))

export type PlayerRegistryOptions = {
	Players: Types.PlayersLike,
	Bus: EventBus.EventBus,
	Logger: Logger.Logger,
	Clock: Types.Clock,
	GenerateId: () -> string,
}

function PlayerRegistry.new(options: PlayerRegistryOptions): PlayerRegistry
	local logger = options.Logger
	return setmetatable({
		_players = options.Players,
		_bus = options.Bus,
		_logger = logger,
		_clock = options.Clock,
		_generateId = options.GenerateId,
		_byUserId = {},
		_connections = Cleanup.new(function(message)
			logger:Error("Registry connection cleanup failed", { Error = message })
		end),
		_running = false,
		_started = 0,
		_ended = 0,
	}, PlayerRegistry)
end

local function onCharacterAdded(self: PlayerRegistry, session: Session, character: any)
	if not session.Active then
		return
	end
	session.Character = character
	session.CharacterGeneration += 1
	self._bus:Publish(Topics.CharacterSpawned, {
		Session = session,
		Character = character,
		Generation = session.CharacterGeneration,
	})
end

local function onCharacterRemoving(self: PlayerRegistry, session: Session, character: any)
	if not session.Active then
		return
	end
	self._bus:Publish(Topics.CharacterRemoving, {
		Session = session,
		Character = character,
		Generation = session.CharacterGeneration,
	})
	if session.Character == character then
		session.Character = nil
	end
end

local function beginSession(self: PlayerRegistry, player: Types.PlayerLike)
	local userId = player.UserId
	if type(userId) ~= "number" then
		self._logger:Error("Ignoring player without numeric UserId")
		return
	end
	local existing = self._byUserId[userId]
	if existing then
		if existing.Player == player then
			return -- duplicate PlayerAdded (e.g. raced with the initial GetPlayers pass)
		end
		-- Same UserId on a different Player object: the old one never got
		-- PlayerRemoving. End it so state cannot leak across sessions.
		self._logger:Warn("Replacing stale session for rejoining user", { UserId = userId })
		self:EndSession(userId)
	end

	local logger = self._logger
	local session: Session = setmetatable({
		SessionId = self._generateId(),
		UserId = userId,
		Name = tostring(player.Name),
		Player = player,
		JoinedAt = self._clock.Now(),
		JoinedWallTime = self._clock.Wall(),
		Character = nil,
		CharacterGeneration = 0,
		Active = true,
		Cleanup = Cleanup.new(function(message)
			logger:Error("Session cleanup task failed", { UserId = userId, Error = message })
		end),
		_state = {},
	}, Session)
	self._byUserId[userId] = session
	self._started += 1

	session.Cleanup:Add(player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
		onCharacterAdded(self, session, character)
	end))
	session.Cleanup:Add(player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function(character)
		onCharacterRemoving(self, session, character)
	end))

	self._logger:Debug("Session started", { UserId = userId, SessionId = session.SessionId })
	self._bus:Publish(Topics.PlayerSessionStarted, { Session = session })

	local character = player.Character
	if character ~= nil and session.Active and session.Character == nil then
		onCharacterAdded(self, session, character)
	end
end

function PlayerRegistry.Start(self: PlayerRegistry)
	if self._running then
		return
	end
	self._running = true
	local players = self._players
	self._connections:Add(players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
		beginSession(self, player)
	end))
	self._connections:Add(players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
		local session = self._byUserId[player.UserId]
		if session and session.Player == player then
			self:EndSession(player.UserId)
		end
	end))
	for _, player in players:GetPlayers() do
		beginSession(self, player)
	end
end

-- Stops tracking and ends every active session.
function PlayerRegistry.Stop(self: PlayerRegistry)
	if not self._running then
		return
	end
	self._running = false
	self._connections:Clean()
	local userIds = {}
	for userId in self._byUserId do
		table.insert(userIds, userId)
	end
	for _, userId in userIds do
		self:EndSession(userId)
	end
end

function PlayerRegistry.EndSession(self: PlayerRegistry, userId: number)
	local session: Session? = self._byUserId[userId]
	if session == nil then
		return
	end
	self._byUserId[userId] = nil
	self._bus:Publish(Topics.PlayerSessionEnded, { Session = session })
	session.Active = false
	session.Cleanup:Clean()
	table.clear(session._state)
	session.Character = nil
	self._ended += 1
	self._logger:Debug("Session ended", { UserId = userId, SessionId = session.SessionId })
end

-- Accepts a Player, a UserId or a Session. Returns the active session or nil.
function PlayerRegistry.GetSession(self: PlayerRegistry, target: any): Session?
	local kind = type(target)
	if kind == "number" then
		return self._byUserId[target]
	end
	if target == nil then
		return nil
	end
	if getmetatable(target) == Session then
		local session = target :: Session
		if session.Active and self._byUserId[session.UserId] == session then
			return session
		end
		return nil
	end
	local ok, userId = pcall(function()
		return target.UserId
	end)
	if ok and type(userId) == "number" then
		local session = self._byUserId[userId]
		if session and session.Player == target then
			return session
		end
	end
	return nil
end

function PlayerRegistry.GetSessions(self: PlayerRegistry): { Session }
	local list: { Session } = {}
	for _, session in self._byUserId do
		table.insert(list, session)
	end
	table.sort(list, function(a, b)
		return a.JoinedAt < b.JoinedAt
	end)
	return list
end

function PlayerRegistry.Count(self: PlayerRegistry): number
	local n = 0
	for _ in self._byUserId do
		n += 1
	end
	return n
end

function PlayerRegistry.IsRunning(self: PlayerRegistry): boolean
	return self._running
end

function PlayerRegistry.GetStats(self: PlayerRegistry): { Active: number, Started: number, Ended: number }
	return { Active = self:Count(), Started = self._started, Ended = self._ended }
end

PlayerRegistry.Session = Session

return PlayerRegistry

```

`src/Server/Core/RateLimiter.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Keyed token-bucket rate limiter with bounded key storage.

	Each key gets a bucket of `capacity` tokens that refills continuously at
	`refillPerSecond`. Allow() spends tokens. Buckets that have refilled completely carry
	no information and are pruned when the key table reaches `maxKeys`. If pruning cannot
	free space, all buckets are reset (bounded memory beats perfect accounting); this is
	counted in GetStats().Overflows.
]]

type Bucket = { tokens: number, updated: number }

local RateLimiter = {}
RateLimiter.__index = RateLimiter

export type RateLimiter = typeof(setmetatable(
	{} :: {
		_capacity: number,
		_refill: number,
		_now: () -> number,
		_maxKeys: number,
		_buckets: { [string]: Bucket },
		_keyCount: number,
		_overflows: number,
	},
	RateLimiter
))

function RateLimiter.new(capacity: number, refillPerSecond: number, now: () -> number, maxKeys: number?): RateLimiter
	assert(type(capacity) == "number" and capacity >= 1, "RateLimiter: capacity must be >= 1")
	assert(type(refillPerSecond) == "number" and refillPerSecond > 0, "RateLimiter: refill rate must be > 0")
	assert(type(now) == "function", "RateLimiter: now must be a function")
	return setmetatable({
		_capacity = capacity,
		_refill = refillPerSecond,
		_now = now,
		_maxKeys = maxKeys or 1024,
		_buckets = {},
		_keyCount = 0,
		_overflows = 0,
	}, RateLimiter)
end

local function refilled(self: RateLimiter, bucket: Bucket, now: number): number
	local elapsed = now - bucket.updated
	if elapsed <= 0 then
		return bucket.tokens
	end
	return math.min(self._capacity, bucket.tokens + elapsed * self._refill)
end

function RateLimiter.Allow(self: RateLimiter, key: string, cost: number?): boolean
	local price = cost or 1
	local now = self._now()
	local bucket = self._buckets[key]
	if bucket == nil then
		if self._keyCount >= self._maxKeys then
			self:Prune()
		end
		bucket = { tokens = self._capacity, updated = now }
		self._buckets[key] = bucket
		self._keyCount += 1
	else
		bucket.tokens = refilled(self, bucket, now)
		bucket.updated = now
	end
	if bucket.tokens >= price then
		bucket.tokens -= price
		return true
	end
	return false
end

-- Removes buckets that have fully refilled. Resets everything if still at capacity.
function RateLimiter.Prune(self: RateLimiter)
	local now = self._now()
	for key, bucket in self._buckets do
		if refilled(self, bucket, now) >= self._capacity then
			self._buckets[key] = nil
			self._keyCount -= 1
		end
	end
	if self._keyCount >= self._maxKeys then
		table.clear(self._buckets)
		self._keyCount = 0
		self._overflows += 1
	end
end

function RateLimiter.Reset(self: RateLimiter, key: string?)
	if key == nil then
		table.clear(self._buckets)
		self._keyCount = 0
	elseif self._buckets[key] ~= nil then
		self._buckets[key] = nil
		self._keyCount -= 1
	end
end

function RateLimiter.GetStats(self: RateLimiter): { Keys: number, Overflows: number }
	return { Keys = self._keyCount, Overflows = self._overflows }
end

return RateLimiter

```

`src/Server/Core/Scheduler.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Central interval scheduler.

	Instead of every detector connecting its own Heartbeat handler, modules register
	interval tasks here. One Heartbeat connection drives Step(), which runs whichever
	tasks are due. This gives one place to measure per-task CPU cost, isolate errors and
	disable tasks that keep failing.

	Semantics:
	  - Every(name, interval, fn): fn(now, dt) runs roughly every `interval` seconds. If the
	    server stalls, missed runs are skipped rather than replayed in a burst.
	  - After(name, delay, fn): one-shot.
	  - A repeating task that fails MaxConsecutiveFailures times in a row is disabled and
	    logged at Error level.
	  - Statistics are aggregated by task name, so memory stays bounded by the number of
	    distinct names (which are defined in code).
]]

local Logger = require(script.Parent.Logger)

type Logger = Logger.Logger

type Task = {
	Name: string,
	Interval: number?,
	NextRun: number,
	LastRun: number,
	Callback: (now: number, dt: number) -> (),
	Active: boolean,
	ConsecutiveFailures: number,
}

export type TaskStats = {
	Runs: number,
	Failures: number,
	Disabled: number,
	TotalCpuSeconds: number,
	MaxCpuSeconds: number,
}

export type TaskHandle = {
	Name: string,
	Cancel: (self: TaskHandle) -> (),
	IsActive: (self: TaskHandle) -> boolean,
	-- Alias of Cancel so handles can be added to a Cleanup bag.
	Disconnect: (self: TaskHandle) -> (),
}

local Scheduler = {}
Scheduler.__index = Scheduler

export type Scheduler = typeof(setmetatable(
	{} :: {
		_now: () -> number,
		_cpu: () -> number,
		_logger: Logger,
		_maxFailures: number,
		_tasks: { Task },
		_stats: { [string]: TaskStats },
		_stepping: boolean,
		_binding: any?,
		_steps: number,
	},
	Scheduler
))

export type SchedulerOptions = {
	Now: () -> number,
	CpuClock: (() -> number)?,
	Logger: Logger,
	MaxConsecutiveFailures: number?,
}

function Scheduler.new(options: SchedulerOptions): Scheduler
	return setmetatable({
		_now = options.Now,
		_cpu = options.CpuClock or os.clock,
		_logger = options.Logger,
		_maxFailures = options.MaxConsecutiveFailures or 5,
		_tasks = {},
		_stats = {},
		_stepping = false,
		_binding = nil,
		_steps = 0,
	}, Scheduler)
end

local function isPositiveFinite(value: any): boolean
	return type(value) == "number" and value == value and value > 0 and value < math.huge
end

local function makeHandle(entry: Task): TaskHandle
	local function cancel()
		entry.Active = false
	end
	return {
		Name = entry.Name,
		Cancel = cancel,
		Disconnect = cancel,
		IsActive = function()
			return entry.Active
		end,
	}
end

local function addTask(self: Scheduler, name: string, interval: number?, firstDelay: number, callback): TaskHandle
	if type(name) ~= "string" or #name == 0 then
		error("Scheduler: task name must be a non-empty string", 3)
	end
	if type(callback) ~= "function" then
		error("Scheduler: callback must be a function", 3)
	end
	local now = self._now()
	local entry: Task = {
		Name = name,
		Interval = interval,
		NextRun = now + firstDelay,
		LastRun = now,
		Callback = callback,
		Active = true,
		ConsecutiveFailures = 0,
	}
	table.insert(self._tasks, entry)
	if self._stats[name] == nil then
		self._stats[name] = { Runs = 0, Failures = 0, Disabled = 0, TotalCpuSeconds = 0, MaxCpuSeconds = 0 }
	end
	return makeHandle(entry)
end

-- Runs callback(now, dt) every `interval` seconds. `phase` delays the first run
-- (defaults to one interval) and can be used to spread tasks with equal intervals.
function Scheduler.Every(
	self: Scheduler,
	name: string,
	interval: number,
	callback: (now: number, dt: number) -> (),
	phase: number?
): TaskHandle
	if not isPositiveFinite(interval) then
		error("Scheduler:Every interval must be a positive finite number", 2)
	end
	local first = phase or interval
	if type(first) ~= "number" or first < 0 or first ~= first then
		error("Scheduler:Every phase must be a non-negative number", 2)
	end
	return addTask(self, name, interval, first, callback)
end

function Scheduler.After(self: Scheduler, name: string, delay: number, callback: (now: number, dt: number) -> ()): TaskHandle
	if type(delay) ~= "number" or delay < 0 or delay ~= delay or delay == math.huge then
		error("Scheduler:After delay must be a non-negative finite number", 2)
	end
	return addTask(self, name, nil, delay, callback)
end

function Scheduler.Step(self: Scheduler)
	if self._stepping then
		return
	end
	self._stepping = true
	self._steps += 1
	local now = self._now()
	local tasks = self._tasks
	local count = #tasks
	local anyInactive = false

	for i = 1, count do
		local entry = tasks[i]
		if entry.Active and now >= entry.NextRun then
			local dt = now - entry.LastRun
			entry.LastRun = now
			local interval = entry.Interval
			if interval then
				entry.NextRun += interval
				if entry.NextRun <= now then
					-- Fell behind (server stall): skip missed runs.
					entry.NextRun = now + interval
				end
			else
				entry.Active = false
			end

			local stats = self._stats[entry.Name]
			local started = self._cpu()
			local ok: boolean, err: any = pcall(entry.Callback, now, dt)
			local spent = self._cpu() - started
			stats.Runs += 1
			stats.TotalCpuSeconds += spent
			if spent > stats.MaxCpuSeconds then
				stats.MaxCpuSeconds = spent
			end

			if ok then
				entry.ConsecutiveFailures = 0
			else
				stats.Failures += 1
				entry.ConsecutiveFailures += 1
				self._logger:Error("Scheduled task failed", {
					Task = entry.Name,
					Error = tostring(err),
					ConsecutiveFailures = entry.ConsecutiveFailures,
				})
				if interval and entry.ConsecutiveFailures >= self._maxFailures then
					entry.Active = false
					stats.Disabled += 1
					self._logger:Error("Scheduled task disabled after repeated failures", { Task = entry.Name })
				end
			end
		end
		if not entry.Active then
			anyInactive = true
		end
	end

	if anyInactive then
		local kept = {}
		for _, entry in tasks do
			if entry.Active then
				table.insert(kept, entry)
			end
		end
		self._tasks = kept
	end
	self._stepping = false
end

-- Connects Step() to a per-frame signal (RunService.Heartbeat in production).
function Scheduler.Bind(self: Scheduler, signal: any)
	if self._binding then
		error("Scheduler is already bound to a signal", 2)
	end
	self._binding = signal:Connect(function()
		self:Step()
	end)
end

function Scheduler.Unbind(self: Scheduler)
	local binding = self._binding
	if binding then
		binding:Disconnect()
		self._binding = nil
	end
end

function Scheduler.IsBound(self: Scheduler): boolean
	return self._binding ~= nil
end

function Scheduler.ActiveCount(self: Scheduler): number
	local n = 0
	for _, entry in self._tasks do
		if entry.Active then
			n += 1
		end
	end
	return n
end

function Scheduler.GetStats(self: Scheduler): { [string]: TaskStats }
	local copy = {}
	for name, stats in self._stats do
		copy[name] = table.freeze(table.clone(stats))
	end
	return copy
end

function Scheduler.CancelAll(self: Scheduler)
	for _, entry in self._tasks do
		entry.Active = false
	end
	table.clear(self._tasks)
end

function Scheduler.Destroy(self: Scheduler)
	self:Unbind()
	self:CancelAll()
end

return Scheduler

```

`src/Server/Core/Topics.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Core event-bus topics. Topics in the "ACF." namespace are published only by the
	framework itself; modules may subscribe to them but cannot publish them.

	Payloads:
	  FrameworkStateChanged  { From: FrameworkState, To: FrameworkState }
	  ModuleStateChanged     { Name: string, From: ModuleState, To: ModuleState, Error: string? }
	  PlayerSessionStarted   { Session: Session }
	  PlayerSessionEnded     { Session: Session }
	  CharacterSpawned       { Session: Session, Character: Model, Generation: number }
	  CharacterRemoving      { Session: Session, Character: Model, Generation: number }
	  DetectionReported      { Event: DetectionEvent, Record: EvidenceRecord, IsNewRecord: boolean }
	  RiskChanged            { UserId: number, Session: Session?, Risk: number, Previous: number,
	                           Band: string, PreviousBand: string, Confidence: number,
	                           Explanation: string } (published when the band changes)
	  EnforcementAudit       AuditLog entry (every enforcement decision and staff action)
	  EnforcementCase        Case snapshot when a case is opened, updated or decided
]]

local Topics = table.freeze({
	FrameworkStateChanged = "ACF.FrameworkStateChanged",
	ModuleStateChanged = "ACF.ModuleStateChanged",
	PlayerSessionStarted = "ACF.PlayerSessionStarted",
	PlayerSessionEnded = "ACF.PlayerSessionEnded",
	CharacterSpawned = "ACF.CharacterSpawned",
	CharacterRemoving = "ACF.CharacterRemoving",
	DetectionReported = "ACF.DetectionReported",
	RiskChanged = "ACF.RiskChanged",
	EnforcementAudit = "ACF.EnforcementAudit",
	EnforcementCase = "ACF.EnforcementCase",
})

return Topics
```

`src/Server/Detection/Behavioral/Behavioral.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Behavioral: statistical detection over time (built-in, Category "Behavioral").
	The models are documented in BehavioralDetectors and docs/BEHAVIORAL.md.

	Data sources:
	  - Signals:  ACF:DefineSignal("Coins", options) and ACF:RecordSignal(player, "Coins", n)
	              from the game's own server code (resource gain, XP, progression).
	  - Actions:  ACF:RecordAction(player, "Click", valid?) for timing regularity and
	              repeated invalid actions.
	  - Combat:   the Combat module's "Combat.Shot" topic (server-validated shots), when
	              Combat is running. Nothing comes from the client.

	Nothing here acts on one observation: signals need a CUSUM alarm over several buckets,
	ratios need a Wilson lower bound over a minimum sample, timing needs full windows, and
	every violation still goes through a decaying ViolationTracker before it is reported.
	Statistical evidence is capped at MaxConfidence 0.8 by default.
]]

local Configuration = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Core.Configuration)
local Statistics = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Intelligence.Statistics)
local ViolationTracker = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Intelligence.ViolationTracker)
local BehavioralDetectors = require(script.Parent.BehavioralDetectors)

local S = Configuration.Schema

local MODULE_NAME = "Behavioral"
local STATE_KEY = "Behavioral.State"
local SHOT_TOPIC = "Combat.Shot"
local MAX_NAME_LENGTH = 64
local MAX_AMOUNT = 1e12

local CONFIG_SCHEMA = S.Table({
	Signals = S.Table({
		BucketSeconds = S.Number(10, { Min = 1, Max = 3600 }),
		SelfAlpha = S.Number(0.2, { Min = 0.01, Max = 1 }),
		MinPlayerBuckets = S.Number(3, { Min = 1, Max = 1000, Integer = true }),
		ActiveWithinBuckets = S.Number(6, { Min = 1, Max = 1000, Integer = true }),
		MinContributors = S.Number(4, { Min = 2, Max = 1000, Integer = true }),
		MinRelativeStdDev = S.Number(0.1, { Min = 0, Max = 10 }),
		ZClamp = S.Number(3, { Min = 1, Max = 20 }),
		CusumK = S.Number(0.5, { Min = 0, Max = 10 }),
		CusumH = S.Number(6, { Min = 0.5, Max = 1000 }),
		MinRatio = S.Number(3, { Min = 1, Max = 1000 }),
		HardLimitTolerance = S.Number(0.1, { Min = 0, Max = 10 }),
		MaxSignals = S.Number(64, { Min = 1, Max = 1024, Integer = true }),
	}),
	Accuracy = S.Table({
		HalfLifeSeconds = S.Number(600, { Min = 10, Max = 86400 }),
		EvaluateIntervalSeconds = S.Number(10, { Min = 1, Max = 3600 }),
		ConfidenceZ = S.Number(3.09, { Min = 1, Max = 6 }),
		MinShots = S.Number(40, { Min = 5, Max = 100000 }),
		AccuracyMargin = S.Number(0.35, { Min = 0, Max = 1 }),
		AccuracyFloor = S.Number(0.9, { Min = 0, Max = 1 }),
		AccuracyFloorWithoutBaseline = S.Number(0.95, { Min = 0, Max = 1 }),
		MinPopulationShots = S.Number(200, { Min = 1, Max = 1e7 }),
		MinHits = S.Number(20, { Min = 5, Max = 100000 }),
		HeadshotMargin = S.Number(0.4, { Min = 0, Max = 1 }),
		HeadshotFloor = S.Number(0.75, { Min = 0, Max = 1 }),
		HeadshotFloorWithoutBaseline = S.Number(0.9, { Min = 0, Max = 1 }),
		MinPopulationHits = S.Number(100, { Min = 1, Max = 1e7 }),
	}),
	Actions = S.Table({
		WindowSize = S.Number(30, { Min = 5, Max = 1000, Integer = true }),
		MaxCV = S.Number(0.02, { Min = 0, Max = 1 }),
		MinMeanIntervalSeconds = S.Number(0.1, { Min = 0, Max = 3600 }),
		MaxMeanIntervalSeconds = S.Number(10, { Min = 0.01, Max = 86400 }),
		MaxGapSeconds = S.Number(30, { Min = 0.1, Max = 86400 }),
		InvalidHalfLifeSeconds = S.Number(60, { Min = 1, Max = 86400 }),
		MinInvalid = S.Number(20, { Min = 1, Max = 100000 }),
		MaxInvalidRatio = S.Number(0.8, { Min = 0, Max = 1 }),
		InvalidConfidenceZ = S.Number(2.33, { Min = 1, Max = 6 }),
		MaxActionsPerPlayer = S.Number(32, { Min = 1, Max = 1024, Integer = true }),
	}),
	Detectors = S.Table({
		SignalRateLimit = S.Boolean(true),
		SignalAnomaly = S.Boolean(true),
		Accuracy = S.Boolean(true),
		Headshots = S.Boolean(true),
		Timing = S.Boolean(true),
		InvalidActions = S.Boolean(true),
	}),
	Severity = S.Table({
		SignalRateLimit = S.Number(30, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
		SignalAnomaly = S.Number(30, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
		Accuracy = S.Number(30, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
		Headshots = S.Number(30, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
		Timing = S.Number(15, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
		InvalidActions = S.Number(10, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
	}),
	Violation = S.Table({
		HalfLifeSeconds = S.Number(120, { Min = 1, Max = 86400 }),
		ReportThreshold = S.Number(3, { Min = 0.1, Max = 10000 }),
		ReportIntervalSeconds = S.Number(60, { Min = 0, Max = 86400 }),
		ConfidenceScale = S.Number(3, { Min = 0.1, Max = 10000 }),
		MaxConfidence = S.Number(0.8, { Min = 0, Max = 1 }),
	}),
})

type Definition = BehavioralDetectors.SignalDefinition

type SessionState = {
	tracker: ViolationTracker.ViolationTracker,
	signals: { [string]: BehavioralDetectors.SignalPlayer },
	weapons: { [string]: BehavioralDetectors.HitCounts },
	actions: { [string]: BehavioralDetectors.ActionState },
	actionCount: number,
}

local Behavioral = {}
Behavioral.Name = MODULE_NAME
Behavioral.ConfigSchema = CONFIG_SCHEMA

local function validName(name: any): boolean
	return type(name) == "string" and #name > 0 and #name <= MAX_NAME_LENGTH
end

local function finite(value: any): boolean
	return type(value) == "number" and value == value and value > -math.huge and value < math.huge
end

local function normalizeSignal(name: string, options: any): Definition
	if not validName(name) then
		error("ACF Behavioral: signal name must be a string of 1-64 characters", 3)
	end
	options = options or {}
	if type(options) ~= "table" then
		error(`ACF Behavioral: options for signal '{name}' must be a table`, 3)
	end
	for key in options do
		if key ~= "MaxPerSecond" and key ~= "Baseline" and key ~= "MinStdDev" and key ~= "Statistical" then
			error(`ACF Behavioral: unknown option '{tostring(key)}' for signal '{name}'`, 3)
		end
	end
	local maxPerSecond = options.MaxPerSecond
	if maxPerSecond ~= nil and not (finite(maxPerSecond) and maxPerSecond > 0) then
		error(`ACF Behavioral: signal '{name}' MaxPerSecond must be a positive number`, 3)
	end
	local baseline = options.Baseline
	if baseline ~= nil then
		if type(baseline) ~= "table" or not finite(baseline.Mean) or baseline.Mean < 0 or not finite(baseline.StdDev) or baseline.StdDev <= 0 then
			error(`ACF Behavioral: signal '{name}' Baseline must be {"{"} Mean >= 0, StdDev > 0 {"}"}`, 3)
		end
		baseline = { Mean = baseline.Mean, StdDev = baseline.StdDev }
	end
	local minStdDev = options.MinStdDev or 0
	if not finite(minStdDev) or minStdDev < 0 then
		error(`ACF Behavioral: signal '{name}' MinStdDev must be a number >= 0`, 3)
	end
	local statistical = options.Statistical
	if statistical ~= nil and type(statistical) ~= "boolean" then
		error(`ACF Behavioral: signal '{name}' Statistical must be a boolean`, 3)
	end
	return table.freeze({
		Name = name,
		MaxPerSecond = maxPerSecond,
		Baseline = if baseline then table.freeze(baseline) else nil,
		MinStdDev = minStdDev,
		Statistical = statistical ~= false,
	}) :: any
end

function Behavioral.new()
	local module: any = {
		Name = MODULE_NAME,
		Version = "1.0.0",
		Category = "Behavioral",
		EnabledByDefault = true,
		ConfigSchema = CONFIG_SCHEMA,
	}

	local context: any = nil
	local running = false
	local definitions: { [string]: Definition } = {}
	local signalCount = 0
	local population: { [string]: BehavioralDetectors.HitCounts } = {}
	local baselines: { [string]: BehavioralDetectors.Baseline } = {}
	local accuracySettings: any = nil
	local actionSettings: any = nil
	local combatConnected = false
	local stats = { Violations = {} :: { [string]: number }, Reports = 0, ShotsObserved = 0, ActionsDropped = 0 }

	local function stateFor(session: any): SessionState
		return session:GetState(STATE_KEY, function()
			return {
				tracker = ViolationTracker.new(context.Config.Violation),
				signals = {},
				weapons = {},
				actions = {},
				actionCount = 0,
			}
		end)
	end

	local function handle(session: any, state: SessionState, violation: BehavioralDetectors.Violation, subject: string, now: number)
		local config = context.Config
		if not config.Detectors[violation.Detector] then
			return
		end
		stats.Violations[violation.Detector] = (stats.Violations[violation.Detector] or 0) + 1
		local report, score, confidence = state.tracker:Record(`{violation.Detector}:{subject}`, violation.Weight, now)
		if report then
			stats.Reports += 1
			local details = table.clone(violation.Details)
			details.Score = math.floor(score * 100) / 100
			context:Report(session, {
				Kind = violation.Kind,
				Severity = config.Severity[violation.Detector],
				Confidence = confidence,
				Details = details,
			})
		end
	end

	-- Signals ----------------------------------------------------------------------

	local function closeSignals(now: number)
		local settings = context.Config.Signals
		local sessions = context.Players:GetSessions()
		for name, definition in definitions do
			local players: { BehavioralDetectors.SignalPlayer } = {}
			local active: { { session: any, state: SessionState, player: BehavioralDetectors.SignalPlayer, rate: number } } = {}
			for _, session in sessions do
				local state = stateFor(session)
				local player = state.signals[name]
				if player then
					local rate = BehavioralDetectors.closeBucket(player, now, settings)
					table.insert(players, player)
					if rate and BehavioralDetectors.recentlyActive(player, settings) then
						table.insert(active, { session = session, state = state, player = player, rate = rate })
					end
				end
			end

			local baseline: BehavioralDetectors.Baseline? = nil
			local fixed = definition.Baseline
			if fixed then
				baseline = {
					Mean = fixed.Mean,
					Scale = math.max(fixed.StdDev, definition.MinStdDev),
					Players = 0,
					Source = "Defined",
				}
			else
				baseline = BehavioralDetectors.populationBaseline(players, settings, definition.MinStdDev)
			end
			if baseline then
				baselines[name] = baseline
			end

			for _, entry in active do
				local violations = BehavioralDetectors.evaluateSignal(definition, entry.player, entry.rate, baseline, settings)
				for _, violation in violations do
					handle(entry.session, entry.state, violation, name, now)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	-- Combat accuracy --------------------------------------------------------------

	local function onShot(payload: any)
		if not running or type(payload) ~= "table" then
			return
		end
		local kind = payload.WeaponKind
		if kind ~= "Hitscan" and kind ~= "Projectile" then
			return
		end
		local session = payload.Session
		local weapon = payload.Weapon
		if session == nil or type(weapon) ~= "string" then
			return
		end
		local now = context.Clock.Now()
		local halfLife = accuracySettings.HalfLifeSeconds
		local state = stateFor(session)
		local counts = state.weapons[weapon]
		if counts == nil then
			counts = BehavioralDetectors.newCounts(now)
			state.weapons[weapon] = counts
		end
		local totals = population[weapon]
		if totals == nil then
			totals = BehavioralDetectors.newCounts(now)
			population[weapon] = totals
		end
		BehavioralDetectors.ageCounts(counts, now, halfLife)
		BehavioralDetectors.ageCounts(totals, now, halfLife)

		local hit = payload.Hit == true
		local head = hit and payload.Headshot == true
		if payload.Event == "Shot" then
			stats.ShotsObserved += 1
			counts.Shots += 1
			totals.Shots += 1
		elseif payload.Event ~= "Hit" then
			return
		end
		if hit then
			counts.Hits += 1
			totals.Hits += 1
			if head then
				counts.Heads += 1
				totals.Heads += 1
			end
		end
	end

	local function evaluate(now: number)
		local config = context.Config
		local halfLife = accuracySettings.HalfLifeSeconds
		for _, totals in population do
			BehavioralDetectors.ageCounts(totals, now, halfLife)
		end
		for _, session in context.Players:GetSessions() do
			local state = stateFor(session)
			if config.Detectors.Accuracy or config.Detectors.Headshots then
				for weapon, counts in state.weapons do
					BehavioralDetectors.ageCounts(counts, now, halfLife)
					local totals = population[weapon]
					if totals then
						for _, violation in BehavioralDetectors.evaluateAccuracy(weapon, counts, totals, accuracySettings) do
							handle(session, state, violation, weapon, now)
						end
					end
				end
			end
			if config.Detectors.InvalidActions then
				for action, actionState in state.actions do
					local violation = BehavioralDetectors.evaluateInvalid(actionState, action, now, actionSettings)
					if violation then
						handle(session, state, violation, action, now)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	-- Public surface -----------------------------------------------------------------

	-- Declares a game signal. options: { MaxPerSecond?, Baseline? = { Mean, StdDev }
	-- (per second), MinStdDev?, Statistical? = true }.
	function module:DefineSignal(name: string, options: any)
		if definitions[name] ~= nil then
			error(`ACF Behavioral: signal '{tostring(name)}' is already defined`, 2)
		end
		local maxSignals = if context then context.Config.Signals.MaxSignals else 64
		if signalCount >= maxSignals then
			error(`ACF Behavioral: at most {maxSignals} signals can be defined`, 2)
		end
		definitions[name] = normalizeSignal(name, options)
		signalCount += 1
	end

	function module:IsSignalDefined(name: string): boolean
		return definitions[name] ~= nil
	end

	-- Adds `amount` (>= 0) to the player's signal. Returns false when not recorded.
	function module:RecordSignal(player: any, name: string, amount: number): boolean
		if definitions[name] == nil then
			error(`ACF Behavioral: signal '{tostring(name)}' is not defined (call DefineSignal first)`, 2)
		end
		if not running or not finite(amount) or amount < 0 or amount > MAX_AMOUNT then
			return false
		end
		local session = context.Players:GetSession(player)
		if session == nil then
			return false
		end
		local state = stateFor(session)
		local signal = state.signals[name]
		if signal == nil then
			signal = BehavioralDetectors.newSignalPlayer()
			state.signals[name] = signal
		end
		BehavioralDetectors.recordSignal(signal, amount)
		return true
	end

	-- Records one game action. valid = false marks an action the game's own client
	-- should never produce (see docs/BEHAVIORAL.md). Returns false when not recorded.
	function module:RecordAction(player: any, action: string, valid: boolean?): boolean
		if not validName(action) then
			error("ACF Behavioral: action name must be a string of 1-64 characters", 2)
		end
		if not running then
			return false
		end
		local session = context.Players:GetSession(player)
		if session == nil then
			return false
		end
		local now = context.Clock.Now()
		local state = stateFor(session)
		local actionState = state.actions[action]
		if actionState == nil then
			if state.actionCount >= actionSettings.MaxActionsPerPlayer then
				stats.ActionsDropped += 1
				return false
			end
			actionState = BehavioralDetectors.newActionState(now)
			state.actions[action] = actionState
			state.actionCount += 1
		end
		local violation = BehavioralDetectors.recordAction(actionState, action, valid ~= false, now, actionSettings)
		if violation then
			handle(session, state, violation, action, now)
		end
		return true
	end

	-- Snapshot of one player's behavioural statistics (diagnostics, Phase 6 risk input).
	function module:GetProfile(target: any): { [string]: any }?
		if context == nil then
			return nil
		end
		local session = context.Players:GetSession(target)
		if session == nil then
			return nil
		end
		local now = context.Clock.Now()
		local state = stateFor(session)
		local signals = {}
		for name, signal in state.signals do
			signals[name] = {
				MeanRate = signal.Self.Mean,
				StdDev = Statistics.stdDev(signal.Self),
				ActiveBuckets = signal.ActiveBuckets,
				LastRate = signal.LastRate,
				LastZ = signal.LastZ,
				Cusum = signal.Cusum.S,
			}
		end
		local weapons = {}
		for weapon, counts in state.weapons do
			local factor = Statistics.decayFactor(now - counts.Updated, accuracySettings.HalfLifeSeconds)
			local shots, hits, heads = counts.Shots * factor, counts.Hits * factor, counts.Heads * factor
			weapons[weapon] = {
				Shots = shots,
				Hits = hits,
				Headshots = heads,
				Accuracy = if shots > 0 then math.min(1, hits / shots) else nil,
				HeadshotRatio = if hits > 0 then math.min(1, heads / hits) else nil,
			}
		end
		local actions = {}
		for action, actionState in state.actions do
			local mean, std = Statistics.meanStd(actionState.Intervals)
			local factor = Statistics.decayFactor(now - actionState.Updated, actionSettings.InvalidHalfLifeSeconds)
			actions[action] = {
				Intervals = #actionState.Intervals,
				MeanInterval = if #actionState.Intervals > 0 then mean else nil,
				CV = if #actionState.Intervals > 1 and mean > 0 then std / mean else nil,
				Invalid = actionState.Invalid * factor,
				Total = actionState.Total * factor,
			}
		end
		return { Signals = signals, Weapons = weapons, Actions = actions }
	end

	-- The most recent baseline used for a signal, or nil before one exists.
	function module:GetBaseline(name: string): { [string]: any }?
		local baseline = baselines[name]
		return if baseline then table.clone(baseline) else nil
	end

	function module:GetStats(): { [string]: any }
		local signals = {}
		for name, definition in definitions do
			local baseline = baselines[name]
			signals[name] = {
				MaxPerSecond = definition.MaxPerSecond,
				BaselineSource = if baseline then baseline.Source else nil,
				BaselineMean = if baseline then baseline.Mean else nil,
				BaselinePlayers = if baseline then baseline.Players else nil,
			}
		end
		return {
			Signals = signals,
			CombatConnected = combatConnected,
			ShotsObserved = stats.ShotsObserved,
			ActionsDropped = stats.ActionsDropped,
			Violations = table.clone(stats.Violations),
			Reports = stats.Reports,
		}
	end

	-- Lifecycle ----------------------------------------------------------------------

	function module:Initialize(ctx)
		context = ctx
		local config = ctx.Config
		accuracySettings = table.clone(config.Accuracy)
		accuracySettings.CheckAccuracy = config.Detectors.Accuracy
		accuracySettings.CheckHeadshots = config.Detectors.Headshots
		actionSettings = table.clone(config.Actions)
		actionSettings.CheckTiming = config.Detectors.Timing
		if signalCount > config.Signals.MaxSignals then
			error(`ACF Behavioral: {signalCount} signals defined, MaxSignals is {config.Signals.MaxSignals}`)
		end
	end

	function module:Start()
		local config = context.Config
		running = true
		combatConnected = false
		if context.Events:Has(SHOT_TOPIC) then
			context.Events:Subscribe(SHOT_TOPIC, onShot)
			combatConnected = true
		end
		context.Scheduler:Every("Signals", config.Signals.BucketSeconds, closeSignals)
		context.Scheduler:Every("Evaluate", config.Accuracy.EvaluateIntervalSeconds, evaluate)
	end

	function module:Stop()
		running = false
		combatConnected = false
	end

	function module:Destroy()
		running = false
		context = nil
	end

	return module
end

return Behavioral

```

`src/Server/Detection/Behavioral/BehavioralDetectors.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Pure behavioural detectors. The Behavioral module owns state and timing; everything
	here takes values and times in and returns violations, so it is fully unit-tested.

	Signals (game-supplied rates: currency, XP, items, progression)
	  Input:  amounts recorded per player, closed into fixed buckets every BucketSeconds.
	          Every bucket after the player's first (partial) one counts, empty buckets
	          included, so a player paid in lumps (100 coins every 20 s) and one paid
	          steadily (5 per second) have the same mean rate.
	  Model:  each player keeps an EWMA mean/variance of their bucket rate. The baseline
	          is cross-sectional and robust: the MEDIAN of eligible players' means, with
	          scale = max(median per-player std, 1.4826 * MAD of means, MinStdDev,
	          MinRelativeStdDev * median). Eligible players have MinPlayerBuckets
	          buckets and activity within the last ActiveWithinBuckets buckets, so AFK
	          players do not drag the baseline down. One cheater cannot move a median:
	          the baseline assumes only that most players are honest. A game can
	          instead give a fixed Baseline { Mean, StdDev } (needed for small servers).
	          Each bucket of a recently active player is standardised (z, clamped to
	          ZClamp) and fed to an upper CUSUM (k = CusumK, h = CusumH); empty buckets
	          give negative z and pull it back down.
	  Output: SignalAnomaly when the CUSUM alarms AND the player's own mean is at least
	          MinRatio x the baseline (statistically and practically significant).
	          SignalRateLimit when a bucket's rate exceeds the game's MaxPerSecond.
	  Guard:  with ZClamp 3, k 0.5, h 6 an alarm needs three or more strongly anomalous
	          buckets; no single bucket can raise one.

	Accuracy (from Combat's server-validated shots; hitscan and projectile weapons)
	  Input:  per player and weapon, exponentially decayed counts of shots, character
	          hits and headshots; the same counts for the whole server.
	  Model:  Wilson lower bound (ConfidenceZ) of the player's hit ratio versus the
	          server's ratio for that weapon with the player's own shots removed, plus a
	          margin, never below a floor. Without enough population shots, only a high
	          absolute floor applies. Headshots per hit are judged the same way.
	  Output: AccuracyAnomaly / HeadshotAnomaly.
	  Guard:  MinShots / MinHits, and the lower bound itself: a skilled player with a
	          short hot streak has a wide interval and is not flagged.

	Timing (game-recorded actions: clicks, interactions, purchases)
	  Input:  intervals between consecutive actions of one kind; a gap longer than
	          MaxGapSeconds starts a new run.
	  Model:  coefficient of variation (std / mean) over non-overlapping windows of
	          WindowSize intervals.
	  Output: RegularTiming when CV < MaxCV for a window whose mean interval is within
	          [MinMeanIntervalSeconds, MaxMeanIntervalSeconds]. Human motor timing has a
	          CV of several percent even when deliberately rhythmic.

	Invalid actions (actions the game marks as impossible for its own client)
	  Model:  decayed invalid / total counts; Wilson lower bound of the invalid ratio.
	  Output: RepeatedInvalidActions when the bound exceeds MaxInvalidRatio with at
	          least MinInvalid decayed invalid actions.
]]

local Statistics = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Intelligence.Statistics)

export type Violation = { Detector: string, Kind: string, Weight: number, Details: { [string]: any } }

export type SignalDefinition = {
	Name: string,
	MaxPerSecond: number?,
	Baseline: { Mean: number, StdDev: number }?,
	MinStdDev: number,
	Statistical: boolean,
}

export type SignalPlayer = {
	Sum: number,
	BucketStart: number?,
	Self: Statistics.Ewma,
	Cusum: Statistics.Cusum,
	LastRate: number?,
	LastZ: number?,
	ActiveBuckets: number,
	BucketsSinceActive: number,
}

export type Baseline = { Mean: number, Scale: number, Players: number, Source: string }

export type HitCounts = { Shots: number, Hits: number, Heads: number, Updated: number }

export type ActionState = {
	Last: number?,
	Intervals: { number },
	SinceEvaluation: number,
	Invalid: number,
	Total: number,
	Updated: number,
}

local BehavioralDetectors = {}

local function round(value: number, places: number?): number
	local factor = 10 ^ (places or 2)
	return math.floor(value * factor + 0.5) / factor
end

local function median(sorted: { number }): number
	local n = #sorted
	if n == 0 then
		return 0
	end
	local middle = (n + 1) // 2
	if n % 2 == 1 then
		return sorted[middle]
	end
	return (sorted[middle] + sorted[middle + 1]) / 2
end

-- Signals --------------------------------------------------------------------------

function BehavioralDetectors.newSignalPlayer(): SignalPlayer
	return {
		Sum = 0,
		BucketStart = nil,
		Self = Statistics.newEwma(),
		Cusum = Statistics.newCusum(),
		LastRate = nil,
		LastZ = nil,
		ActiveBuckets = 0,
		BucketsSinceActive = 0,
	}
end

function BehavioralDetectors.recordSignal(player: SignalPlayer, amount: number)
	player.Sum += amount
end

-- Closes the player's current bucket at `now` and returns its rate (sum / elapsed),
-- or nil. The first bucket after a player starts being tracked is partial and is
-- discarded; a bucket shorter than half of BucketSeconds keeps accumulating. Updates the
-- player's own EWMA with the rate, empty buckets included.
function BehavioralDetectors.closeBucket(player: SignalPlayer, now: number, settings: any): number?
	local start = player.BucketStart
	if start == nil then
		player.Sum = 0
		player.BucketStart = now
		return nil
	end
	local elapsed = now - start
	if elapsed < settings.BucketSeconds * 0.5 then
		return nil
	end
	local sum = player.Sum
	player.Sum = 0
	player.BucketStart = now
	local rate = sum / elapsed
	player.LastRate = rate
	if sum > 0 then
		player.ActiveBuckets += 1
		player.BucketsSinceActive = 0
	else
		player.BucketsSinceActive += 1
	end
	Statistics.updateEwma(player.Self, rate, settings.SelfAlpha)
	return rate
end

-- Whether a player has had activity within the last ActiveWithinBuckets buckets.
function BehavioralDetectors.recentlyActive(player: SignalPlayer, settings: any): boolean
	return player.ActiveBuckets > 0 and player.BucketsSinceActive < settings.ActiveWithinBuckets
end

-- Robust cross-sectional baseline from players with at least MinPlayerBuckets buckets
-- who were recently active. Nil when fewer than MinContributors players qualify.
function BehavioralDetectors.populationBaseline(players: { SignalPlayer }, settings: any, minStdDev: number): Baseline?
	local means: { number } = {}
	local stds: { number } = {}
	for _, player in players do
		if player.Self.Count >= settings.MinPlayerBuckets and BehavioralDetectors.recentlyActive(player, settings) then
			table.insert(means, player.Self.Mean)
			table.insert(stds, Statistics.stdDev(player.Self))
		end
	end
	if #means < settings.MinContributors then
		return nil
	end
	table.sort(means)
	table.sort(stds)
	local center = median(means)
	local deviations: { number } = {}
	for _, value in means do
		table.insert(deviations, math.abs(value - center))
	end
	table.sort(deviations)
	local mad = 1.4826 * median(deviations)
	local scale = math.max(median(stds), mad, minStdDev, settings.MinRelativeStdDev * math.abs(center))
	return { Mean = center, Scale = scale, Players = #means, Source = "Population" }
end

-- Evaluates one active bucket `rate` for a player against `baseline` (nil = none yet).
function BehavioralDetectors.evaluateSignal(
	definition: SignalDefinition,
	player: SignalPlayer,
	rate: number,
	baseline: Baseline?,
	settings: any
): { Violation }
	local violations: { Violation } = {}

	local limit = definition.MaxPerSecond
	if limit ~= nil and rate > limit * (1 + settings.HardLimitTolerance) then
		local ratio = rate / limit
		table.insert(violations, {
			Detector = "SignalRateLimit",
			Kind = "SignalRateLimit",
			Weight = math.clamp(ratio - 1, 1, 2),
			Details = {
				Signal = definition.Name,
				RatePerSecond = round(rate),
				MaxPerSecond = limit,
			},
		})
	end

	if baseline ~= nil and definition.Statistical then
		local z = Statistics.zScore(rate, baseline.Mean, baseline.Scale, 0, 0, settings.ZClamp)
		player.LastZ = z
		local alarm, statistic, steps = Statistics.cusumStep(player.Cusum, z, settings.CusumK, settings.CusumH)
		local ratio = if baseline.Mean > 0 then player.Self.Mean / baseline.Mean else math.huge
		if alarm and ratio >= settings.MinRatio then
			table.insert(violations, {
				Detector = "SignalAnomaly",
				Kind = "SignalAnomaly",
				Weight = 3,
				Details = {
					Signal = definition.Name,
					RatePerSecond = round(rate),
					PlayerMean = round(player.Self.Mean),
					BaselineMean = round(baseline.Mean),
					BaselineScale = round(baseline.Scale),
					BaselineSource = baseline.Source,
					BaselinePlayers = baseline.Players,
					Ratio = if ratio == math.huge then -1 else round(ratio),
					Cusum = round(statistic),
					Buckets = steps,
				},
			})
		end
	end
	return violations
end

-- Accuracy -------------------------------------------------------------------------

function BehavioralDetectors.newCounts(now: number): HitCounts
	return { Shots = 0, Hits = 0, Heads = 0, Updated = now }
end

function BehavioralDetectors.ageCounts(counts: HitCounts, now: number, halfLife: number)
	local factor = Statistics.decayFactor(now - counts.Updated, halfLife)
	if factor < 1 then
		counts.Shots *= factor
		counts.Hits *= factor
		counts.Heads *= factor
	end
	if now > counts.Updated then
		counts.Updated = now
	end
end

local function ratioCheck(
	successes: number,
	trials: number,
	popSuccesses: number,
	popTrials: number,
	z: number,
	margin: number,
	floor: number,
	floorWithoutBaseline: number,
	minPopulation: number
): (boolean, { [string]: any })
	local lower = Statistics.wilsonLower(successes, trials, z)
	local threshold, basis, popRatio
	if popTrials >= minPopulation then
		popRatio = popSuccesses / popTrials
		threshold = math.max(popRatio + margin, floor)
		basis = "Population"
	else
		threshold = floorWithoutBaseline
		basis = "Absolute"
	end
	return lower > threshold,
		{
			Ratio = round(successes / trials, 3),
			LowerBound = round(lower, 3),
			Threshold = round(threshold, 3),
			PopulationRatio = if popRatio then round(popRatio, 3) else nil,
			Basis = basis,
		}
end

-- Compares a player's counts for one weapon with the population counts for that weapon
-- (which include the player; their own share is subtracted). Both must be aged to the
-- same time with the same half-life.
function BehavioralDetectors.evaluateAccuracy(weapon: string, player: HitCounts, population: HitCounts, settings: any): { Violation }
	local violations: { Violation } = {}
	local popShots = math.max(0, population.Shots - player.Shots)
	local popHits = math.max(0, population.Hits - player.Hits)
	local popHeads = math.max(0, population.Heads - player.Heads)

	if settings.CheckAccuracy and player.Shots >= settings.MinShots then
		local flagged, details = ratioCheck(
			math.min(player.Hits, player.Shots),
			player.Shots,
			math.min(popHits, popShots),
			popShots,
			settings.ConfidenceZ,
			settings.AccuracyMargin,
			settings.AccuracyFloor,
			settings.AccuracyFloorWithoutBaseline,
			settings.MinPopulationShots
		)
		if flagged then
			details.Weapon = weapon
			details.Shots = round(player.Shots, 1)
			details.Hits = round(player.Hits, 1)
			table.insert(violations, { Detector = "Accuracy", Kind = "AccuracyAnomaly", Weight = 1, Details = details })
		end
	end

	if settings.CheckHeadshots and player.Hits >= settings.MinHits then
		local flagged, details = ratioCheck(
			math.min(player.Heads, player.Hits),
			player.Hits,
			math.min(popHeads, popHits),
			popHits,
			settings.ConfidenceZ,
			settings.HeadshotMargin,
			settings.HeadshotFloor,
			settings.HeadshotFloorWithoutBaseline,
			settings.MinPopulationHits
		)
		if flagged then
			details.Weapon = weapon
			details.Hits = round(player.Hits, 1)
			details.Headshots = round(player.Heads, 1)
			table.insert(violations, { Detector = "Headshots", Kind = "HeadshotAnomaly", Weight = 1, Details = details })
		end
	end
	return violations
end

-- Actions --------------------------------------------------------------------------

function BehavioralDetectors.newActionState(now: number): ActionState
	return { Last = nil, Intervals = {}, SinceEvaluation = 0, Invalid = 0, Total = 0, Updated = now }
end

-- Records one action. Returns a RegularTiming violation when a full window is too
-- regular (only when settings.CheckTiming is true).
function BehavioralDetectors.recordAction(state: ActionState, action: string, valid: boolean, now: number, settings: any): Violation?
	-- Validity counts (decayed).
	local factor = Statistics.decayFactor(now - state.Updated, settings.InvalidHalfLifeSeconds)
	state.Invalid *= factor
	state.Total *= factor
	if now > state.Updated then
		state.Updated = now
	end
	state.Total += 1
	if not valid then
		state.Invalid += 1
	end

	-- Timing.
	local last = state.Last
	state.Last = now
	if last == nil then
		return nil
	end
	local interval = now - last
	local intervals = state.Intervals
	if interval > settings.MaxGapSeconds or interval < 0 then
		table.clear(intervals)
		state.SinceEvaluation = 0
		return nil
	end
	table.insert(intervals, interval)
	if #intervals > settings.WindowSize then
		table.remove(intervals, 1)
	end
	state.SinceEvaluation += 1
	if not settings.CheckTiming or #intervals < settings.WindowSize or state.SinceEvaluation < settings.WindowSize then
		return nil
	end
	state.SinceEvaluation = 0

	local mean, std = Statistics.meanStd(intervals)
	if mean < settings.MinMeanIntervalSeconds or mean > settings.MaxMeanIntervalSeconds then
		return nil
	end
	local cv = std / mean
	if cv >= settings.MaxCV then
		return nil
	end
	return {
		Detector = "Timing",
		Kind = "RegularTiming",
		Weight = 1.5,
		Details = {
			Action = action,
			Samples = #intervals,
			MeanInterval = round(mean, 4),
			StdDev = round(std, 4),
			CV = round(cv, 4),
		},
	}
end

-- Invalid-action ratio check for one action kind, aged to `now`.
function BehavioralDetectors.evaluateInvalid(state: ActionState, action: string, now: number, settings: any): Violation?
	local factor = Statistics.decayFactor(now - state.Updated, settings.InvalidHalfLifeSeconds)
	local invalid, total = state.Invalid * factor, state.Total * factor
	if invalid < settings.MinInvalid or total <= 0 then
		return nil
	end
	local lower = Statistics.wilsonLower(invalid, total, settings.InvalidConfidenceZ)
	if lower <= settings.MaxInvalidRatio then
		return nil
	end
	return {
		Detector = "InvalidActions",
		Kind = "RepeatedInvalidActions",
		Weight = 1,
		Details = {
			Action = action,
			Invalid = round(invalid, 1),
			Total = round(total, 1),
			LowerBound = round(lower, 3),
			MaxRatio = settings.MaxInvalidRatio,
		},
	}
end

return BehavioralDetectors
```

```luau
	one (Telemetry) with Modules = { Telemetry = true }.
```

`src/Server/Detection/Combat/Combat.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Combat: server-authoritative combat integrity (built-in, Category "Combat").

	Games declare weapons and abilities (CombatRules) and route client attack *claims*
	through ValidateAttack / FireProjectile / ValidateProjectileHit. ACF decides whether
	the shot happened and whether the hit is plausible, and computes the damage. The
	client never supplies damage, fire rate, range or ammo.

	Stage 1, shot legitimacy (a passing shot consumes ammo and starts cooldowns):
	  UnknownWeapon, InvalidClaim, ShooterDead, NotOwned, Reloading, NoAmmo, Cooldown,
	  OriginMismatch (claimed origin far from the shooter's server position), FireRate.
	Stage 2, hit legitimacy (the hit is refused; the shot still counted):
	  InvalidClaim, HitMismatch (claimed hit point far from the part), SelfHit,
	  TargetDead, OutOfRange, FriendlyFire, NoLineOfSight, and for projectiles
	  UnknownShot, DuplicateShot, ProjectileExpired, ProjectileTiming (arrived before the
	  projectile could), OffTrajectory.

	Latency: ranges and hit tolerances grow with the shooter's ping x MaxTargetSpeed;
	cooldowns have a small tolerance; fire rate uses a token bucket with a refill margin.

	Every rejection feeds a per-player ViolationTracker with a per-reason weight. One
	desynced shot never reports; sustained impossible combat does. Accuracy and
	reaction-time statistics are Phase 5 (Behavioral), so skill is not judged here.
]]

local Configuration = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Core.Configuration)
local RateLimiter = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Core.RateLimiter)
local ViolationTracker = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Intelligence.ViolationTracker)
local Validators = require(script.Parent.Parent.RemoteSecurity.Validators)
local CombatRules = require(script.Parent.CombatRules)
local Types = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Types)

local S = Configuration.Schema

local MODULE_NAME = "Combat"
local STATE_KEY = "Combat.State"

local REASONS = {
	"UnknownWeapon",
	"InvalidClaim",
	"ShooterDead",
	"NotOwned",
	"Reloading",
	"NoAmmo",
	"Cooldown",
	"OriginMismatch",
	"FireRate",
	"HitMismatch",
	"SelfHit",
	"TargetDead",
	"OutOfRange",
	"FriendlyFire",
	"NoLineOfSight",
	"UnknownShot",
	"DuplicateShot",
	"ProjectileExpired",
	"ProjectileTiming",
	"OffTrajectory",
}

local DEFAULT_WEIGHTS = {
	UnknownWeapon = 3,
	InvalidClaim = 2,
	ShooterDead = 1,
	NotOwned = 3,
	Reloading = 1,
	NoAmmo = 1,
	Cooldown = 1,
	OriginMismatch = 2,
	FireRate = 1.5,
	HitMismatch = 1,
	SelfHit = 0.5,
	TargetDead = 0,
	OutOfRange = 2,
	FriendlyFire = 0.5,
	NoLineOfSight = 1.5,
	UnknownShot = 2,
	DuplicateShot = 2,
	ProjectileExpired = 1,
	ProjectileTiming = 2,
	OffTrajectory = 2,
}

local DEFAULT_SEVERITY = {
	UnknownWeapon = 30,
	InvalidClaim = 20,
	ShooterDead = 15,
	NotOwned = 35,
	Reloading = 10,
	NoAmmo = 15,
	Cooldown = 15,
	OriginMismatch = 30,
	FireRate = 30,
	HitMismatch = 20,
	SelfHit = 5,
	TargetDead = 0,
	OutOfRange = 30,
	FriendlyFire = 5,
	NoLineOfSight = 25,
	UnknownShot = 25,
	DuplicateShot = 25,
	ProjectileExpired = 10,
	ProjectileTiming = 30,
	OffTrajectory = 25,
}

local function reasonTable(defaults: { [string]: number }, max: number)
	local fields = {}
	for _, reason in REASONS do
		fields[reason] = S.Number(defaults[reason], { Min = 0, Max = max })
	end
	return S.Table(fields)
```
