```luau
end

local CONFIG_SCHEMA = S.Table({
	OriginToleranceStuds = S.Number(8, { Min = 0, Max = 10000 }),
	RangeToleranceFraction = S.Number(0.1, { Min = 0, Max = 10 }),
	RangeToleranceStuds = S.Number(5, { Min = 0, Max = 10000 }),
	HitToleranceStuds = S.Number(6, { Min = 0, Max = 10000 }),
	MaxTargetSpeed = S.Number(32, { Min = 0, Max = 100000 }),
	MaxPingCompensationSeconds = S.Number(0.5, { Min = 0, Max = 5 }),
	LineOfSightEndToleranceStuds = S.Number(2, { Min = 0, Max = 1000 }),
	CooldownToleranceSeconds = S.Number(0.1, { Min = 0, Max = 60 }),
	FireRateTolerance = S.Number(1.15, { Min = 1, Max = 10 }),
	ProjectileTimingToleranceSeconds = S.Number(0.15, { Min = 0, Max = 10 }),
	ProjectileTrajectoryToleranceStuds = S.Number(6, { Min = 0, Max = 10000 }),
	ProjectileTrajectoryToleranceFraction = S.Number(0.03, { Min = 0, Max = 10 }),
	MaxPendingProjectiles = S.Number(32, { Min = 1, Max = 4096, Integer = true }),
	Weights = reasonTable(DEFAULT_WEIGHTS, 1000),
	Severity = reasonTable(DEFAULT_SEVERITY, 100),
	Violation = S.Table({
		HalfLifeSeconds = S.Number(20, { Min = 0.5, Max = 3600 }),
		ReportThreshold = S.Number(6, { Min = 0.1, Max = 10000 }),
		ReportIntervalSeconds = S.Number(10, { Min = 0, Max = 3600 }),
		ConfidenceScale = S.Number(10, { Min = 0.1, Max = 10000 }),
	}),
})

-- Remote argument validator for attack claims; use in ProtectEvent Arguments.
local CLAIM_VALIDATOR = Validators.struct({
	Origin = Validators.optional(Validators.vector3({ MaxMagnitude = 1e6 })),
	Direction = Validators.optional(Validators.vector3({ MaxMagnitude = 1e6 })),
	Target = Validators.optional(Validators.instance("BasePart")),
	HitPosition = Validators.optional(Validators.vector3({ MaxMagnitude = 1e6 })),
	ShotId = Validators.optional(Validators.string({ MinLength = 1, MaxLength = 64 })),
})

type Definition = CombatRules.Definition

type Projectile = {
	id: string,
	weapon: string,
	t: number,
	ox: number,
	oy: number,
	oz: number,
	dx: number,
	dy: number,
	dz: number,
	pierceLeft: number,
	-- The first character hit is counted for accuracy statistics; pierce hits are not.
	hitCounted: boolean,
}

type WeaponState = {
	limiter: RateLimiter.RateLimiter?,
	lastUse: number?,
	magazine: number,
	reserve: number,
	reloadUntil: number?,
}

type CombatState = {
	tracker: ViolationTracker.ViolationTracker,
	weapons: { [string]: WeaponState },
	projectiles: { [string]: Projectile },
	projectileOrder: { string },
}

export type AttackResult = {
	Allowed: boolean,
	Reason: string?,
	-- true when the shot itself was legitimate (ammo consumed) even if the hit was refused.
	ShotAccepted: boolean,
	Damage: number,
	Headshot: boolean,
	Target: Types.TargetInfo?,
	Distance: number?,
}

export type Claim = {
	Origin: any?,
	Direction: any?,
	Target: any?,
	HitPosition: any?,
	ShotId: string?,
}

local Combat = {}
Combat.Name = MODULE_NAME
Combat.ConfigSchema = CONFIG_SCHEMA
Combat.ClaimValidator = CLAIM_VALIDATOR
Combat.Reasons = table.freeze(table.clone(REASONS))

local function defaultInventory(player: any, weaponId: string, definition: Definition): boolean
	if definition.Kind == "Ability" then
		return true
	end
	local character = player.Character
	if character and character:FindFirstChild(weaponId) then
		return true
	end
	local backpack = player:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	return backpack ~= nil and backpack:FindFirstChild(weaponId) ~= nil
end

local function defaultCanDamage(attacker: any, target: Types.TargetInfo): boolean
	local victim = target.Player
	if victim == nil then
		return true
	end
	local ok, sameTeam = pcall(function()
		return attacker.Team ~= nil and attacker.Team == victim.Team and not attacker.Neutral
	end)
	return not (ok and sameTeam)
end

local PROVIDERS = { Inventory = true, CanDamage = true, DamageCalculator = true }

local function rejected(reason: string, shotAccepted: boolean): AttackResult
	return { Allowed = false, Reason = reason, ShotAccepted = shotAccepted, Damage = 0, Headshot = false }
end

local function round(value: number): number
	return math.floor(value * 100 + 0.5) / 100
end

function Combat.new()
	local module: any = {
		Name = MODULE_NAME,
		Version = "1.0.0",
		Category = "Combat",
		EnabledByDefault = true,
		ConfigSchema = CONFIG_SCHEMA,
	}

	local context: any = nil
	local running = false
	local definitions: { [string]: Definition } = {}
	local providers: { [string]: any } = {
		Inventory = defaultInventory,
		CanDamage = defaultCanDamage,
		DamageCalculator = nil,
	}
	local stats: { [string]: { Shots: number, Hits: number, Rejected: { [string]: number } } } = {}
	local globalRejected: { [string]: number } = {}
	local shotTopic: string? = nil

	local function combatState(session: any): CombatState
		return session:GetState(STATE_KEY, function()
			return {
				tracker = ViolationTracker.new(context.Config.Violation),
				weapons = {},
				projectiles = {},
				projectileOrder = {},
			}
		end)
	end

	local function finishReload(ws: WeaponState, definition: Definition)
		local ammo = definition.Ammo
		if ammo then
			local take = math.min(ammo.Magazine - ws.magazine, ws.reserve)
			ws.magazine += take
			ws.reserve -= take
		end
		ws.reloadUntil = nil
	end

	local function weaponState(state: CombatState, definition: Definition, now: number): WeaponState
		local ws = state.weapons[definition.Id]
		if ws == nil then
			local ammo = definition.Ammo
			local limiter = nil
			if definition.FireRate then
				limiter = RateLimiter.new(
					definition.Burst,
					definition.FireRate * context.Config.FireRateTolerance,
					context.Clock.Now,
					1
				)
			end
			ws = {
				limiter = limiter,
				lastUse = nil,
				magazine = if ammo then ammo.Magazine else math.huge,
				reserve = if ammo then ammo.Reserve else math.huge,
				reloadUntil = nil,
			}
			state.weapons[definition.Id] = ws
		end
		local reloadUntil = ws.reloadUntil
		if reloadUntil and now >= reloadUntil then
			finishReload(ws, definition)
		end
		return ws
	end

	local function weaponStats(id: string)
		local entry = stats[id]
		if entry == nil then
			entry = { Shots = 0, Hits = 0, Rejected = {} }
			stats[id] = entry
		end
		return entry
	end

	-- Records a rejection, scores it, reports when sustained. Returns the result.
	local function reject(
		session: any?,
		state: CombatState?,
		weaponId: string?,
		reason: string,
		shotAccepted: boolean,
		details: { [string]: any }?
	): AttackResult
		globalRejected[reason] = (globalRejected[reason] or 0) + 1
		if weaponId and definitions[weaponId] then
			local entry = weaponStats(weaponId)
			entry.Rejected[reason] = (entry.Rejected[reason] or 0) + 1
		end
		if session and state then
			local config = context.Config
			local now = context.Clock.Now()
			local report, score, confidence = state.tracker:Record(reason, config.Weights[reason], now)
			if report then
				local reportDetails: { [string]: any } = if details then table.clone(details) else {}
				reportDetails.Weapon = weaponId
				reportDetails.Score = round(score)
				context:Report(session, {
					Kind = "Combat" .. reason,
					Severity = config.Severity[reason],
					Confidence = confidence,
					Details = reportDetails,
				})
			end
		end
		return rejected(reason, shotAccepted)
	end

	local function callProvider(name: string, ...: any): (boolean, any)
		local fn = providers[name]
		if fn == nil then
			return true, nil
		end
		local ok, result = pcall(fn, ...)
		if not ok then
			context.Logger:Error("Combat provider errored; treated as denial", { Provider = name, Error = tostring(result) })
			return false, nil
		end
		return true, result
	end

	local function pingCompensation(sample: any): number
		local ping = sample.Ping
		if type(ping) ~= "number" or ping ~= ping or ping < 0 then
			return 0
		end
		return math.min(ping, context.Config.MaxPingCompensationSeconds)
	end

	type Shooter = { session: any, state: CombatState, definition: Definition, ws: WeaponState, sample: any, ox: number, oy: number, oz: number }

	-- Stage 1. Returns (Shooter, nil) or (nil, rejection).
	local function admitShot(player: any, weaponId: any, claim: any, expectedKind: string?): (Shooter?, AttackResult?)
		if not running then
			return nil, rejected("NotRunning", false)
		end
		local session = context.Players:GetSession(player)
		if session == nil then
			return nil, rejected("NoSession", false)
		end
		local state = combatState(session)
		local definition = if type(weaponId) == "string" then definitions[weaponId] else nil
		if definition == nil then
			return nil, reject(session, state, nil, "UnknownWeapon", false)
		end
		-- Enforcement (Phase 7): players restricted for combat or aim evidence cannot
		-- deal damage. Not player behaviour, so not scored.
		local enforcement = context.Enforcement
		local operation = `Combat.{weaponId}`
		if enforcement:IsRestricted(session, "Combat", operation) or enforcement:IsRestricted(session, "Aim", operation) then
			globalRejected.Restricted = (globalRejected.Restricted or 0) + 1
			return nil, rejected("Restricted", false)
		end
		if expectedKind == "Projectile" and definition.Kind ~= "Projectile" then
			return nil, reject(session, state, weaponId, "InvalidClaim", false, { Problem = "not a projectile weapon" })
		elseif expectedKind ~= "Projectile" and definition.Kind == "Projectile" then
			return nil, reject(session, state, weaponId, "InvalidClaim", false, { Problem = "use FireProjectile" })
		end
		if type(claim) ~= "table" then
			return nil, reject(session, state, weaponId, "InvalidClaim", false, { Problem = "claim must be a table" })
		end

		local physics = context.Platform.Physics
		local character = session.Character
		local sample = nil
		if character and physics then
			local ok, result = pcall(physics.Sample, physics, character, session.Player)
			sample = if ok then result else nil
		end
		if sample == nil or sample.Health <= 0 then
			return nil, reject(session, state, weaponId, "ShooterDead", false)
		end

		local owned, result = callProvider("Inventory", player, weaponId, definition)
		if not owned or not result then
			return nil, reject(session, state, weaponId, "NotOwned", false)
		end

		local now = context.Clock.Now()
		local ws = weaponState(state, definition, now)
		if ws.reloadUntil ~= nil then
			return nil, reject(session, state, weaponId, "Reloading", false)
		end
		if definition.Ammo and ws.magazine <= 0 then
			return nil, reject(session, state, weaponId, "NoAmmo", false)
		end
		local config = context.Config
		local lastUse = ws.lastUse
		if definition.Cooldown > 0 and lastUse ~= nil and now - lastUse < definition.Cooldown - config.CooldownToleranceSeconds then
			return nil, reject(session, state, weaponId, "Cooldown", false, {
				SinceLastUse = round(now - lastUse),
				Cooldown = definition.Cooldown,
			})
		end

		local ox, oy, oz = sample.X, sample.Y, sample.Z
		if claim.Origin ~= nil then
			local cx, cy, cz = CombatRules.vector(claim.Origin)
			if cx == nil then
				return nil, reject(session, state, weaponId, "InvalidClaim", false, { Problem = "Origin" })
			end
			local offset = CombatRules.distance(cx :: number, cy :: number, cz :: number, sample.X, sample.Y, sample.Z)
			local allowed = config.OriginToleranceStuds + pingCompensation(sample) * math.max(sample.WalkSpeed, 16) * 1.25
			if offset > allowed then
				return nil, reject(session, state, weaponId, "OriginMismatch", false, {
					Offset = round(offset),
					Allowed = round(allowed),
				})
			end
			ox, oy, oz = cx :: number, cy :: number, cz :: number
		elseif definition.Kind == "Hitscan" or definition.Kind == "Projectile" then
			return nil, reject(session, state, weaponId, "InvalidClaim", false, { Problem = "Origin required" })
		end

		local limiter = ws.limiter
		if limiter and not limiter:Allow("shot") then
			return nil, reject(session, state, weaponId, "FireRate", false, { FireRate = definition.FireRate })
		end

		-- Commit the shot.
		if definition.Ammo then
			ws.magazine -= 1
		end
		ws.lastUse = now
		weaponStats(weaponId).Shots += 1
		return {
			session = session,
			state = state,
			definition = definition,
			ws = ws,
			sample = sample,
			ox = ox,
			oy = oy,
			oz = oz,
		},
			nil
	end

	-- Stage 2. originX.. is where the shot left (validated); rangeFrom.. measures range.
	local function evaluateHit(shooter: Shooter, claim: Claim, checkLineOfSight: boolean): AttackResult
		local session, state, definition = shooter.session, shooter.state, shooter.definition
		local weaponId = definition.Id
		local config = context.Config
		local sample = shooter.sample
		local slack = pingCompensation(sample) * config.MaxTargetSpeed

		local target = claim.Target
		if target == nil then
			if definition.RequireTarget then
				return reject(session, state, weaponId, "InvalidClaim", true, { Problem = "Target required" })
			end
			if claim.HitPosition ~= nil then
				local hx, hy, hz = CombatRules.vector(claim.HitPosition)
				if hx == nil then
					return reject(session, state, weaponId, "InvalidClaim", true, { Problem = "HitPosition" })
				end
				local distance = CombatRules.distance(shooter.ox, shooter.oy, shooter.oz, hx :: number, hy :: number, hz :: number)
				local maxRange = definition.MaxRange * (1 + config.RangeToleranceFraction) + config.RangeToleranceStuds + slack
				if distance > maxRange then
					return reject(session, state, weaponId, "OutOfRange", true, { Distance = round(distance), MaxRange = definition.MaxRange })
				end
				return { Allowed = true, ShotAccepted = true, Damage = 0, Headshot = false, Target = nil, Distance = distance }
			end
			return { Allowed = true, ShotAccepted = true, Damage = 0, Headshot = false, Target = nil }
		end

		local physics = context.Platform.Physics
		local info: Types.TargetInfo? = nil
		if typeof(target) == "Instance" and physics and physics.Describe then
			local ok, result = pcall(physics.Describe, physics, target)
			info = if ok then result else nil
		end
		if info == nil then
			return reject(session, state, weaponId, "InvalidClaim", true, { Problem = "Target is not a hittable part" })
		end
		local targetInfo = info :: Types.TargetInfo

		local hx, hy, hz = targetInfo.X, targetInfo.Y, targetInfo.Z
		if claim.HitPosition ~= nil then
			local cx, cy, cz = CombatRules.vector(claim.HitPosition)
			if cx == nil then
				return reject(session, state, weaponId, "InvalidClaim", true, { Problem = "HitPosition" })
			end
			local mismatch = CombatRules.distance(cx :: number, cy :: number, cz :: number, targetInfo.X, targetInfo.Y, targetInfo.Z)
			if mismatch > config.HitToleranceStuds + slack then
				return reject(session, state, weaponId, "HitMismatch", true, { Offset = round(mismatch) })
			end
			hx, hy, hz = cx :: number, cy :: number, cz :: number
		end

		if targetInfo.Model ~= nil and targetInfo.Model == session.Character then
			return reject(session, state, weaponId, "SelfHit", true)
		end
		if targetInfo.Model ~= nil and not targetInfo.Alive then
			return reject(session, state, weaponId, "TargetDead", true)
		end

		local distance = CombatRules.distance(shooter.ox, shooter.oy, shooter.oz, hx, hy, hz)
		local maxRange = definition.MaxRange * (1 + config.RangeToleranceFraction) + config.RangeToleranceStuds + slack
		if distance > maxRange then
			return reject(session, state, weaponId, "OutOfRange", true, { Distance = round(distance), MaxRange = definition.MaxRange })
		end

		if targetInfo.Player ~= nil then
			local ok, allowed = callProvider("CanDamage", session.Player, targetInfo)
			if not ok or allowed == false then
				return reject(session, state, weaponId, "FriendlyFire", true)
			end
		end

		if checkLineOfSight and definition.RequireLineOfSight and physics and distance > 0 then
			local dx, dy, dz = hx - shooter.ox, hy - shooter.oy, hz - shooter.oz
			local ok, blocked = pcall(physics.Cast, physics, shooter.ox, shooter.oy, shooter.oz, dx, dy, dz, session.Character)
			if ok and blocked ~= nil and blocked < distance - config.LineOfSightEndToleranceStuds then
				return reject(session, state, weaponId, "NoLineOfSight", true, {
					BlockedAt = round(blocked),
					Distance = round(distance),
				})
			end
		end

		local damage = CombatRules.damage(definition, distance, targetInfo.IsHead)
		local calculator = providers.DamageCalculator
		if calculator then
			local ok, custom = pcall(calculator, {
				Attacker = session.Player,
				Weapon = definition,
				Target = targetInfo,
				Distance = distance,
				Headshot = targetInfo.IsHead,
				BaseDamage = damage,
			})
			if ok and type(custom) == "number" and custom == custom and custom >= 0 and custom < math.huge then
				damage = custom
			elseif not ok then
				context.Logger:Error("DamageCalculator errored; using base damage", { Error = tostring(custom) })
			end
		end

		weaponStats(weaponId).Hits += 1
		return {
			Allowed = true,
			ShotAccepted = true,
			Damage = damage,
			Headshot = targetInfo.IsHead,
			Target = targetInfo,
			Distance = distance,
		}
	end

	-- Publishes "Combat.Shot" for behavioural statistics. Event "Shot" is one accepted
	-- shot (Hit says whether it hit a character); "Hit" is a later projectile impact.
	local function publishShot(session: any, definition: Definition, event: string, result: AttackResult?)
		if shotTopic == nil then
			return
		end
		local target = if result then result.Target else nil
		local hit = result ~= nil and result.Allowed and target ~= nil and target.Model ~= nil
		context.Events:Publish(shotTopic :: string, {
			Session = session,
			Weapon = definition.Id,
			WeaponKind = definition.Kind,
			Event = event,
			Hit = hit,
			Headshot = hit and (result :: AttackResult).Headshot,
			Time = context.Clock.Now(),
		})
	end

	local function requireRunning(action: string)
		if context == nil then
			error(`ACF Combat: {action} requires ACF to be started`, 3)
		end
	end

	-- Integration surface -------------------------------------------------------------

	function module:Define(id: string, definition: any)
		if definitions[id] ~= nil then
			error(`ACF Combat: weapon '{tostring(id)}' is already defined`, 2)
		end
		definitions[id] = CombatRules.normalize(id, definition)
	end

	function module:IsDefined(id: string): boolean
		return definitions[id] ~= nil
	end

	function module:GetDefinition(id: string): Definition?
		return definitions[id]
	end

	-- name: "Inventory" (player, weaponId, definition) -> boolean
	--       "CanDamage" (attacker, targetInfo) -> boolean
	--       "DamageCalculator" (info) -> number
	-- Pass nil to restore the default (DamageCalculator default: none).
	function module:SetProvider(name: string, fn: any)
		if not PROVIDERS[name] then
			error(`ACF Combat: unknown provider '{tostring(name)}'`, 2)
		end
		if fn ~= nil and type(fn) ~= "function" then
			error("ACF Combat: provider must be a function or nil", 2)
		end
		if fn == nil then
			providers[name] = if name == "Inventory"
				then defaultInventory
				elseif name == "CanDamage" then defaultCanDamage
				else nil
		else
			providers[name] = fn
		end
	end

	-- Hitscan, Melee and Ability attacks. Claim: { Origin?, Target?, HitPosition? }.
	function module:ValidateAttack(player: any, weaponId: string, claim: Claim): AttackResult
		requireRunning("ValidateAttack")
		local shooter, failure = admitShot(player, weaponId, claim, nil)
		if shooter == nil then
			return failure :: AttackResult
		end
		local result = evaluateHit(shooter, claim, true)
		publishShot(shooter.session, shooter.definition, "Shot", result)
		return result
	end

	-- Registers a projectile at fire time. Claim: { Origin, Direction, ShotId }.
	function module:FireProjectile(player: any, weaponId: string, claim: Claim): AttackResult
		requireRunning("FireProjectile")
		local shooter, failure = admitShot(player, weaponId, claim, "Projectile")
		if shooter == nil then
			return failure :: AttackResult
		end
		local session, state, definition = shooter.session, shooter.state, shooter.definition
		local shotId = claim.ShotId
		if type(shotId) ~= "string" or #shotId == 0 or #shotId > 64 then
			return reject(session, state, weaponId, "InvalidClaim", true, { Problem = "ShotId" })
		end
		local dx, dy, dz = CombatRules.vector(claim.Direction)
		if dx == nil then
			return reject(session, state, weaponId, "InvalidClaim", true, { Problem = "Direction" })
		end
		local length = math.sqrt((dx :: number) ^ 2 + (dy :: number) ^ 2 + (dz :: number) ^ 2)
		if length < 1e-6 then
			return reject(session, state, weaponId, "InvalidClaim", true, { Problem = "Direction" })
		end
		if state.projectiles[shotId] ~= nil then
			return reject(session, state, weaponId, "DuplicateShot", true)
		end

		local now = context.Clock.Now()
		local order = state.projectileOrder
		-- Drop expired and excess projectiles (oldest first).
		while #order > 0 do
			local oldest = state.projectiles[order[1]]
			local expired = oldest == nil
				or now - oldest.t > (definitions[oldest.weapon] :: Definition).ProjectileLifetime + 1
			if expired or #order >= context.Config.MaxPendingProjectiles then
				state.projectiles[order[1]] = nil
				table.remove(order, 1)
			else
				break
			end
		end
		state.projectiles[shotId] = {
			id = shotId,
			weapon = definition.Id,
			t = now,
			ox = shooter.ox,
			oy = shooter.oy,
			oz = shooter.oz,
			dx = (dx :: number) / length,
			dy = (dy :: number) / length,
			dz = (dz :: number) / length,
			pierceLeft = definition.Pierce,
			hitCounted = false,
		}
		table.insert(order, shotId)
		publishShot(session, definition, "Shot", nil)
		return { Allowed = true, ShotAccepted = true, Damage = 0, Headshot = false }
	end

	-- Validates a projectile impact reported after FireProjectile. Claim: { Target?, HitPosition? }.
	function module:ValidateProjectileHit(player: any, shotId: string, claim: Claim): AttackResult
		requireRunning("ValidateProjectileHit")
		if not running then
			return rejected("NotRunning", false)
		end
		local session = context.Players:GetSession(player)
		if session == nil then
			return rejected("NoSession", false)
		end
		local state = combatState(session)
		local projectile = if type(shotId) == "string" then state.projectiles[shotId] else nil
		if projectile == nil then
			return reject(session, state, nil, "UnknownShot", false)
		end
		if type(claim) ~= "table" then
			return reject(session, state, projectile.weapon, "InvalidClaim", true, { Problem = "claim must be a table" })
		end
		local definition = definitions[projectile.weapon] :: Definition
		local config = context.Config
		local now = context.Clock.Now()
		local elapsed = now - projectile.t

		-- Impact point: the claimed hit position, else the target part's position.
		local hx, hy, hz
		if claim.HitPosition ~= nil then
			hx, hy, hz = CombatRules.vector(claim.HitPosition)
		elseif claim.Target ~= nil and context.Platform.Physics and context.Platform.Physics.Describe then
			local ok, info = pcall(context.Platform.Physics.Describe, context.Platform.Physics, claim.Target)
			if ok and info then
				hx, hy, hz = info.X, info.Y, info.Z
			end
		end
		if hx == nil then
			return reject(session, state, projectile.weapon, "InvalidClaim", true, { Problem = "impact position" })
		end
		local ix, iy, iz = hx :: number, hy :: number, hz :: number

		if elapsed > definition.ProjectileLifetime + config.ProjectileTimingToleranceSeconds then
			state.projectiles[shotId] = nil
			return reject(session, state, projectile.weapon, "ProjectileExpired", true, { Elapsed = round(elapsed) })
		end

		local sample = nil
		local physics = context.Platform.Physics
		if session.Character and physics then
			local ok, result = pcall(physics.Sample, physics, session.Character, session.Player)
			sample = if ok then result else nil
		end
		local ping = if sample then pingCompensation(sample) else 0
		local speed = definition.ProjectileSpeed :: number
		local travel = CombatRules.distance(projectile.ox, projectile.oy, projectile.oz, ix, iy, iz)
		local allowedDeviation = config.ProjectileTrajectoryToleranceStuds + travel * config.ProjectileTrajectoryToleranceFraction
		-- Where along the real (possibly arcing) path does it first come within tolerance?
		local arrival, deviation = CombatRules.closestApproach(
			projectile.ox,
			projectile.oy,
			projectile.oz,
			projectile.dx,
			projectile.dy,
			projectile.dz,
			speed,
			definition.ProjectileGravity,
			ix,
			iy,
			iz,
			definition.ProjectileLifetime,
			allowedDeviation
		)
		if arrival == nil then
			return reject(session, state, projectile.weapon, "OffTrajectory", true, {
				Deviation = round(deviation),
				Allowed = round(allowedDeviation),
			})
		end
		if elapsed + ping + config.ProjectileTimingToleranceSeconds < (arrival :: number) then
			return reject(session, state, projectile.weapon, "ProjectileTiming", true, {
				Elapsed = round(elapsed),
				EarliestArrival = round(arrival :: number),
			})
		end

		local shooter: Shooter = {
			session = session,
			state = state,
			definition = definition,
			ws = weaponState(state, definition, now),
			sample = sample or { Ping = 0 },
			ox = projectile.ox,
			oy = projectile.oy,
			oz = projectile.oz,
		}
		local result = evaluateHit(shooter, claim, definition.ProjectileGravity == 0)
		if result.Allowed and result.Target ~= nil and result.Target.Model ~= nil and not projectile.hitCounted then
			projectile.hitCounted = true
			publishShot(session, definition, "Hit", result)
		end
		if result.Allowed and result.Target ~= nil then
			if projectile.pierceLeft > 0 then
				projectile.pierceLeft -= 1
			else
				state.projectiles[shotId] = nil
				local index = table.find(state.projectileOrder, shotId)
				if index then
					table.remove(state.projectileOrder, index)
				end
			end
		end
		return result
	end

	-- Starts a server-side reload. Returns (true) or (false, reason).
	function module:Reload(player: any, weaponId: string): (boolean, string?)
		requireRunning("Reload")
		local session = context.Players:GetSession(player)
		local definition = definitions[weaponId]
		if session == nil or definition == nil or definition.Ammo == nil then
			return false, "not reloadable"
		end
		local ammo = definition.Ammo :: CombatRules.Ammo
		local now = context.Clock.Now()
		local ws = weaponState(combatState(session), definition, now)
		if ws.reloadUntil ~= nil then
			return false, "already reloading"
		end
		if ws.magazine >= ammo.Magazine then
			return false, "magazine full"
		end
		if ws.reserve <= 0 then
			return false, "no reserve ammo"
		end
		if ammo.ReloadSeconds <= 0 then
			finishReload(ws, definition)
		else
			ws.reloadUntil = now + ammo.ReloadSeconds
		end
		return true
	end

	-- Returns magazine, reserve, reloading (server truth).
	function module:GetAmmo(player: any, weaponId: string): (number?, number?, boolean)
		requireRunning("GetAmmo")
		local session = context.Players:GetSession(player)
		local definition = definitions[weaponId]
		if session == nil or definition == nil then
			return nil, nil, false
		end
		local ws = weaponState(combatState(session), definition, context.Clock.Now())
		return ws.magazine, ws.reserve, ws.reloadUntil ~= nil
	end

	-- Server-side ammo grant / sync (pickups, loadouts).
	function module:SetAmmo(player: any, weaponId: string, magazine: number, reserve: number?)
		requireRunning("SetAmmo")
		local definition = definitions[weaponId]
		if definition == nil or definition.Ammo == nil then
			error(`ACF Combat: weapon '{tostring(weaponId)}' has no Ammo definition`, 2)
		end
		local ammo = definition.Ammo :: CombatRules.Ammo
		if type(magazine) ~= "number" or magazine < 0 or magazine > ammo.Magazine or magazine ~= math.floor(magazine) then
			error(`ACF Combat: magazine must be an integer in [0, {ammo.Magazine}]`, 2)
		end
		if reserve ~= nil and (type(reserve) ~= "number" or reserve ~= reserve or reserve < 0) then
			error("ACF Combat: reserve must be a number >= 0", 2)
		end
		local session = context.Players:GetSession(player)
		if session == nil then
			return false
		end
		local ws = weaponState(combatState(session), definition, context.Clock.Now())
		ws.magazine = magazine
		if reserve ~= nil then
			ws.reserve = reserve
		end
		ws.reloadUntil = nil
		return true
	end

	function module:GetStats(): { [string]: any }
		local weapons = {}
		for id, entry in stats do
			weapons[id] = { Shots = entry.Shots, Hits = entry.Hits, Rejected = table.clone(entry.Rejected) }
		end
		return { Weapons = weapons, Rejected = table.clone(globalRejected) }
	end

	-- Lifecycle -----------------------------------------------------------------------

	function module:Initialize(ctx)
		context = ctx
		shotTopic = ctx.Events:Declare("Shot")
	end

	function module:Start()
		running = true
	end

	function module:Stop()
		running = false
	end

	function module:Destroy()
		running = false
		context = nil
	end

	return module
end

return Combat

```

`src/Server/Detection/Combat/CombatRules.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Pure combat rules: weapon/ability definitions, damage, and geometry. No Roblox APIs.

	A definition describes what a weapon or ability is allowed to do. The Combat module
	enforces it; the client never supplies damage, rate, range or ammo.

		Kind = "Hitscan" | "Projectile" | "Melee" | "Ability"
		Damage            number >= 0 (base damage per hit)
		HeadshotMultiplier number >= 1 (default 1)
		Falloff           { Start, End, MinMultiplier } (optional; linear between Start and End)
		FireRate          shots per second (optional for Ability/Melee; required for Hitscan/Projectile)
		Burst             shots allowed back-to-back above FireRate (default 1, i.e. no burst)
		Cooldown          seconds between uses (default 0)
		MaxRange          studs (required)
		Ammo              { Magazine, Reserve (number or math.huge), ReloadSeconds } (optional = infinite)
		ProjectileSpeed   studs/s (required for Projectile)
		ProjectileGravity studs/s^2 downward (default 0)
		ProjectileLifetime seconds (default 5)
		Pierce            extra targets one projectile may hit (default 0)
		RequireLineOfSight boolean (default true)
		RequireTarget     boolean (default false for Ability, true for Melee, false otherwise)
]]

export type Falloff = { Start: number, End: number, MinMultiplier: number }
export type Ammo = { Magazine: number, Reserve: number, ReloadSeconds: number }

export type Definition = {
	Id: string,
	Kind: "Hitscan" | "Projectile" | "Melee" | "Ability",
	Damage: number,
	HeadshotMultiplier: number,
	Falloff: Falloff?,
	FireRate: number?,
	Burst: number,
	Cooldown: number,
	MaxRange: number,
	Ammo: Ammo?,
	ProjectileSpeed: number?,
	ProjectileGravity: number,
	ProjectileLifetime: number,
	Pierce: number,
	RequireLineOfSight: boolean,
	RequireTarget: boolean,
}

local KINDS = { Hitscan = true, Projectile = true, Melee = true, Ability = true }
local FIELDS = {
	Kind = true,
	Damage = true,
	HeadshotMultiplier = true,
	Falloff = true,
	FireRate = true,
	Burst = true,
	Cooldown = true,
	MaxRange = true,
	Ammo = true,
	ProjectileSpeed = true,
	ProjectileGravity = true,
	ProjectileLifetime = true,
	Pierce = true,
	RequireLineOfSight = true,
	RequireTarget = true,
}

local CombatRules = {}

local function finite(value: any): boolean
	return type(value) == "number" and value == value and value ~= math.huge and value ~= -math.huge
end

-- Validates a definition. Raises at the caller's caller with a precise message.
function CombatRules.normalize(id: any, raw: any): Definition
	local function fail(message: string): never
		error(`ACF Combat: '{tostring(id)}': {message}`, 4)
	end
	if type(id) ~= "string" or #id == 0 or #id > 64 then
		error("ACF Combat: weapon id must be a non-empty string of at most 64 characters", 4)
	end
	if type(raw) ~= "table" then
		fail("definition must be a table")
	end
	for key in raw do
		if not FIELDS[key] then
			fail(`unknown field '{tostring(key)}'`)
		end
	end
	local kind = raw.Kind
	if not KINDS[kind] then
		fail("Kind must be Hitscan, Projectile, Melee or Ability")
	end
	local function number(name: string, default: number?, min: number, allowHuge: boolean?): number
		local value = raw[name]
		if value == nil then
			if default == nil then
				fail(`{name} is required`)
			end
			return default :: number
		end
		local ok = if allowHuge then (type(value) == "number" and value == value) else finite(value)
		if not ok or value < min then
			fail(`{name} must be a number >= {min}`)
		end
		return value
	end

	local damage = number("Damage", 0, 0)
	local maxRange = number("MaxRange", nil, 0.01)
	local fireRate: number? = nil
	if raw.FireRate ~= nil or kind == "Hitscan" or kind == "Projectile" then
		fireRate = number("FireRate", nil, 0.001)
	end
	local burst = number("Burst", 1, 1)
	if burst ~= math.floor(burst) then
		fail("Burst must be an integer")
	end

	local falloff: Falloff? = nil
	if raw.Falloff ~= nil then
		local f = raw.Falloff
		if type(f) ~= "table" or not finite(f.Start) or not finite(f.End) or not finite(f.MinMultiplier) then
			fail("Falloff must be { Start, End, MinMultiplier }")
		end
		if f.Start < 0 or f.End <= f.Start or f.MinMultiplier < 0 or f.MinMultiplier > 1 then
			fail("Falloff needs 0 <= Start < End and 0 <= MinMultiplier <= 1")
		end
		falloff = table.freeze({ Start = f.Start, End = f.End, MinMultiplier = f.MinMultiplier })
	end

	local ammo: Ammo? = nil
	if raw.Ammo ~= nil then
		local a = raw.Ammo
		if type(a) ~= "table" then
			fail("Ammo must be { Magazine, Reserve, ReloadSeconds }")
		end
		local magazine = a.Magazine
		if not finite(magazine) or magazine < 1 or magazine ~= math.floor(magazine) then
			fail("Ammo.Magazine must be an integer >= 1")
		end
		local reserve = if a.Reserve == nil then math.huge else a.Reserve
		if type(reserve) ~= "number" or reserve ~= reserve or reserve < 0 then
			fail("Ammo.Reserve must be a number >= 0 (math.huge for unlimited)")
		end
		local reload = if a.ReloadSeconds == nil then 0 else a.ReloadSeconds
		if not finite(reload) or reload < 0 then
			fail("Ammo.ReloadSeconds must be a number >= 0")
		end
		ammo = table.freeze({ Magazine = magazine, Reserve = reserve, ReloadSeconds = reload })
	end

	local projectileSpeed: number? = nil
	if kind == "Projectile" then
		projectileSpeed = number("ProjectileSpeed", nil, 0.01)
	elseif raw.ProjectileSpeed ~= nil then
		fail("ProjectileSpeed only applies to Kind = Projectile")
	end

	local pierce = number("Pierce", 0, 0)
	if pierce ~= math.floor(pierce) then
		fail("Pierce must be an integer")
	end

	local function boolean(name: string, default: boolean): boolean
		local value = raw[name]
		if value == nil then
			return default
		end
		if type(value) ~= "boolean" then
			fail(`{name} must be a boolean`)
		end
		return value
	end

	return table.freeze({
		Id = id,
		Kind = kind,
		Damage = damage,
		HeadshotMultiplier = number("HeadshotMultiplier", 1, 1),
		Falloff = falloff,
		FireRate = fireRate,
		Burst = burst,
		Cooldown = number("Cooldown", 0, 0),
		MaxRange = maxRange,
		Ammo = ammo,
		ProjectileSpeed = projectileSpeed,
		ProjectileGravity = number("ProjectileGravity", 0, 0),
		ProjectileLifetime = number("ProjectileLifetime", 5, 0.01),
		Pierce = pierce,
		RequireLineOfSight = boolean("RequireLineOfSight", true),
		RequireTarget = boolean("RequireTarget", kind == "Melee"),
	}) :: any
end

-- Server-computed damage for a hit at `distance`.
function CombatRules.damage(definition: Definition, distance: number, headshot: boolean): number
	local multiplier = 1
	local falloff = definition.Falloff
	if falloff then
		if distance >= falloff.End then
			multiplier = falloff.MinMultiplier
		elseif distance > falloff.Start then
			local t = (distance - falloff.Start) / (falloff.End - falloff.Start)
			multiplier = 1 - t * (1 - falloff.MinMultiplier)
		end
	end
	if headshot then
		multiplier *= definition.HeadshotMultiplier
	end
	return definition.Damage * multiplier
end

function CombatRules.distance(ax: number, ay: number, az: number, bx: number, by: number, bz: number): number
	local dx, dy, dz = bx - ax, by - ay, bz - az
	return math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)
end

-- Where a projectile fired from origin along unit direction (dx,dy,dz) at `speed`,
-- with downward `gravity`, is after `t` seconds.
function CombatRules.projectilePosition(
	ox: number,
	oy: number,
	oz: number,
	dx: number,
	dy: number,
	dz: number,
	speed: number,
	gravity: number,
	t: number
): (number, number, number)
	return ox + dx * speed * t, oy + dy * speed * t - 0.5 * gravity * t * t, oz + dz * speed * t
end

-- Earliest flight time at which the projectile passes within `tolerance` of point h,
-- searched over [0, maxTime]. Returns (time, deviation) for that moment, or
-- (nil, minimumDeviation) if the path never comes within tolerance. Handles arcs:
-- a lobbed shot that passes near h on the way up is matched on that first pass.
function CombatRules.closestApproach(
	ox: number,
	oy: number,
	oz: number,
	dx: number,
	dy: number,
	dz: number,
	speed: number,
	gravity: number,
	hx: number,
	hy: number,
	hz: number,
	maxTime: number,
	tolerance: number
): (number?, number)
	local function deviationAt(t: number): number
		local px, py, pz = CombatRules.projectilePosition(ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz, speed, gravity, t)
		return CombatRules.distance(px, py, pz, hx, hy, hz)
	end
	local steps = 96
	local step = maxTime / steps
	local minimum = math.huge
	for i = 0, steps do
		local t = i * step
		local deviation = deviationAt(t)
		minimum = math.min(minimum, deviation)
		if deviation <= tolerance then
			-- Refine the first entry into tolerance by bisection on [t - step, t].
			local lo, hi = math.max(0, t - step), t
			for _ = 1, 24 do
				local mid = (lo + hi) / 2
				if deviationAt(mid) <= tolerance then
					hi = mid
				else
					lo = mid
				end
			end
			return hi, deviationAt(hi)
		end
	end
	return nil, minimum
end

-- Reads a Vector3-like claim value as finite numbers, or nil if it is not one.
function CombatRules.vector(value: any): (number?, number?, number?)
	if typeof(value) ~= "Vector3" then
		return nil, nil, nil
	end
	local x, y, z = value.X, value.Y, value.Z
	if not (finite(x) and finite(y) and finite(z)) then
		return nil, nil, nil
	end
	return x, y, z
end

return CombatRules

```

`src/Server/Detection/DetectionManager.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Detection ingestion pipeline.

	Modules call context:Report(target, input). The context binds the detector's name
	and category, so a module cannot report under another module's identity. This
	manager then:

	  1. checks the reporting module is Running,
	  2. resolves the target to an active session,
	  3. validates the input schema (Kind, Severity 0..100, Confidence 0..1),
	  4. applies a per-(session, detector) token bucket so a misbehaving detector cannot
	     exhaust memory or CPU,
	  5. sanitises Details (string keys, bounded size and depth, Instances converted to
	     their full names so destroyed parts are not kept alive),
	  6. builds a frozen DetectionEvent, records it as evidence, and publishes
	     DetectionReported.

	Rejections return (false, reason) where reason is one of the REASONS values; they are
	never thrown.
]]

local EventBus = require(script.Parent.Parent.Core.EventBus)
local Logger = require(script.Parent.Parent.Core.Logger)
local PlayerRegistry = require(script.Parent.Parent.Core.PlayerRegistry)
local RateLimiter = require(script.Parent.Parent.Core.RateLimiter)
local Topics = require(script.Parent.Parent.Core.Topics)
local EvidenceManager = require(script.Parent.Parent.Intelligence.EvidenceManager)
local Types = require(script.Parent.Parent.Types)

type DetectionEvent = Types.DetectionEvent

local REASONS = table.freeze({
	NotRunning = "module_not_running",
	NoSession = "no_session",
	InvalidInput = "invalid_input",
	RateLimited = "rate_limited",
})

local MAX_KIND_LENGTH = 64
local MAX_KEY_LENGTH = 64
local MAX_DETAIL_DEPTH = 2
local LIMITER_STATE_KEY = "ACF.DetectionLimiter"

local PRIMITIVE_TYPES = {
	number = true,
	boolean = true,
	Vector3 = true,
	Vector2 = true,
	CFrame = true,
	EnumItem = true,
	Color3 = true,
	UDim2 = true,
}

export type DetectionSettings = {
	ReportsPerSecond: number,
	ReportBurst: number,
	MaxDetailKeys: number,
	MaxDetailStringLength: number,
}

export type ReportSource = {
	Name: string,
	Category: Types.DetectionCategory,
	State: Types.ModuleState,
}

local DetectionManager = {}
DetectionManager.__index = DetectionManager
DetectionManager.Reasons = REASONS

export type DetectionManager = typeof(setmetatable(
	{} :: {
		_registry: PlayerRegistry.PlayerRegistry,
		_evidence: EvidenceManager.EvidenceManager,
		_bus: EventBus.EventBus,
		_logger: Logger.Logger,
		_clock: Types.Clock,
		_generateId: () -> string,
		_settings: DetectionSettings,
		_accepted: number,
		_rejected: { [string]: number },
	},
	DetectionManager
))

export type DetectionManagerOptions = {
	Registry: PlayerRegistry.PlayerRegistry,
	Evidence: EvidenceManager.EvidenceManager,
	Bus: EventBus.EventBus,
	Logger: Logger.Logger,
	Clock: Types.Clock,
	GenerateId: () -> string,
	Settings: DetectionSettings,
}

function DetectionManager.new(options: DetectionManagerOptions): DetectionManager
	return setmetatable({
		_registry = options.Registry,
		_evidence = options.Evidence,
		_bus = options.Bus,
		_logger = options.Logger,
		_clock = options.Clock,
		_generateId = options.GenerateId,
		_settings = options.Settings,
		_accepted = 0,
		_rejected = {},
	}, DetectionManager)
end

local function isFinite(value: any): boolean
	return type(value) == "number" and value == value and value ~= math.huge and value ~= -math.huge
end

local sanitize: (value: any, depth: number, settings: DetectionSettings) -> any

local function sanitizeTable(input: { [any]: any }, depth: number, settings: DetectionSettings): { [string]: any }
	local output = {}
	local keys = 0
	for key, value in input do
		if keys >= settings.MaxDetailKeys then
			output["_truncated"] = true
			break
		end
		local keyString = if type(key) == "string" then key elseif type(key) == "number" then tostring(key) else nil
		if keyString and #keyString <= MAX_KEY_LENGTH then
			local clean = sanitize(value, depth, settings)
			if clean ~= nil then
				output[keyString] = clean
				keys += 1
			end
		end
	end
	return table.freeze(output)
end

sanitize = function(value: any, depth: number, settings: DetectionSettings): any
	local kind = typeof(value)
	if kind == "string" then
		if #value > settings.MaxDetailStringLength then
			return string.sub(value, 1, settings.MaxDetailStringLength) .. "..."
		end
		return value
	elseif kind == "number" then
		return if isFinite(value) then value else tostring(value)
	elseif PRIMITIVE_TYPES[kind] then
		return value
	elseif kind == "Instance" then
		local ok, name = pcall(function()
			return value:GetFullName()
		end)
		return if ok then name else "<Instance>"
	elseif kind == "table" then
		if depth >= MAX_DETAIL_DEPTH then
			return "<table>"
		end
		return sanitizeTable(value, depth + 1, settings)
	end
	return nil -- functions, threads, userdata: dropped
end

local function validateInput(input: any): string?
	if type(input) ~= "table" then
		return "input must be a table"
	end
	local kind = input.Kind
	if type(kind) ~= "string" or #kind == 0 or #kind > MAX_KIND_LENGTH then
		return `Kind must be a non-empty string of at most {MAX_KIND_LENGTH} characters`
	end
	if not isFinite(input.Severity) or input.Severity < 0 or input.Severity > 100 then
		return "Severity must be a number between 0 and 100"
	end
	if not isFinite(input.Confidence) or input.Confidence < 0 or input.Confidence > 1 then
		return "Confidence must be a number between 0 and 1"
	end
	if input.Details ~= nil and type(input.Details) ~= "table" then
		return "Details must be a table when provided"
	end
	return nil
end

local function reject(self: DetectionManager, reason: string, source: ReportSource, extra: string?): (boolean, string)
	self._rejected[reason] = (self._rejected[reason] or 0) + 1
	if reason == REASONS.InvalidInput then
		-- Invalid input is a module bug, not player behaviour: make it visible.
		self._logger:Warn("Detection report rejected: invalid input", { Detector = source.Name, Problem = extra })
	elseif reason == REASONS.RateLimited then
		self._logger:Warn("Detection reports rate limited", { Detector = source.Name })
	else
		self._logger:Debug("Detection report rejected", { Detector = source.Name, Reason = reason })
	end
	return false, if extra then `{reason}: {extra}` else reason
end

function DetectionManager.Report(self: DetectionManager, source: ReportSource, target: any, input: Types.DetectionInput): (boolean, string)
	if source.State ~= "Running" then
		return reject(self, REASONS.NotRunning, source)
	end
	local problem = validateInput(input)
	if problem then
		return reject(self, REASONS.InvalidInput, source, problem)
	end
	local session = self._registry:GetSession(target)
	if session == nil then
		return reject(self, REASONS.NoSession, source)
	end

	local settings = self._settings
	local limiter = session:GetState(LIMITER_STATE_KEY, function()
		return RateLimiter.new(settings.ReportBurst, settings.ReportsPerSecond, self._clock.Now, 256)
	end)
	if not limiter:Allow(source.Name) then
		return reject(self, REASONS.RateLimited, source)
	end

	local details = if input.Details then sanitizeTable(input.Details, 1, settings) else table.freeze({})
	local event: DetectionEvent = table.freeze({
		Id = self._generateId(),
		Detector = source.Name,
		Category = source.Category,
		Kind = input.Kind,
		UserId = session.UserId,
		SessionId = session.SessionId,
		Severity = input.Severity,
		Confidence = input.Confidence,
		Time = self._clock.Now(),
		WallTime = self._clock.Wall(),
		Details = details,
	})

	local record, isNew = self._evidence:Record(event)
	self._accepted += 1
	self._logger:Debug("Detection recorded", {
		Detector = event.Detector,
		Kind = event.Kind,
		UserId = event.UserId,
		Severity = event.Severity,
		Confidence = event.Confidence,
	})
	self._bus:Publish(Topics.DetectionReported, table.freeze({ Event = event, Record = record, IsNewRecord = isNew }))
	return true, event.Id
end

function DetectionManager.GetStats(self: DetectionManager): { Accepted: number, Rejected: { [string]: number } }
	return { Accepted = self._accepted, Rejected = table.clone(self._rejected) }
end

return DetectionManager

```

`src/Server/Detection/Movement/Movement.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Movement: server-observed movement validation (built-in, Category "Movement").

	Every SampleIntervalSeconds the module samples each character through
	Environment.Physics (root position/velocity, humanoid state, ground clearance) and
	runs MovementDetectors. Violations feed a per-player ViolationTracker; only sustained
	evidence is reported. A single unusual position update never is.

	Legitimate movement handled explicitly:
	  - Respawn / character replacement: state resets whenever CharacterGeneration changes.
	  - Seats and vehicles: detectors pause while seated; history resets on leaving.
	  - Death: detectors pause; history resets.
	  - Climbing and swimming: exempt from flight / air-jump checks.
	  - Falling: speed is horizontal; downward motion is never a violation.
	  - Server WalkSpeed changes: the allowed speed follows the server-side WalkSpeed.
	  - Latency: ping compensation plus "time credit" after freezes (see MovementDetectors).
	  - Game mechanics: AuthorizeTeleport(player) and Allow(player, kind, seconds) for
	    teleports, dashes/speed boosts, jetpacks, knockback and scripted noclip.

	Each sample is published on "Movement.Sample" for dependent modules (Physics).
]]

local Configuration = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Core.Configuration)
local ViolationTracker = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Intelligence.ViolationTracker)
local MovementDetectors = require(script.Parent.MovementDetectors)

local S = Configuration.Schema

local MODULE_NAME = "Movement"
local STATE_KEY = "Movement.State"

local DETECTORS = { "Speed", "Teleport", "Flight", "InfiniteJump", "Acceleration", "Noclip" }
local ALLOWANCE_KINDS = { Teleport = true, Speed = true, Flight = true, Noclip = true, Impulse = true, All = true }

local function flags(default: boolean)
	local fields = {}
	for _, name in DETECTORS do
		fields[name] = S.Boolean(default)
	end
	return S.Table(fields)
end

local CONFIG_SCHEMA = S.Table({
	SampleIntervalSeconds = S.Number(0.2, { Min = 0.05, Max = 2 }),
	MinAllowedSpeed = S.Number(8, { Min = 0, Max = 1000 }),
	SpeedTolerance = S.Number(1.25, { Min = 1, Max = 10 }),
	SpeedSlackStuds = S.Number(4, { Min = 0, Max = 1000 }),
	MinWindowSeconds = S.Number(0.5, { Min = 0.1, Max = 10 }),
	MaxWindowSeconds = S.Number(2, { Min = 0.2, Max = 30 }),
	MaxPingCompensationSeconds = S.Number(0.5, { Min = 0, Max = 5 }),
	TeleportSlackStuds = S.Number(10, { Min = 0, Max = 10000 }),
	TeleportMinDistance = S.Number(20, { Min = 1, Max = 100000 }),
	VerticalSlackStuds = S.Number(8, { Min = 0, Max = 10000 }),
	MaxVerticalPlatformSpeed = S.Number(25, { Min = 0, Max = 10000 }),
	AirborneClearance = S.Number(1.5, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
	MaxAirtimeExtraSeconds = S.Number(1, { Min = 0, Max = 60 }),
	MinFallStudsPerSecond = S.Number(4, { Min = 0, Max = 1000 }),
	-- Airborne samples whose position is frozen while the reported velocity is at least
	-- StallMinVelocity are replication stalls (lag mid-jump); up to this much stalled
	-- time per airborne period is not counted as airtime.
	MaxStallCreditSeconds = S.Number(3, { Min = 0, Max = 60 }),
	-- Longest stationary period credited as lag when a player then moves (see
	-- MovementDetectors, "time credit").
	MaxTimeCreditSeconds = S.Number(3, { Min = 0.2, Max = 60 }),
	StallMinVelocity = S.Number(1, { Min = 0, Max = 1000 }),
	MaxAirJumps = S.Number(0, { Min = 0, Max = 100, Integer = true }),
	AirJumpRiseStuds = S.Number(3, { Min = 0.5, Max = 1000 }),
	MaxHorizontalAcceleration = S.Number(600, { Min = 1, Max = 100000 }),
	NoclipMinDistance = S.Number(1, { Min = 0.1, Max = 1000 }),
	NoclipMinThickness = S.Number(0.5, { Min = 0.01, Max = 1000 }),
	Detectors = flags(true),
	Severity = S.Table({
		Speed = S.Number(25, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
		Teleport = S.Number(30, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
		Flight = S.Number(30, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
		InfiniteJump = S.Number(25, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
		Acceleration = S.Number(10, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
		Noclip = S.Number(30, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
	}),
	Violation = S.Table({
		HalfLifeSeconds = S.Number(10, { Min = 0.5, Max = 3600 }),
		ReportThreshold = S.Number(5, { Min = 0.1, Max = 10000 }),
		ReportIntervalSeconds = S.Number(10, { Min = 0, Max = 3600 }),
		ConfidenceScale = S.Number(8, { Min = 0.1, Max = 10000 }),
	}),
})

type SessionState = {
	detectors: MovementDetectors.State,
	tracker: ViolationTracker.ViolationTracker,
	allowances: { [string]: number },
	speedMultiplier: { untilTime: number, value: number }?,
	paused: boolean,
	-- Last grounded position with no violations: the correction anchor (Phase 7).
	lastGood: { X: number, Y: number, Z: number, Generation: number }?,
}

-- Violations that server-side correction may undo.
local CORRECTABLE = { Speed = true, Teleport = true, Flight = true, Noclip = true }

local Movement = {}
Movement.Name = MODULE_NAME
Movement.ConfigSchema = CONFIG_SCHEMA

function Movement.new()
	local module: any = {
		Name = MODULE_NAME,
		Version = "1.0.0",
		Category = "Movement",
		EnabledByDefault = true,
		ConfigSchema = CONFIG_SCHEMA,
	}

	local context: any = nil
	local sampleTopic: string? = nil
	local stats = { Samples = 0, Violations = {} :: { [string]: number }, Reports = 0 }

	local function stateFor(session: any): SessionState
		return session:GetState(STATE_KEY, function()
			return {
				detectors = MovementDetectors.newState(session.CharacterGeneration),
				tracker = ViolationTracker.new(context.Config.Violation),
				allowances = {},
				speedMultiplier = nil,
				paused = false,
				lastGood = nil,
			}
		end)
	end

	local function activeAllowances(state: SessionState, now: number): MovementDetectors.Allowances
		local a = state.allowances
		local all = (a.All or 0) > now
		local multiplier = 1
		local boost = state.speedMultiplier
		-- The boost keeps covering the speed window after it expires, so the window that
		-- still contains the fast samples is not judged at normal speed.
		if boost and boost.untilTime + context.Config.MaxWindowSeconds > now then
			multiplier = boost.value
		end
		if all then
			multiplier = math.max(multiplier, 1e6)
		end
		return {
			Teleport = all or (a.Teleport or 0) > now,
			Flight = all or (a.Flight or 0) > now,
			Noclip = all or (a.Noclip or 0) > now,
			Impulse = all or (a.Impulse or 0) > now,
			SpeedMultiplier = multiplier,
		}
	end

	local function sampleSession(session: any, physics: any, now: number)
		local character = session.Character
		if character == nil then
			return
		end
		local ok, sample = pcall(physics.Sample, physics, character, session.Player)
		if not ok then
			context.Logger:Warn("Character sampling failed", { UserId = session.UserId, Error = tostring(sample) })
			return
		end
		if sample == nil then
			return
		end

		local state = stateFor(session)
		if state.detectors.generation ~= session.CharacterGeneration then
			state.detectors = MovementDetectors.newState(session.CharacterGeneration)
		end
		stats.Samples += 1

		-- Pause (and forget history) while seated or dead; resume fresh afterwards.
		if sample.Seated or sample.Health <= 0 or sample.State == "Dead" then
			state.paused = true
			return
		end
		if state.paused then
			state.paused = false
			state.detectors = MovementDetectors.newState(session.CharacterGeneration)
		end

		local allowances = activeAllowances(state, now)
		local function cast(ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz)
			return physics:Cast(ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz, character)
		end
		local result = MovementDetectors.evaluate(state.detectors, sample, now, context.Config, allowances, cast)

		for _, violation in result.Violations do
			stats.Violations[violation.Detector] = (stats.Violations[violation.Detector] or 0) + 1
			local report, score, confidence = state.tracker:Record(violation.Detector, violation.Weight, now)
			if report then
				stats.Reports += 1
				local details = table.clone(violation.Details)
				details.Score = math.floor(score * 100) / 100
				details.Generation = session.CharacterGeneration
				context:Report(session, {
					Kind = violation.Kind,
					Severity = context.Config.Severity[violation.Detector],
					Confidence = confidence,
					Details = details,
				})
			end
		end

		-- Enforcement level 3 (Phase 7): rubber-band a Movement-restricted player to the
		-- last grounded position that produced no violation. Rate-limited and distance-
		-- capped by Enforcement.Correction; the correction itself is authorised so it is
		-- never scored as a teleport.
		local anchor = state.lastGood
		if #result.Violations == 0 then
			if sample.GroundClearance <= 0.5 then
				state.lastGood = { X = sample.X, Y = sample.Y, Z = sample.Z, Generation = session.CharacterGeneration }
			end
		elseif anchor and anchor.Generation == session.CharacterGeneration and physics.Correct then
			local correctable = false
			for _, violation in result.Violations do
				if CORRECTABLE[violation.Detector] then
					correctable = true
					break
				end
			end
			local distance = math.sqrt((sample.X - anchor.X) ^ 2 + (sample.Y - anchor.Y) ^ 2 + (sample.Z - anchor.Z) ^ 2)
			if
				correctable
				and distance <= context:GetConfig("Enforcement.Correction.MaxDistance")
				and context.Enforcement:ShouldCorrect(session)
			then
				local corrected, problem = pcall(physics.Correct, physics, character, anchor.X, anchor.Y, anchor.Z)
				if corrected then
					state.allowances.Teleport = math.max(state.allowances.Teleport or 0, now + 1)
					state.detectors = MovementDetectors.newState(session.CharacterGeneration)
					context.Enforcement:NoteCorrected(session, {
						Distance = math.floor(distance * 10) / 10,
						Violations = #result.Violations,
					})
				else
					context.Logger:Warn("Movement correction failed", { UserId = session.UserId, Error = tostring(problem) })
				end
			end
		end

		if sampleTopic then
			context.Events:Publish(sampleTopic, {
				Session = session,
				Sample = sample,
				Time = now,
				ObservedSpeed = result.ObservedSpeed,
				AllowedSpeed = result.AllowedSpeed,
				Airborne = result.Airborne,
				Allowances = allowances,
			})
		end
	end

	local function tick(now: number)
		local physics = context.Platform.Physics
		for _, session in context.Players:GetSessions() do
			sampleSession(session, physics, now)
		end
	end

	-- Public surface ------------------------------------------------------------------

	-- Grants a temporary movement allowance for game mechanics.
	-- kind: "Teleport" | "Speed" | "Flight" | "Noclip" | "Impulse" | "All".
	-- For "Speed", options.Multiplier scales the allowed speed (default 2).
	function module:Allow(target: any, kind: string, seconds: number, options: { Multiplier: number? }?)
		if context == nil then
			error("ACF Movement: Allow requires ACF to be started", 2)
		end
		if not ALLOWANCE_KINDS[kind] then
			error(`ACF Movement: unknown allowance kind '{tostring(kind)}'`, 2)
		end
		if type(seconds) ~= "number" or seconds <= 0 or seconds ~= seconds or seconds > 3600 then
			error("ACF Movement: seconds must be a number in (0, 3600]", 2)
		end
		local session = context.Players:GetSession(target)
		if session == nil then
			return false
		end
		local state = stateFor(session)
		local untilTime = context.Clock.Now() + seconds
		if kind == "Speed" then
			local multiplier = (options and options.Multiplier) or 2
			if type(multiplier) ~= "number" or multiplier < 1 or multiplier ~= multiplier then
				error("ACF Movement: Multiplier must be >= 1", 2)
			end
			state.speedMultiplier = { untilTime = untilTime, value = multiplier }
		else
			state.allowances[kind] = math.max(state.allowances[kind] or 0, untilTime)
		end
		return true
	end

	-- Call right before moving a character on the server (PivotTo, spawn points, portals).
	function module:AuthorizeTeleport(target: any, seconds: number?)
		return self:Allow(target, "Teleport", seconds or 1)
	end

	function module:GetStats(): { [string]: any }
		return { Samples = stats.Samples, Violations = table.clone(stats.Violations), Reports = stats.Reports }
	end

	-- Lifecycle -----------------------------------------------------------------------

	function module:Initialize(ctx)
		context = ctx
		if ctx.Config.MaxWindowSeconds <= ctx.Config.MinWindowSeconds then
			error("ModuleSettings.Movement.MaxWindowSeconds must be greater than MinWindowSeconds")
		end
		sampleTopic = ctx.Events:Declare("Sample")
	end

	function module:Start()
		if context.Platform.Physics == nil then
			error("Movement requires Environment.Physics (character sampling)")
		end
		context.Scheduler:Every("Sample", context.Config.SampleIntervalSeconds, tick)
	end

	function module:Stop() end

	function module:Destroy()
		context = nil
	end

	return module
end

return Movement

```

`src/Server/Detection/Movement/MovementDetectors.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Pure movement detectors. No Roblox APIs: every input is a plain-number sample, so each
	detector is tested against recorded legitimate and malicious traces.

	evaluate() runs, in order:
	  Teleport      consecutive displacement beyond what the allowed speed covers in the
	                elapsed time (+ ping, + slack), or a rise above jump apex.
	  Speed         horizontal distance over a sliding window [MinWindow, MaxWindow].
	  Flight        airborne past the jump airtime while not falling.
	  InfiniteJump  rising again mid-air (after descending) more than MaxAirJumps times.
	  Acceleration  change in reported horizontal velocity per second.
	  Noclip        the segment between samples passes through solid geometry.

	Lag tolerance ("time credit"): the path only records *distinct* positions, and an
	unchanged position keeps its original timestamp. When a frozen client catches up,
	the displacement is measured against the time since it last moved, not against the
	sample interval, so a catch-up burst is not mistaken for speed or teleporting.
	The credit is capped at MaxTimeCreditSeconds: without the cap, a player who stood
	still for a minute could then move WalkSpeed x 60 studs in one sample unflagged
	(found in the Phase 8 Studio run). Freezes longer than the cap are rare, and a single
	resulting violation never reports on its own.

	Every detector returns violations with weights. None of them reports on its own;
	ViolationTracker decides when evidence is sustained.
]]

local Types = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Types)

type Sample = Types.MovementSample

export type Point = { t: number, x: number, y: number, z: number }

export type AirState = {
	since: number,
	peak: number,
	trough: number,
	falling: boolean,
	airJumps: number,
	history: { Point },
	-- Replication-stall credit (see air()): the previous airborne sample and the credit
	-- already granted in this airborne period.
	previous: { t: number, x: number, y: number, z: number }?,
	stallCredit: number,
}

export type State = {
	generation: number,
	path: { Point },
	last: { t: number, x: number, y: number, z: number, vx: number, vz: number }?,
	air: AirState?,
}

export type Violation = {
	Detector: string,
	Kind: string,
	Weight: number,
	Details: { [string]: any },
}

export type Allowances = {
	Teleport: boolean,
	Flight: boolean,
	Noclip: boolean,
	Impulse: boolean,
	SpeedMultiplier: number,
}

export type Settings = {
	MinAllowedSpeed: number,
	SpeedTolerance: number,
	SpeedSlackStuds: number,
	MinWindowSeconds: number,
	MaxWindowSeconds: number,
	MaxPingCompensationSeconds: number,
	TeleportSlackStuds: number,
	TeleportMinDistance: number,
	VerticalSlackStuds: number,
	MaxVerticalPlatformSpeed: number,
	AirborneClearance: number,
	MaxAirtimeExtraSeconds: number,
	MinFallStudsPerSecond: number,
	MaxStallCreditSeconds: number,
	MaxTimeCreditSeconds: number,
	StallMinVelocity: number,
	MaxAirJumps: number,
	AirJumpRiseStuds: number,
	MaxHorizontalAcceleration: number,
	NoclipMinDistance: number,
	NoclipMinThickness: number,
	Detectors: { [string]: boolean },
}

export type CastFn = (ox: number, oy: number, oz: number, dx: number, dy: number, dz: number) -> number?

export type Result = {
	Violations: { Violation },
	ObservedSpeed: number,
	AllowedSpeed: number,
	Airborne: boolean,
}

local MAX_PATH = 48
local MAX_AIR_HISTORY = 24
local SAME_POSITION = 0.05

local AIR_EXEMPT_STATES = { Climbing = true, Swimming = true, Seated = true }

local MovementDetectors = {}

function MovementDetectors.newState(generation: number): State
	return { generation = generation, path = {}, last = nil, air = nil }
end

local function horizontal(ax: number, az: number, bx: number, bz: number): number
	local dx, dz = bx - ax, bz - az
	return math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz)
end

local function round(value: number, places: number?): number
	local scale = 10 ^ (places or 2)
	return math.floor(value * scale + 0.5) / scale
end

function MovementDetectors.allowedSpeed(sample: Sample, settings: Settings, allowances: Allowances): number
	local ground = sample.GroundSpeed
	local platform = if type(ground) == "number" and ground == ground and ground > 0 then ground else 0
	return math.max(sample.WalkSpeed, settings.MinAllowedSpeed) * settings.SpeedTolerance * allowances.SpeedMultiplier
		+ platform * settings.SpeedTolerance
end

local function pingSeconds(sample: Sample, settings: Settings): number
	local ping = sample.Ping
	if ping ~= ping or ping < 0 then
		return 0
	end
	return math.min(ping, settings.MaxPingCompensationSeconds)
end

-- Appends a distinct position; an unchanged position keeps its old timestamp.
local function recordPoint(state: State, point: Point, settings: Settings)
	local path = state.path
	local previous = path[#path]
	if previous then
		local moved = math.abs(point.x - previous.x) + math.abs(point.y - previous.y) + math.abs(point.z - previous.z)
		if moved < SAME_POSITION then
			return
		end
	end
	table.insert(path, point)
	local cutoff = point.t - settings.MaxWindowSeconds
	-- Keep the newest point older than the window: it is the reference across a gap.
	while #path > 2 and path[2].t < cutoff do
		table.remove(path, 1)
	end
	while #path > MAX_PATH do
		table.remove(path, 1)
	end
end

function MovementDetectors.resetPath(state: State, point: Point)
	table.clear(state.path)
	table.insert(state.path, point)
end

-- Oldest recorded point aged within [MinWindow, MaxWindow]; if none, the youngest point
-- older than MaxWindow (the position before a gap).
local function speedReference(state: State, now: number, settings: Settings): Point?
	local reference: Point? = nil
	local path = state.path
	for i = #path, 1, -1 do
		local point = path[i]
		local age = now - point.t
		if age >= settings.MinWindowSeconds then
			if age <= settings.MaxWindowSeconds or reference == nil then
				reference = point
			end
			if age > settings.MaxWindowSeconds then
				break
			end
		end
	end
	return reference
end

local function teleport(state: State, sample: Sample, now: number, settings: Settings, allowedSpeed: number): Violation?
	local previous = state.path[#state.path]
	if previous == nil then
		return nil
	end
	local elapsed = math.clamp(now - previous.t, 0, settings.MaxTimeCreditSeconds)
	local ping = pingSeconds(sample, settings)
	local h = horizontal(previous.x, previous.z, sample.X, sample.Z)
	local allowedH = allowedSpeed * (elapsed + ping) + settings.TeleportSlackStuds
	if h > allowedH and h >= settings.TeleportMinDistance then
		return {
			Detector = "Teleport",
			Kind = "TeleportDetected",
			Weight = 4,
			Details = {
				Distance = round(h),
				AllowedDistance = round(allowedH),
				ElapsedSeconds = round(elapsed),
				Axis = "Horizontal",
			},
		}
	end
	local rise = sample.Y - previous.y
	local apex = if sample.Gravity > 0 then (sample.JumpVelocity * sample.JumpVelocity) / (2 * sample.Gravity) else 0
	local allowedRise = apex + settings.VerticalSlackStuds + settings.MaxVerticalPlatformSpeed * (elapsed + ping)
	if rise > allowedRise then
		return {
			Detector = "Teleport",
			Kind = "TeleportDetected",
			Weight = 4,
			Details = {
				Distance = round(rise),
				AllowedDistance = round(allowedRise),
				ElapsedSeconds = round(elapsed),
				Axis = "Vertical",
			},
		}
	end
	return nil
end

local function speed(state: State, sample: Sample, now: number, settings: Settings, allowedSpeed: number): (Violation?, number)
	local reference = speedReference(state, now, settings)
	if reference == nil then
		return nil, 0
	end
	local elapsed = math.min(now - reference.t, math.max(settings.MaxTimeCreditSeconds, settings.MaxWindowSeconds))
	local distance = horizontal(reference.x, reference.z, sample.X, sample.Z)
	local observed = distance / elapsed
	local allowedDistance = allowedSpeed * (elapsed + pingSeconds(sample, settings)) + settings.SpeedSlackStuds
	if distance <= allowedDistance then
		return nil, observed
	end
	local ratio = distance / allowedDistance
	return {
		Detector = "Speed",
		Kind = "SpeedExceeded",
		Weight = math.min(4, 1 + (ratio - 1) * 2),
		Details = {
			ObservedSpeed = round(observed),
			AllowedSpeed = round(allowedSpeed),
			WindowSeconds = round(elapsed),
			Ratio = round(ratio),
		},
	},
		observed
end

-- Updates airborne tracking; returns (flightViolation?, infiniteJumpViolation?, airborne).
local function air(state: State, sample: Sample, now: number, settings: Settings, allowances: Allowances): (Violation?, Violation?, boolean)
	local grounded = sample.GroundClearance <= settings.AirborneClearance
		or AIR_EXEMPT_STATES[sample.State] == true
		or allowances.Flight
	if grounded then
		state.air = nil
		return nil, nil, false
	end

	local y = sample.Y
	local airState: AirState = state.air
		or { since = now, peak = y, trough = y, falling = false, airJumps = 0, history = {}, previous = nil, stallCredit = 0 }
	state.air = airState

	-- Replication stall: a client that stops sending updates mid-jump (a lag spike)
	-- leaves the server holding its last position AND its last, non-zero velocity. A
	-- position that does not change while the reported velocity says it is moving is a
	-- stall, not a hover (a hovering character reports ~zero velocity). Stalled time is
	-- not counted as airtime, up to MaxStallCreditSeconds per airborne period, so a
	-- cheater cannot hover indefinitely by spoofing velocity while frozen.
	local previous = airState.previous
	if previous then
		local still = math.abs(sample.X - previous.x) < 1e-3 and math.abs(y - previous.y) < 1e-3 and math.abs(sample.Z - previous.z) < 1e-3
		local reportedSpeed = math.sqrt(sample.VX * sample.VX + sample.VY * sample.VY + sample.VZ * sample.VZ)
		if still and reportedSpeed >= settings.StallMinVelocity then
			local credit = math.min(now - previous.t, settings.MaxStallCreditSeconds - airState.stallCredit)
			if credit > 0 then
				airState.since += credit
				airState.stallCredit += credit
				for _, point in airState.history do
					point.t += credit
				end
			end
		end
	end
	airState.previous = { t = now, x = sample.X, y = y, z = sample.Z }
	local history = airState.history
	table.insert(history, { t = now, x = sample.X, y = y, z = sample.Z })
	while #history > MAX_AIR_HISTORY or (#history > 2 and now - history[2].t >= 1.5) do
		table.remove(history, 1)
	end

	-- Infinite jump: a rise of AirJumpRiseStuds after descending, while still airborne.
	local jumpViolation: Violation? = nil
	if not airState.falling then
		airState.peak = math.max(airState.peak, y)
		if airState.peak - y >= 1 then
			airState.falling = true
			airState.trough = y
		end
	else
		airState.trough = math.min(airState.trough, y)
		if y - airState.trough >= settings.AirJumpRiseStuds then
			airState.airJumps += 1
			airState.falling = false
			airState.peak = y
			airState.trough = y
			if airState.airJumps > settings.MaxAirJumps then
				jumpViolation = {
					Detector = "InfiniteJump",
					Kind = "AirJump",
					Weight = 2.5,
					Details = { AirJumps = airState.airJumps, Allowed = settings.MaxAirJumps },
				}
			end
		end
	end

	-- Flight: still airborne after a full jump arc and not falling.
	local flightViolation: Violation? = nil
	if sample.Gravity > 0 then
		local airtime = now - airState.since
		local allowedAirtime = 2 * sample.JumpVelocity / sample.Gravity + settings.MaxAirtimeExtraSeconds
		if airtime > allowedAirtime then
			local reference: Point? = nil
			for i = #history, 1, -1 do
				if now - history[i].t >= 1 then
					reference = history[i]
					break
				end
			end
			if reference then
				local fallRate = (reference.y - y) / (now - reference.t)
				if fallRate < settings.MinFallStudsPerSecond then
					flightViolation = {
						Detector = "Flight",
						Kind = "Flight",
						Weight = 1,
						Details = {
							AirtimeSeconds = round(airtime),
							AllowedAirtimeSeconds = round(allowedAirtime),
							FallRate = round(fallRate),
							GroundClearance = if sample.GroundClearance == math.huge then "none" else round(sample.GroundClearance),
						},
					}
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return flightViolation, jumpViolation, true
end

local function acceleration(state: State, sample: Sample, now: number, settings: Settings): Violation?
	local last = state.last
	if last == nil then
		return nil
	end
	local dt = now - last.t
	if dt <= 0 or dt > 1 then
		return nil
	end
	local accel = horizontal(last.vx, last.vz, sample.VX, sample.VZ) / dt
	if accel > settings.MaxHorizontalAcceleration then
		return {
			Detector = "Acceleration",
			Kind = "AbnormalAcceleration",
			Weight = 1,
			Details = { Acceleration = round(accel), Allowed = settings.MaxHorizontalAcceleration },
		}
	end
	return nil
end

local function noclip(state: State, sample: Sample, settings: Settings, cast: CastFn?): Violation?
	local last = state.last
	if last == nil or cast == nil then
		return nil
	end
	local dx, dy, dz = sample.X - last.x, sample.Y - last.y, sample.Z - last.z
	local distance = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)
	if distance < settings.NoclipMinDistance then
		return nil
	end
	local forward = cast(last.x, last.y, last.z, dx, dy, dz)
	if forward == nil or forward >= distance then
		return nil
	end
	-- Rays that start inside a part do not hit it (Roblox semantics). So:
	--   backward hit  -> the segment crossed a solid of thickness distance-forward-backward
	--   backward miss -> the new position is inside the solid the forward ray hit,
	--                    penetrating distance-forward. Collision keeps a legitimate root's
	--                    centre out of walls, so both are evidence of noclip.
	local backward = cast(sample.X, sample.Y, sample.Z, -dx, -dy, -dz)
	local mode, depth
	if backward == nil then
		mode, depth = "Embedded", distance - forward
	else
		mode, depth = "Crossed", distance - forward - backward
	end
	if depth < settings.NoclipMinThickness then
		return nil
	end
	return {
		Detector = "Noclip",
		Kind = "PassedThroughGeometry",
		Weight = 2.5,
		Details = { Mode = mode, Distance = round(distance), Depth = round(depth) },
	}
end

-- Runs all enabled detectors for one sample and advances the state.
function MovementDetectors.evaluate(
	state: State,
	sample: Sample,
	now: number,
	settings: Settings,
	allowances: Allowances,
	cast: CastFn?
): Result
	local enabled = settings.Detectors
	local violations: { Violation } = {}
	local allowedSpeed = MovementDetectors.allowedSpeed(sample, settings, allowances)
	local point: Point = { t = now, x = sample.X, y = sample.Y, z = sample.Z }
	local observed = 0
	local airborne = false

	if allowances.Teleport or allowances.Impulse then
		-- Authorized teleport / physics impulse (knockback, launch pad): displacement is
		-- server-sanctioned, so re-anchor the path at the current position.
		MovementDetectors.resetPath(state, point)
		if allowances.Teleport then
			state.air = nil
		else
			local _, _, isAirborne = air(state, sample, now, settings, allowances)
			airborne = isAirborne
		end
	else
		local teleported: Violation? = if enabled.Teleport then teleport(state, sample, now, settings, allowedSpeed) else nil
		if teleported then
			table.insert(violations, teleported)
			-- Count the jump once, not again in every speed window that spans it.
			MovementDetectors.resetPath(state, point)
			state.air = nil
		elseif enabled.Speed then
			local violation
			violation, observed = speed(state, sample, now, settings, allowedSpeed)
			if violation then
				table.insert(violations, violation)
			end
		end

		local flightViolation, jumpViolation
		flightViolation, jumpViolation, airborne = air(state, sample, now, settings, allowances)
		if flightViolation and enabled.Flight then
			table.insert(violations, flightViolation)
		end
		if jumpViolation and enabled.InfiniteJump then
			table.insert(violations, jumpViolation)
		end

		if enabled.Acceleration and not allowances.Impulse then
			local violation = acceleration(state, sample, now, settings)
			if violation then
				table.insert(violations, violation)
			end
		end

		if enabled.Noclip and not allowances.Noclip and not teleported then
			local violation = noclip(state, sample, settings, cast)
			if violation then
				table.insert(violations, violation)
			end
		end

		recordPoint(state, point, settings)
	end

	state.last = { t = now, x = sample.X, y = sample.Y, z = sample.Z, vx = sample.VX, vz = sample.VZ }
	return {
		Violations = violations,
		ObservedSpeed = observed,
		AllowedSpeed = allowedSpeed,
		Airborne = airborne,
	}
end

return MovementDetectors

```

`src/Server/Detection/Physics/Physics.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Physics: character physics-state validation (built-in, Category "Physics").

	Consumes Movement's samples (Dependencies = { "Movement" }) and checks:

	  Fling             root angular speed above MaxAngularSpeed. Fling exploits spin the
	                    character root at extreme rates to launch other players.
	  ExtremeVelocity   horizontal or upward linear speed above the configured limits.
	                    Downward speed is ignored: long falls are legitimate.
	  VelocityMismatch  observed displacement speed far above the reported velocity
	                    (CFrame "tp-walk"), only when observed speed also approaches or
	                    exceeds the allowed speed. It corroborates Speed violations.

	All three are suppressed by an "Impulse" or "All" movement allowance (knockback,
	explosions, launch pads) and by teleport allowances, and feed a per-player
	ViolationTracker like every other detector.
]]

local Configuration = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Core.Configuration)
local ViolationTracker = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Intelligence.ViolationTracker)

local S = Configuration.Schema

local MODULE_NAME = "Physics"
local STATE_KEY = "Physics.State"

local CONFIG_SCHEMA = S.Table({
	MaxAngularSpeed = S.Number(60, { Min = 1, Max = 1e9 }),
	MaxHorizontalSpeed = S.Number(300, { Min = 1, Max = 1e9 }),
	MaxUpwardSpeed = S.Number(250, { Min = 1, Max = 1e9 }),
	MismatchRatio = S.Number(2.5, { Min = 1, Max = 1000 }),
	MismatchSlack = S.Number(8, { Min = 0, Max = 10000 }),
	MismatchMinFractionOfAllowed = S.Number(0.8, { Min = 0, Max = 10 }),
	Detectors = S.Table({
		Fling = S.Boolean(true),
		ExtremeVelocity = S.Boolean(true),
		VelocityMismatch = S.Boolean(true),
	}),
	Severity = S.Table({
		Fling = S.Number(35, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
		ExtremeVelocity = S.Number(25, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
		VelocityMismatch = S.Number(10, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
	}),
	Violation = S.Table({
		HalfLifeSeconds = S.Number(10, { Min = 0.5, Max = 3600 }),
		ReportThreshold = S.Number(4, { Min = 0.1, Max = 10000 }),
		ReportIntervalSeconds = S.Number(10, { Min = 0, Max = 3600 }),
		ConfidenceScale = S.Number(8, { Min = 0.1, Max = 10000 }),
	}),
})

local Physics = {}
Physics.Name = MODULE_NAME
Physics.ConfigSchema = CONFIG_SCHEMA

local function round(value: number): number
	return math.floor(value * 100 + 0.5) / 100
end

function Physics.new()
	local module: any = {
		Name = MODULE_NAME,
		Version = "1.0.0",
		Category = "Physics",
		Dependencies = { "Movement" },
		EnabledByDefault = true,
		ConfigSchema = CONFIG_SCHEMA,
	}

	local context: any = nil
	local stats = { Violations = {} :: { [string]: number }, Reports = 0 }

	local function record(session: any, detector: string, kind: string, weight: number, now: number, details: { [string]: any })
		stats.Violations[detector] = (stats.Violations[detector] or 0) + 1
		local tracker = session:GetState(STATE_KEY, function()
			return ViolationTracker.new(context.Config.Violation)
		end)
		local report, score, confidence = tracker:Record(detector, weight, now)
		if report then
			stats.Reports += 1
			details.Score = round(score)
			context:Report(session, {
				Kind = kind,
				Severity = context.Config.Severity[detector],
				Confidence = confidence,
				Details = details,
			})
		end
	end

	local function onSample(payload: any)
		local allowances = payload.Allowances
		if allowances.Impulse or allowances.Teleport then
			return
		end
		local config = context.Config
		local enabled = config.Detectors
		local sample = payload.Sample
		local session = payload.Session
		local now = payload.Time

		if enabled.Fling and sample.AngularSpeed > config.MaxAngularSpeed then
			record(session, "Fling", "FlingDetected", 2, now, {
				AngularSpeed = round(sample.AngularSpeed),
				Limit = config.MaxAngularSpeed,
			})
		end

		local horizontalSpeed = math.sqrt(sample.VX * sample.VX + sample.VZ * sample.VZ)
		if enabled.ExtremeVelocity then
			if horizontalSpeed > config.MaxHorizontalSpeed * allowances.SpeedMultiplier then
				record(session, "ExtremeVelocity", "ExtremeVelocity", 2, now, {
					Axis = "Horizontal",
					Speed = round(horizontalSpeed),
					Limit = config.MaxHorizontalSpeed,
				})
			elseif sample.VY > config.MaxUpwardSpeed then
				record(session, "ExtremeVelocity", "ExtremeVelocity", 2, now, {
					Axis = "Upward",
```
