```luau
					Speed = round(sample.VY),
					Limit = config.MaxUpwardSpeed,
				})
			end
		end

		if enabled.VelocityMismatch then
			local observed = payload.ObservedSpeed
			local relevant = observed >= payload.AllowedSpeed * config.MismatchMinFractionOfAllowed
			if relevant and observed > horizontalSpeed * config.MismatchRatio + config.MismatchSlack then
				record(session, "VelocityMismatch", "VelocityMismatch", 1, now, {
					ObservedSpeed = round(observed),
					ReportedSpeed = round(horizontalSpeed),
				})
			end
		end
	end

	function module:GetStats(): { [string]: any }
		return { Violations = table.clone(stats.Violations), Reports = stats.Reports }
	end

	function module:Initialize(ctx)
		context = ctx
	end

	function module:Start()
		context.Events:Subscribe("Movement.Sample", onSample)
	end

	function module:Stop() end

	function module:Destroy()
		context = nil
	end

	return module
end

return Physics

```

`src/Server/Detection/RemoteSecurity/Policies.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Reusable permission and ownership checks for protected remotes.

	A check is a function(player, ...args) -> (allowed: boolean, reason: string?). It
	runs after argument validation, so it can rely on argument types. Checks that throw
	are treated as a denial (fail closed). Checks may yield (e.g. GetRankInGroup).

	Permission = "may this player use this remote at all / with these arguments?"
	Ownership  = "does this player own the object the request refers to?"
	They behave identically but are tracked as separate rejection reasons, because an
	ownership violation (acting on someone else's object) is the stronger signal.
]]

export type Check = (player: any, ...any) -> (boolean, string?)

local Permissions = {}
local Ownership = {}

-- Allows only the listed UserIds.
function Permissions.userIds(list: { number }): Check
	local allowed = {}
	for _, id in list do
		allowed[id] = true
	end
	return function(player)
		return allowed[player.UserId] == true, "user not allowed"
	end
end

-- Allows players whose rank in groupId is at least minRank.
function Permissions.groupRank(groupId: number, minRank: number): Check
	return function(player)
		local ok, rank = pcall(function()
			return player:GetRankInGroup(groupId)
		end)
		if not ok or type(rank) ~= "number" then
			return false, "group rank unavailable"
		end
		return rank >= minRank, "group rank too low"
	end
end

-- Passes when every check passes. Reports the first failing reason.
function Permissions.all(...: Check): Check
	local checks = { ... }
	return function(player, ...)
		for _, check in checks do
			local allowed, reason = check(player, ...)
			if not allowed then
				return false, reason
			end
		end
		return true
	end
end

-- Passes when any check passes.
function Permissions.any(...: Check): Check
	local checks = { ... }
	return function(player, ...)
		local lastReason = nil
		for _, check in checks do
			local allowed, reason = check(player, ...)
			if allowed then
				return true
			end
			lastReason = reason
		end
		return false, lastReason
	end
end

local function argument(index: number, ...: any): any
	return (select(index, ...))
end

-- The Instance at argument `index` must be inside the player's current character.
function Ownership.descendantOfCharacter(index: number): Check
	return function(player, ...)
		local target = argument(index, ...)
		local character = player.Character
		if character == nil or typeof(target) ~= "Instance" then
			return false, "no character"
		end
		return target == character or target:IsDescendantOf(character), "not part of the player's character"
	end
end

-- The Instance at argument `index` must be inside getRoot(player), e.g. the player's plot.
function Ownership.descendantOf(index: number, getRoot: (player: any) -> any): Check
	return function(player, ...)
		local target = argument(index, ...)
		local root = getRoot(player)
		if root == nil or typeof(target) ~= "Instance" then
			return false, "no owned container"
		end
		return target:IsDescendantOf(root), "outside the player's container"
	end
end

-- The Instance at argument `index` (or an ancestor) must carry attribute `name` equal
-- to the player's UserId. Searches at most `maxDepth` ancestors (default 8).
function Ownership.attribute(index: number, name: string, maxDepth: number?): Check
	local depth = maxDepth or 8
	return function(player, ...)
		local target = argument(index, ...)
		if typeof(target) ~= "Instance" then
			return false, "not an Instance"
		end
		local node: Instance? = target
		for _ = 0, depth do
			if not node then
				break
			end
			local owner = node:GetAttribute(name)
			if owner ~= nil then
				return owner == player.UserId, "owned by someone else"
			end
			node = node.Parent
		end
		return false, "no owner attribute"
	end
end

return table.freeze({
	Permissions = table.freeze(Permissions),
	Ownership = table.freeze(Ownership),
})

```

`src/Server/Detection/RemoteSecurity/RemoteSecurity.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	RemoteSecurity: server-side protection for RemoteEvents and RemoteFunctions.

	Developers describe each remote once:

		ACF:ProtectEvent(remotes.BuyItem, {
			Arguments = { V.string({ MaxLength = 32 }), V.integer({ Min = 1, Max = 99 }) },
			RateLimit = { Burst = 5, PerSecond = 1 },
			Cooldown = 0.25,
			Permission = ACF.Permissions.groupRank(123, 10),
			Ownership = nil,
			Idempotency = nil,
			Handler = function(player, itemId, quantity) ... end,
		})

	Pipeline per request, cheapest checks first; the first failure rejects:
	  1. active session            (no_session, not suspicious)
	  2. rate limit                (rate_limited)
	  2b. enforcement restriction  (restricted, not suspicious; spec.Restrict only):
	      rejected while Enforcement restricts the player for that tag (Phase 7)
	  3. argument count and schema (invalid_arguments)
	  4. character present         (no_character, not suspicious; RequireCharacter only)
	  5. cooldown since last accept(cooldown)
	  6. Permission check          (permission_denied)
	  7. Ownership check           (ownership_denied)
	  8. duplicate transaction id  (duplicate_transaction; Idempotency only)
	  9. Handler(player, ...)      errors are logged, never treated as player behaviour

	Fail closed: while the module is not Running, events are ignored and functions
	return RejectResponse. Rejected function calls return RejectResponse (default nil).

	Suspicion model (do not treat one bad request as cheating):
	  Each (player, remote, reason) keeps a score that decays exponentially with
	  half-life H. A rejection adds the reason's weight w. When the score reaches the
	  report threshold T (and the last report for that pair is older than the report
	  interval), a detection is reported with
	      Confidence = min(0.95, 1 - exp(-score / ConfidenceScale)).
	  With the defaults (w = 2 for invalid arguments, T = 6, H = 30 s) a single malformed
	  request never reports; three in quick succession do. Weights are configurable per
	  reason and can be overridden per remote.
]]

local Configuration = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Core.Configuration)
local RateLimiter = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Core.RateLimiter)
local ViolationTracker = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Intelligence.ViolationTracker)
local Validators = require(script.Parent.Validators)

local S = Configuration.Schema

local MODULE_NAME = "RemoteSecurity"
local STATE_KEY = "RemoteSecurity.State"

export type Reason =
	"no_session"
	| "rate_limited"
	| "invalid_arguments"
	| "no_character"
	| "cooldown"
	| "permission_denied"
	| "ownership_denied"
	| "duplicate_transaction"
	| "restricted"
	| "not_running"

-- Reason -> configuration key for weights/severity, and reported detection Kind.
local REASON_INFO: { [string]: { Key: string, Kind: string }? } = {
	rate_limited = { Key = "RateLimited", Kind = "RemoteRateLimitExceeded" },
	invalid_arguments = { Key = "InvalidArguments", Kind = "RemoteInvalidArguments" },
	cooldown = { Key = "Cooldown", Kind = "RemoteCooldownViolation" },
	permission_denied = { Key = "PermissionDenied", Kind = "RemotePermissionDenied" },
	ownership_denied = { Key = "OwnershipDenied", Kind = "RemoteOwnershipViolation" },
	duplicate_transaction = { Key = "Duplicate", Kind = "RemoteDuplicateTransaction" },
}

local WEIGHT_KEYS = { "RateLimited", "InvalidArguments", "Cooldown", "PermissionDenied", "OwnershipDenied", "Duplicate" }

local SPEC_KEYS = {
	Name = true,
	Arguments = true,
	Handler = true,
	RateLimit = true,
	Cooldown = true,
	Permission = true,
	Ownership = true,
	Idempotency = true,
	RequireCharacter = true,
	SuspicionWeights = true,
	RejectResponse = true,
	Restrict = true,
}

local function weightTable(defaults: { [string]: number }, severityMode: boolean)
	local fields = {}
	for _, key in WEIGHT_KEYS do
		fields[key] = S.Number(defaults[key], { Min = 0, Max = if severityMode then 100 else 1000 })
	end
	return S.Table(fields)
end

local CONFIG_SCHEMA = S.Table({
	DefaultRateLimit = S.Table({
		Burst = S.Number(10, { Min = 1, Max = 10000, Integer = true }),
		PerSecond = S.Number(5, { Min = 0.01, Max = 1000 }),
	}),
	Suspicion = S.Table({
		HalfLifeSeconds = S.Number(30, { Min = 1, Max = 3600 }),
		ReportThreshold = S.Number(6, { Min = 0.1, Max = 10000 }),
		ReportIntervalSeconds = S.Number(10, { Min = 0, Max = 3600 }),
		ConfidenceScale = S.Number(10, { Min = 0.1, Max = 10000 }),
		Weights = weightTable({
			RateLimited = 1,
			InvalidArguments = 2,
			Cooldown = 0.5,
			PermissionDenied = 3,
			OwnershipDenied = 3,
			Duplicate = 0.5,
		}, false),
		Severity = weightTable({
			RateLimited = 10,
			InvalidArguments = 15,
			Cooldown = 5,
			PermissionDenied = 20,
			OwnershipDenied = 25,
			Duplicate = 5,
		}, true),
	}),
})

type Spec = {
	Name: string,
	Arguments: { Validators.Validator },
	Handler: (...any) -> ...any,
	RateLimit: { Burst: number, PerSecond: number }?,
	Cooldown: number,
	Permission: ((...any) -> (boolean, string?))?,
	Ownership: ((...any) -> (boolean, string?))?,
	Idempotency: { Argument: number, WindowSeconds: number, MaxEntries: number }?,
	RequireCharacter: boolean,
	SuspicionWeights: { [string]: number },
	RejectResponse: any,
	-- Enforcement tag (a risk group such as "Economy"), or true for any restriction.
	Restrict: (string | boolean)?,
}

type Stats = {
	Accepted: number,
	Rejected: { [string]: number },
	HandlerErrors: number,
	Reports: number,
}

type Protection = {
	Name: string,
	Remote: any,
	Kind: "Event" | "Function",
	Spec: Spec,
	Stats: Stats,
	Connection: any?,
	Active: boolean,
}

type RemoteState = {
	limiter: RateLimiter.RateLimiter,
	lastAccepted: number?,
	seenIds: { [any]: number },
	idOrder: { any },
	suspicion: ViolationTracker.ViolationTracker,
}

local RemoteSecurity = {}
RemoteSecurity.Name = MODULE_NAME
RemoteSecurity.ConfigSchema = CONFIG_SCHEMA

local function isA(remote: any, className: string): boolean
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return remote:IsA(className)
	end)
	return ok and result == true
end

local function remoteName(remote: any): string
	local ok, name = pcall(function()
		return remote.Name
	end)
	return if ok and type(name) == "string" then name else "Remote"
end

-- Validates a developer-supplied spec. Raises (level 3 = the ProtectX caller's caller)
-- on any problem, listing the offending field.
local function normalizeSpec(remote: any, raw: any, kind: "Event" | "Function"): Spec
	local function fail(message: string): never
		error(`ACF RemoteSecurity: {message}`, 4)
	end
	if type(raw) ~= "table" then
		fail("protection spec must be a table")
	end
	for key in raw do
		if not SPEC_KEYS[key] then
			fail(`unknown spec field '{tostring(key)}'`)
		end
	end
	if raw.RejectResponse ~= nil and kind ~= "Function" then
		fail("RejectResponse only applies to RemoteFunctions")
	end

	local name = raw.Name or remoteName(remote)
	if type(name) ~= "string" or #name == 0 or #name > 64 then
		fail("Name must be a non-empty string of at most 64 characters")
	end
	if type(raw.Handler) ~= "function" then
		fail(`'{name}': Handler function is required`)
	end
	local arguments = raw.Arguments
	if type(arguments) ~= "table" then
		fail(`'{name}': Arguments must be an array of validators (use {"{}"} for none)`)
	end
	local argCount = 0
	for key in arguments do
		argCount += 1
		if type(key) ~= "number" then
			fail(`'{name}': Arguments must be an array`)
		end
	end
	for i = 1, argCount do
		if not Validators.isValidator(arguments[i]) then
			fail(`'{name}': Arguments[{i}] is not a validator`)
		end
	end

	local rateLimit = raw.RateLimit
	if rateLimit ~= nil then
		if type(rateLimit) ~= "table" or type(rateLimit.Burst) ~= "number" or type(rateLimit.PerSecond) ~= "number" then
			fail(`'{name}': RateLimit must be {"{ Burst = number, PerSecond = number }"}`)
		end
		if rateLimit.Burst < 1 or rateLimit.PerSecond <= 0 then
			fail(`'{name}': RateLimit needs Burst >= 1 and PerSecond > 0`)
		end
	end

	local cooldown = raw.Cooldown or 0
	if type(cooldown) ~= "number" or cooldown < 0 or cooldown ~= cooldown then
		fail(`'{name}': Cooldown must be a non-negative number`)
	end
	if raw.Permission ~= nil and type(raw.Permission) ~= "function" then
		fail(`'{name}': Permission must be a function`)
	end
	if raw.Ownership ~= nil and type(raw.Ownership) ~= "function" then
		fail(`'{name}': Ownership must be a function`)
	end
	local restrict = raw.Restrict
	if restrict ~= nil and restrict ~= true and (type(restrict) ~= "string" or #restrict == 0 or #restrict > 64) then
		fail(`'{name}': Restrict must be a tag string (e.g. "Economy") or true`)
	end
	if raw.RequireCharacter ~= nil and type(raw.RequireCharacter) ~= "boolean" then
		fail(`'{name}': RequireCharacter must be a boolean`)
	end

	local idempotency = nil
	if raw.Idempotency ~= nil then
		local idem = raw.Idempotency
		if type(idem) ~= "table" or type(idem.Argument) ~= "number" then
			fail(`'{name}': Idempotency must be {"{ Argument = index, WindowSeconds?, MaxEntries? }"}`)
		end
		if idem.Argument < 1 or idem.Argument > argCount or idem.Argument ~= math.floor(idem.Argument) then
			fail(`'{name}': Idempotency.Argument must index one of the Arguments`)
		end
		local kindName = arguments[idem.Argument].Kind
		if kindName ~= "string" and kindName ~= "number" then
			fail(`'{name}': the transaction id argument must be validated as a string or number`)
		end
		local window = idem.WindowSeconds or 60
		local maxEntries = idem.MaxEntries or 64
		if type(window) ~= "number" or window <= 0 then
			fail(`'{name}': Idempotency.WindowSeconds must be > 0`)
		end
		if type(maxEntries) ~= "number" or maxEntries < 1 or maxEntries > 4096 then
			fail(`'{name}': Idempotency.MaxEntries must be between 1 and 4096`)
		end
		idempotency = { Argument = idem.Argument, WindowSeconds = window, MaxEntries = math.floor(maxEntries) }
	end

	local weights = {}
	if raw.SuspicionWeights ~= nil then
		if type(raw.SuspicionWeights) ~= "table" then
			fail(`'{name}': SuspicionWeights must be a table`)
		end
		for key, value in raw.SuspicionWeights do
			if not table.find(WEIGHT_KEYS, key) then
				fail(`'{name}': unknown SuspicionWeights key '{tostring(key)}'`)
			end
			if type(value) ~= "number" or value < 0 then
				fail(`'{name}': SuspicionWeights.{key} must be a non-negative number`)
			end
			weights[key] = value
		end
	end

	return table.freeze({
		Name = name,
		Arguments = table.freeze(table.clone(arguments)),
		Handler = raw.Handler,
		RateLimit = if rateLimit then table.freeze({ Burst = rateLimit.Burst, PerSecond = rateLimit.PerSecond }) else nil,
		Cooldown = cooldown,
		Permission = raw.Permission,
		Ownership = raw.Ownership,
		Idempotency = if idempotency then table.freeze(idempotency) else nil,
		RequireCharacter = raw.RequireCharacter == true,
		SuspicionWeights = table.freeze(weights),
		RejectResponse = raw.RejectResponse,
		Restrict = restrict,
	}) :: any
end

function RemoteSecurity.new()
	local module: any = {
		Name = MODULE_NAME,
		Version = "1.0.0",
		Category = "Remote",
		EnabledByDefault = true,
		ConfigSchema = CONFIG_SCHEMA,
	}

	local context: any = nil
	local running = false
	local destroyed = false
	local protections: { [string]: Protection } = {}
	local byRemote: { [any]: Protection } = {}

	-- Per-session state ----------------------------------------------------------------

	local function remoteState(session: any, protection: Protection): RemoteState
		local all = session:GetState(STATE_KEY, function()
			return {}
		end)
		local state = all[protection.Name]
		if state == nil then
			local limit = protection.Spec.RateLimit or context.Config.DefaultRateLimit
			state = {
				limiter = RateLimiter.new(limit.Burst, limit.PerSecond, context.Clock.Now, 1),
				lastAccepted = nil,
				seenIds = {},
				idOrder = {},
				suspicion = ViolationTracker.new(context.Config.Suspicion),
			}
			all[protection.Name] = state
		end
		return state
	end

	local function recordSuspicion(protection: Protection, session: any, state: RemoteState, reason: string, problem: string?)
		local info = REASON_INFO[reason]
		if info == nil then
			return
		end
		local settings = context.Config.Suspicion
		local weight = protection.Spec.SuspicionWeights[info.Key] or settings.Weights[info.Key]
		if weight <= 0 then
			return
		end
		local shouldReport, score, confidence = state.suspicion:Record(reason, weight, context.Clock.Now())
		if not shouldReport then
			return
		end
		protection.Stats.Reports += 1
		context:Report(session, {
			Kind = info.Kind,
			Severity = settings.Severity[info.Key],
			Confidence = confidence,
			Details = {
				Remote = protection.Name,
				Reason = reason,
				Score = math.floor(score * 100) / 100,
				Threshold = settings.ReportThreshold,
				HalfLifeSeconds = settings.HalfLifeSeconds,
				LastProblem = problem,
			},
		})
	end

	local function reject(protection: Protection, session: any?, state: RemoteState?, reason: string, problem: string?): false
		local rejected = protection.Stats.Rejected
		rejected[reason] = (rejected[reason] or 0) + 1
		if context then
			-- Guarded so floods of rejections cost no allocations when Debug is off.
			if context.Logger:IsEnabled("Debug") then
				context.Logger:Debug("Remote request rejected", {
					Remote = protection.Name,
					Reason = reason,
					Problem = problem,
					UserId = if session then session.UserId else nil,
				})
			end
			if session and state then
				recordSuspicion(protection, session, state, reason, problem)
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	-- Runs a Permission/Ownership check. Returns nil when it passes (or is absent),
	-- otherwise the denial text ("" when the check gave no reason).
	local function runPolicy(protection: Protection, fn: ((...any) -> (boolean, string?))?, reason: string, player: any, args: any): string?
		if fn == nil then
			return nil
		end
		local ok, allowed, reasonText = pcall(fn, player, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
		if not ok then
			context.Logger:Error("Remote policy check errored; request denied", {
				Remote = protection.Name,
				Check = reason,
				Error = tostring(allowed),
			})
			return "policy check errored"
		elseif not allowed then
			return if type(reasonText) == "string" then reasonText else ""
		end
		return nil
	end

	-- Runs checks 1-8. Returns (true, packedArgs) or (false).
	local function admit(protection: Protection, player: any, ...: any): (boolean, any)
		if not running then
			return reject(protection, nil, nil, "not_running")
		end
		local session = context.Players:GetSession(player)
		if session == nil then
			return reject(protection, nil, nil, "no_session")
		end
		local state = remoteState(session, protection)
		local spec = protection.Spec

		if not state.limiter:Allow("r") then
			return reject(protection, session, state, "rate_limited")
		end

		-- An enforcement decision, not player behaviour: no suspicion is recorded.
		if spec.Restrict ~= nil and context.Enforcement:IsRestricted(session, spec.Restrict, protection.Name) then
			return reject(protection, nil, nil, "restricted")
		end

		local count = select("#", ...)
		if count > #spec.Arguments then
			return reject(protection, session, state, "invalid_arguments", `expected at most {#spec.Arguments} arguments`)
		end
		local args = table.pack(...)
		for i, validator in spec.Arguments do
			local ok, valid, problem = pcall(validator.Check, args[i], `arg{i}`)
			if not ok then
				return reject(protection, session, state, "invalid_arguments", `arg{i}: validator error`)
			elseif not valid then
				return reject(protection, session, state, "invalid_arguments", problem)
			end
		end
		args.n = #spec.Arguments

		if spec.RequireCharacter and session.Character == nil then
			return reject(protection, session, state, "no_character")
		end

		local now = context.Clock.Now()
		if spec.Cooldown > 0 and state.lastAccepted ~= nil and now - state.lastAccepted < spec.Cooldown then
			return reject(protection, session, state, "cooldown")
		end

		local problem = runPolicy(protection, spec.Permission, "permission_denied", player, args)
		if problem then
			return reject(protection, session, state, "permission_denied", if problem == "" then nil else problem)
		end
		problem = runPolicy(protection, spec.Ownership, "ownership_denied", player, args)
		if problem then
			return reject(protection, session, state, "ownership_denied", if problem == "" then nil else problem)
		end

		local idem = spec.Idempotency
		if idem then
			local id = args[idem.Argument]
			local seenAt = state.seenIds[id]
			if seenAt ~= nil and now - seenAt <= idem.WindowSeconds then
				return reject(protection, session, state, "duplicate_transaction")
			end
			-- Expire old ids and enforce the cap (oldest first).
			local order = state.idOrder
			while #order > 0 and (now - (state.seenIds[order[1]] or -math.huge) > idem.WindowSeconds or #order >= idem.MaxEntries) do
				local oldest = table.remove(order, 1)
				state.seenIds[oldest] = nil
			end
			if seenAt ~= nil then
				local index = table.find(order, id)
				if index then
					table.remove(order, index)
				end
			end
			state.seenIds[id] = now
			table.insert(order, id)
		end

		state.lastAccepted = now
		protection.Stats.Accepted += 1
		return true, args
	end

	local function onEvent(protection: Protection, player: any, ...: any)
		local admitted, args = admit(protection, player, ...)
		if not admitted then
			return
		end
		local ok, err = pcall(protection.Spec.Handler, player, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
		if not ok then
			protection.Stats.HandlerErrors += 1
			context.Logger:Error("Remote handler errored", { Remote = protection.Name, Error = tostring(err) })
		end
	end

	local function onInvoke(protection: Protection, player: any, ...: any): ...any
		local admitted, args = admit(protection, player, ...)
		if not admitted then
			return protection.Spec.RejectResponse
		end
		local results = table.pack(pcall(protection.Spec.Handler, player, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n)))
		if not results[1] then
			protection.Stats.HandlerErrors += 1
			context.Logger:Error("Remote handler errored", { Remote = protection.Name, Error = tostring(results[2]) })
			return protection.Spec.RejectResponse
		end
		return table.unpack(results, 2, results.n)
	end

	local function connect(protection: Protection)
		if protection.Kind == "Event" then
			if protection.Connection == nil then
				protection.Connection = protection.Remote.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player, ...)
					onEvent(protection, player, ...)
				end)
			end
		else
			protection.Remote.OnServerInvoke = function(player, ...)
				return onInvoke(protection, player, ...)
			end
		end
	end

	local function disconnect(protection: Protection, closeFunction: boolean)
		if protection.Kind == "Event" then
			local connection = protection.Connection
			if connection then
				connection:Disconnect()
				protection.Connection = nil
			end
		elseif closeFunction then
			-- Fail closed without leaving clients yielding forever.
			local response = protection.Spec.RejectResponse
			protection.Remote.OnServerInvoke = function()
				return response
			end
		else
			protection.Remote.OnServerInvoke = nil
		end
	end

	local function protect(remote: any, rawSpec: any, kind: "Event" | "Function")
		if destroyed then
			error("ACF RemoteSecurity: module has been destroyed", 3)
		end
		if remote == nil then
			error("ACF RemoteSecurity: remote is nil", 3)
		end
		if kind == "Event" then
			if not (isA(remote, "RemoteEvent") or isA(remote, "UnreliableRemoteEvent")) then
				error("ACF RemoteSecurity: ProtectEvent expects a RemoteEvent or UnreliableRemoteEvent", 3)
			end
		elseif not isA(remote, "RemoteFunction") then
			error("ACF RemoteSecurity: ProtectFunction expects a RemoteFunction", 3)
		end
		if byRemote[remote] then
			error(`ACF RemoteSecurity: remote '{remoteName(remote)}' is already protected`, 3)
		end
		local spec = normalizeSpec(remote, rawSpec, kind)
		if protections[spec.Name] then
			error(`ACF RemoteSecurity: a protection named '{spec.Name}' already exists; set a unique Name`, 3)
		end

		local protection: Protection = {
			Name = spec.Name,
			Remote = remote,
			Kind = kind,
			Spec = spec,
			Stats = { Accepted = 0, Rejected = {}, HandlerErrors = 0, Reports = 0 },
			Connection = nil,
			Active = true,
		}
		protections[spec.Name] = protection
		byRemote[remote] = protection
		if running then
			connect(protection)
		elseif kind == "Function" then
			disconnect(protection, true)
		end

		return table.freeze({
			Name = spec.Name,
			Unprotect = function()
				if not protection.Active then
					return
				end
				protection.Active = false
				disconnect(protection, false)
				protections[protection.Name] = nil
				byRemote[remote] = nil
			end,
		})
	end

	-- Public surface ------------------------------------------------------------------

	-- Protects a RemoteEvent or UnreliableRemoteEvent. Returns { Name, Unprotect() }.
	function module:ProtectEvent(remote: any, spec: any)
		return protect(remote, spec, "Event")
	end

	-- Protects a RemoteFunction (server side; the server never invokes the client).
	function module:ProtectFunction(remote: any, spec: any)
		return protect(remote, spec, "Function")
	end

	function module:IsProtected(remote: any): boolean
		return byRemote[remote] ~= nil
	end

	-- Remote instances under `root` that have no protection. Use during development to
	-- find remotes that bypass validation.
	function module:FindUnprotected(root: any): { any }
		local result = {}
		for _, descendant in root:GetDescendants() do
			local isRemote = isA(descendant, "RemoteEvent")
				or isA(descendant, "UnreliableRemoteEvent")
				or isA(descendant, "RemoteFunction")
			if isRemote and byRemote[descendant] == nil then
				table.insert(result, descendant)
			end
		end
		return result
	end

	function module:GetStats(): { [string]: any }
		local result = {}
		for name, protection in protections do
			result[name] = {
				Kind = protection.Kind,
				Accepted = protection.Stats.Accepted,
				Rejected = table.clone(protection.Stats.Rejected),
				HandlerErrors = protection.Stats.HandlerErrors,
				Reports = protection.Stats.Reports,
			}
		end
		return result
	end

	-- Lifecycle -----------------------------------------------------------------------

	function module:Initialize(ctx)
		context = ctx
	end

	function module:Start()
		running = true
		for _, protection in protections do
			connect(protection)
		end
		context.Logger:Info("Remote protection active", { Remotes = #self:GetProtectedNames() })
	end

	function module:Stop()
		running = false
		for _, protection in protections do
			disconnect(protection, true)
		end
	end

	function module:Destroy()
		running = false
		destroyed = true
		for _, protection in protections do
			disconnect(protection, true)
		end
		table.clear(protections)
		table.clear(byRemote)
	end

	function module:GetProtectedNames(): { string }
		local names = {}
		for name in protections do
			table.insert(names, name)
		end
		table.sort(names)
		return names
	end

	return module
end

return RemoteSecurity

```

`src/Server/Detection/RemoteSecurity/Validators.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Composable argument validators for remote requests.

	Everything a client sends is attacker-controlled. Validators therefore:
	  - use secure defaults (strings <= 256 chars and valid UTF-8, arrays <= 64 items,
	    maps <= 32 entries, NaN and infinity rejected);
	  - bound their work: tables are walked with an entry budget, so a huge table costs
	    at most `limit + 1` iterations before rejection;
	  - never throw on hostile input (the caller also wraps them in pcall);
	  - never echo attacker-supplied text in error messages. Paths use schema field
	    names, and only value *types* are described.

	A validator is a frozen table { Kind, Description, Check } where
	Check(value, path) returns (true) or (false, message).

		local V = ACF.Validators
		V.struct({ ItemId = V.string({ MaxLength = 32 }), Quantity = V.integer({ Min = 1, Max = 99 }) })
]]

export type CheckFn = (value: any, path: string) -> (boolean, string?)

export type Validator = {
	Kind: string,
	Description: string,
	Check: CheckFn,
}

local DEFAULT_STRING_MAX = 256
local DEFAULT_ARRAY_MAX = 64
local DEFAULT_MAP_MAX = 32

local VALIDATOR_MARKER = newproxy(false)

local Validators = {}

local function make(kind: string, description: string, check: CheckFn): Validator
	return table.freeze({
		Kind = kind,
		Description = description,
		Check = check,
		[VALIDATOR_MARKER] = true,
	}) :: any
end

function Validators.isValidator(value: any): boolean
	return type(value) == "table" and rawget(value, VALIDATOR_MARKER) == true
end

local function assertValidator(value: any, where: string)
	if not Validators.isValidator(value) then
		error(`Validators: {where} must be a validator (build it with ACF.Validators)`, 3)
	end
end

local function isFinite(value: number): boolean
	return value == value and value ~= math.huge and value ~= -math.huge
end

local function got(value: any): string
	return `got {typeof(value)}`
end

-- Scalars ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

export type NumberOptions = { Min: number?, Max: number?, Integer: boolean? }

function Validators.number(options: NumberOptions?): Validator
	local opts: NumberOptions = options or {}
	local min, max, integer = opts.Min, opts.Max, opts.Integer == true
	return make("number", if integer then "integer" else "number", function(value, path)
		if type(value) ~= "number" then
			return false, `{path}: expected number, {got(value)}`
		end
		if not isFinite(value) then
			return false, `{path}: number must be finite`
		end
		if integer and value ~= math.floor(value) then
			return false, `{path}: expected an integer`
		end
		if min ~= nil and value < min then
			return false, `{path}: below minimum {min}`
		end
		if max ~= nil and value > max then
			return false, `{path}: above maximum {max}`
		end
		return true
	end)
end

function Validators.integer(options: { Min: number?, Max: number? }?): Validator
	local opts: { Min: number?, Max: number? } = options or {}
	return Validators.number({ Min = opts.Min, Max = opts.Max, Integer = true })
end

export type StringOptions = {
	MinLength: number?,
	MaxLength: number?,
	Pattern: string?,
	AllowInvalidUtf8: boolean?,
}

function Validators.string(options: StringOptions?): Validator
	local opts: StringOptions = options or {}
	local minLength = opts.MinLength or 0
	local maxLength = opts.MaxLength or DEFAULT_STRING_MAX
	local pattern = opts.Pattern
	local checkUtf8 = opts.AllowInvalidUtf8 ~= true
	return make("string", `string(<= {maxLength})`, function(value, path)
		if type(value) ~= "string" then
			return false, `{path}: expected string, {got(value)}`
		end
		local length = #value
		if length > maxLength then
			return false, `{path}: longer than {maxLength} bytes`
		end
		if length < minLength then
			return false, `{path}: shorter than {minLength} bytes`
		end
		if checkUtf8 and utf8.len(value) == nil then
			return false, `{path}: invalid UTF-8`
		end
		if pattern and not string.match(value, pattern) then
			return false, `{path}: does not match the required format`
		end
		return true
	end)
end

function Validators.boolean(): Validator
	return make("boolean", "boolean", function(value, path)
		if type(value) ~= "boolean" then
			return false, `{path}: expected boolean, {got(value)}`
		end
		return true
	end)
end

function Validators.literal(expected: any): Validator
	return make("literal", `literal {typeof(expected)}`, function(value, path)
		if value ~= expected then
			return false, `{path}: unexpected value`
		end
		return true
	end)
end

-- One of a fixed set of strings or numbers.
function Validators.enum(values: { any }): Validator
	if type(values) ~= "table" or #values == 0 then
		error("Validators.enum expects a non-empty array", 2)
	end
	local allowed = {}
	for _, v in values do
		allowed[v] = true
	end
	return make("enum", "enum", function(value, path)
		if (type(value) ~= "string" and type(value) ~= "number") or not allowed[value] then
			return false, `{path}: not an allowed value`
		end
		return true
	end)
end

-- Wrappers ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function Validators.optional(inner: Validator): Validator
	assertValidator(inner, "optional(...)")
	return make("optional", `optional {inner.Description}`, function(value, path)
		if value == nil then
			return true
		end
		return inner.Check(value, path)
	end)
end

function Validators.union(...: Validator): Validator
	local options = { ... }
	if #options < 2 then
		error("Validators.union expects at least two validators", 2)
	end
	for i, v in options do
		assertValidator(v, `union argument {i}`)
	end
	return make("union", "union", function(value, path)
		for _, v in options do
			if v.Check(value, path) then
				return true
			end
		end
		return false, `{path}: matched none of the allowed types ({got(value)})`
	end)
end

-- Custom check. `fn(value)` returns true, or false plus an optional reason. Reasons are
-- written by the developer, so they are safe to log. Errors count as rejection.
function Validators.custom(name: string, fn: (value: any) -> (boolean, string?)): Validator
	if type(name) ~= "string" or type(fn) ~= "function" then
		error("Validators.custom expects (name, function)", 2)
	end
	return make("custom", name, function(value, path)
		local ok, passed, reason = pcall(fn, value)
		if not ok then
			return false, `{path}: {name} check errored`
		end
		if not passed then
			return false, `{path}: {reason or ("failed " .. name)}`
		end
		return true
	end)
end

-- Tables -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Sequential array 1..n with every element matching `element`.
function Validators.array(element: Validator, options: { MinLength: number?, MaxLength: number? }?): Validator
	assertValidator(element, "array element")
	local opts: { MinLength: number?, MaxLength: number? } = options or {}
	local minLength = opts.MinLength or 0
	local maxLength = opts.MaxLength or DEFAULT_ARRAY_MAX
	return make("array", `array(<= {maxLength})`, function(value, path)
		if type(value) ~= "table" then
			return false, `{path}: expected array, {got(value)}`
		end
		local count = 0
		for key in value do
			count += 1
			if count > maxLength then
				return false, `{path}: more than {maxLength} items`
			end
			if type(key) ~= "number" or key < 1 or key ~= math.floor(key) then
				return false, `{path}: arrays may only have positive integer keys`
			end
		end
		if count < minLength then
			return false, `{path}: fewer than {minLength} items`
		end
		for i = 1, count do
			local item = value[i]
			if item == nil then
				return false, `{path}: array has holes`
			end
			local ok, err = element.Check(item, `{path}[{i}]`)
			if not ok then
				return false, err
			end
		end
		return true
	end)
end

-- Dictionary with known string keys. Unknown keys are rejected unless AllowExtra.
function Validators.struct(fields: { [string]: Validator }, options: { AllowExtra: boolean? }?): Validator
	if type(fields) ~= "table" then
		error("Validators.struct expects a table of field validators", 2)
	end
	local names = {}
	for name, v in fields do
		if type(name) ~= "string" then
			error("Validators.struct field names must be strings", 2)
		end
		assertValidator(v, `struct field '{name}'`)
		table.insert(names, name)
	end
	table.sort(names)
	local allowExtra = options ~= nil and options.AllowExtra == true
	local budget = #names + (if allowExtra then DEFAULT_MAP_MAX else 0)
	return make("struct", "struct", function(value, path)
		if type(value) ~= "table" then
			return false, `{path}: expected table, {got(value)}`
		end
		local count = 0
		for key in value do
			count += 1
			if count > budget then
				return false, `{path}: too many keys`
			end
			if type(key) ~= "string" then
				return false, `{path}: keys must be strings`
			end
			if not allowExtra and fields[key] == nil then
				return false, `{path}: unexpected key`
			end
		end
		for _, name in names do
			local ok, err = fields[name].Check(value[name], `{path}.{name}`)
			if not ok then
				return false, err
			end
		end
		return true
	end)
end

function Validators.map(key: Validator, valueValidator: Validator, options: { MaxEntries: number? }?): Validator
	assertValidator(key, "map key")
	assertValidator(valueValidator, "map value")
	local maxEntries = (options and options.MaxEntries) or DEFAULT_MAP_MAX
	return make("map", `map(<= {maxEntries})`, function(value, path)
		if type(value) ~= "table" then
			return false, `{path}: expected table, {got(value)}`
		end
		local count = 0
		for k, v in value do
			count += 1
			if count > maxEntries then
				return false, `{path}: more than {maxEntries} entries`
			end
			local ok, err = key.Check(k, `{path}<key>`)
			if not ok then
				return false, err
			end
			ok, err = valueValidator.Check(v, `{path}[{count}]`)
			if not ok then
				return false, err
			end
		end
		return true
	end)
end

-- Roblox types -----------------------------------------------------------------------

-- An Instance, optionally of a class and inside a root. `DescendantOf` may be an
-- Instance or a function(value) -> Instance? (e.g. resolve the player's plot).
function Validators.instance(className: string?, options: { DescendantOf: any? }?): Validator
	local root = options and options.DescendantOf
	return make("instance", className or "Instance", function(value, path)
		if typeof(value) ~= "Instance" then
			return false, `{path}: expected Instance, {got(value)}`
		end
		if className and not value:IsA(className) then
			return false, `{path}: expected {className}`
		end
		if root ~= nil then
			local container = if type(root) == "function" then root(value) else root
			if container == nil or not value:IsDescendantOf(container) then
				return false, `{path}: outside the allowed container`
			end
		end
		return true
	end)
end

function Validators.vector3(options: { MaxMagnitude: number? }?): Validator
	local maxMagnitude = options and options.MaxMagnitude
	return make("vector3", "Vector3", function(value, path)
		if typeof(value) ~= "Vector3" then
			return false, `{path}: expected Vector3, {got(value)}`
		end
		if not (isFinite(value.X) and isFinite(value.Y) and isFinite(value.Z)) then
			return false, `{path}: components must be finite`
		end
		if maxMagnitude and value.Magnitude > maxMagnitude then
			return false, `{path}: magnitude above {maxMagnitude}`
		end
		return true
	end)
end

function Validators.cframe(): Validator
	return make("cframe", "CFrame", function(value, path)
		if typeof(value) ~= "CFrame" then
			return false, `{path}: expected CFrame, {got(value)}`
		end
		for _, component in { value:GetComponents() } do
			if not isFinite(component) then
				return false, `{path}: components must be finite`
			end
		end
		return true
	end)
end

return table.freeze(Validators)

```

`src/Server/Detection/Telemetry/Telemetry.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Telemetry: optional client heartbeat (built-in, Category "Integrity", disabled by
	default; enable with Modules = { Telemetry = true } and install the client script).

	The server creates ReplicatedStorage.ACFRemotes.Telemetry and protects it with
	RemoteSecurity (validated arguments, rate limit). Per player it tracks:

	  TelemetryMissing    no heartbeat for MissingAfterSeconds after StartupGraceSeconds.
	                      Typical causes: the client script was deleted, or the client is
	                      frozen. Packet loss and lag also cause it, so weight and maximum
	                      confidence are low.
	  TelemetrySequence   a heartbeat sequence that goes backwards or repeats (replay, or
	                      a second script firing the remote).

	Telemetry is a weak signal by design. An exploiter can fire the remote themselves, so
	a *present* heartbeat proves nothing; only its absence or malformation is evidence.
	FPS is stored for diagnostics and latency context; it is never scored.
]]

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Protocol = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("ACFShared"):WaitForChild("Protocol"))
local Configuration = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Core.Configuration)
local ViolationTracker = require(script.Parent.Parent.Parent.Intelligence.ViolationTracker)
local Validators = require(script.Parent.Parent.RemoteSecurity.Validators)

local S = Configuration.Schema

local MODULE_NAME = "Telemetry"
local STATE_KEY = "Telemetry.State"

local CONFIG_SCHEMA = S.Table({
	StartupGraceSeconds = S.Number(30, { Min = 0, Max = 3600 }),
	MissingAfterSeconds = S.Number(20, { Min = 1, Max = 3600 }),
	CheckIntervalSeconds = S.Number(5, { Min = 0.5, Max = 3600 }),
	Severity = S.Table({
		Missing = S.Number(5, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
		Sequence = S.Number(10, { Min = 0, Max = 100 }),
	}),
	Violation = S.Table({
		HalfLifeSeconds = S.Number(120, { Min = 1, Max = 86400 }),
		ReportThreshold = S.Number(6, { Min = 0.1, Max = 10000 }),
		ReportIntervalSeconds = S.Number(120, { Min = 0, Max = 86400 }),
		ConfidenceScale = S.Number(20, { Min = 0.1, Max = 10000 }),
		MaxConfidence = S.Number(0.5, { Min = 0, Max = 1 }),
	}),
})

type SessionState = {
	lastSequence: number,
	lastHeartbeat: number?,
	fps: number?,
	tracker: ViolationTracker.ViolationTracker,
}

local Telemetry = {}
Telemetry.Name = MODULE_NAME
Telemetry.ConfigSchema = CONFIG_SCHEMA

function Telemetry.new()
	local module: any = {
		Name = MODULE_NAME,
		Version = "1.0.0",
		Category = "Integrity",
		Dependencies = { "RemoteSecurity" },
		EnabledByDefault = false,
		ConfigSchema = CONFIG_SCHEMA,
	}

	local context: any = nil
	local protection: any = nil

	local function stateFor(session: any): SessionState
		return session:GetState(STATE_KEY, function()
			return {
				lastSequence = 0,
				lastHeartbeat = nil,
				fps = nil,
				tracker = ViolationTracker.new(context.Config.Violation),
			}
		end)
	end

	local function violation(session: any, state: SessionState, key: string, kind: string, details: { [string]: any })
		local now = context.Clock.Now()
		local report, score, confidence = state.tracker:Record(key, 1, now)
		if report then
			details.Score = math.floor(score * 100) / 100
			context:Report(session, {
				Kind = kind,
				Severity = context.Config.Severity[key],
				Confidence = confidence,
				Details = details,
			})
		end
	end

	local function onHeartbeat(player: any, sequence: number, fps: number)
		local session = context.Players:GetSession(player)
		if session == nil then
			return
		end
		local state = stateFor(session)
		if sequence <= state.lastSequence then
			violation(session, state, "Sequence", "TelemetrySequence", {
				Received = sequence,
				Expected = state.lastSequence + 1,
			})
			return
		end
		state.lastSequence = sequence
		state.lastHeartbeat = context.Clock.Now()
		state.fps = fps
	end

	local function check(now: number)
		local config = context.Config
		for _, session in context.Players:GetSessions() do
			if now - session.JoinedAt >= config.StartupGraceSeconds then
				local state = stateFor(session)
				local since = state.lastHeartbeat or session.JoinedAt
				if now - since > config.MissingAfterSeconds then
					violation(session, state, "Missing", "TelemetryMissing", {
						SecondsSinceHeartbeat = math.floor(now - since),
						EverReceived = state.lastHeartbeat ~= nil,
					})
				end
			end
		end
	end

	-- Last reported FPS for a player (diagnostics), or nil.
	function module:GetFps(target: any): number?
		if context == nil then
			return nil
		end
		local session = context.Players:GetSession(target)
		if session == nil then
			return nil
		end
		return stateFor(session).fps
	end

	function module:Initialize(ctx)
		context = ctx
	end

	function module:Start()
		local remotes = context.Platform.Remotes
		if remotes == nil then
			error("Telemetry requires Environment.Remotes")
		end
		local remote = remotes:GetOrCreate(Protocol.TELEMETRY_REMOTE, "RemoteEvent")
		local security = context:GetModule("RemoteSecurity")
		if protection == nil then
			protection = security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Name = "ACF.Telemetry",
				Arguments = {
					Validators.integer({ Min = 1, Max = 2 ^ 31 }),
					Validators.number({ Min = 0, Max = Protocol.MAX_FPS }),
				},
				-- Allows a little jitter around the protocol interval.
				RateLimit = { Burst = 3, PerSecond = 2 / Protocol.TELEMETRY_INTERVAL_SECONDS },
				Handler = onHeartbeat,
			})
		end
		context.Scheduler:Every("Check", context.Config.CheckIntervalSeconds, check)
	end

	function module:Stop() end

	function module:Destroy()
		if protection then
			protection.Unprotect()
			protection = nil
		end
		context = nil
	end

	return module
end

return Telemetry

```

`src/Server/Enforcement/AuditLog.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Enforcement audit log: every enforcement decision and staff action, bounded in memory
	and published on ACF.EnforcementAudit so the game can ship it to external storage.

	Entry: { Id, Time, WallTime, Type, UserId?, Level?, From?, To?, Band?, Risk?, Tags?,
	         By?, Reason?, AppealRef?, CaseId?, Shadow, Count?, Details? }

	Types: LevelChanged, Rejected, Corrected, Kicked, Banned, Unbanned, BanEnforced,
	BanExpired, CaseOpened, CaseUpdated, CaseApproved, CaseRejected, CaseExpired,
	OverrideSet, OverrideCleared, ShadowAction, PersistenceFailed.

	Repeated entries of one (UserId, Type, key) within RepeatIntervalSeconds are
	summarised: the first is written, later ones only increase a counter that is reported
	with the next entry (Count). That keeps a flood of rejections from flooding the log.
]]

local AuditLog = {}
AuditLog.__index = AuditLog

export type Entry = { [string]: any }

export type AuditLog = typeof(setmetatable(
	{} :: {
		_entries: { Entry },
		_max: number,
		_repeatInterval: number,
		_marks: { [string]: { At: number, Suppressed: number } },
		_now: () -> number,
		_wall: () -> number,
		_id: () -> string,
		_publish: (Entry) -> (),
		_total: number,
		_suppressed: number,
	},
	AuditLog
))

function AuditLog.new(options: {
	MaxEntries: number,
	RepeatIntervalSeconds: number,
	Now: () -> number,
	Wall: () -> number,
	GenerateId: () -> string,
	Publish: (Entry) -> (),
}): AuditLog
	return setmetatable({
		_entries = {},
		_max = options.MaxEntries,
		_repeatInterval = options.RepeatIntervalSeconds,
		_marks = {},
		_now = options.Now,
		_wall = options.Wall,
		_id = options.GenerateId,
		_publish = options.Publish,
		_total = 0,
		_suppressed = 0,
	}, AuditLog)
end

-- Appends an entry. With `repeatKey`, repeats inside the interval are summarised.
-- Returns the entry, or nil when it was summarised.
function AuditLog.Write(self: AuditLog, entryType: string, fields: { [string]: any }, repeatKey: string?): Entry?
	local now = self._now()
	local count: number? = nil
	if repeatKey then
		local markKey = `{entryType}:{tostring(fields.UserId)}:{repeatKey}`
		local mark = self._marks[markKey]
		if mark and now - mark.At < self._repeatInterval then
			mark.Suppressed += 1
			self._suppressed += 1
			return nil
		end
		if mark and mark.Suppressed > 0 then
			count = mark.Suppressed + 1
		end
		self._marks[markKey] = { At = now, Suppressed = 0 }
		-- Bound the marks table: forget marks older than the interval.
		local size = 0
		for _ in self._marks do
			size += 1
		end
		if size > self._max then
			for key, other in self._marks do
				if now - other.At >= self._repeatInterval then
					self._marks[key] = nil
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local entry: Entry = table.clone(fields)
	entry.Id = self._id()
	entry.Time = now
	entry.WallTime = self._wall()
	entry.Type = entryType
	entry.Shadow = fields.Shadow == true
	entry.Count = count
	local frozen = table.freeze(entry)
	table.insert(self._entries, frozen)
	while #self._entries > self._max do
		table.remove(self._entries, 1)
	end
	self._total += 1
	self._publish(frozen)
	return frozen
end

-- Entries oldest first, optionally filtered by { UserId?, Type?, Since? }.
function AuditLog.Get(self: AuditLog, filter: { UserId: number?, Type: string?, Since: number? }?): { Entry }
	local list = {}
	for _, entry in self._entries do
		if filter then
			if filter.UserId ~= nil and entry.UserId ~= filter.UserId then
				continue
			end
			if filter.Type ~= nil and entry.Type ~= filter.Type then
				continue
			end
			if filter.Since ~= nil and entry.Time < filter.Since then
				continue
			end
		end
		table.insert(list, entry)
	end
	return list
end

function AuditLog.GetStats(self: AuditLog): { [string]: number }
	return { Entries = #self._entries, Written = self._total, Summarised = self._suppressed }
end

return AuditLog

```

`src/Server/Enforcement/BanRecords.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Pure helpers for persisted enforcement records (one per UserId).

	Record (DataStore "ACF_Bans_v1", key "Bans_<UserId>"):
	  {
	    Version = 1,
	    UserId = number,
	    Active = nil | {                       -- the ban currently in force
	      Level = 6 | 7, AppealRef = "ACF-...", Reason = string, By = string,
	      IssuedAt = unix, ExpiresAt = unix | nil (permanent), ApprovedBy = string?,
	    },
	    History = { {                            -- newest last, bounded
	      Type = "Kick" | "TemporaryBan" | "PermanentBan" | "Unban" | "Expired",
	      At = unix, AppealRef = string?, Reason = string?, By = string?,
	      ExpiresAt = unix?,
	    } },
	  }
]]

local BanRecords = {}

BanRecords.VERSION = 1

export type Ban = {
	Level: number,
	AppealRef: string,
	Reason: string,
	By: string,
	IssuedAt: number,
	ExpiresAt: number?,
	ApprovedBy: string?,
}

export type Entry = {
	Type: string,
	At: number,
	AppealRef: string?,
	Reason: string?,
	By: string?,
	ExpiresAt: number?,
}

export type Record = {
	Version: number,
	UserId: number,
	Active: Ban?,
	History: { Entry },
}

function BanRecords.new(userId: number): Record
	return { Version = BanRecords.VERSION, UserId = userId, Active = nil, History = {} }
end

local function str(value: any, max: number): string?
	if type(value) ~= "string" then
		return nil
	end
	return string.sub(value, 1, max)
end

local function num(value: any): number?
	if type(value) == "number" and value == value and value > -math.huge and value < math.huge then
		return value
	end
	return nil
end

-- Validates a stored value. Returns a clean record, or nil when it is not one.
function BanRecords.fromStored(userId: number, value: any): Record?
	if type(value) ~= "table" or value.Version ~= BanRecords.VERSION then
		return nil
	end
	local record = BanRecords.new(userId)
	local active = value.Active
	if type(active) == "table" and (active.Level == 6 or active.Level == 7) and num(active.IssuedAt) then
		record.Active = {
			Level = active.Level,
			AppealRef = str(active.AppealRef, 32) or "unknown",
			Reason = str(active.Reason, 400) or "",
			By = str(active.By, 100) or "unknown",
			IssuedAt = active.IssuedAt,
			ExpiresAt = num(active.ExpiresAt),
			ApprovedBy = str(active.ApprovedBy, 100),
		}
	end
	if type(value.History) == "table" then
		for _, entry in value.History do
			if type(entry) == "table" and type(entry.Type) == "string" and num(entry.At) then
				table.insert(record.History, {
					Type = string.sub(entry.Type, 1, 32),
					At = entry.At,
					AppealRef = str(entry.AppealRef, 32),
					Reason = str(entry.Reason, 400),
					By = str(entry.By, 100),
					ExpiresAt = num(entry.ExpiresAt),
				})
			end
		end
	end
	return record
end

function BanRecords.addHistory(record: Record, entry: Entry, max: number)
	table.insert(record.History, entry)
	while #record.History > max do
		table.remove(record.History, 1)
	end
end

-- The ban in force at wall time `now`, or nil.
function BanRecords.activeBan(record: Record, now: number): Ban?
	local ban = record.Active
	if ban == nil then
		return nil
	end
	if ban.ExpiresAt ~= nil and now >= ban.ExpiresAt then
		return nil
	end
	return ban
end

-- Moves an expired ban into history. Returns true when the record changed.
function BanRecords.expire(record: Record, now: number, max: number): boolean
	local ban = record.Active
	if ban == nil or ban.ExpiresAt == nil or now < ban.ExpiresAt then
		return false
	end
	record.Active = nil
	BanRecords.addHistory(record, {
		Type = "Expired",
		At = ban.ExpiresAt :: number,
		AppealRef = ban.AppealRef,
		Reason = nil,
		By = nil,
		ExpiresAt = nil,
	}, max)
	return true
end

function BanRecords.countSince(record: Record, entryType: string, since: number): number
	local count = 0
	for _, entry in record.History do
		if entry.Type == entryType and entry.At >= since then
			count += 1
		end
	end
	return count
end

-- Temporary-ban length in hours: base x factor^(previous temporary bans), capped.
function BanRecords.tempBanHours(record: Record, base: number, factor: number, max: number): number
	local previous = BanRecords.countSince(record, "TemporaryBan", -math.huge)
	return math.min(max, base * factor ^ previous)
end

-- Fills {appeal} and {until} in a message template.
function BanRecords.format(template: string, appealRef: string, expiresAt: number?): string
	local untilText = if expiresAt then os.date("!%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC", math.floor(expiresAt)) else "further notice"
	local text = string.gsub(template, "{appeal}", appealRef)
	text = string.gsub(text, "{until}", untilText :: string)
	return text
end

return BanRecords

```

`src/Server/Enforcement/EnforcementManager.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	EnforcementManager: graduated, hybrid enforcement (Phase 7). A core service, separate
	from detection (modules) and evidence/risk (Intelligence). It reads risk and decides
	what to do; modules only ask it questions through context.Enforcement.

	Inputs:
	  ACF.RiskChanged        band changes from the RiskEngine
	  ACF.DetectionReported  new evidence while a player is in the EnforcementReview
	                         band (so a kicked player who rejoins and cheats again escalates)
	  sessions               ban check on join; state kept per UserId

	Outputs (never while ShadowMode, except staff actions):
	  level 2  IsRestricted(user, tag) is true for tags = risk groups >= TagMinScore;
	           RemoteSecurity (spec.Restrict) and Combat consult it
	  level 3  ShouldCorrect(user) lets Movement rubber-band a Movement-tagged player
	  level 4  every tag is restricted for TemporaryRestrictionSeconds
	  5-7      kick / temporary ban / permanent ban: automatic only as EnforcementPolicy
	           allows, otherwise a case for staff approval
	  audit    every decision and staff action (AuditLog, ACF.EnforcementAudit)

	Records (bans, kick history, appeal references) persist through Environment.Store,
	read-modify-write, with retries. Without a Store they last for the server's life.
	Bans also use Environment.Enforcer:Ban (Roblox Players:BanAsync) when enabled.
]]

local EventBus = require(script.Parent.Parent.Core.EventBus)
local Topics = require(script.Parent.Parent.Core.Topics)
local Types = require(script.Parent.Parent.Types)
local AuditLog = require(script.Parent.AuditLog)
local BanRecords = require(script.Parent.BanRecords)
local EnforcementPolicy = require(script.Parent.EnforcementPolicy)

local AUTOMATIC = "ACF (automatic)"

local BAND_ORDER = { Normal = 1, Increased = 2, Restrict = 3, Investigation = 4, EnforcementReview = 5 }

export type Case = {
	CaseId: string,
	AppealRef: string,
	UserId: number,
	Type: string, -- "Action" (approve a level 5-7) | "Review" (investigate)
	ProposedLevel: number?,
	Status: string, -- "Open" | "Approved" | "Rejected" | "Expired" | "Shadow"
	Reason: string,
	Explanation: string,
	Risk: number,
	Band: string,
	CreatedAt: number,
	CreatedTime: number,
	DecidedBy: string?,
	DecidedAt: number?,
	Note: string?,
	ExecutedLevel: number?,
}

type Override = { Exempt: boolean, Level: number?, Until: number?, By: string, Reason: string? }

type RecordSlot = {
	Record: BanRecords.Record?,
	Loaded: boolean,
	Loading: boolean,
	LoadAttempts: number,
	Ops: { (BanRecords.Record) -> () },
	Dirty: boolean,
	SaveAttempts: number,
	Saving: boolean,
	OnLoaded: { (BanRecords.Record) -> () },
}

type User = {
	UserId: number,
	Level: number,
	Tags: { string },
	TagSet: { [string]: boolean },
	RestrictedUntil: number,
	Override: Override?,
	OpenCase: string?,
	AwaitingNewEvidence: boolean,
	-- Time of the last removal; only evidence after it clears AwaitingNewEvidence.
	RemovedAt: number,
	LastCorrection: number,
	InServer: boolean,
	Band: string,
	-- When the current restriction (level >= 2) began, and whether it has been sent for review.
	RestrictedSince: number?,
	RestrictionReviewed: boolean,
	LeftAt: number?,
}

export type Options = {
	Bus: EventBus.EventBus,
	Registry: any,
	Logger: any,
	Clock: Types.Clock,
	GenerateId: () -> string,
	Settings: { [string]: any },
	Risk: any?,
	Store: Types.KeyValueStore?,
	Enforcer: Types.Enforcer?,
}

local EnforcementManager = {}
EnforcementManager.__index = EnforcementManager

export type EnforcementManager = typeof(setmetatable(
	{} :: {
		_bus: EventBus.EventBus,
		_registry: any,
		_logger: any,
		_clock: Types.Clock,
		_id: () -> string,
		_settings: { [string]: any },
		_risk: any?,
		_store: Types.KeyValueStore?,
		_enforcer: Types.Enforcer?,
		_users: { [number]: User },
		_records: { [number]: RecordSlot },
		_cases: { [string]: Case },
		_caseOrder: { string },
		_audit: AuditLog.AuditLog,
		_running: boolean,
		_subscriptions: { EventBus.Subscription },
		_stats: { [string]: number },
	},
	EnforcementManager
))

local function staffName(by: any): string
	if type(by) == "string" and #by > 0 then
		return string.sub(by, 1, 100)
	elseif type(by) == "number" then
		return tostring(by)
	end
	error("ACF: staff actions need options.By (who is acting), for the audit log", 3)
end

local function round(value: number): number
	return math.floor(value * 10 + 0.5) / 10
end

function EnforcementManager.new(options: Options): EnforcementManager
	local self
	self = setmetatable({
		_bus = options.Bus,
		_registry = options.Registry,
		_logger = options.Logger,
		_clock = options.Clock,
		_id = options.GenerateId,
		_settings = options.Settings,
		_risk = options.Risk,
		_store = options.Store,
		_enforcer = options.Enforcer,
		_users = {},
		_records = {},
		_cases = {},
		_caseOrder = {},
		_audit = AuditLog.new({
			MaxEntries = options.Settings.Audit.MaxEntries,
			RepeatIntervalSeconds = options.Settings.Audit.RepeatIntervalSeconds,
			Now = options.Clock.Now,
			Wall = options.Clock.Wall,
			GenerateId = options.GenerateId,
			Publish = function(entry)
				options.Bus:Publish(Topics.EnforcementAudit, entry)
			end,
		}),
		_running = false,
		_subscriptions = {},
		_stats = {
			Kicks = 0,
			Bans = 0,
			Unbans = 0,
			BansEnforcedOnJoin = 0,
			Corrections = 0,
			Rejections = 0,
			CasesOpened = 0,
			RecordLoadFailures = 0,
			RecordSaveFailures = 0,
		},
	}, EnforcementManager)
	if options.Store == nil then
		options.Logger:Warn("No Store in the environment: bans and kick history last only for this server")
	end

	local function subscribe(topic: string, handler: (any) -> ())
		table.insert(self._subscriptions, options.Bus:Subscribe(topic, handler, "Enforcement"))
	end
	subscribe(Topics.RiskChanged, function(payload)
		if self._running then
			self:_reassess(payload.UserId, false)
		end
	end)
	subscribe(Topics.DetectionReported, function(payload)
		if not self._running then
			return
		end
		local user = self._users[payload.Event.UserId]
		if user and user.Band == "EnforcementReview" and user.InServer then
			-- The detection that caused a removal reaches this handler too (after the
			-- RiskEngine's); only evidence from after the removal counts as new.
			if payload.Event.Time > user.RemovedAt then
				user.AwaitingNewEvidence = false
			end
			self:_reassess(user.UserId, false)
		end
	end)
	subscribe(Topics.PlayerSessionStarted, function(payload)
		if self._running then
			self:_onJoin(payload.Session)
		end
	end)
	subscribe(Topics.PlayerSessionEnded, function(payload)
		local user = self._users[payload.Session.UserId]
		if user then
			user.InServer = false
			user.LeftAt = options.Clock.Now()
		end
	end)
	return self
end

-- Helpers -------------------------------------------------------------------------------

function EnforcementManager._user(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number): User
	local user = self._users[userId]
	if user == nil then
		user = {
			UserId = userId,
			Level = 0,
			Tags = {},
			TagSet = {},
			RestrictedUntil = -math.huge,
			Override = nil,
			OpenCase = nil,
			AwaitingNewEvidence = false,
			RemovedAt = -math.huge,
			LastCorrection = -math.huge,
			InServer = false,
			Band = "Normal",
			RestrictedSince = nil,
			RestrictionReviewed = false,
			LeftAt = nil,
		}
		self._users[userId] = user
	end
	return user
end

function EnforcementManager._shadow(self: EnforcementManager): boolean
	return self._settings.ShadowMode == true
end

function EnforcementManager._appealRef(self: EnforcementManager): string
	local raw = string.gsub(self._id(), "[^%w]", "")
	return "ACF-" .. string.upper(string.sub(raw, -8))
end

function EnforcementManager._player(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number): any?
	local session = self._registry:GetSession(userId)
	return if session then session.Player else nil
end

function EnforcementManager._overrideActive(self: EnforcementManager, user: User): Override?
	local override = user.Override
	if override == nil then
		return nil
	end
	if override.Until ~= nil and self._clock.Now() >= override.Until then
		user.Override = nil
		self._audit:Write("OverrideCleared", { UserId = user.UserId, By = AUTOMATIC, Reason = "expired" })
		return nil
	end
	return override
end

-- Records (persistence) -------------------------------------------------------------------

local function recordKey(userId: number): string
	return `Bans_{userId}`
end

function EnforcementManager._slot(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number): RecordSlot
	local slot = self._records[userId]
	if slot == nil then
		slot = {
			Record = nil,
			Loaded = false,
			Loading = false,
			LoadAttempts = 0,
			Ops = {},
			Dirty = false,
			SaveAttempts = 0,
			Saving = false,
			OnLoaded = {},
		}
		self._records[userId] = slot
		if self._store == nil then
			slot.Record = BanRecords.new(userId)
			slot.Loaded = true
		end
	end
	return slot
end

function EnforcementManager._applyOps(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number, slot: RecordSlot)
	local record = slot.Record :: BanRecords.Record
	local ops = slot.Ops
	slot.Ops = {}
	for _, op in ops do
		op(record)
		slot.Dirty = true
	end
	local callbacks = slot.OnLoaded
	slot.OnLoaded = {}
	for _, callback in callbacks do
		callback(record)
	end
end

function EnforcementManager._load(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number)
	local slot = self:_slot(userId)
	if slot.Loaded or slot.Loading or self._store == nil then
		return
	end
	slot.Loading = true
	slot.LoadAttempts += 1
	local store = self._store :: Types.KeyValueStore
	store:Load(self._settings.Bans.DataStoreName, recordKey(userId), function(ok: boolean, value: any, err: string?)
		slot.Loading = false
		if not ok then
			self._stats.RecordLoadFailures += 1
			if slot.LoadAttempts >= self._settings.Bans.MaxAttempts then
				self._logger:Error("Enforcement record could not be loaded; giving up", { UserId = userId, Error = err })
				self._audit:Write("PersistenceFailed", { UserId = userId, Reason = "load: " .. tostring(err) })
				-- Pending operations are applied to a fresh record rather than lost; the
				-- audit entry says the stored history could not be merged.
				slot.Record = BanRecords.new(userId)
				slot.Loaded = true
				self:_applyOps(userId, slot)
				self:_flush()
			else
				self._logger:Warn("Enforcement record load failed; will retry", { UserId = userId, Error = err })
			end
			return
		end
		slot.Record = BanRecords.fromStored(userId, value) or BanRecords.new(userId)
		slot.Loaded = true
		self:_applyOps(userId, slot)
		if slot.Dirty then
			self:_flush()
		end
	end)
end

-- Runs `op` on the user's record once it is loaded, then saves it.
function EnforcementManager._mutate(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number, op: (BanRecords.Record) -> ())
	local slot = self:_slot(userId)
	table.insert(slot.Ops, op)
	if slot.Loaded then
		self:_applyOps(userId, slot)
		self:_flush()
	else
		self:_load(userId)
	end
end

function EnforcementManager._withRecord(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number, callback: (BanRecords.Record) -> ())
	local slot = self:_slot(userId)
	if slot.Loaded then
		callback(slot.Record :: BanRecords.Record)
		return
	end
	table.insert(slot.OnLoaded, callback)
	self:_load(userId)
end

-- Saves dirty records and retries failed loads that still have work queued.
function EnforcementManager._flush(self: EnforcementManager)
	local store = self._store
	if store == nil then
		for _, slot in self._records do
			slot.Dirty = false
		end
		return
	end
	local bans = self._settings.Bans
	for userId, slot in self._records do
		if not slot.Loaded and not slot.Loading and (#slot.Ops > 0 or #slot.OnLoaded > 0) then
			self:_load(userId)
		elseif slot.Loaded and slot.Dirty and not slot.Saving then
			slot.Dirty = false
			slot.Saving = true
			slot.SaveAttempts += 1
			local record = slot.Record :: BanRecords.Record
			local snapshot = {
				Version = record.Version,
				UserId = record.UserId,
				Active = if record.Active then table.clone(record.Active) else nil,
				History = table.clone(record.History),
			}
			store:Save(bans.DataStoreName, recordKey(userId), snapshot, function(ok: boolean, err: string?)
				slot.Saving = false
				if ok then
					slot.SaveAttempts = 0
					return
				end
				self._stats.RecordSaveFailures += 1
				if slot.SaveAttempts < bans.MaxAttempts then
					self._logger:Warn("Enforcement record save failed; will retry", { UserId = userId, Error = err })
					slot.Dirty = true
				else
					self._logger:Error("Enforcement record save failed; giving up", { UserId = userId, Error = err })
					self._audit:Write("PersistenceFailed", { UserId = userId, Reason = "save: " .. tostring(err) })
					slot.SaveAttempts = 0
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end

-- Pending writes (for shutdown).
function EnforcementManager.Pending(self: EnforcementManager): number
	local count = 0
	for _, slot in self._records do
		if slot.Dirty or slot.Saving or slot.Loading or #slot.Ops > 0 then
			count += 1
		end
	end
	return count
end

-- Decisions ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------

function EnforcementManager._history(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number): (number, number)
	local slot = self._records[userId]
	if slot == nil or not slot.Loaded then
		return 0, 0
	end
	local record = slot.Record :: BanRecords.Record
	local since = self._clock.Wall() - self._settings.Bans.RepeatWindowDays * 86400
	return BanRecords.countSince(record, "Kick", since), BanRecords.countSince(record, "TemporaryBan", -math.huge)
end

function EnforcementManager._setLevel(self: EnforcementManager, user: User, level: number, tags: { string }, report: any, reason: string)
	local tagSet = {}
	for _, tag in tags do
		tagSet[tag] = true
	end
	local now = self._clock.Now()
	if level >= 4 and user.Level < 4 then
		user.RestrictedUntil = now + self._settings.TemporaryRestrictionSeconds
	end
	if level >= 2 and user.RestrictedSince == nil then
		user.RestrictedSince = now
	elseif level < 2 then
		user.RestrictedSince = nil
		user.RestrictionReviewed = false
	end
	local changed = level ~= user.Level or table.concat(tags, ",") ~= table.concat(user.Tags, ",")
	local from = user.Level
	user.Level = level
	user.Tags = tags
	user.TagSet = tagSet
	if changed then
		self._audit:Write("LevelChanged", {
			UserId = user.UserId,
			From = from,
			To = level,
			Level = EnforcementPolicy.levelName(level),
			Band = report.Band,
			Risk = round(report.Risk),
			Tags = table.clone(tags),
			Reason = reason,
			Shadow = self:_shadow(),
		})
	end
end

function EnforcementManager._openCase(self: EnforcementManager, user: User, caseType: string, proposed: number?, reason: string, report: any)
	local existing = if user.OpenCase then self._cases[user.OpenCase] else nil
	if existing and (existing.Status == "Open" or existing.Status == "Shadow") then
		if proposed and (existing.ProposedLevel or 0) < proposed then
			existing.Type = "Action"
			existing.ProposedLevel = proposed
			existing.Reason = reason
			existing.Explanation = report.Explanation
			existing.Risk = report.Risk
			existing.Band = report.Band
			self._audit:Write("CaseUpdated", { UserId = user.UserId, CaseId = existing.CaseId, Level = proposed, Reason = reason, Shadow = existing.Status == "Shadow" })
			self._bus:Publish(Topics.EnforcementCase, table.freeze(table.clone(existing)))
		end
		return
	end
	local caseId = self:_appealRef()
	local case: Case = {
		CaseId = caseId,
		AppealRef = caseId,
		UserId = user.UserId,
		Type = caseType,
		ProposedLevel = proposed,
		Status = if self:_shadow() then "Shadow" else "Open",
		Reason = reason,
		Explanation = report.Explanation,
		Risk = report.Risk,
		Band = report.Band,
		CreatedAt = self._clock.Wall(),
		CreatedTime = self._clock.Now(),
	}
	self._cases[caseId] = case
	table.insert(self._caseOrder, caseId)
	user.OpenCase = caseId
	self._stats.CasesOpened += 1
	self:_trimCases()
	self._audit:Write("CaseOpened", {
		UserId = user.UserId,
		CaseId = caseId,
		Level = proposed,
		Band = report.Band,
		Risk = round(report.Risk),
		Reason = reason,
		Shadow = case.Status == "Shadow",
	})
	self._bus:Publish(Topics.EnforcementCase, table.freeze(table.clone(case)))
end

function EnforcementManager._trimCases(self: EnforcementManager)
	local max = self._settings.Cases.MaxOpen
	if #self._caseOrder <= max then
		return
	end
	-- Drop the oldest closed cases first, then the oldest open ones.
	for pass = 1, 2 do
		local index = 1
		while #self._caseOrder > max and index <= #self._caseOrder do
			local case = self._cases[self._caseOrder[index]]
			local closed = case.Status ~= "Open" and case.Status ~= "Shadow"
			if pass == 2 or closed then
				self._cases[case.CaseId] = nil
				table.remove(self._caseOrder, index)
			else
				index += 1
			end
		end
	end
end

-- Re-evaluates one user from their current risk.
function EnforcementManager._reassess(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number, joining: boolean)
	local risk = self._risk
	if risk == nil then
		return
	end
	-- A departed player who has already been forgotten has nothing to restrict; tracking
	-- them again on every decaying band change would grow memory without bound.
	if self._users[userId] == nil and not joining and self._registry:GetSession(userId) == nil then
		return
	end
	local user = self:_user(userId)
	local report = risk:Get(userId)
	local previousBand = user.Band
	user.Band = report.Band
	local rising = BAND_ORDER[report.Band] > BAND_ORDER[previousBand]
	local present = user.InServer or joining
	local override = self:_overrideActive(user)
	if override and override.Exempt then
		self:_setLevel(user, 0, {}, report, "override: exempt")
		return
	end
	local kicks, tempBans = self:_history(userId)
	local decision = EnforcementPolicy.decide({
		Band = report.Band,
		Risk = report.Risk,
		Confidence = report.Confidence,
		Groups = report.Groups,
		RecentKicks = kicks,
		TemporaryBans = tempBans,
	}, self._settings)

	local level = math.min(decision.Level, 4)
	if override and override.Level then
		level = override.Level
	end
	self:_setLevel(user, level, decision.Tags, report, if override then "override: level" else decision.Reason)

	-- Review cases only when the band RISES into Investigation for a present player:
	-- decaying through the band, or having left, is no reason to call staff.
	if report.Band == "Investigation" and decision.Proposed == nil and rising and present then
		self:_openCase(user, "Review", nil, decision.Reason, report)
	end
	if not user.InServer and not joining then
		return
	end
	-- After a removal, wait for new evidence before removing again.
	if user.AwaitingNewEvidence then
		return
	end
	if decision.Level >= 5 then
		if self:_shadow() then
			self._audit:Write("ShadowAction", {
				UserId = userId,
				Level = EnforcementPolicy.levelName(decision.Level),
				Band = report.Band,
				Risk = round(report.Risk),
				Reason = decision.Reason,
				Shadow = true,
			}, EnforcementPolicy.levelName(decision.Level))
			return
		end
		self:_execute(userId, decision.Level, AUTOMATIC, decision.Reason, nil, nil)
	elseif decision.Proposed then
		self:_openCase(user, "Action", decision.Proposed, decision.Reason, report)
	end
end

-- Executes a removal (5 kick, 6 temporary ban, 7 permanent ban).
function EnforcementManager._execute(
	self: EnforcementManager,
	userId: number,
	level: number,
	by: string,
	reason: string,
	approvedBy: string?,
	hours: number?
): string
	local settings = self._settings
	local appeal = self:_appealRef()
	local user = self:_user(userId)
	user.AwaitingNewEvidence = true
	user.RemovedAt = self._clock.Now()
	local wall = self._clock.Wall()
	local player = self:_player(userId)
	local enforcer = self._enforcer

	if level == 5 then
		self._stats.Kicks += 1
		self:_mutate(userId, function(record)
			BanRecords.addHistory(record, { Type = "Kick", At = wall, AppealRef = appeal, Reason = reason, By = by }, settings.Bans.MaxHistory)
		end)
		self._audit:Write("Kicked", { UserId = userId, AppealRef = appeal, By = by, ApprovedBy = approvedBy, Reason = reason, Offline = player == nil })
		if player and enforcer then
			enforcer:Kick(player, BanRecords.format(settings.Messages.Kick, appeal, nil))
		end
		return appeal
	end

	-- Bans: level 6 temporary, level 7 permanent. The ban takes effect immediately; the
	-- record write follows when the stored record has loaded.
	self._stats.Bans += 1
	local expiresAt: number? = nil
	if level == 6 then
		local slot = self._records[userId]
		local known = if slot and slot.Loaded then slot.Record else nil
		local duration = hours
			or BanRecords.tempBanHours(
				known or BanRecords.new(userId),
				settings.Bans.TempBanHours,
				settings.Bans.EscalationFactor,
				settings.Bans.MaxTempBanHours
			)
		expiresAt = wall + duration * 3600
	end
	local message = BanRecords.format(
		if level == 6 then settings.Messages.TempBan else settings.Messages.PermanentBan,
		appeal,
		expiresAt
	)
	self:_mutate(userId, function(record)
		record.Active = {
			Level = level,
			AppealRef = appeal,
			Reason = reason,
			By = by,
			IssuedAt = wall,
			ExpiresAt = expiresAt,
			ApprovedBy = approvedBy,
		}
		BanRecords.addHistory(record, {
			Type = if level == 6 then "TemporaryBan" else "PermanentBan",
			At = wall,
			AppealRef = appeal,
			Reason = reason,
			By = by,
			ExpiresAt = expiresAt,
		}, settings.Bans.MaxHistory)
	end)
	self._audit:Write("Banned", {
		UserId = userId,
		AppealRef = appeal,
		Level = EnforcementPolicy.levelName(level),
		By = by,
		ApprovedBy = approvedBy,
		Reason = reason,
		ExpiresAt = expiresAt,
		Offline = player == nil,
	})
	local banApi = if enforcer then enforcer.Ban else nil
	if banApi and settings.Bans.UseRobloxBanApi then
		local seconds = if expiresAt then math.max(1, math.floor(expiresAt - wall)) else nil
		banApi(enforcer, userId, seconds, message, `{appeal}: {reason}`, function(ok: boolean, err: string?)
			if not ok then
				self._logger:Error("Roblox ban API call failed; the ACF record still enforces the ban on join", {
					UserId = userId,
					Error = err,
				})
			end
		end)
	end
	if player and enforcer then
		enforcer:Kick(player, message)
	end
	return appeal
end

-- Join: enforce an active ban, expire an old one, and resume restrictions.
function EnforcementManager._onJoin(self: EnforcementManager, session: any)
	local userId = session.UserId
	local user = self:_user(userId)
	user.InServer = true
	user.LeftAt = nil
	if self._settings.Bans.CheckOnJoin then
		self:_withRecord(userId, function(record)
			local wall = self._clock.Wall()
			if BanRecords.expire(record, wall, self._settings.Bans.MaxHistory) then
				self:_slot(userId).Dirty = true
				self._audit:Write("BanExpired", { UserId = userId })
				self:_flush()
			end
			local ban = BanRecords.activeBan(record, wall)
			local current = self._registry:GetSession(userId)
			if ban and current and current.SessionId == session.SessionId and self._enforcer then
				self._stats.BansEnforcedOnJoin += 1
				self._audit:Write("BanEnforced", { UserId = userId, AppealRef = ban.AppealRef, ExpiresAt = ban.ExpiresAt })
				local template = if ban.Level == 6 then self._settings.Messages.TempBan else self._settings.Messages.PermanentBan
				local enforcer = self._enforcer :: Types.Enforcer
				enforcer:Kick(current.Player, BanRecords.format(template, ban.AppealRef, ban.ExpiresAt))
			end
		end)
	end
	self:_reassess(userId, true)
end

-- Whether nothing about a departed user still needs memory.
function EnforcementManager._forgettable(self: EnforcementManager, user: User, now: number): boolean
	if user.InServer or user.LeftAt == nil or now - user.LeftAt < self._settings.ForgetAfterSeconds then
		return false
	end
	if user.Override ~= nil then
		return false
	end
	-- Open cases do not pin a user: they refer to the UserId, and approving one works
	-- without in-memory state.
	local slot = self._records[user.UserId]
	return slot == nil or not (slot.Dirty or slot.Saving or slot.Loading or #slot.Ops > 0 or #slot.OnLoaded > 0)
end

-- Periodic: expire temporary restrictions, overrides and old cases; send long
-- restrictions for review; forget departed users.
function EnforcementManager.Tick(self: EnforcementManager)
	local now = self._clock.Now()
	local reviewAfter = self._settings.Cases.ReviewAfterRestrictedSeconds
	for userId, user in self._users do
		self:_overrideActive(user)
		if user.Level >= 4 and user.RestrictedUntil <= now then
			self:_reassess(userId, false)
		end
		local since = user.RestrictedSince
		if
			user.InServer
			and since ~= nil
			and not user.RestrictionReviewed
			and now - since >= reviewAfter
			and self._risk ~= nil
		then
			user.RestrictionReviewed = true
			local report = (self._risk :: any):Get(userId)
			self:_openCase(user, "Review", nil, `restricted for {math.floor((now - since) / 60)} min (level {user.Level})`, report)
		end
		if self:_forgettable(user, now) then
			self._users[userId] = nil
			if self._store ~= nil then
				self._records[userId] = nil
			end
		end
	end
	-- Record slots of users no longer tracked (offline bans, lookups) once idle. Without a
	-- Store the slot is the only copy of a ban, so it is kept.
	if self._store ~= nil then
		for userId, slot in self._records do
			if self._users[userId] == nil and not (slot.Dirty or slot.Saving or slot.Loading or #slot.Ops > 0 or #slot.OnLoaded > 0) then
				self._records[userId] = nil
			end
		end
	end
	local expiry = self._settings.Cases.ExpiryHours * 3600
	local wall = self._clock.Wall()
	for _, caseId in self._caseOrder do
		local case = self._cases[caseId]
```
