```luau
		if (case.Status == "Open" or case.Status == "Shadow") and wall - case.CreatedAt >= expiry then
			case.Status = "Expired"
			self._audit:Write("CaseExpired", { UserId = case.UserId, CaseId = caseId })
			local user = self._users[case.UserId]
			if user and user.OpenCase == caseId then
				user.OpenCase = nil
			end
		end
	end
	self:_flush()
end

-- Questions modules ask (context.Enforcement) -----------------------------------------------

-- Whether operations tagged `tag` (a risk group name, or true for "any") are restricted.
function EnforcementManager.IsRestricted(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number, tag: string | boolean, operation: string?): boolean
	local user = self._users[userId]
	if user == nil or user.Level < 2 then
		return false
	end
	local override = self:_overrideActive(user)
	if override and override.Exempt then
		return false
	end
	local restricted = false
	if user.Level >= 4 and user.RestrictedUntil > self._clock.Now() then
		restricted = true
	elseif tag == true then
		restricted = #user.Tags > 0
	elseif type(tag) == "string" then
		restricted = user.TagSet[tag] == true
	end
	if not restricted then
		return false
	end
	local shadow = self:_shadow()
	self._audit:Write("Rejected", {
		UserId = userId,
		Tags = table.clone(user.Tags),
		Reason = operation,
		Level = EnforcementPolicy.levelName(user.Level),
		Shadow = shadow,
	}, tostring(operation))
	if shadow then
		return false
	end
	self._stats.Rejections += 1
	return true
end

-- Whether Movement may correct this player now (consumes the cooldown when true).
function EnforcementManager.ShouldCorrect(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number): boolean
	local correction = self._settings.Correction
	local user = self._users[userId]
	if not correction.Enabled or user == nil or user.Level < 3 or not user.TagSet.Movement then
		return false
	end
	local override = self:_overrideActive(user)
	if override and override.Exempt then
		return false
	end
	local now = self._clock.Now()
	if now - user.LastCorrection < correction.CooldownSeconds then
		return false
	end
	if self:_shadow() then
		self._audit:Write("Corrected", { UserId = userId, Shadow = true }, "movement")
		return false
	end
	user.LastCorrection = now
	return true
end

function EnforcementManager.NoteCorrected(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number, details: { [string]: any })
	self._stats.Corrections += 1
	self._audit:Write("Corrected", { UserId = userId, Details = details }, "movement")
end

-- Staff and API ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

function EnforcementManager.Approve(self: EnforcementManager, caseId: string, options: { By: any, Level: number?, Note: string?, Hours: number? })
	local by = staffName(options.By)
	local case = self._cases[caseId]
	if case == nil then
		error(`ACF: unknown case '{tostring(caseId)}'`, 2)
	end
	if case.Status ~= "Open" then
		error(`ACF: case {caseId} is {case.Status}, not Open`, 2)
	end
	local level = options.Level or case.ProposedLevel
	if level == nil or level < 5 or level > 7 then
		error("ACF: Approve needs a removal Level of 5 (kick), 6 (temporary ban) or 7 (permanent ban)", 2)
	end
	case.Status = "Approved"
	case.DecidedBy = by
	case.DecidedAt = self._clock.Wall()
	case.Note = options.Note
	case.ExecutedLevel = level
	local user = self:_user(case.UserId)
	if user.OpenCase == caseId then
		user.OpenCase = nil
	end
	self._audit:Write("CaseApproved", { UserId = case.UserId, CaseId = caseId, Level = EnforcementPolicy.levelName(level), By = by, Reason = options.Note })
	self:_execute(case.UserId, level, by, `case {caseId}: {case.Reason}`, by, options.Hours)
	self._bus:Publish(Topics.EnforcementCase, table.freeze(table.clone(case)))
end

function EnforcementManager.Reject(self: EnforcementManager, caseId: string, options: { By: any, Note: string? })
	local by = staffName(options.By)
	local case = self._cases[caseId]
	if case == nil then
		error(`ACF: unknown case '{tostring(caseId)}'`, 2)
	end
	if case.Status ~= "Open" and case.Status ~= "Shadow" then
		error(`ACF: case {caseId} is {case.Status}`, 2)
	end
	case.Status = "Rejected"
	case.DecidedBy = by
	case.DecidedAt = self._clock.Wall()
	case.Note = options.Note
	local user = self._users[case.UserId]
	if user and user.OpenCase == caseId then
		user.OpenCase = nil
	end
	self._audit:Write("CaseRejected", { UserId = case.UserId, CaseId = caseId, By = by, Reason = options.Note })
	self._bus:Publish(Topics.EnforcementCase, table.freeze(table.clone(case)))
end

function EnforcementManager.Kick(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number, options: { By: any, Reason: string? }): string
	local by = staffName(options.By)
	return self:_execute(userId, 5, by, options.Reason or "staff kick", by, nil)
end

-- options.Hours = nil bans permanently.
function EnforcementManager.Ban(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number, options: { By: any, Reason: string?, Hours: number? }): string
	local by = staffName(options.By)
	local hours = options.Hours
	if hours ~= nil and (type(hours) ~= "number" or hours <= 0 or hours ~= hours) then
		error("ACF: Ban Hours must be a positive number (omit it for a permanent ban)", 2)
	end
	return self:_execute(userId, if hours then 6 else 7, by, options.Reason or "staff ban", by, hours)
end

function EnforcementManager.Unban(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number, options: { By: any, Reason: string? })
	local by = staffName(options.By)
	self._stats.Unbans += 1
	local wall = self._clock.Wall()
	local bans = self._settings.Bans
	self:_mutate(userId, function(record)
		local previous = record.Active
		record.Active = nil
		BanRecords.addHistory(record, {
			Type = "Unban",
			At = wall,
			AppealRef = if previous then previous.AppealRef else nil,
			Reason = options.Reason,
			By = by,
		}, bans.MaxHistory)
	end)
	local enforcer = self._enforcer
	local unbanApi = if enforcer then enforcer.Unban else nil
	if unbanApi and bans.UseRobloxBanApi then
		unbanApi(enforcer, userId, function(ok: boolean, err: string?)
			if not ok then
				self._logger:Error("Roblox unban API call failed", { UserId = userId, Error = err })
			end
		end)
	end
	self._audit:Write("Unbanned", { UserId = userId, By = by, Reason = options.Reason })
end

-- options: { Exempt = true } or { Level = 0-4 }, plus Seconds?, By, Reason?.
function EnforcementManager.SetOverride(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number, options: { [string]: any })
	local by = staffName(options.By)
	local level = options.Level
	if options.Exempt ~= true and (type(level) ~= "number" or level < 0 or level > 4 or level ~= math.floor(level)) then
		error("ACF: SetOverride needs Exempt = true or a Level from 0 to 4 (use Kick/Ban for removals)", 2)
	end
	local seconds = options.Seconds
	if seconds ~= nil and (type(seconds) ~= "number" or seconds <= 0) then
		error("ACF: SetOverride Seconds must be a positive number", 2)
	end
	local user = self:_user(userId)
	user.Override = {
		Exempt = options.Exempt == true,
		Level = if options.Exempt == true then nil else level,
		Until = if seconds then self._clock.Now() + seconds else nil,
		By = by,
		Reason = options.Reason,
	}
	self._audit:Write("OverrideSet", {
		UserId = userId,
		By = by,
		Reason = options.Reason,
		Level = if options.Exempt == true then "Exempt" else EnforcementPolicy.levelName(level),
		Seconds = seconds,
	})
	self:_reassess(userId, false)
	if self._risk == nil then
		user.Level = if options.Exempt == true then 0 else level
	end
end

function EnforcementManager.ClearOverride(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number, options: { By: any, Reason: string? })
	local by = staffName(options.By)
	local user = self:_user(userId)
	user.Override = nil
	self._audit:Write("OverrideCleared", { UserId = userId, By = by, Reason = options.Reason })
	self:_reassess(userId, false)
end

function EnforcementManager.Get(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number): { [string]: any }
	local user = self._users[userId]
	local slot = self._records[userId]
	local record = if slot and slot.Loaded then slot.Record else nil
	local now = self._clock.Now()
	local override = if user then self:_overrideActive(user) else nil
	local level = if user then user.Level else 0
	return {
		UserId = userId,
		Level = level,
		LevelName = EnforcementPolicy.levelName(level),
		Tags = if user then table.clone(user.Tags) else {},
		RestrictedFor = if user and user.Level >= 4 then math.max(0, user.RestrictedUntil - now) else 0,
		Shadow = self:_shadow(),
		Override = if override then table.clone(override) else nil,
		OpenCase = if user then user.OpenCase else nil,
		RecordLoaded = record ~= nil,
		Ban = if record and BanRecords.activeBan(record, self._clock.Wall()) then table.clone(record.Active :: any) else nil,
		History = if record then table.clone(record.History) else nil,
	}
end

function EnforcementManager.GetCases(self: EnforcementManager, filter: { Status: string?, UserId: number? }?): { Case }
	local list = {}
	for _, caseId in self._caseOrder do
		local case = self._cases[caseId]
		if filter then
			if filter.Status ~= nil and case.Status ~= filter.Status then
				continue
			end
			if filter.UserId ~= nil and case.UserId ~= filter.UserId then
				continue
			end
		end
		table.insert(list, table.freeze(table.clone(case)))
	end
	return list
end

function EnforcementManager.GetAudit(self: EnforcementManager, filter: any?): { AuditLog.Entry }
	return self._audit:Get(filter)
end

-- Loads (if needed) and returns a user's record through `callback(record)`.
function EnforcementManager.LoadRecord(self: EnforcementManager, userId: number, callback: (any) -> ())
	self:_withRecord(userId, function(record)
		callback({
			UserId = record.UserId,
			Ban = BanRecords.activeBan(record, self._clock.Wall()),
			History = table.clone(record.History),
		})
	end)
end

function EnforcementManager.GetStats(self: EnforcementManager): { [string]: any }
	local stats = table.clone(self._stats) :: { [string]: any }
	local open, levels = 0, {}
	for _, case in self._cases do
		if case.Status == "Open" then
			open += 1
		end
	end
	for _, user in self._users do
		if user.Level > 0 then
			local name = EnforcementPolicy.levelName(user.Level)
			levels[name] = (levels[name] or 0) + 1
		end
	end
	stats.OpenCases = open
	stats.Levels = levels
	stats.Shadow = self:_shadow()
	stats.Mode = self._settings.Mode
	stats.PendingWrites = self:Pending()
	local users, records = 0, 0
	for _ in self._users do
		users += 1
	end
	for _ in self._records do
		records += 1
	end
	stats.Users = users
	stats.Records = records
	stats.Audit = self._audit:GetStats()
	return stats
end

-- Lifecycle -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

function EnforcementManager.Start(self: EnforcementManager, scheduler: any, cleanup: any, onShutdown: any?)
	self._running = true
	cleanup:Add(scheduler:Every("ACF.Enforcement", self._settings.Bans.FlushIntervalSeconds, function()
		self:Tick()
	end))
	if onShutdown and not (self :: any)._shutdownRegistered then
		(self :: any)._shutdownRegistered = true
		onShutdown(function()
			self:_flush()
		end, function(): number
			return self:Pending()
		end)
	end
end

function EnforcementManager.Stop(self: EnforcementManager)
	self:_flush()
	self._running = false
end

function EnforcementManager.Destroy(self: EnforcementManager)
	self._running = false
	for _, subscription in self._subscriptions do
		subscription:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(self._subscriptions)
end

return EnforcementManager

```

`src/Server/Enforcement/EnforcementPolicy.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Pure enforcement policy: risk report + offence history + configuration -> decision.
	No clocks and no side effects; EnforcementManager applies the result.

	Levels:
	  0 Logging                1 Increased monitoring      2 Reject suspicious actions
	  3 Server-side correction 4 Temporary restriction     5 Kick
	  6 Temporary ban          7 Permanent ban

	Levels 0-4 never remove a player. Their only effects are on tagged operations
	(restriction tags) and movement correction. They come from the risk band
	(Enforcement.BandLevels) in Hybrid and Manual modes.

	Levels 5-7 are proposed only at the EnforcementReview band. The candidate escalates
	with the offence history: kicks within RepeatWindowDays lead to a temporary ban, and
	temporary bans to a permanent-ban proposal. A candidate runs automatically only when
	ALL of these hold:
	  - Mode = Hybrid;
	  - its switch is on (AutoKick for 5; AutoBan for 6; AutoBan and NOT
	    RequireApprovalForPermanentBan for 7);
	  - the evidence is corroborated:
	      * confidence >= MinConfidence, AND
	      * one non-statistical group >= HardEvidenceMinScore, OR >= MinIndependentGroups
	        qualifying groups of which at least one is non-statistical.
	    Behavioural statistics (Aim, Economy, Automation) alone never corroborate.
	Otherwise it becomes a proposal awaiting staff approval (a case).
]]

local EnforcementPolicy = {}

EnforcementPolicy.LEVEL_NAMES = table.freeze({
	[0] = "Logging",
	[1] = "Monitoring",
	[2] = "Rejection",
	[3] = "Correction",
	[4] = "Restriction",
	[5] = "Kick",
	[6] = "TemporaryBan",
	[7] = "PermanentBan",
})

export type Input = {
	Band: string,
	Risk: number,
	Confidence: number,
	Groups: { { Group: string, Score: number } },
	RecentKicks: number,
	TemporaryBans: number,
}

export type Decision = {
	-- Level applied automatically (0-4, or 5-7 when allowed).
	Level: number,
	-- Groups at or above TagMinScore: operations tagged with these are restricted.
	Tags: { string },
	-- Level 5-7 candidate needing staff approval, or nil.
	Proposed: number?,
	Corroborated: boolean,
	Reason: string,
}

function EnforcementPolicy.levelName(level: number): string
	return EnforcementPolicy.LEVEL_NAMES[level] or tostring(level)
end

-- Whether the evidence may justify an automatic removal. Returns (ok, why).
function EnforcementPolicy.corroborated(input: Input, config: { [string]: any }): (boolean, string)
	local rules = config.Corroboration
	local statistical = config.StatisticalGroups
	if input.Confidence < rules.MinConfidence then
		return false, `confidence {math.floor(input.Confidence * 100) / 100} < {rules.MinConfidence}`
	end
	local qualifying, hardQualifying = 0, 0
	for _, group in input.Groups do
		local isStatistical = statistical[group.Group] == true
		if not isStatistical and group.Score >= rules.HardEvidenceMinScore then
			return true, `strong {group.Group} evidence ({math.floor(group.Score)})`
		end
		if group.Score >= config.TagMinScore then
			qualifying += 1
			if not isStatistical then
				hardQualifying += 1
			end
		end
	end
	if qualifying >= rules.MinIndependentGroups and hardQualifying >= 1 then
		return true, `{qualifying} independent groups`
	end
	if hardQualifying == 0 and qualifying > 0 then
		return false, "only statistical evidence"
	end
	return false, `{qualifying} independent group(s), need {rules.MinIndependentGroups}`
end

function EnforcementPolicy.decide(input: Input, config: { [string]: any }): Decision
	local tags = {}
	for _, group in input.Groups do
		if group.Score >= config.TagMinScore then
			table.insert(tags, group.Group)
		end
	end
	if config.Mode == "Off" then
		return { Level = 0, Tags = tags, Proposed = nil, Corroborated = false, Reason = "enforcement mode Off" }
	end

	local level = config.BandLevels[input.Band] or 0
	local ok, why = EnforcementPolicy.corroborated(input, config)
	if input.Band ~= "EnforcementReview" then
		return { Level = level, Tags = tags, Proposed = nil, Corroborated = ok, Reason = `band {input.Band}` }
	end

	local bans = config.Bans
	local candidate = 5
	if input.TemporaryBans >= bans.TempBansBeforePermanentProposal then
		candidate = 7
	elseif input.RecentKicks >= bans.KicksBeforeTempBan then
		candidate = 6
	end

	local automatic = false
	if config.Mode == "Hybrid" and ok then
		if candidate == 5 then
			automatic = config.AutoKick
		elseif candidate == 6 then
			automatic = config.AutoBan
		else
			automatic = config.AutoBan and not bans.RequireApprovalForPermanentBan
		end
	end

	local reason = `band EnforcementReview; {why}`
	if automatic then
		return { Level = candidate, Tags = tags, Proposed = nil, Corroborated = ok, Reason = reason }
	end
	return { Level = level, Tags = tags, Proposed = candidate, Corroborated = ok, Reason = reason }
end

return EnforcementPolicy

```

`src/Server/Intelligence/EvidenceManager.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Bounded, per-user evidence store.

	Evidence is keyed by UserId (not SessionId), so leaving and rejoining the same server
	does not wipe a player's recent evidence. Each record keeps the SessionId it was
	first seen in.

	Duplicate suppression: a detection with the same (Detector, Kind) as an existing
	record whose LastSeen is within DuplicateWindowSeconds is merged into that record
	(Count += 1, Severity/Confidence take the maximum, Details take the latest) instead of
	creating a new record. A flood of one signal therefore costs O(1) memory.

	Bounds: at most MaxRecordsPerPlayer records per user (the least recently seen record
	is evicted), and records not seen for RetentionSeconds are pruned. A user's store is
	dropped once it is empty.

	This module stores and summarises evidence. It does not score risk (Phase 6) or
	enforce anything (Phase 7). Persistence across servers is Phase 6.
]]

local Logger = require(script.Parent.Parent.Core.Logger)
local Types = require(script.Parent.Parent.Types)

type DetectionEvent = Types.DetectionEvent
type EvidenceRecord = Types.EvidenceRecord

type MutableRecord = {
	Id: string,
	UserId: number,
	SessionId: string,
	Detector: string,
	Category: Types.DetectionCategory,
	Kind: string,
	Severity: number,
	Confidence: number,
	Count: number,
	FirstSeen: number,
	LastSeen: number,
	FirstWallTime: number,
	LastWallTime: number,
	FirstEventId: string,
	Details: { [string]: any },
}

type Store = {
	records: { MutableRecord },
	-- (Detector|Kind) -> newest record with that key
	latest: { [string]: MutableRecord },
	totalDetections: number,
	merged: number,
	evicted: number,
	expired: number,
}

export type EvidenceSummary = {
	UserId: number,
	Records: number,
	TotalDetections: number,
	Merged: number,
	Evicted: number,
	Expired: number,
	ByCategory: { [string]: number },
}

export type EvidenceSettings = {
	MaxRecordsPerPlayer: number,
	RetentionSeconds: number,
	DuplicateWindowSeconds: number,
}

local EvidenceManager = {}
EvidenceManager.__index = EvidenceManager

export type EvidenceManager = typeof(setmetatable(
	{} :: {
		_clock: Types.Clock,
		_logger: Logger.Logger,
		_generateId: () -> string,
		_settings: EvidenceSettings,
		_stores: { [number]: Store },
	},
	EvidenceManager
))

export type EvidenceManagerOptions = {
	Clock: Types.Clock,
	Logger: Logger.Logger,
	GenerateId: () -> string,
	Settings: EvidenceSettings,
}

function EvidenceManager.new(options: EvidenceManagerOptions): EvidenceManager
	return setmetatable({
		_clock = options.Clock,
		_logger = options.Logger,
		_generateId = options.GenerateId,
		_settings = options.Settings,
		_stores = {},
	}, EvidenceManager)
end

local function snapshot(record: MutableRecord): EvidenceRecord
	return table.freeze(table.clone(record)) :: any
end

local function getStore(self: EvidenceManager, userId: number): Store
	local store = self._stores[userId]
	if store == nil then
		store = { records = {}, latest = {}, totalDetections = 0, merged = 0, evicted = 0, expired = 0 }
		self._stores[userId] = store
	end
	return store
end

local function removeAt(store: Store, index: number)
	local record = table.remove(store.records, index) :: MutableRecord
	local key = record.Detector .. "|" .. record.Kind
	if store.latest[key] == record then
		store.latest[key] = nil
	end
end

-- Records a detection event. Returns a frozen snapshot of the resulting record and
-- whether a new record was created (false = merged into an existing one).
function EvidenceManager.Record(self: EvidenceManager, event: DetectionEvent): (EvidenceRecord, boolean)
	local settings = self._settings
	local store = getStore(self, event.UserId)
	store.totalDetections += 1

	local key = event.Detector .. "|" .. event.Kind
	local existing = store.latest[key]
	if existing and event.Time - existing.LastSeen <= settings.DuplicateWindowSeconds then
		existing.Count += 1
		existing.LastSeen = event.Time
		existing.LastWallTime = event.WallTime
		existing.Severity = math.max(existing.Severity, event.Severity)
		existing.Confidence = math.max(existing.Confidence, event.Confidence)
		existing.Details = event.Details
		store.merged += 1
		return snapshot(existing), false
	end

	local record: MutableRecord = {
		Id = self._generateId(),
		UserId = event.UserId,
		SessionId = event.SessionId,
		Detector = event.Detector,
		Category = event.Category,
		Kind = event.Kind,
		Severity = event.Severity,
		Confidence = event.Confidence,
		Count = 1,
		FirstSeen = event.Time,
		LastSeen = event.Time,
		FirstWallTime = event.WallTime,
		LastWallTime = event.WallTime,
		FirstEventId = event.Id,
		Details = event.Details,
	}
	table.insert(store.records, record)
	store.latest[key] = record

	if #store.records > settings.MaxRecordsPerPlayer then
		local oldestIndex, oldestSeen = 1, math.huge
		for index, candidate in store.records do
			if candidate.LastSeen < oldestSeen then
				oldestIndex, oldestSeen = index, candidate.LastSeen
			end
		end
		removeAt(store, oldestIndex)
		store.evicted += 1
	end

	return snapshot(record), true
end

-- Frozen snapshots of a user's records, oldest first. Optional filter by Detector,
-- Category, or minimum LastSeen.
function EvidenceManager.Get(
	self: EvidenceManager,
	userId: number,
	filter: { Detector: string?, Category: string?, Since: number? }?
): { EvidenceRecord }
	local store = self._stores[userId]
	local result = {}
	if store == nil then
		return result
	end
	for _, record in store.records do
		if filter then
			if filter.Detector and record.Detector ~= filter.Detector then
				continue
			end
			if filter.Category and record.Category ~= filter.Category then
				continue
			end
			if filter.Since and record.LastSeen < filter.Since then
				continue
			end
		end
		table.insert(result, snapshot(record))
	end
	return result
end

function EvidenceManager.Summary(self: EvidenceManager, userId: number): EvidenceSummary
	local store = self._stores[userId]
	local byCategory: { [string]: number } = {}
	if store == nil then
		return { UserId = userId, Records = 0, TotalDetections = 0, Merged = 0, Evicted = 0, Expired = 0, ByCategory = byCategory }
	end
	for _, record in store.records do
		byCategory[record.Category] = (byCategory[record.Category] or 0) + record.Count
	end
	return {
		UserId = userId,
		Records = #store.records,
		TotalDetections = store.totalDetections,
		Merged = store.merged,
		Evicted = store.evicted,
		Expired = store.expired,
		ByCategory = byCategory,
	}
end

-- Removes records not seen within RetentionSeconds and drops empty stores.
-- Returns the number of records removed.
function EvidenceManager.Prune(self: EvidenceManager): number
	local now = self._clock.Now()
	local cutoff = now - self._settings.RetentionSeconds
	local removed = 0
	for userId, store in self._stores do
		local records = store.records
		for index = #records, 1, -1 do
			if records[index].LastSeen < cutoff then
				removeAt(store, index)
				store.expired += 1
				removed += 1
			end
		end
		if #records == 0 then
			self._stores[userId] = nil
		end
	end
	if removed > 0 then
		self._logger:Debug("Expired evidence pruned", { Removed = removed })
	end
	return removed
end

function EvidenceManager.Clear(self: EvidenceManager, userId: number?)
	if userId == nil then
		table.clear(self._stores)
	else
		self._stores[userId] = nil
	end
end

function EvidenceManager.GetStats(self: EvidenceManager): { Users: number, Records: number }
	local users, records = 0, 0
	for _, store in self._stores do
		users += 1
		records += #store.records
	end
	return { Users = users, Records = records }
end

return EvidenceManager

```

`src/Server/Intelligence/RiskEngine.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	RiskEngine: centralised risk assessment (Phase 6). A core service, not a module:
	it consumes every accepted detection (ACF.DetectionReported) from every module and
	keeps one risk score per user. The mathematics lives in RiskModel.

	Responsibilities:
	  - Confidence-weighted, exponentially decaying evidence per (Detector, Kind) key.
	  - Duplicate suppression: repeats of one key within RepeatSuppressSeconds are not
	    added; repeats beyond that saturate (RiskModel.add).
	  - Correlation: keys are grouped (Risk.Groups) so related detections count once.
	  - Bands: Normal / Increased / Restrict / Investigation / EnforcementReview;
	    ACF.RiskChanged is published whenever a user's band changes (up or down).
	  - Session risk keyed by UserId within this server, so leaving and rejoining does
	    not reset it; it decays like any other evidence.
	  - Persistent risk (opt-in, Risk.Persistence): a session that ends at or above
	    MinRiskToSave is saved, in batches, with retries. On the next join, the saved risk
	    (weighted, decayed by wall-clock time, capped) seeds the session as "History".
	    Storage failures never raise risk: a failed load means no history.
	  - Evidence history and explanations for every user.

	This service never punishes anyone. Phase 7 enforcement consumes RiskChanged.
]]

local EventBus = require(script.Parent.Parent.Core.EventBus)
local Topics = require(script.Parent.Parent.Core.Topics)
local RiskModel = require(script.Parent.RiskModel)
local Types = require(script.Parent.Parent.Types)

local RECORD_VERSION = 1
local PRUNE_SCORE = 0.05

export type HistoryEntry = {
	Time: number,
	WallTime: number,
	Type: string, -- "Detection" | "Band" | "History" | "Reset"
	Detector: string?,
	Kind: string?,
	Group: string?,
	Contribution: number?,
	Risk: number,
	Band: string,
	Note: string?,
}

type User = {
	UserId: number,
	Keys: { [string]: RiskModel.Key },
	KeyCount: number,
	Persistent: number,
	Risk: number,
	Peak: number,
	Confidence: number,
	Band: string,
	Groups: { RiskModel.GroupSummary },
	History: { HistoryEntry },
	InServer: boolean,
	SessionId: string?,
	Loaded: boolean,
	Stored: { [string]: any }?,
}

type QueueItem = { UserId: number, Record: { [string]: any }, Attempts: number }

export type Options = {
	Bus: EventBus.EventBus,
	Registry: any,
	Logger: any,
	Clock: Types.Clock,
	Settings: { [string]: any },
	Store: Types.KeyValueStore?,
	OnShutdown: ((flush: () -> (), pending: () -> number) -> ())?,
}

local RiskEngine = {}
RiskEngine.__index = RiskEngine

export type RiskEngine = typeof(setmetatable(
	{} :: {
		_bus: EventBus.EventBus,
		_registry: any,
		_logger: any,
		_clock: Types.Clock,
		_settings: { [string]: any },
		_store: Types.KeyValueStore?,
		_persist: boolean,
		_users: { [number]: User },
		_queue: { QueueItem },
		_inFlight: number,
		_running: boolean,
		_subscriptions: { EventBus.Subscription },
		_shutdownRegistered: boolean,
		_onShutdown: ((flush: () -> (), pending: () -> number) -> ())?,
		_stats: { [string]: number },
	},
	RiskEngine
))

local function validateBands(bands: { [string]: number })
	local order = { "Increased", "Restrict", "Investigation", "EnforcementReview" }
	for index = 2, #order do
		if bands[order[index]] <= bands[order[index - 1]] then
			error(`ACF configuration is invalid:\n  - Risk.Bands.{order[index]} must be greater than Risk.Bands.{order[index - 1]}`, 0)
		end
	end
end

function RiskEngine.new(options: Options): RiskEngine
	local settings = options.Settings
	validateBands(settings.Bands)
	local persist = settings.Persistence.Enabled == true
	if persist and options.Store == nil then
		options.Logger:Warn("Risk.Persistence is enabled but the environment has no Store; persistence is off")
		persist = false
	end
	local self = setmetatable({
		_bus = options.Bus,
		_registry = options.Registry,
		_logger = options.Logger,
		_clock = options.Clock,
		_settings = settings,
		_store = options.Store,
		_persist = persist,
		_users = {},
		_queue = {},
		_inFlight = 0,
		_running = false,
		_subscriptions = {},
		_shutdownRegistered = false,
		_onShutdown = options.OnShutdown,
		_stats = {
			Detections = 0,
			Suppressed = 0,
			BandChanges = 0,
			Loaded = 0,
			LoadFailed = 0,
			Saved = 0,
			SaveRetries = 0,
			SaveFailed = 0,
			Evicted = 0,
		},
	}, RiskEngine)

	-- Subscribed for the framework's whole life; gated by _running.
	table.insert(self._subscriptions, options.Bus:Subscribe(Topics.DetectionReported, function(payload)
		self:_onDetection(payload)
	end, "Intelligence.Risk"))
	table.insert(self._subscriptions, options.Bus:Subscribe(Topics.PlayerSessionStarted, function(payload)
		self:_onSessionStarted(payload.Session)
	end, "Intelligence.Risk"))
	table.insert(self._subscriptions, options.Bus:Subscribe(Topics.PlayerSessionEnded, function(payload)
		self:_onSessionEnded(payload.Session)
	end, "Intelligence.Risk"))
	return self
end

-- Users and evaluation ---------------------------------------------------------------

local function newUser(userId: number): User
	return {
		UserId = userId,
		Keys = {},
		KeyCount = 0,
		Persistent = 0,
		Risk = 0,
		Peak = 0,
		Confidence = 0,
		Band = "Normal",
		Groups = {},
		History = {},
		InServer = false,
		SessionId = nil,
		Loaded = false,
		Stored = nil,
	}
end

function RiskEngine._user(self: RiskEngine, userId: number): User
	local user = self._users[userId]
	if user == nil then
		user = newUser(userId)
		self._users[userId] = user
	end
	return user
end

function RiskEngine._record(self: RiskEngine, user: User, entry: HistoryEntry)
	local history = user.History
	table.insert(history, entry)
	while #history > self._settings.MaxHistory do
		table.remove(history, 1)
	end
end

-- Recomputes a user's risk at `now`; publishes RiskChanged on a band change.
function RiskEngine._evaluate(self: RiskEngine, user: User, now: number)
	local settings = self._settings
	local keys: { RiskModel.Key } = {}
	for _, key in user.Keys do
		RiskModel.age(key, now, settings.HalfLifeSeconds)
		table.insert(keys, key)
	end
	local groups = RiskModel.summarize(keys, settings.SecondaryWeight)
	local risk, confidence = RiskModel.combine(groups, user.Persistent)
	local previous, previousBand = user.Risk, user.Band
	local band = RiskModel.band(risk, settings.Bands)
	user.Risk = risk
	user.Confidence = confidence
	user.Groups = groups
	user.Peak = math.max(user.Peak, risk)
	user.Band = band
	if band ~= previousBand then
		self._stats.BandChanges += 1
		local explanation = RiskModel.explain(risk, band, groups, user.Persistent)
		self:_record(user, {
			Time = now,
			WallTime = self._clock.Wall(),
			Type = "Band",
			Risk = risk,
			Band = band,
			Note = `{previousBand} -> {band}`,
		})
		local raised = RiskModel.bandIndex(band) > RiskModel.bandIndex(previousBand)
		local fields = { UserId = user.UserId, Risk = math.floor(risk * 10 + 0.5) / 10, From = previousBand, To = band }
		if raised and RiskModel.bandIndex(band) >= RiskModel.bandIndex("Investigation") then
			self._logger:Warn("Risk band raised", fields)
		else
			self._logger:Info(if raised then "Risk band raised" else "Risk band lowered", fields)
		end
		self._bus:Publish(Topics.RiskChanged, table.freeze({
			UserId = user.UserId,
			Session = self._registry:GetSession(user.UserId),
			Risk = risk,
			Previous = previous,
			Band = band,
			PreviousBand = previousBand,
			Confidence = confidence,
			Explanation = explanation,
		}))
	end
end

function RiskEngine._onDetection(self: RiskEngine, payload: any)
	if not self._running then
		return
	end
	local event = payload.Event
	local settings = self._settings
	local now = self._clock.Now()
	local user = self:_user(event.UserId)
	local name = `{event.Detector}.{event.Kind}`
	local key = user.Keys[name]
	if key == nil then
		if user.KeyCount >= settings.MaxKeysPerUser then
			-- Evict the weakest key; bounded memory per user.
			local weakestName, weakestScore = nil, math.huge
			for otherName, other in user.Keys do
				RiskModel.age(other, now, settings.HalfLifeSeconds)
				if other.Score < weakestScore then
					weakestName, weakestScore = otherName, other.Score
				end
			end
			if weakestName then
				user.Keys[weakestName] = nil
				user.KeyCount -= 1
				self._stats.Evicted += 1
			end
		end
		key = {
			Detector = event.Detector,
			Kind = event.Kind,
			Category = event.Category,
			Group = RiskModel.groupFor(event.Detector, event.Kind, event.Category, settings.Groups),
			Score = 0,
			Updated = now,
			Severity = 0,
			Confidence = 0,
			Count = 0,
			Suppressed = 0,
			LastAccepted = -math.huge,
		}
		user.Keys[name] = key
		user.KeyCount += 1
	end
	local current = key :: RiskModel.Key
	RiskModel.age(current, now, settings.HalfLifeSeconds)
	if now - current.LastAccepted < settings.RepeatSuppressSeconds then
		current.Suppressed += 1
		self._stats.Suppressed += 1
		return
	end
	current.LastAccepted = now
	local contribution = RiskModel.add(current, event.Severity, event.Confidence, settings.RepeatCap)
	self._stats.Detections += 1
	self:_evaluate(user, now)
	self:_record(user, {
		Time = now,
		WallTime = event.WallTime,
		Type = "Detection",
		Detector = event.Detector,
		Kind = event.Kind,
		Group = current.Group,
		Contribution = contribution,
		Risk = user.Risk,
		Band = user.Band,
	})
end

-- Sessions and persistence -----------------------------------------------------------

local function storeKey(userId: number): string
	return `Risk_{userId}`
end

function RiskEngine._onSessionStarted(self: RiskEngine, session: any)
	if not self._running then
		return
	end
	local user = self:_user(session.UserId)
	user.InServer = true
	user.SessionId = session.SessionId
	if not self._persist or user.Loaded then
		return
	end
	local sessionId = session.SessionId
	local persistence = self._settings.Persistence
	local store = self._store :: Types.KeyValueStore
	store:Load(persistence.DataStoreName, storeKey(user.UserId), function(ok: boolean, value: any, err: string?)
		self:_onLoaded(user.UserId, sessionId, ok, value, err)
	end)
end

function RiskEngine._onLoaded(self: RiskEngine, userId: number, sessionId: string, ok: boolean, value: any, err: string?)
	local user = self._users[userId]
	if user == nil or user.SessionId ~= sessionId or not user.InServer then
		return -- the player left before the load finished
	end
	if not ok then
		self._stats.LoadFailed += 1
		self._logger:Warn("Risk history could not be loaded; continuing without it", { UserId = userId, Error = err })
		return
	end
	user.Loaded = true
	self._stats.Loaded += 1
	if type(value) ~= "table" or value.Version ~= RECORD_VERSION or type(value.Risk) ~= "number" or type(value.WallTime) ~= "number" then
		return
	end
	user.Stored = value
	local persistence = self._settings.Persistence
	local hours = math.max(0, self._clock.Wall() - value.WallTime) / 3600
	local seeded = math.min(persistence.Cap, math.max(0, value.Risk) * persistence.Weight * 0.5 ^ (hours / persistence.HalfLifeHours))
	if seeded < 0.5 then
		return
	end
	user.Persistent = seeded
	local now = self._clock.Now()
	self:_evaluate(user, now)
	self:_record(user, {
		Time = now,
		WallTime = self._clock.Wall(),
		Type = "History",
		Contribution = seeded,
		Risk = user.Risk,
		Band = user.Band,
		Note = `saved risk {math.floor(value.Risk)} from {math.floor(hours * 10) / 10} h ago`,
	})
end

function RiskEngine._enqueue(self: RiskEngine, userId: number, record: { [string]: any })
	for _, item in self._queue do
		if item.UserId == userId then
			item.Record = record
			item.Attempts = 0
			return
		end
	end
	table.insert(self._queue, { UserId = userId, Record = record, Attempts = 0 })
end

function RiskEngine._onSessionEnded(self: RiskEngine, session: any)
	local user = self._users[session.UserId]
	if user == nil then
		return
	end
	user.InServer = false
	local now = self._clock.Now()
	self:_evaluate(user, now)
	local persistence = self._settings.Persistence
	if not self._persist or user.Risk < persistence.MinRiskToSave then
		return
	end
	local sessions = {}
	local stored = user.Stored
	if stored and type(stored.Sessions) == "table" then
		for _, entry in stored.Sessions do
			table.insert(sessions, entry)
		end
	end
	local top = {}
	for index, group in user.Groups do
		if index > 3 then
			break
		end
		table.insert(top, { Group = group.Group, Score = math.floor(group.Score), Kind = group.Keys[1] and group.Keys[1].Kind or nil })
	end
	table.insert(sessions, {
		WallTime = math.floor(self._clock.Wall()),
		Risk = math.floor(user.Risk),
		Peak = math.floor(user.Peak),
		Band = user.Band,
		Top = top,
	})
	while #sessions > persistence.MaxSessions do
		table.remove(sessions, 1)
	end
	local record = {
		Version = RECORD_VERSION,
		Risk = math.floor(user.Risk * 10) / 10,
		WallTime = math.floor(self._clock.Wall()),
		Sessions = sessions,
	}
	user.Stored = record
	self:_enqueue(user.UserId, record)
end

-- Writes up to `limit` queued records. Failures are re-queued until MaxAttempts.
function RiskEngine._flush(self: RiskEngine, limit: number)
	if not self._persist or #self._queue == 0 then
		return
	end
	local persistence = self._settings.Persistence
	local store = self._store :: Types.KeyValueStore
	local batch = {}
	while #batch < limit and #self._queue > 0 do
		table.insert(batch, table.remove(self._queue, 1) :: QueueItem)
	end
	for _, item in batch do
		item.Attempts += 1
		self._inFlight += 1
		store:Save(persistence.DataStoreName, storeKey(item.UserId), item.Record, function(ok: boolean, err: string?)
			self._inFlight -= 1
			if ok then
				self._stats.Saved += 1
			elseif item.Attempts < persistence.MaxAttempts then
				self._stats.SaveRetries += 1
				self._logger:Warn("Risk save failed; will retry", { UserId = item.UserId, Attempt = item.Attempts, Error = err })
				table.insert(self._queue, item)
			else
				self._stats.SaveFailed += 1
				self._logger:Error("Risk save failed; giving up", { UserId = item.UserId, Attempts = item.Attempts, Error = err })
			end
		end)
	end
end

-- Lifecycle ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Starts tracking. `scheduler` and `cleanup` belong to the framework's run scope.
function RiskEngine.Start(self: RiskEngine, scheduler: any, cleanup: any)
	self._running = true
	local settings = self._settings
	cleanup:Add(scheduler:Every("ACF.RiskRecompute", settings.RecomputeIntervalSeconds, function()
		self:Recompute()
	end))
	if self._persist then
		cleanup:Add(scheduler:Every("ACF.RiskFlush", settings.Persistence.FlushIntervalSeconds, function()
			self:_flush(settings.Persistence.MaxWritesPerFlush)
		end))
		local onShutdown = self._onShutdown
		if onShutdown and not self._shutdownRegistered then
			self._shutdownRegistered = true
			onShutdown(function()
				self:FlushAll()
			end, function(): number
				return self:Pending()
			end)
		end
	end
end

-- Called after sessions have ended on Stop: writes everything queued.
function RiskEngine.Stop(self: RiskEngine)
	self:FlushAll()
	self._running = false
end

function RiskEngine.Destroy(self: RiskEngine)
	self._running = false
	for _, subscription in self._subscriptions do
		subscription:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(self._subscriptions)
	table.clear(self._users)
end

function RiskEngine.FlushAll(self: RiskEngine)
	self:_flush(#self._queue)
end

-- Records queued or being written.
function RiskEngine.Pending(self: RiskEngine): number
	return #self._queue + self._inFlight
end

-- Periodic: decays everyone (bands may fall), prunes dead keys and departed users.
function RiskEngine.Recompute(self: RiskEngine)
	local now = self._clock.Now()
	for userId, user in self._users do
		self:_evaluate(user, now)
		for name, key in user.Keys do
			if key.Score < PRUNE_SCORE then
				user.Keys[name] = nil
				user.KeyCount -= 1
			end
		end
		-- A departed user below risk 1 is forgotten even if tiny residual keys remain.
		if not user.InServer and user.Risk < 1 then
			local queued = false
			for _, item in self._queue do
				if item.UserId == userId then
					queued = true
					break
				end
			end
			if not queued then
				self._users[userId] = nil
			end
		end
	end
end

-- Queries --------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export type RiskReport = {
	UserId: number,
	Risk: number,
	Band: string,
	Confidence: number,
	Persistent: number,
	Peak: number,
	Groups: { { Group: string, Score: number, Confidence: number, Kinds: { { [string]: any } } } },
	Explanation: string,
}

function RiskEngine.Get(self: RiskEngine, userId: number): RiskReport
	local user = self._users[userId]
	if user == nil then
		return {
			UserId = userId,
			Risk = 0,
			Band = "Normal",
			Confidence = 0,
			Persistent = 0,
			Peak = 0,
			Groups = {},
			Explanation = "Risk 0 (Normal)",
		}
	end
	self:_evaluate(user, self._clock.Now())
	local groups = {}
	for _, group in user.Groups do
		local kinds = {}
		for _, key in group.Keys do
			table.insert(kinds, {
				Detector = key.Detector,
				Kind = key.Kind,
				Category = key.Category,
				Score = key.Score,
				Count = key.Count,
				Suppressed = key.Suppressed,
				Severity = key.Severity,
				Confidence = key.Confidence,
			})
		end
		table.insert(groups, { Group = group.Group, Score = group.Score, Confidence = group.Confidence, Kinds = kinds })
	end
	return {
		UserId = userId,
		Risk = user.Risk,
		Band = user.Band,
		Confidence = user.Confidence,
		Persistent = user.Persistent,
		Peak = user.Peak,
		Groups = groups,
		Explanation = RiskModel.explain(user.Risk, user.Band, user.Groups, user.Persistent),
	}
end

function RiskEngine.GetHistory(self: RiskEngine, userId: number): { HistoryEntry }
	local user = self._users[userId]
	if user == nil then
		return {}
	end
	local list = {}
	for _, entry in user.History do
		table.insert(list, table.freeze(table.clone(entry)))
	end
	return list
end

-- Clears a user's risk (staff override). With options.Persistent, the saved record is
-- also cleared (a Risk 0 record that keeps the session list, for audit).
function RiskEngine.Reset(self: RiskEngine, userId: number, options: { Persistent: boolean?, Reason: string? }?)
	local opts: { Persistent: boolean?, Reason: string? } = options or { Persistent = nil, Reason = nil }
	local user = self:_user(userId)
	table.clear(user.Keys)
	user.KeyCount = 0
	user.Persistent = 0
	user.Peak = 0
	local now = self._clock.Now()
	self:_evaluate(user, now)
	self:_record(user, {
		Time = now,
		WallTime = self._clock.Wall(),
		Type = "Reset",
		Risk = user.Risk,
		Band = user.Band,
		Note = opts.Reason,
	})
	self._logger:Info("Risk reset", { UserId = userId, Persistent = opts.Persistent == true, Reason = opts.Reason })
	if opts.Persistent and self._persist then
		local stored = user.Stored
		local record = {
			Version = RECORD_VERSION,
			Risk = 0,
			WallTime = math.floor(self._clock.Wall()),
			Sessions = if stored and type(stored.Sessions) == "table" then stored.Sessions else {},
			ResetReason = opts.Reason,
		}
		user.Stored = record
		self:_enqueue(userId, record)
	end
end

function RiskEngine.GetStats(self: RiskEngine): { [string]: any }
	local tracked, elevated = 0, 0
	for _, user in self._users do
		tracked += 1
		if user.Band ~= "Normal" then
			elevated += 1
		end
	end
	local stats = table.clone(self._stats) :: { [string]: any }
	stats.Tracked = tracked
	stats.Elevated = elevated
	stats.Persistence = self._persist
	stats.Queued = #self._queue
	stats.InFlight = self._inFlight
	return stats
end

return RiskEngine

```

`src/Server/Intelligence/RiskModel.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Pure risk mathematics. No clocks, no Roblox: RiskEngine passes times in.

	Evidence key: one (Detector, Kind) pair for one user. Its score decays exponentially
	with half-life H (lambda = ln 2 / H) and grows with each accepted detection:

	    S <- S * 0.5^(dt / H)                                    (decay)
	    S <- S + w * c * max(0, 1 - S / (w * c * RepeatCap))     (saturating add)

	w = detection severity (0-100) and c = confidence (0-1). The first detection adds
	exactly w * c, matching R = sum(w_i * c_i * exp(-lambda * t_i)). Repeats of the SAME
	kind have diminishing returns and approach w * c * RepeatCap, so one cheat repeating
	forever cannot climb the bands on its own: duplicate suppression.

	Correlation group: kinds that observe the same underlying behaviour (Movement and
	Physics; accuracy and headshots) are grouped. Within a group the strongest key counts
	fully and the rest at SecondaryWeight:

	    G = max(S_k) + SecondaryWeight * (sum(S_k) - max(S_k))

	so one teleport that also trips speed and velocity-mismatch checks is not counted
	three times.

	Risk:        R = min(100, sum over groups G + persistent)
	Confidence:  noisy-OR over groups of each group's strongest confidence,
	             1 - prod(1 - c_g). Groups are treated as independent evidence, which
	             is exactly why correlated kinds are grouped first.
]]

local RiskModel = {}

export type Key = {
	Detector: string,
	Kind: string,
	Category: string,
	Group: string,
	Score: number,
	Updated: number,
	Severity: number,
	Confidence: number,
	Count: number,
	Suppressed: number,
	LastAccepted: number,
}

export type GroupSummary = {
	Group: string,
	Score: number,
	Confidence: number,
	Keys: { Key },
}

RiskModel.BANDS = table.freeze({ "Normal", "Increased", "Restrict", "Investigation", "EnforcementReview" })

function RiskModel.decay(score: number, elapsed: number, halfLife: number): number
	if elapsed <= 0 then
		return score
	end
	return score * 0.5 ^ (elapsed / halfLife)
end

-- Ages a key to `now`.
function RiskModel.age(key: Key, now: number, halfLife: number)
	if now > key.Updated then
		key.Score = RiskModel.decay(key.Score, now - key.Updated, halfLife)
		key.Updated = now
	end
end

-- Adds one detection to a key (already aged to `now`). Returns the contribution.
function RiskModel.add(key: Key, severity: number, confidence: number, repeatCap: number): number
	local base = severity * confidence
	if base <= 0 then
		return 0
	end
	local cap = base * repeatCap
	local contribution = base * math.max(0, 1 - key.Score / cap)
	key.Score += contribution
	key.Count += 1
	key.Severity = math.max(key.Severity, severity)
	key.Confidence = math.max(key.Confidence, confidence)
	return contribution
end

-- Resolves the correlation group for a detection: "Detector.Kind", then Detector, then
-- Category in `groups`; otherwise the category itself.
function RiskModel.groupFor(detector: string, kind: string, category: string, groups: { [string]: string }): string
	return groups[`{detector}.{kind}`] or groups[detector] or groups[category] or category
end

-- Combines keys into groups. Keys must be aged to the same time.
function RiskModel.summarize(keys: { Key }, secondaryWeight: number): { GroupSummary }
	local byGroup: { [string]: GroupSummary } = {}
	local sums: { [string]: number } = {}
	local maxima: { [string]: number } = {}
	for _, key in keys do
		local summary = byGroup[key.Group]
		if summary == nil then
			summary = { Group = key.Group, Score = 0, Confidence = 0, Keys = {} }
			byGroup[key.Group] = summary
			sums[key.Group] = 0
			maxima[key.Group] = 0
		end
		table.insert(summary.Keys, key)
		sums[key.Group] += key.Score
		if key.Score > maxima[key.Group] then
			maxima[key.Group] = key.Score
			summary.Confidence = key.Confidence
		end
	end
	local list: { GroupSummary } = {}
	for name, summary in byGroup do
		summary.Score = maxima[name] + secondaryWeight * (sums[name] - maxima[name])
		table.sort(summary.Keys, function(a, b)
			return a.Score > b.Score
		end)
		table.insert(list, summary)
	end
	table.sort(list, function(a, b)
		if a.Score ~= b.Score then
			return a.Score > b.Score
		end
		return a.Group < b.Group
	end)
	return list
end

-- Total risk (0-100) and noisy-OR confidence from group summaries.
function RiskModel.combine(groups: { GroupSummary }, persistent: number): (number, number)
	local total = persistent
	local miss = 1
	for _, group in groups do
		total += group.Score
		miss *= 1 - math.clamp(group.Confidence, 0, 1)
	end
	return math.min(100, math.max(0, total)), 1 - miss
end

-- Band name for a risk value. thresholds = { Increased, Restrict, Investigation,
-- EnforcementReview } lower bounds.
function RiskModel.band(risk: number, thresholds: { [string]: number }): string
	if risk >= thresholds.EnforcementReview then
		return "EnforcementReview"
	elseif risk >= thresholds.Investigation then
		return "Investigation"
	elseif risk >= thresholds.Restrict then
		return "Restrict"
	elseif risk >= thresholds.Increased then
		return "Increased"
	end
	return "Normal"
end

function RiskModel.bandIndex(band: string): number
	return table.find(RiskModel.BANDS, band) or 1
end

local function round(value: number): number
	return math.floor(value * 10 + 0.5) / 10
end

-- One human-readable line per group, strongest first, e.g.
-- "Movement 41.2: TeleportDetected x6 (Movement, sev 30, conf 0.71) + SpeedExceeded x2".
function RiskModel.explain(risk: number, band: string, groups: { GroupSummary }, persistent: number): string
	local lines = { `Risk {round(risk)} ({band})` }
	for _, group in groups do
		local parts = {}
		for index, key in group.Keys do
			if index > 3 then
				table.insert(parts, `+{#group.Keys - 3} more`)
				break
			end
			table.insert(
				parts,
				`{key.Kind} x{key.Count} ({key.Detector}, sev {key.Severity}, conf {round(key.Confidence * 100) / 100}, {round(key.Score)})`
			)
		end
		table.insert(lines, `  {group.Group} {round(group.Score)}: {table.concat(parts, " + ")}`)
	end
	if persistent > 0 then
		table.insert(lines, `  History {round(persistent)}: risk carried from earlier sessions`)
	end
	return table.concat(lines, "\n")
end

return RiskModel

```

`src/Server/Intelligence/Statistics.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Pure statistics used by behavioural detection. No Roblox APIs, no clocks: callers pass
	values and times in, so every function is deterministic and unit-tested.

	  Ewma          exponentially weighted mean and variance (West's incremental form).
	                Tracks a baseline that adapts slowly; alpha is the weight of each new
	                value (0.02 means about the last 50 values matter).
	  Cusum         one-sided (upper) cumulative-sum change detector on standardised
	                values:  S <- max(0, S + z - k);  alarm when S > h, then S resets.
	                k is the allowance (drift ignored per step) and h the decision interval.
	                With z clamped to zMax, an alarm needs at least ceil(h / (zMax - k))
	                steps: no single value can raise one.
	  zScore        standardises a value against a baseline, with a floor on the standard
	                deviation so a population with near-zero variance cannot turn a tiny
	                difference into a huge z.
	  wilsonLower   lower bound of the Wilson score interval for a proportion. A high
	                proportion observed over few trials gets a low bound, so it is the
	                sample-size guard for ratios (accuracy, headshots, invalid actions).
	  decayFactor   multiplier that ages exponentially decayed counts by `elapsed`.
	  meanStd       mean and sample standard deviation of a list.
]]

local Statistics = {}

export type Ewma = { Mean: number, Variance: number, Count: number }
export type Cusum = { S: number, Steps: number }

function Statistics.newEwma(): Ewma
	return { Mean = 0, Variance = 0, Count = 0 }
end

function Statistics.updateEwma(ewma: Ewma, value: number, alpha: number)
	if ewma.Count == 0 then
		ewma.Mean = value
		ewma.Variance = 0
	else
		local diff = value - ewma.Mean
		local increment = alpha * diff
		ewma.Mean += increment
		ewma.Variance = (1 - alpha) * (ewma.Variance + diff * increment)
	end
	ewma.Count += 1
end

function Statistics.stdDev(ewma: Ewma): number
	return math.sqrt(math.max(ewma.Variance, 0))
end

function Statistics.newCusum(): Cusum
	return { S = 0, Steps = 0 }
end

-- Adds one standardised value. Returns (alarm, statisticBeforeReset, stepsInRun).
function Statistics.cusumStep(cusum: Cusum, z: number, k: number, h: number): (boolean, number, number)
	cusum.S = math.max(0, cusum.S + z - k)
	if cusum.S > 0 then
		cusum.Steps += 1
	else
		cusum.Steps = 0
	end
	if cusum.S > h then
		local s, steps = cusum.S, cusum.Steps
		cusum.S = 0
		cusum.Steps = 0
		return true, s, steps
	end
	return false, cusum.S, cusum.Steps
end

-- (value - mean) / max(std, minStd, minRelative * |mean|), clamped to [-zMax, zMax].
function Statistics.zScore(value: number, mean: number, std: number, minStd: number, minRelative: number, zMax: number): number
	local scale = math.max(std, minStd, minRelative * math.abs(mean))
	if scale <= 0 then
		return 0
	end
	local z = (value - mean) / scale
	return math.clamp(z, -zMax, zMax)
end

-- Lower Wilson bound for `successes` out of `trials` (both may be fractional when the
-- counts are exponentially decayed). z = 3.09 is a one-sided 99.9% bound.
function Statistics.wilsonLower(successes: number, trials: number, z: number): number
	if trials <= 0 then
		return 0
	end
	local p = math.clamp(successes / trials, 0, 1)
	local z2 = z * z
	local denominator = 1 + z2 / trials
	local centre = p + z2 / (2 * trials)
	local margin = z * math.sqrt(p * (1 - p) / trials + z2 / (4 * trials * trials))
	return math.max(0, (centre - margin) / denominator)
end

function Statistics.decayFactor(elapsed: number, halfLife: number): number
	if elapsed <= 0 then
		return 1
	end
	return 0.5 ^ (elapsed / halfLife)
end

function Statistics.meanStd(values: { number }): (number, number)
	local n = #values
	if n == 0 then
		return 0, 0
	end
	local sum = 0
	for _, value in values do
		sum += value
	end
	local mean = sum / n
	if n == 1 then
		return mean, 0
	end
	local squares = 0
	for _, value in values do
		squares += (value - mean) ^ 2
	end
	return mean, math.sqrt(squares / (n - 1))
end

return Statistics

```

`src/Server/Intelligence/ViolationTracker.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Decaying violation scores: the shared "never punish a single sample" mechanism.

	Input:  violations, each with a key (e.g. a detector name) and a weight.
	Model:  per key, a leaky integrator with half-life H:
	            score <- score * 0.5^(dt / H) + weight
	Output: Record() returns shouldReport = true when the score reaches ReportThreshold
	        and the last report for that key is at least ReportIntervalSeconds old, plus
	        the score and a confidence
	            confidence = min(MaxConfidence, 1 - exp(-score / ConfidenceScale)).

	A single violation whose weight is below the threshold can never report; isolated
	violations spaced more than a few half-lives apart decay to nothing. Memory is one
	small table per key, and keys come from code (detector names / reasons), so a
	tracker stays bounded.
]]

export type Settings = {
	HalfLifeSeconds: number,
	ReportThreshold: number,
	ReportIntervalSeconds: number,
	ConfidenceScale: number,
	MaxConfidence: number?,
}

type Entry = { score: number, updated: number, lastReport: number? }

local ViolationTracker = {}
ViolationTracker.__index = ViolationTracker

export type ViolationTracker = typeof(setmetatable(
	{} :: { _settings: Settings, _entries: { [string]: Entry } },
	ViolationTracker
))

function ViolationTracker.new(settings: Settings): ViolationTracker
	assert(settings.HalfLifeSeconds > 0, "ViolationTracker: HalfLifeSeconds must be > 0")
	assert(settings.ConfidenceScale > 0, "ViolationTracker: ConfidenceScale must be > 0")
	return setmetatable({ _settings = settings, _entries = {} }, ViolationTracker)
end

local function decayed(self: ViolationTracker, entry: Entry, now: number): number
	local elapsed = now - entry.updated
	if elapsed <= 0 then
		return entry.score
	end
	return entry.score * 0.5 ^ (elapsed / self._settings.HalfLifeSeconds)
end

function ViolationTracker.Confidence(self: ViolationTracker, score: number): number
	local settings = self._settings
	return math.min(settings.MaxConfidence or 0.95, 1 - math.exp(-score / settings.ConfidenceScale))
end

-- Adds a weighted violation. Returns (shouldReport, score, confidence).
function ViolationTracker.Record(self: ViolationTracker, key: string, weight: number, now: number): (boolean, number, number)
	if weight <= 0 then
		local current = self:Score(key, now)
		return false, current, self:Confidence(current)
	end
	local entry = self._entries[key]
	if entry == nil then
		entry = { score = 0, updated = now, lastReport = nil }
		self._entries[key] = entry
	end
	entry.score = decayed(self, entry, now) + weight
	entry.updated = now

	local settings = self._settings
	local report = entry.score >= settings.ReportThreshold
		and (entry.lastReport == nil or now - entry.lastReport >= settings.ReportIntervalSeconds)
	if report then
		entry.lastReport = now
	end
	return report, entry.score, self:Confidence(entry.score)
end

function ViolationTracker.Score(self: ViolationTracker, key: string, now: number): number
	local entry = self._entries[key]
	return if entry then decayed(self, entry, now) else 0
end

function ViolationTracker.Reset(self: ViolationTracker, key: string?)
	if key then
		self._entries[key] = nil
	else
		table.clear(self._entries)
	end
end

return ViolationTracker

```

`src/Server/Platform/RobloxEnforcer.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Roblox implementation of Environment.Enforcer.

	  Kick   Player:Kick(message)
	  Ban    Players:BanAsync (universe-wide; Duration -1 = permanent). Runs in its own
	         thread, pcall-wrapped; the outcome reaches the callback.
	  Unban  Players:UnbanAsync

	ACF's own ban records (DataStore) enforce bans on join whether or not the Roblox ban
	API call succeeds, so a failed BanAsync never lets a banned player back in to a
	server running ACF. Places where the ban API is unavailable (old Studio builds,
	disabled API access) should set Enforcement.Bans.UseRobloxBanApi = false.

	Studio-only code path; verify with the Studio run.
]]

local Types = require(script.Parent.Parent.Types)

local RobloxEnforcer = {}

function RobloxEnforcer.create(): Types.Enforcer
	local Players = game:GetService("Players")
	local enforcer = {}

	function enforcer.Kick(_self: any, player: any, message: string)
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			(player :: Player):Kick(message)
		end)
		if not ok then
			warn("[ACF] Kick failed: " .. tostring(err))
		end
	end

	function enforcer.Ban(
		_self: any,
		userId: number,
		seconds: number?,
		displayReason: string,
		privateReason: string,
		callback: (boolean, string?) -> ()
	)
		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, err = pcall(function()
				(Players :: any):BanAsync({
					UserIds = { userId },
					ApplyToUniverse = true,
					Duration = seconds or -1,
					DisplayReason = string.sub(displayReason, 1, 400),
					PrivateReason = string.sub(privateReason, 1, 1000),
					ExcludeAltAccounts = false,
				})
			end)
			callback(ok, if ok then nil else tostring(err))
		end)
	end

	function enforcer.Unban(_self: any, userId: number, callback: (boolean, string?) -> ())
		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, err = pcall(function()
				(Players :: any):UnbanAsync({ UserIds = { userId }, ApplyToUniverse = true })
			end)
			callback(ok, if ok then nil else tostring(err))
		end)
	end

	return enforcer :: any
end

return RobloxEnforcer

```

`src/Server/Platform/RobloxEnvironment.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Builds the production Environment from real Roblox services.

	This is the only place ACF's core reaches for Players, RunService and HttpService.
	Everything else receives these through the Environment, which is what makes the core
	testable outside Roblox with fakes.
]]

local Types = require(script.Parent.Parent.Types)
local RobloxPhysics = require(script.Parent.RobloxPhysics)
local RobloxStore = require(script.Parent.RobloxStore)
local RobloxEnforcer = require(script.Parent.RobloxEnforcer)

-- Longest time BindToClose waits for queued saves (Roblox allows 30 s in total).
local SHUTDOWN_WAIT_SECONDS = 25

local RobloxEnvironment = {}

function RobloxEnvironment.create(): Types.Environment
	local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
	if not RunService:IsServer() then
		error("ACF can only run on the server", 2)
	end
	local Players = game:GetService("Players")
	local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	local Protocol = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("ACFShared"):WaitForChild("Protocol"))

	local remotes = {}
	function remotes.GetOrCreate(_self: any, name: string, className: string): Instance
		local folder = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(Protocol.REMOTES_FOLDER)
		if folder == nil then
			folder = Instance.new("Folder")
			folder.Name = Protocol.REMOTES_FOLDER
			folder.Parent = ReplicatedStorage
		end
		local existing = folder:FindFirstChild(name)
		if existing and existing.ClassName == className then
			return existing
		end
		local remote = Instance.new(className)
		remote.Name = name
		remote.Parent = folder
		return remote
	end

	return {
		Players = Players,
		Heartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat,
		Physics = RobloxPhysics.create(),
		Remotes = remotes,
		Clock = {
			Now = os.clock,
			Wall = function(): number
				return DateTime.now().UnixTimestampMillis / 1000
			end,
		},
		GenerateId = function(): string
			return HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)
		end,
		LogSink = nil,
		Store = RobloxStore.create(),
		Enforcer = RobloxEnforcer.create(),
		OnShutdown = function(flush: () -> (), pending: () -> number)
			game:BindToClose(function()
				flush()
				local started = os.clock()
				while pending() > 0 and os.clock() - started < SHUTDOWN_WAIT_SECONDS do
					task.wait(0.25)
				end
			end)
		end,
	}
end

return RobloxEnvironment

```

`src/Server/Platform/RobloxPhysics.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Roblox implementation of Environment.Physics: character sampling and spatial queries.

	Studio-only code path (the Lune suite uses a fake world); verify with the Studio run.

	Sample(character, player):
	  - root = HumanoidRootPart, humanoid = first Humanoid; nil if either is missing
	  - position / velocities from the root assembly
	  - GroundClearance: downward raycast (characters excluded, water counts as ground)
	    minus the standing height (HipHeight + half root height, + 2 for R6 legs)
	  - JumpVelocity from the server-side JumpPower / JumpHeight
	  - Ping from Player:GetNetworkPing() (seconds, round trip estimate)

	Cast(...): workspace:Raycast with RespectCanCollide, excluding every player
	character. The exclusion list is refreshed at most once per second.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local Types = require(script.Parent.Parent.Types)

local GROUND_PROBE_DISTANCE = 64
local EXCLUDE_REFRESH_SECONDS = 1

-- Accessory attachment points that sit on the head (hats, hair, face accessories).
local HEAD_ATTACHMENTS = {
	HatAttachment = true,
	HairAttachment = true,
	FaceFrontAttachment = true,
	FaceCenterAttachment = true,
}

-- True when `part` is an accessory Handle worn on the head: hits on hats and hair count as
-- headshots. Checks the weld to the head first, then the accessory's attachment name.
local function isHeadAccessory(part: Instance): boolean
	local accessory = part.Parent
	if accessory == nil or not accessory:IsA("Accessory") then
		return false
	end
	for _, child in part:GetChildren() do
		local other: Instance? = nil
		if child:IsA("JointInstance") then
			local joint = child :: JointInstance
			other = if joint.Part0 == part then joint.Part1 else joint.Part0
		elseif child:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
			local weld = child :: WeldConstraint
			other = if weld.Part0 == part then weld.Part1 else weld.Part0
		end
		if other and other.Name == "Head" then
			return true
		end
		if child:IsA("Attachment") and HEAD_ATTACHMENTS[child.Name] then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

local RobloxPhysics = {}

export type Options = {
	-- Characters excluded from ground and line-of-sight raycasts. Defaults to every
	-- player's character. Games with server-controlled characters (bots, NPCs tracked by
	-- ACF) supply their own list so a character's own limbs never block its ground ray.
	GetCharacters: (() -> { Instance })?,
}

local function playerCharacters(): { Instance }
	local list = {}
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		local character = player.Character
		if character then
			table.insert(list, character)
		end
	end
	return list
end

function RobloxPhysics.create(options: Options?): Types.PhysicsService
	local getCharacters: () -> { Instance } = playerCharacters
	if options and options.GetCharacters then
		getCharacters = options.GetCharacters
	end
	local groundParams = RaycastParams.new()
	groundParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	groundParams.IgnoreWater = false
	groundParams.RespectCanCollide = true

	local castParams = RaycastParams.new()
	castParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	castParams.IgnoreWater = true
	castParams.RespectCanCollide = true

	local characters: { Instance } = {}
	local refreshedAt = -math.huge

	local function refreshCharacters()
		local now = os.clock()
		if now - refreshedAt < EXCLUDE_REFRESH_SECONDS then
			return
		end
		refreshedAt = now
		table.clear(characters)
		for _, character in getCharacters() do
			table.insert(characters, character)
		end
		groundParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = characters
		castParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = characters
	end

	local service = {}

	function service.Sample(_self: any, character: Model, player: Player?): Types.MovementSample?
		local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if root == nil or humanoid == nil or not root:IsA("BasePart") then
			return nil
		end
		refreshCharacters()
		if not table.find(characters, character) then
			-- Newly spawned character not yet in the exclusion list.
			refreshedAt = -math.huge
			refreshCharacters()
		end

		local position = root.Position
		local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
		local gravity = Workspace.Gravity
		local jumpVelocity = if humanoid.UseJumpPower
			then humanoid.JumpPower
			else math.sqrt(2 * gravity * math.max(humanoid.JumpHeight, 0))

		local legs = if humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then 2 else 0
		local standingHeight = humanoid.HipHeight + root.Size.Y / 2 + legs
		local hit = Workspace:Raycast(position, Vector3.new(0, -GROUND_PROBE_DISTANCE, 0), groundParams)
		local clearance = if hit then math.max(0, (position.Y - hit.Position.Y) - standingHeight) else math.huge
		local groundSpeed = 0
		if hit and clearance <= 2 and hit.Instance:IsA("BasePart") then
			local groundVelocity = (hit.Instance :: BasePart).AssemblyLinearVelocity
			groundSpeed = math.sqrt(groundVelocity.X * groundVelocity.X + groundVelocity.Z * groundVelocity.Z)
		end

		local ping = 0
		if player then
			local ok, value = pcall(function()
				return player:GetNetworkPing()
			end)
			if ok and type(value) == "number" then
				ping = value
			end
		end

		return {
			X = position.X,
			Y = position.Y,
			Z = position.Z,
			VX = velocity.X,
			VY = velocity.Y,
			VZ = velocity.Z,
			AngularSpeed = root.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude,
			WalkSpeed = humanoid.WalkSpeed,
			JumpVelocity = jumpVelocity,
			Gravity = gravity,
			GroundClearance = clearance,
			State = humanoid:GetState().Name,
			Seated = humanoid.SeatPart ~= nil,
			Health = humanoid.Health,
			Ping = ping,
			GroundSpeed = groundSpeed,
		}
	end

	function service.Describe(_self: any, part: Instance): Types.TargetInfo?
		if typeof(part) ~= "Instance" or not part:IsA("BasePart") then
			return nil
		end
		local position = (part :: BasePart).Position
		local model = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
		local humanoid = if model then model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") else nil
		return {
			X = position.X,
			Y = position.Y,
			Z = position.Z,
			Model = if humanoid then model else nil,
			Humanoid = humanoid,
			Alive = humanoid ~= nil and humanoid.Health > 0,
			Player = if model then Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model) else nil,
			IsHead = part.Name == "Head" or isHeadAccessory(part),
		}
	end

	-- Server-side correction: keeps orientation, clears momentum. The owning client
	-- receives the new CFrame through replication.
	function service.Correct(_self: any, character: Model, x: number, y: number, z: number)
		local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if root == nil or not root:IsA("BasePart") then
			error("character has no HumanoidRootPart")
		end
		local pivot = character:GetPivot()
		character:PivotTo(CFrame.new(x, y, z) * pivot.Rotation)
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
	end

	function service.Cast(_self: any, ox: number, oy: number, oz: number, dx: number, dy: number, dz: number, _character: any): number?
		refreshCharacters()
		local result = Workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(ox, oy, oz), Vector3.new(dx, dy, dz), castParams)
		return if result then result.Distance else nil
	end

	return service :: any
end

return RobloxPhysics

```

`src/Server/Platform/RobloxStore.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	Roblox implementation of Environment.Store (DataStoreService).

	Each Load/Save runs in its own thread (task.spawn) so callers never yield, and every
	DataStore call is wrapped in pcall: failures reach the callback as (false, message)
	and never raise. Retries and batching are the caller's job (RiskEngine), so they are
	covered by the offline tests.

	GetDataStore itself can fail (Studio without "Enable Studio Access to API Services",
	or a place without DataStore access); that is reported the same way.

	Studio-only code path; verify with the Studio run.
]]

local Types = require(script.Parent.Parent.Types)

local RobloxStore = {}

function RobloxStore.create(): Types.KeyValueStore
	local DataStoreService = game:GetService("DataStoreService")
	local stores: { [string]: any } = {}

	local function storeFor(scope: string): (boolean, any)
		local cached = stores[scope]
		if cached then
			return true, cached
		end
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return DataStoreService:GetDataStore(scope)
		end)
		if ok then
			stores[scope] = result
		end
		return ok, result
	end

	local service = {}

	function service.Load(_self: any, scope: string, key: string, callback: (boolean, any, string?) -> ())
		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, store = storeFor(scope)
			if not ok then
				callback(false, nil, tostring(store))
				return
			end
			local loaded, value = pcall(function()
				return store:GetAsync(key)
			end)
			if loaded then
				callback(true, value, nil)
			else
				callback(false, nil, tostring(value))
			end
		end)
	end

	function service.Save(_self: any, scope: string, key: string, value: any, callback: (boolean, string?) -> ())
		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, store = storeFor(scope)
			if not ok then
				callback(false, tostring(store))
				return
			end
			local saved, err = pcall(function()
				store:SetAsync(key, value)
			end)
			callback(saved, if saved then nil else tostring(err))
		end)
	end

	return service :: any
end

return RobloxStore

```

`src/Server/Types.luau`:

```luau
--!strict
--[[
	ACF server-side type definitions.

	Lives under ServerScriptService and never replicates to clients. Runtime value of
	this module is an empty table; it exists for its exported types.
]]

export type LogLevel = "Debug" | "Info" | "Warn" | "Error"

export type LogFields = { [string]: any }

export type LogEntry = {
	Time: number,
	WallTime: number,
	Level: LogLevel,
	Scope: string,
	Message: string,
	Fields: LogFields?,
	-- Number of identical (scope + message) entries dropped by rate limiting since the
	-- previous emitted entry with the same key.
	Suppressed: number?,
}

export type Clock = {
	-- Monotonic seconds. Used for all durations, windows and decay.
	Now: () -> number,
	-- Unix epoch seconds. Used only for audit timestamps.
	Wall: () -> number,
}

-- Minimal shapes ACF needs from Roblox objects. Real RBXScriptSignals, Players and
-- Player instances satisfy them; tests supply plain-table fakes.
export type ConnectionLike = any
export type SignalLike = any
export type PlayersLike = any
export type PlayerLike = any

-- One server-side observation of a character, produced by Environment.Physics:Sample.
-- Plain numbers so detectors run (and are tested) without Roblox datatypes.
export type MovementSample = {
	X: number,
	Y: number,
	Z: number,
	VX: number,
	VY: number,
	VZ: number,
	-- Magnitude of the root's angular velocity, rad/s.
	AngularSpeed: number,
	-- Server-side Humanoid.WalkSpeed (client changes never replicate, so this is authorized).
	WalkSpeed: number,
	-- Initial jump velocity implied by the server-side JumpPower / JumpHeight.
	JumpVelocity: number,
	Gravity: number,
	-- Distance from the feet to the ground below (0 when standing; math.huge when none
	-- within the probe range). Water counts as ground.
	GroundClearance: number,
	-- Humanoid state name, e.g. "Running", "Freefall", "Climbing", "Swimming", "Dead".
	State: string,
	Seated: boolean,
	Health: number,
	-- Round-trip network latency estimate in seconds (0 if unknown).
	Ping: number,
	-- Horizontal speed of the part the character stands on (moving platforms, trains,
	-- conveyors). Added to the allowed speed. Optional; 0 when absent.
	GroundSpeed: number?,
}

-- What the server knows about a part a client claims to have hit.
export type TargetInfo = {
	X: number,
	Y: number,
	Z: number,
	-- The character/NPC model the part belongs to (identity key), or nil for world parts.
	Model: any?,
	Humanoid: any?,
	Alive: boolean,
	-- The Player owning that character, if any.
	Player: any?,
	IsHead: boolean,
}

export type PhysicsService = {
	-- Describes a hit part (position, owning model, alive, head). nil if not a BasePart.
	Describe: ((self: any, part: any) -> TargetInfo?)?,
	-- Observes a character. Returns nil if it has no root part / humanoid yet.
	Sample: (self: any, character: any, player: any) -> MovementSample?,
	-- Casts a ray against collidable world geometry (characters excluded). Returns the
	-- distance to the first hit, or nil.
	Cast: (self: any, ox: number, oy: number, oz: number, dx: number, dy: number, dz: number, character: any) -> number?,
	-- Moves a character back to a position (enforcement level 3). Optional.
	Correct: ((self: any, character: any, x: number, y: number, z: number) -> ())?,
}

export type RemoteProvider = {
	-- Returns ACF's own remote with this name, creating it if needed.
	GetOrCreate: (self: any, name: string, className: string) -> any,
}

export type Environment = {
	Players: PlayersLike,
	-- Character observation and spatial queries (required by Movement/Physics).
	Physics: PhysicsService?,
	-- Creates ACF-owned remotes (required by Telemetry).
	Remotes: RemoteProvider?,
	-- Per-frame signal that drives the Scheduler. When nil, the host must call
	-- Framework:Step() itself (tests do this with a fake clock).
	Heartbeat: SignalLike?,
	Clock: Clock,
	GenerateId: () -> string,
	-- Receives every emitted log entry. Defaults to print/warn formatting.
	LogSink: ((LogEntry) -> ())?,
	-- Persistent key-value storage (DataStore). Required only for Risk.Persistence.
	Store: KeyValueStore?,
	-- Called once when the server shuts down: `flush` starts saving, and the host waits
	-- (bounded) while `pending()` > 0. Roblox: game:BindToClose.
	OnShutdown: ((flush: () -> (), pending: () -> number) -> ())?,
	-- Removes players (required for kicks and bans to take effect).
	Enforcer: Enforcer?,
}

-- Platform actions for enforcement. Ban/Unban (Roblox Players:BanAsync) are optional;
-- ACF's own records enforce bans on join either way.
export type Enforcer = {
	Kick: (self: any, player: any, message: string) -> (),
	Ban: ((self: any, userId: number, seconds: number?, displayReason: string, privateReason: string, callback: (boolean, string?) -> ()) -> ())?,
	Unban: ((self: any, userId: number, callback: (boolean, string?) -> ()) -> ())?,
}

-- Asynchronous storage. Callbacks may run later (Roblox yields in a spawned thread).
export type KeyValueStore = {
	Load: (self: any, scope: string, key: string, callback: (ok: boolean, value: any, err: string?) -> ()) -> (),
	Save: (self: any, scope: string, key: string, value: any, callback: (ok: boolean, err: string?) -> ()) -> (),
}

export type DetectionCategory =
	"Movement"
	| "Physics"
	| "Remote"
	| "Combat"
	| "Economy"
	| "Behavioral"
	| "Integrity"
	| "Custom"

-- What a detection module passes to context:Report().
export type DetectionInput = {
	-- Short machine-readable identifier of what was observed, e.g. "SpeedExceeded".
	Kind: string,
	-- Risk weight of this observation at full confidence, 0..100.
	Severity: number,
	-- Probability-like certainty that the observation indicates cheating, 0..1.
	Confidence: number,
	-- Optional structured context. Sanitised and size-bounded before storage.
	Details: { [string]: any }?,
}

-- Normalised, immutable detection event produced by the DetectionManager.
export type DetectionEvent = {
	Id: string,
	Detector: string,
	Category: DetectionCategory,
	Kind: string,
	UserId: number,
	SessionId: string,
	Severity: number,
	Confidence: number,
	Time: number,
	WallTime: number,
	Details: { [string]: any },
}

-- Aggregated evidence. Repeated identical detections inside the duplicate window are
-- merged into one record instead of creating new ones.
export type EvidenceRecord = {
	Id: string,
	UserId: number,
	SessionId: string,
	Detector: string,
	Category: DetectionCategory,
	Kind: string,
	Severity: number,
	Confidence: number,
	Count: number,
	FirstSeen: number,
	LastSeen: number,
	FirstWallTime: number,
	LastWallTime: number,
	FirstEventId: string,
	Details: { [string]: any },
}

export type ModuleState =
	"Registered"
	| "Initializing"
	| "Initialized"
	| "Starting"
	| "Running"
	| "Stopping"
	| "Stopped"
	| "Failed"
	| "Blocked"
	| "Disabled"
	| "Destroying"
	| "Destroyed"

export type FrameworkState = "Created" | "Initialized" | "Running" | "Stopped" | "Destroyed"

-- The handle a module receives in Initialize(context). See docs/MODULES.md.
export type ModuleContext = {
	Name: string,
	Config: { [string]: any },
	Logger: any,
	Clock: Clock,
	Cleanup: any,
	Events: any,
	Scheduler: any,
	Players: any,
	-- Read-only platform services; fields are nil when the host provides none.
	Platform: { Physics: PhysicsService?, Remotes: RemoteProvider? },
	-- Read-only enforcement questions (Phase 7).
	Enforcement: {
		IsRestricted: (self: any, target: any, tag: string | boolean, operation: string?) -> boolean,
		ShouldCorrect: (self: any, target: any) -> boolean,
		NoteCorrected: (self: any, target: any, details: { [string]: any }) -> (),
	},
	Report: (self: any, target: any, input: DetectionInput) -> (boolean, string),
	GetConfig: (self: any, path: string) -> any,
	GetModule: (self: any, name: string) -> any,
}

export type ModuleDefinition = {
	Name: string,
	Version: string?,
	Category: DetectionCategory?,
	Dependencies: { string }?,
	Critical: boolean?,
	EnabledByDefault: boolean?,
	ConfigSchema: any?,
	Initialize: (self: any, context: ModuleContext) -> (),
	Start: (self: any) -> (),
	Stop: (self: any) -> (),
	Destroy: (self: any) -> (),
	[string]: any,
}

return {}
```
