```luau
--!strict
--[[
	ACF client/server protocol. Replicates to clients: names and intervals only.

	Telemetry heartbeat (client -> server, RemoteEvent):
		ReplicatedStorage[REMOTES_FOLDER][TELEMETRY_REMOTE]:FireServer(sequence: integer, fps: number)
	sequence increases by exactly 1 per heartbeat, starting at 1.

	Everything a client sends is untrusted. The server validates the payload with
	RemoteSecurity and treats telemetry as a weak hint only.
]]

local Protocol = table.freeze({
	REMOTES_FOLDER = "ACFRemotes",
	TELEMETRY_REMOTE = "Telemetry",
	TELEMETRY_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 5,
	MAX_FPS = 1000,
})

return Protocol
```

`tests/Specs/ExploitSimulation/Behavioral.spec.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Behavioural exploits: currency/progression exploits (fast, moderate, colluding, from
	join), aimbots and head-lock, autoclickers, invalid-action floods, and hostile inputs
	to the recording API. Honest players in the same world must stay clean.
]]

local Harness = require(script.Parent.Parent.Integration.BehavioralHarness)

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	-- Runs an economy: honest players earn ~5/s; `cheat(i, second)` returns an override.
	local function economy(players: number, seconds: number, cheat, options)
		local opts = options or {}
		local w = Harness.world({ Players = players, Signals = { Coins = opts.Signal or {} } })
		local rng = Harness.rng(opts.Seed or 3)
		local firstReport = {}
		for second = 1, seconds do
			for i = 1, players do
				local amount = cheat(i, second)
				if amount == nil then
					amount = math.max(0, rng.normal(5, 1.5))
				end
				w.behavioral:RecordSignal(w.players[i], "Coins", amount)
			end
			w.advance(1)
			for i = 1, players do
				if firstReport[i] == nil and next(w.kinds(i)) ~= nil then
					firstReport[i] = second
				end
			end
		end
		return w, firstReport
	end

	local function frame(time: number): number
		return math.ceil(time * 60 - 1e-9) / 60
	end

	t.describe("Behavioural exploits", function()
		t.it("a 10x currency exploit starting mid-session is reported within a minute", function()
			local w, first = economy(6, 400, function(i, second)
				if i == 6 and second > 200 then
					return 50
				end
				return nil
			end)
			expect(first[6] ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(first[6] - 200 <= 60).toBe(true)
			local record = w.kinds(6).SignalAnomaly
			expect(record ~= nil).toBe(true)
			local details = record.Details
			expect(details.Signal).toBe("Coins")
			expect(details.Ratio > 3).toBe(true)
			expect(details.BaselineSource).toBe("Population")
			expect(record.Confidence <= 0.8).toBe(true)
			expect(#w.flagged()).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("a moderate 4x exploit is reported", function()
			local w, first = economy(6, 600, function(i)
				if i == 1 then
					return 20
				end
				return nil
			end)
			expect(first[1] ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(w.flagged()).toEqual({ 1 })
		end)

		t.it("two colluding exploiters cannot shift the median baseline", function()
			local w = economy(7, 400, function(i)
				if i >= 6 then
					return 60
				end
				return nil
			end)
			expect(w.flagged()).toEqual({ 6, 7 })
		end)

		t.it("an exploiter cheating from the moment they join is still compared with honest players", function()
			local w = economy(5, 300, function(i)
				if i == 3 then
					return 40
				end
				return nil
			end)
			expect(w.flagged()).toEqual({ 3 })
		end)

		t.it("a game-defined Baseline catches exploits on a two-player server", function()
			local w = economy(2, 300, function(i)
				if i == 2 then
					return 40
				end
				return nil
			end, { Signal = { Baseline = { Mean = 5, StdDev = 2 } } })
			expect(w.flagged()).toEqual({ 2 })
			expect(w.kinds(2).SignalAnomaly.Details.BaselineSource).toBe("Defined")
		end)

		t.it("MaxPerSecond reports impossible gains (duplication) without any baseline", function()
			local w = economy(1, 60, function()
				return 1000
			end, { Signal = { MaxPerSecond = 100, Statistical = false } })
			local record = w.kinds(1).SignalRateLimit
			expect(record ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(record.Details.MaxPerSecond).toBe(100)
		end)

		t.it("aimbot accuracy is reported; honest shooters are not", function()
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 6, Combat = true })
			local rng = Harness.rng(41)
			local accuracy = { 0.35, 0.4, 0.5, 0.55, 0.6, 0.99 }
			for _ = 1, 1500 do
				for i = 1, 6 do
					local outcome = "miss"
					if rng.chance(accuracy[i]) then
						outcome = if rng.chance(0.25) then "head" else "body"
					end
					w.shoot(i, outcome)
				end
				w.advance(0.2)
			end
			expect(w.flagged()).toEqual({ 6 })
			local details = w.kinds(6).AccuracyAnomaly.Details
			expect(details.Weapon).toBe("Rifle")
			expect(details.LowerBound > details.Threshold).toBe(true)
			expect(details.Basis).toBe("Population")
		end)

		t.it("head-lock (95% headshots at normal accuracy) is reported", function()
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 6, Combat = true })
			local rng = Harness.rng(43)
			local headshots = { 0.15, 0.2, 0.25, 0.3, 0.2, 0.95 }
			for _ = 1, 1500 do
				for i = 1, 6 do
					local outcome = "miss"
					if rng.chance(0.5) then
						outcome = if rng.chance(headshots[i]) then "head" else "body"
					end
					w.shoot(i, outcome)
				end
				w.advance(0.2)
			end
			expect(w.flagged()).toEqual({ 6 })
			expect(w.kinds(6).HeadshotAnomaly ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(w.kinds(6).AccuracyAnomaly).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("aimbot on a server too small for a baseline is caught by the absolute floor", function()
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 1, Combat = true })
			for _ = 1, 1500 do
				w.shoot(1, "head")
				w.advance(0.2)
			end
			local kinds = w.kinds(1)
			expect(kinds.AccuracyAnomaly.Details.Basis).toBe("Absolute")
			expect(kinds.HeadshotAnomaly ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		-- Clicks every `interval` s, starting at `phase` s into a 60 Hz frame, with normal
		-- network jitter (std `jitter`), as received on frame boundaries by the server.
		local function autoclick(interval: number, phase: number, jitter: number)
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 1 })
			local rng = Harness.rng(99)
			local now = 0
			for k = 1, 200 do
				local at = math.max(now, frame(k * interval + phase + rng.normal(0, jitter)))
				w.advance(at - now)
				now = at
				w.behavioral:RecordAction(w.players[1], "Click")
			end
			return w.kinds(1).RegularTiming
		end

		t.it("a 1 s autoclicker is reported even at the worst frame phase", function()
			-- Phase 0 puts every click on a frame boundary, so jitter flips it between two
			-- frames: the worst case for the server's view of regularity.
			local record = autoclick(1, 0, 0.003)
			expect(record ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(record.Details.CV < 0.02).toBe(true)
			expect(autoclick(1, 0.008, 0.005) ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("a 0.5 s autoclicker is reported mid-frame with low jitter", function()
			expect(autoclick(0.5, 0.008, 0.003) ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("known limitation: fast clickers on a frame boundary or a jittery connection are missed", function()
			-- Documented in docs/BEHAVIORAL.md. Server-observed spread comes from network
			-- jitter and frame flips; with CV < 0.02 required, fast intervals need almost
			-- jitter-free delivery to be reported.
			expect(autoclick(0.5, 0, 0.003)).toBeNil()
			expect(autoclick(0.25, 0.008, 0.015)).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("an invalid-action flood (exploit firing a purchase remote) is reported", function()
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 1 })
			local rng = Harness.rng(5)
			for _ = 1, 200 do
				w.behavioral:RecordAction(w.players[1], "Buy", false)
				w.advance(frame(math.max(0.05, rng.normal(0.33, 0.1))))
			end
			local record = w.kinds(1).RepeatedInvalidActions
			expect(record ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(record.Details.Action).toBe("Buy")
			expect(record.Confidence <= 0.8).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("hostile values passed to the recording API are refused without changing state", function()
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 1, Signals = { Coins = {} } })
			local b = w.behavioral
			local p = w.players[1]
			expect(b:RecordSignal(p, "Coins", 0 / 0)).toBe(false)
			expect(b:RecordSignal(p, "Coins", math.huge)).toBe(false)
			expect(b:RecordSignal(p, "Coins", -5)).toBe(false)
			expect(b:RecordSignal(p, "Coins", 1e13)).toBe(false)
			expect(b:RecordSignal(p, "Coins", "5" :: any)).toBe(false)
			expect(b:GetProfile(p).Signals.Coins).toBeNil()
			expect(function()
				b:RecordSignal(p, "Gems", 1)
			end).toThrow()
			expect(function()
				b:RecordAction(p, string.rep("x", 65))
			end).toThrow()
			expect(function()
				b:DefineSignal("Coins", {})
			end).toThrow()
			expect(function()
				b:DefineSignal("Bad", { MaxPerSecond = -1 })
			end).toThrow()
			expect(function()
				b:DefineSignal("Bad2", { Typo = 1 })
			end).toThrow()
		end)

		t.it("per-player action names are bounded", function()
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 1 })
			local recorded = 0
			for i = 1, 100 do
				if w.behavioral:RecordAction(w.players[1], "Action" .. i) then
					recorded += 1
				end
			end
			expect(recorded).toBe(32)
			expect(w.behavioral:GetStats().ActionsDropped).toBe(68)
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/ExploitSimulation/Combat.spec.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Combat exploits as claims a modified client would send. Each must be refused, and
	sustained abuse must be reported. The client never sends damage; ACF computes it, so
	"damage modifiers" are impossible by construction (asserted below).
]]

local Harness = require(script.Parent.Parent.Integration.CombatHarness)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("Exploit simulation: combat", function()
		t.it("rapid fire beyond the weapon's fire rate", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			local accepted = 0
			for _ = 1, 30 do
				if a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim()).Allowed then
					accepted += 1
				end
				a.advance(0.01) -- 100 shots/s requested; 10/s allowed
			end
			expect(accepted <= 5).toBe(true)
			expect(a.kinds().CombatFireRate ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("infinite ammo: firing past an empty magazine", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			local accepted = 0
			for _ = 1, 45 do
				if a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim()).Allowed then
					accepted += 1
				end
				a.advance(0.1)
			end
			expect(accepted).toBe(30)
			expect(a.kinds().CombatNoAmmo ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("instant reload: firing during a reload", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			a.combat:SetAmmo(a.shooter, "Rifle", 0)
			a.combat:Reload(a.shooter, "Rifle")
			local refused = 0
			for _ = 1, 15 do
				local result = a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim())
				if result.Reason == "Reloading" then
					refused += 1
				end
				a.advance(0.1)
			end
			expect(refused).toBe(15)
			expect(a.kinds().CombatReloading ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("using a weapon the player does not own", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE, Sniper = Harness.RIFLE }, Owned = { "Rifle" } })
			for _ = 1, 3 do
				local result = a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Sniper", a.claim())
				expect(result.Reason).toBe("NotOwned")
				a.advance(0.2)
			end
			expect(a.kinds().CombatNotOwned ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("fabricated weapon ids", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			for i = 1, 3 do
				expect(a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "AdminGun" .. i, a.claim()).Reason).toBe("UnknownWeapon")
			end
			expect(a.kinds().CombatUnknownWeapon ~= nil).toBe(true)
			-- Unknown ids create no per-weapon state (bounded memory).
			local state = a.framework:GetSession(a.shooter):GetState("Combat.State")
			local weapons = 0
			for _ in state.weapons do
				weapons += 1
			end
			expect(weapons).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("kill aura / reach: hitting targets far beyond range", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Sword = Harness.SWORD }, VictimDistance = 40 })
			for _ = 1, 4 do
				local result = a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Sword", { Target = a.torso })
				expect(result.Reason).toBe("OutOfRange")
				a.advance(0.5)
			end
			expect(a.kinds().CombatOutOfRange ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("wall-bang: hitting through solid geometry", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			a.fake.Physics:AddBox(20, 0, -50, 22, 30, 50)
			for _ = 1, 6 do
				expect(a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim()).Reason).toBe("NoLineOfSight")
				a.advance(0.1)
			end
			expect(a.kinds().CombatNoLineOfSight ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("spoofed shot origin (shooting from somewhere else)", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			for _ = 1, 4 do
				local result = a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim({ Origin = Fakes.vector3(48, 4.5, 0) }))
				expect(result.Reason).toBe("OriginMismatch")
				a.advance(0.1)
			end
			expect(a.kinds().CombatOriginMismatch ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("silent aim: claiming a hit point far from the target part", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			for _ = 1, 8 do
				local result = a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim({ HitPosition = Fakes.vector3(10, 3, 40) }))
				expect(result.Reason).toBe("HitMismatch")
				a.advance(0.1)
			end
			expect(a.kinds().CombatHitMismatch ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("ability cooldown bypass", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Fireball = Harness.FIREBALL } })
			local accepted = 0
			for _ = 1, 10 do
				if a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Fireball", { HitPosition = Fakes.vector3(20, 3, 0) }).Allowed then
					accepted += 1
				end
				a.advance(0.5)
			end
			expect(accepted).toBe(1)
			expect(a.kinds().CombatCooldown ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("instant-hit projectiles (impact before the projectile could arrive)", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rocket = Harness.ROCKET }, VictimDistance = 300 })
			for shot = 1, 4 do
				local id = "r" .. shot
				a.combat:FireProjectile(a.shooter, "Rocket", { Origin = Fakes.vector3(0, 3, 0), Direction = Fakes.vector3(1, 0, 0), ShotId = id })
				a.advance(0.1) -- 300 studs needs 3 s
				expect(a.combat:ValidateProjectileHit(a.shooter, id, { Target = a.torso }).Reason).toBe("ProjectileTiming")
				a.advance(1)
			end
			expect(a.kinds().CombatProjectileTiming ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("magic bullets: impacts off the fired trajectory", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rocket = Harness.ROCKET }, VictimDistance = 100 })
			for shot = 1, 4 do
				local id = "m" .. shot
				-- Fired straight up the z axis, but "hits" a target on the x axis.
				a.combat:FireProjectile(a.shooter, "Rocket", { Origin = Fakes.vector3(0, 3, 0), Direction = Fakes.vector3(0, 0, 1), ShotId = id })
				a.advance(1.1)
				expect(a.combat:ValidateProjectileHit(a.shooter, id, { Target = a.torso }).Reason).toBe("OffTrajectory")
			end
			expect(a.kinds().CombatOffTrajectory ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("replayed projectile hits and fabricated shot ids", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rocket = Harness.ROCKET }, VictimDistance = 100 })
			a.combat:FireProjectile(a.shooter, "Rocket", { Origin = Fakes.vector3(0, 3, 0), Direction = Fakes.vector3(1, 0, 0), ShotId = "once" })
			a.advance(1.1)
			expect(a.combat:ValidateProjectileHit(a.shooter, "once", { Target = a.torso }).Allowed).toBe(true)
			for _ = 1, 3 do
				expect(a.combat:ValidateProjectileHit(a.shooter, "once", { Target = a.torso }).Reason).toBe("UnknownShot")
				expect(a.combat:ValidateProjectileHit(a.shooter, "never-fired", { Target = a.torso }).Reason).toBe("UnknownShot")
			end
			expect(a.kinds().CombatUnknownShot ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("shooting while dead", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			a.fake.Physics:Set(a.shooterCharacter, { Health = 0 })
			for _ = 1, 7 do
				expect(a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim()).Reason).toBe("ShooterDead")
				a.advance(0.1)
			end
			expect(a.kinds().CombatShooterDead ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("malformed claims never error and are refused", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE, Rocket = Harness.ROCKET } })
			local cases = {
				"not a table",
				{ Origin = "here" },
				{ Origin = Fakes.vector3(0 / 0, 0, 0), Target = a.torso },
				{ Origin = Fakes.vector3(0, 4.5, 0), Target = "torso" },
				{ Origin = Fakes.vector3(0, 4.5, 0), Target = a.torso, HitPosition = { X = 1 } },
			}
			for _, claim in cases do
				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					return a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", claim)
				end)
				expect(ok).toBe(true)
				expect(result.Allowed).toBe(false)
				a.advance(0.1)
			end
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return a.combat:FireProjectile(a.shooter, "Rocket", { Origin = Fakes.vector3(0, 3, 0), Direction = Fakes.vector3(0, 0, 0), ShotId = "z" })
			end)
			expect(ok).toBe(true)
			expect(result.Reason).toBe("InvalidClaim")
		end)

		t.it("damage cannot be supplied by the client", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			local claim = a.claim()
			claim.Damage = 9999
			claim.Headshot = true
			local result = a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", claim)
			expect(result.Damage).toBe(25)
			expect(result.Headshot).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("projectile memory stays bounded under fire-and-forget spam", function()
			local weapons = { Pellet = { Kind = "Projectile", Damage = 1, FireRate = 1000, Burst = 1000, MaxRange = 100, ProjectileSpeed = 100 } }
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = weapons })
			for i = 1, 500 do
				a.combat:FireProjectile(a.shooter, "Pellet", { Origin = Fakes.vector3(0, 3, 0), Direction = Fakes.vector3(1, 0, 0), ShotId = "p" .. i })
			end
			local state = a.framework:GetSession(a.shooter):GetState("Combat.State")
			expect(#state.projectileOrder <= 32).toBe(true)
			local stored = 0
			for _ in state.projectiles do
				stored += 1
			end
			expect(stored <= 32).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("a single desynced shot is never reported", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim({ Origin = Fakes.vector3(48, 4.5, 0) }))
			a.advance(0.1)
			a.fake.Physics:AddBox(20, 0, -50, 22, 30, 50)
			a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim())
			expect(#a.combatEvidence()).toBe(0)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Combat module robustness", function()
		t.it("providers that throw fail closed", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			a.combat:SetProvider("Inventory", function()
				error("inventory service down")
			end)
			expect(a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim()).Reason).toBe("NotOwned")
			expect(#Fakes.findLogs(a.fake.Logs, "Combat provider errored", "Error")).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("rejects duplicate definitions and unknown providers", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			expect(function()
				a.combat:Define("Rifle", Harness.RIFLE)
			end).toThrow("already defined")
			expect(function()
				a.combat:SetProvider("Teams", function() end)
			end).toThrow("unknown provider")
		end)

		t.it("refuses attacks while stopped", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			a.framework:Stop()
			expect(a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim()).Reason).toBe("NotRunning")
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/ExploitSimulation/Movement.spec.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Movement and physics exploits as the server observes them. Each must be reported once
	the behaviour is sustained, and isolated single events must not be reported.
]]

local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Framework = require(ACF.Core.Framework)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local DT = 0.2

local function setup(config, envOptions)
	local fake = Fakes.environment(envOptions)
	fake.Physics:AddFloor()
	local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
	framework:Initialize(config or {})
	framework:Start()
	local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
	local character = player:Spawn()
	fake.Physics:Set(character, {})
	local world = { framework = framework, fake = fake, player = player, character = character }
	function world.step(fields)
		if fields then
			fake.Physics:Set(world.character, fields)
		end
		fake.Clock.Advance(DT)
		framework:Step()
	end
	function world.kinds()
		local kinds = {}
		for _, record in framework:GetEvidence(player) do
			kinds[record.Kind] = record
		end
		return kinds
	end
	return world
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("Exploit simulation: movement", function()
		t.it("reports a sustained 2x speed hack with an explanation", function()
			local w = setup()
			local x = 0
			for _ = 1, 25 do
				x += 32 * DT
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 32 })
			end
			local record = w.kinds().SpeedExceeded
			expect(record ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(record.Category).toBe("Movement")
			expect(record.Details.ObservedSpeed > 25).toBe(true)
			expect(record.Details.AllowedSpeed).toBe(20)
		end)

		t.it("reports a speed hack quickly (within 2 seconds)", function()
			local w = setup()
			local x = 0
			for _ = 1, 10 do
				x += 40 * DT
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 40 })
			end
			expect(w.kinds().SpeedExceeded ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("does not report a single teleport", function()
			local w = setup()
			for i = 1, 5 do
				w.step({ X = i })
			end
			w.step({ X = 300 })
			for i = 1, 10 do
				w.step({ X = 300 + i })
			end
			expect(w.kinds().TeleportDetected).toBeNil()
			expect(w.kinds().SpeedExceeded).toBeNil()
			expect(w.framework:GetModule("Movement"):GetStats().Violations.Teleport).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("reports repeated teleports", function()
			local w = setup()
			local x = 0
			for _ = 1, 3 do
				for _ = 1, 5 do
					x += 1
					w.step({ X = x })
				end
				x += 300
				w.step({ X = x })
			end
			local record = w.kinds().TeleportDetected
			expect(record ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(record.Details.Axis).toBe("Horizontal")
		end)

		t.it("reports repeated vertical teleports", function()
			local w = setup()
			w.fake.Physics:AddBox(-50, 0, -50, 50, 500, 50)
			w.step({ Y = 3 })
			w.step({ Y = 503 })
			w.step({ Y = 503 })
			w.fake.Physics:AddBox(-50, 500, -50, 50, 1000, 50)
			w.step({ Y = 1003 })
			local record = w.kinds().TeleportDetected
			expect(record ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(record.Details.Axis).toBe("Vertical")
		end)

		t.it("reports hovering flight", function()
			local w = setup()
			w.step({ Y = 3 })
			for _ = 1, 25 do
				w.step({ Y = 60, VY = 0 })
			end
			local record = w.kinds().Flight
			expect(record ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(record.Details.FallRate).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("standing still for a minute does not license a teleport (found in the Phase 8 Studio run)", function()
			local w = setup()
			for _ = 1, 300 do -- 60 s idle
				w.step({ X = 0, VX = 0 })
			end
			-- Before the cap, 60 s of credit allowed ~1,200 studs. Now ~71 (a 3 s lag catch-up).
			w.step({ X = 150 })
			for _ = 1, 15 do -- 3 s idle again
				w.step({ X = 150, VX = 0 })
			end
			w.step({ X = 300 })
			expect(w.kinds().TeleportDetected ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("hovering while spoofing velocity is reported once the stall credit runs out", function()
			-- Frozen position + non-zero reported velocity looks like a lag stall; the credit
			-- is capped at MaxStallCreditSeconds (3 s) per airborne period.
			local w = setup()
			w.step({ Y = 3 })
			for _ = 1, 40 do
				w.step({ Y = 60, VY = 25, VX = 10 })
			end
			expect(w.kinds().Flight ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("reports slowly-rising fly scripts and PlatformStand fly", function()
			local w = setup()
			local y = 3
			for _ = 1, 30 do
				y += 2
				w.step({ Y = y, VY = 10, State = "PlatformStanding" })
			end
			expect(w.kinds().Flight ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("reports infinite jump", function()
			local w = setup()
			local samples = { 3, 9, 13, 14, 12, 18, 21, 21, 18, 24, 27, 27, 24, 30, 33, 33, 29, 35, 38 }
			for _, y in samples do
				w.step({ Y = y })
			end
			local record = w.kinds().AirJump
			expect(record ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(record.Details.AirJumps > 1).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("reports slow noclip that lands inside walls (embedded)", function()
			local w = setup()
			-- Walls 3 studs thick at x = 10, 25, 40, 55; 3-stud steps land inside them.
			for _, wx in { 10, 25, 40, 55 } do
				w.fake.Physics:AddBox(wx, 0, -100, wx + 3, 20, 100)
			end
			local x = 0
			for _ = 1, 22 do
				x += 3
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 15 })
			end
			local record = w.kinds().PassedThroughGeometry
			expect(record ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(record.Details.Mode).toBe("Embedded")
			expect(record.Details.Depth).toBeCloseTo(2, 0.01)
		end)

		t.it("reports fast noclip that crosses a wall between samples", function()
			local w = setup()
			for _, wx in { 10, 30, 50 } do
				w.fake.Physics:AddBox(wx, 0, -100, wx + 2, 20, 100)
			end
			local x = 2
			for _ = 1, 12 do
				x += 5
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 25, WalkSpeed = 25 })
			end
			local record = w.kinds().PassedThroughGeometry
			expect(record ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(record.Details.Mode).toBe("Crossed")
			expect(record.Details.Depth).toBeCloseTo(2, 0.01)
		end)

		t.it("does not report noclip when touching but not crossing a wall", function()
			local w = setup()
			w.fake.Physics:AddBox(10, 0, -100, 13, 20, 100)
			for _ = 1, 3 do
				w.step({ X = 8 })
				w.step({ X = 9.5 })
			end
			expect(w.kinds().PassedThroughGeometry).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("reports abnormal acceleration (velocity flipping)", function()
			local w = setup()
			for i = 1, 20 do
				local v = if i % 2 == 0 then 150 else -150
				w.step({ VX = v })
			end
			expect(w.kinds().AbnormalAcceleration ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("can disable individual detectors", function()
			local w = setup({ ModuleSettings = { Movement = { Detectors = { Speed = false } } } })
			local x = 0
			for _ = 1, 25 do
				x += 40 * DT
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 40 })
			end
			expect(w.kinds().SpeedExceeded).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("an expired allowance no longer covers cheating", function()
			local w = setup()
			w.framework:GetModule("Movement"):Allow(w.player, "Flight", 1)
			for _ = 1, 30 do
				w.step({ Y = 60, VY = 0 })
			end
			expect(w.kinds().Flight ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Exploit simulation: physics", function()
		t.it("reports fling (extreme angular velocity)", function()
			local w = setup()
			for _ = 1, 3 do
				w.step({ AngularSpeed = 5000 })
			end
			local record = w.kinds().FlingDetected
			expect(record ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(record.Category).toBe("Physics")
		end)

		t.it("reports extreme horizontal velocity", function()
			local w = setup()
			for _ = 1, 3 do
				w.step({ VX = 2000 })
			end
			expect(w.kinds().ExtremeVelocity ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("ignores extreme downward velocity (long falls)", function()
			local w = setup()
			w.fake.Physics:AddBox(-10, -10, -10, 10, 5000, 10)
			for i = 1, 5 do
				w.step({ Y = 5003 - i * 100, VY = -900, X = 50 })
			end
			expect(w.kinds().ExtremeVelocity).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("reports tp-walk: displacement without matching velocity", function()
			local w = setup()
			local x = 0
			for _ = 1, 25 do
				x += 30 * DT
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 0 })
			end
			local kinds = w.kinds()
			expect(kinds.VelocityMismatch ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(kinds.SpeedExceeded ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Movement module robustness", function()
		t.it("fails cleanly without a physics service and blocks Physics", function()
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			fake.Env.Physics = nil
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			framework:Initialize({})
			local status = framework:Start()
			expect(status.State).toBe("Running")
			expect(status.Modules.Movement.State).toBe("Failed")
			expect(status.Modules.Movement.Error).toContain("Environment.Physics")
			expect(status.Modules.Physics.State).toBe("Blocked")
		end)

		t.it("validates allowance arguments", function()
			local w = setup()
			local movement = w.framework:GetModule("Movement")
			expect(function()
				movement:Allow(w.player, "Fly", 1)
			end).toThrow("unknown allowance kind")
			expect(function()
				movement:Allow(w.player, "Speed", 0)
			end).toThrow("seconds")
			expect(function()
				movement:Allow(w.player, "Speed", 1, { Multiplier = 0.5 })
			end).toThrow("Multiplier")
			expect(movement:Allow(Fakes.player(99), "Flight", 1)).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("survives a throwing physics probe", function()
			local w = setup()
			w.fake.Physics.Sample = function()
				error("probe exploded")
			end
			for _ = 1, 10 do
				w.step(nil)
			end
			expect(w.framework:GetState()).toBe("Running")
			expect(#Fakes.findLogs(w.fake.Logs, "Character sampling failed") >= 1).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("keeps per-player movement state bounded", function()
			local w = setup()
			local x = 0
			for _ = 1, 2000 do
				x += 3
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 15 })
			end
			local state = w.framework:GetSession(w.player):GetState("Movement.State")
			expect(#state.detectors.path <= 48).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("validates movement configuration", function()
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			expect(function()
				framework:Initialize({ ModuleSettings = { Movement = { SpeedTolerance = 0.5 } } })
			end).toThrow("ModuleSettings.Movement.SpeedTolerance")
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/ExploitSimulation/Pipeline.spec.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Adversarial scenarios against the Phase 1 foundation. Phase 1 has no client-facing
	surface yet, so these cover the ways hostile *input reaching detectors* (or a
	misbehaving detector) could break the pipeline: memory exhaustion, poisoned payloads,
	identity spoofing and evidence-reset by rejoining.
]]

local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Framework = require(ACF.Core.Framework)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local function boot(config, modules)
	local fake = Fakes.environment()
	local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
	framework:Initialize(config)
	for _, def in modules do
		framework:RegisterModule(def)
	end
	framework:Start()
	return framework, fake
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("Exploit simulation: detection flood", function()
		t.it("bounds memory when a detector reports thousands of distinct kinds", function()
			local def = Fakes.module("Noisy")
			local framework, fake = boot({
				Evidence = { MaxRecordsPerPlayer = 32 },
				Detection = { ReportBurst = 10000, ReportsPerSecond = 1000 },
			}, { def })
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			for i = 1, 5000 do
				def.Context:Report(player, { Kind = "K" .. i, Severity = 1, Confidence = 0.1 })
			end
			expect(#framework:GetEvidence(player)).toBe(32)
			expect(framework:GetEvidenceSummary(player).Evicted).toBe(5000 - 32)
		end)

		t.it("rate limits a single detector per player without affecting other detectors", function()
			local noisy = Fakes.module("Noisy")
			local quiet = Fakes.module("Quiet")
			local framework, fake = boot({ Detection = { ReportBurst = 5, ReportsPerSecond = 1 } }, { noisy, quiet })
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			local accepted = 0
			for _ = 1, 100 do
				if noisy.Context:Report(player, { Kind = "A", Severity = 1, Confidence = 1 }) then
					accepted += 1
				end
			end
			expect(accepted).toBe(5)
			expect((quiet.Context:Report(player, { Kind = "A", Severity = 1, Confidence = 1 }))).toBe(true)
			expect(framework:GetStatus().Detections.Rejected.rate_limited).toBe(95)
			-- Rate-limit warnings are themselves rate limited.
			expect(#Fakes.findLogs(fake.Logs, "rate limited") <= 20).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("merges a repeated identical signal into one record", function()
			local def = Fakes.module("Speed")
			local framework, fake = boot({ Detection = { ReportBurst = 10000 } }, { def })
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			for _ = 1, 1000 do
				def.Context:Report(player, { Kind = "SpeedExceeded", Severity = 10, Confidence = 0.5 })
			end
			local records = framework:GetEvidence(player)
			expect(#records).toBe(1)
			expect(records[1].Count).toBe(1000)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Exploit simulation: hostile detail payloads", function()
		t.it("sanitises cyclic, oversized and non-serialisable details", function()
			local def = Fakes.module("Remote")
			local framework, fake = boot({ Detection = { MaxDetailKeys = 8, MaxDetailStringLength = 16 } }, { def })
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			local cyclic = {}
			cyclic.self = cyclic
			local huge = {}
			for i = 1, 100 do
				huge["k" .. i] = i
			end
			local ok = def.Context:Report(player, {
				Kind = "Malformed",
				Severity = 5,
				Confidence = 0.5,
				Details = {
					Cyclic = cyclic,
					Huge = huge,
					Long = string.rep("A", 1000),
					Fn = function() end,
					Thread = coroutine.create(function() end),
					Nan = 0 / 0,
					[{}] = "table key",
				},
			})
			expect(ok).toBe(true)
			local details = framework:GetEvidence(player)[1].Details
			expect(details.Fn).toBeNil()
			expect(details.Thread).toBeNil()
			expect(#details.Long).toBe(16 + 3)
			expect(details.Nan).toBe("nan")
			expect(details.Cyclic.self).toBe("<table>")
			local hugeKeys = 0
			for _ in details.Huge do
				hugeKeys += 1
			end
			expect(hugeKeys <= 9).toBe(true) -- 8 keys + _truncated marker
			expect(table.isfrozen(details)).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("does not let the caller mutate stored evidence afterwards", function()
			local def = Fakes.module("Speed")
			local framework, fake = boot({}, { def })
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			local details = { Speed = 50 }
			def.Context:Report(player, { Kind = "A", Severity = 5, Confidence = 1, Details = details })
			details.Speed = 0
			expect(framework:GetEvidence(player)[1].Details.Speed).toBe(50)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Exploit simulation: identity and reset", function()
		t.it("binds reports to the reporting module's identity and category", function()
			local def = Fakes.module("Speed", { Category = "Movement" })
			local framework, fake = boot({}, { def })
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			def.Context:Report(player, {
				Kind = "A",
				Severity = 5,
				Confidence = 1,
				Detector = "Combat",
				Category = "Combat",
			} :: any)
			local record = framework:GetEvidence(player)[1]
			expect(record.Detector).toBe("Speed")
			expect(record.Category).toBe("Movement")
		end)

		t.it("does not reset evidence when a player rejoins the same server", function()
			local def = Fakes.module("Speed")
			local framework, fake = boot({}, { def })
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			def.Context:Report(player, { Kind = "A", Severity = 5, Confidence = 1 })
			fake.Players:Leave(player)
			local again = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			def.Context:Report(again, { Kind = "B", Severity = 5, Confidence = 1 })
			local records = framework:GetEvidence(again)
			expect(#records).toBe(2)
			expect(records[1].SessionId ~= records[2].SessionId).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("does not accept a stale Session object after rejoin", function()
			local def = Fakes.module("Speed")
			local framework, fake = boot({}, { def })
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			local stale = framework:GetSession(player)
			fake.Players:Leave(player)
			fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			local ok, reason = def.Context:Report(stale, { Kind = "A", Severity = 5, Confidence = 1 })
			expect(ok).toBe(false)
			expect(reason).toBe("no_session")
		end)

		t.it("does not resolve a forged table carrying another player's UserId", function()
			local def = Fakes.module("Speed")
			local framework, fake = boot({}, { def })
			fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			local forged = { UserId = 1 }
			expect(framework:GetSession(forged)).toBeNil()
			local ok = def.Context:Report(forged, { Kind = "A", Severity = 5, Confidence = 1 })
			expect(ok).toBe(false)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Exploit simulation: framework robustness", function()
		t.it("keeps per-module state isolated across framework instances", function()
			local a1, a2 = Fakes.module("A"), Fakes.module("A")
			local f1, fake1 = boot({}, { a1 })
			local f2 = boot({}, { a2 })
			local player = fake1.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			a1.Context:Report(player, { Kind = "X", Severity = 5, Confidence = 1 })
			expect(#f1:GetEvidence(1)).toBe(1)
			expect(#f2:GetEvidence(1)).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("survives a module that throws inside every event it handles", function()
			local Topics = require(ACF.Core.Topics)
			local hostile = Fakes.module("Hostile", {
				Start = function(self)
					self.Context.Events:Subscribe(Topics.PlayerSessionStarted, function()
						error("hostile handler")
					end)
				end,
			})
			local framework, fake = boot({}, { hostile })
			for i = 1, 50 do
				fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(i))
			end
			expect(#framework:GetSessions()).toBe(50)
			expect(framework:GetState()).toBe("Running")
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/ExploitSimulation/Remotes.spec.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Adversarial remote traffic: what an exploit executor can send through a RemoteEvent
	or RemoteFunction, and what an honest-but-laggy client might send. The first must be
	rejected (and reported when sustained); the second must not be reported.
]]

local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Framework = require(ACF.Core.Framework)
local V = require(ACF.Detection.RemoteSecurity.Validators)
local Policies = require(ACF.Detection.RemoteSecurity.Policies)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local function setup(config)
	local fake = Fakes.environment()
	local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
	framework:Initialize(config or {})
	framework:Start()
	local security = framework:GetModule("RemoteSecurity")
	local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
	local calls = { n = 0 }
	return framework, fake, security, player, calls
end

local function buyRemote(security, calls, extra)
	local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Buy")
	local spec = {
		Arguments = { V.string({ MaxLength = 32 }), V.integer({ Min = 1, Max = 99 }) },
		Handler = function()
			calls.n += 1
		end,
	}
	for k, v in extra or {} do
		spec[k] = v
	end
	security:ProtectEvent(remote, spec)
	return remote
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("Exploit simulation: malformed payloads", function()
		t.it("rejects every type-confusion payload without reaching the handler", function()
			local _, _, security, player, calls = setup()
			local remote = buyRemote(security, calls, { RateLimit = { Burst = 1000, PerSecond = 1000 } })
			local payloads = {
				{},
				{ "Sword" },
				{ "Sword", -1 },
				{ "Sword", 0 / 0 },
				{ "Sword", math.huge },
				{ "Sword", 1.5 },
				{ "Sword", "1" },
				{ "Sword", { 1 } },
				{ string.rep("A", 100000), 1 },
				{ "\255\255", 1 },
				{ 123, 1 },
				{ { __index = "x" }, 1 },
				{ "Sword", 1, "extra" },
				{ "Sword", 1, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, "far extra" },
				{ Fakes.instance("Part"), 1 },
				{ Fakes.vector3(1, 1, 1), 1 },
			}
			for _, payload in payloads do
				Fakes.fire(remote, player, table.unpack(payload, 1, 12))
			end
			expect(calls.n).toBe(0)
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, "Sword", 1)
			expect(calls.n).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("bounds work on huge and deeply nested tables", function()
			local _, _, security, player, calls = setup()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Inventory")
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = { V.array(V.struct({ Id = V.string(), Count = V.integer() }), { MaxLength = 10 }) },
				Handler = function()
					calls.n += 1
				end,
			})
			local huge = table.create(200000, { Id = "a", Count = 1 })
			local deep = {}
			local cursor = deep
			for _ = 1, 10000 do
				cursor.next = {}
				cursor = cursor.next
			end
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, huge)
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, { deep })
			expect(calls.n).toBe(0)
			expect(security:GetStats().Inventory.Rejected.invalid_arguments).toBe(2)
		end)

		t.it("rejects forged instances and ownership of other players' objects", function()
			local _, fake, security, player, calls = setup()
			local victim = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(2))
			local victimCharacter = victim:Spawn()
			local victimTool = Fakes.instance("Part", "Handle", victimCharacter)
			player:Spawn()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("UseTool")
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = { V.instance("BasePart") },
				Ownership = Policies.Ownership.descendantOfCharacter(1),
				Handler = function()
					calls.n += 1
				end,
			})
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, victimTool)
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, { ClassName = "Part", IsA = function()
				return true
			end })
			expect(calls.n).toBe(0)
			expect(security:GetStats().UseTool.Rejected.ownership_denied).toBe(1)
			expect(security:GetStats().UseTool.Rejected.invalid_arguments).toBe(1)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Exploit simulation: suspicion reporting", function()
		t.it("does not report a single invalid request", function()
			local framework, _, security, player, calls = setup()
			local remote = buyRemote(security, calls)
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, "Sword", -5)
			expect(#framework:GetEvidence(player)).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("reports sustained invalid requests with an explanation", function()
			local framework, _, security, player, calls = setup()
			local remote = buyRemote(security, calls)
			for _ = 1, 3 do
				Fakes.fire(remote, player, "Sword", -5)
			end
			local records = framework:GetEvidence(player)
			expect(#records).toBe(1)
			local record = records[1]
			expect(record.Detector).toBe("RemoteSecurity")
			expect(record.Category).toBe("Remote")
			expect(record.Kind).toBe("RemoteInvalidArguments")
			expect(record.Details.Remote).toBe("Buy")
			expect(record.Details.LastProblem).toBe("arg2: below minimum 1")
			expect(record.Confidence > 0 and record.Confidence < 0.95).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("lets suspicion decay so occasional mistakes never add up", function()
			local framework, fake, security, player, calls = setup()
			local remote = buyRemote(security, calls)
			for _ = 1, 20 do
				Fakes.fire(remote, player, "Sword", -5)
				fake.Clock.Advance(60) -- two half-lives between mistakes
			end
			expect(#framework:GetEvidence(player)).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("does not report a short burst from a laggy but honest client", function()
			local framework, _, security, player, calls = setup()
			local remote = buyRemote(security, calls, { RateLimit = { Burst = 5, PerSecond = 2 } })
			-- Network hiccup: 8 queued clicks delivered at once -> 3 rate-limited.
			for _ = 1, 8 do
				Fakes.fire(remote, player, "Sword", 1)
			end
			expect(calls.n).toBe(5)
			expect(#framework:GetEvidence(player)).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("reports sustained flooding", function()
			local framework, _, security, player, calls = setup()
			local remote = buyRemote(security, calls, { RateLimit = { Burst = 5, PerSecond = 2 } })
			for _ = 1, 200 do
				Fakes.fire(remote, player, "Sword", 1)
			end
			local records = framework:GetEvidence(player, { Detector = "RemoteSecurity" })
			expect(#records).toBe(1)
			expect(records[1].Kind).toBe("RemoteRateLimitExceeded")
		end)

		t.it("throttles reports with the report interval", function()
			local _, _, security, player, calls = setup()
			local remote = buyRemote(security, calls, { RateLimit = { Burst = 1000, PerSecond = 1000 } })
			for _ = 1, 50 do
				Fakes.fire(remote, player, "Sword", -5)
			end
			expect(security:GetStats().Buy.Reports).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("honours per-remote weight overrides", function()
			local framework, _, security, player, calls = setup()
			local remote = buyRemote(security, calls, {
				SuspicionWeights = { InvalidArguments = 0 },
				RateLimit = { Burst = 1000, PerSecond = 1000 },
			})
			for _ = 1, 50 do
				Fakes.fire(remote, player, "Sword", -5)
			end
			expect(#framework:GetEvidence(player)).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("ranks ownership violations above invalid arguments", function()
			local framework, _, security, player = setup()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Steal")
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = {},
				Ownership = function()
					return false, "not yours"
				end,
				Handler = function() end,
			})
			Fakes.fire(remote, player)
			Fakes.fire(remote, player)
			local records = framework:GetEvidence(player)
			expect(#records).toBe(1)
			expect(records[1].Kind).toBe("RemoteOwnershipViolation")
			expect(records[1].Severity).toBe(25)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Exploit simulation: replay and isolation", function()
		t.it("blocks replayed purchase transactions", function()
			local _, _, security, player = setup()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Purchase")
			local grants = 0
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = { V.string({ MaxLength = 36 }) },
				RateLimit = { Burst = 1000, PerSecond = 1000 },
				Idempotency = { Argument = 1 },
				Handler = function()
					grants += 1
				end,
			})
			for _ = 1, 100 do
				Fakes.fire(remote, player, "same-receipt")
			end
			expect(grants).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("keeps one player's flooding from limiting another player", function()
			local _, fake, security, player, calls = setup()
			local other = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(2))
			local remote = buyRemote(security, calls, { RateLimit = { Burst = 3, PerSecond = 1 } })
			for _ = 1, 100 do
				Fakes.fire(remote, player, "Sword", 1)
			end
			local before = calls.n
			Fakes.fire(remote, other, "Sword", 1)
			expect(calls.n).toBe(before + 1)
		end)

		t.it("forgets per-player remote state when the player leaves", function()
			local framework, fake, security, player, calls = setup()
			local remote = buyRemote(security, calls)
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, "Sword", 1)
			local session = framework:GetSession(player)
			expect(session:GetState("RemoteSecurity.State") ~= nil).toBe(true)
			fake.Players:Leave(player)
			expect(session:GetState("RemoteSecurity.State")).toBeNil()
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/Fakes.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Test doubles for the platform side of ACF's Environment: signals, players, the
	Players service, a controllable clock, deterministic ids and a capturing log sink.

	These replace Roblox services only. ACF logic under test is always the real code.
]]

local Fakes = {}

-- Sentinel for "remove this field" in merge-style helpers (nil cannot express it).
Fakes.NONE = table.freeze({})

-- Signal -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

local Signal = {}
Signal.__index = Signal

function Signal.new()
	return setmetatable({ _handlers = {} }, Signal)
end

function Signal:Connect(handler)
	local connection = { Connected = true }
	local handlers = self._handlers
	function connection.Disconnect()
		connection.Connected = false
		local index = table.find(handlers, connection)
		if index then
			table.remove(handlers, index)
		end
	end
	connection.Handler = handler
	table.insert(handlers, connection)
	return connection
end

function Signal:Fire(...)
	for _, connection in table.clone(self._handlers) do
		if connection.Connected then
			connection.Handler(...)
		end
	end
end

function Signal:ConnectionCount()
	return #self._handlers
end

Fakes.Signal = Signal

-- Player -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

local Player = {}
Player.__index = Player

function Fakes.player(userId: number, name: string?)
	return setmetatable({
		UserId = userId,
		Name = name or `Player{userId}`,
		Character = nil,
		CharacterAdded = Signal.new(),
		CharacterRemoving = Signal.new(),
		Kicked = nil,
		_children = {},
	}, Player)
end

function Player:Spawn()
	if self.Character then
		self:Despawn()
	end
	local character = Fakes.instance("Model", self.Name)
	self.Character = character
	self.CharacterAdded:Fire(character)
	return character
end

function Player:Despawn()
	local character = self.Character
	if character then
		self.CharacterRemoving:Fire(character)
		self.Character = nil
	end
end

function Player:Kick(message: string?)
	self.Kicked = message or ""
end

function Player:FindFirstChild(name: string)
	return self._children[name]
end

function Player:SetChild(name: string, value: any)
	self._children[name] = value
end

-- Players service --------------------------------------------------------------------

function Fakes.players()
	local service = {
		PlayerAdded = Signal.new(),
		PlayerRemoving = Signal.new(),
		_list = {},
	}
	function service.GetPlayers(_self)
		return table.clone(service._list)
	end
	function service.Join(_self, player)
		table.insert(service._list, player)
		service.PlayerAdded:Fire(player)
		return player
	end
	function service.Leave(_self, player)
		service.PlayerRemoving:Fire(player)
		local index = table.find(service._list, player)
		if index then
			table.remove(service._list, index)
		end
	end
	-- Adds a player without firing PlayerAdded (simulates players present before ACF starts).
	function service.Preload(_self, player)
		table.insert(service._list, player)
		return player
	end
	return service
end

-- Clock ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

function Fakes.clock(start: number?)
	local now = start or 1000
	local clock = {}
	clock.Now = function()
		return now
	end
	clock.Wall = function()
		return 1700000000 + now
	end
	clock.Advance = function(seconds: number)
		now += seconds
	end
	return clock
end

-- Physics world ----------------------------------------------------------------------
-- A world of axis-aligned boxes with real ray casting, plus scripted character samples.
-- Samples default to a healthy, standing R15-like character; GroundClearance is computed
-- from the boxes below the feet unless a test sets it explicitly.

local STANDING_HEIGHT = 3

local DEFAULT_SAMPLE = {
	X = 0,
	Y = STANDING_HEIGHT,
	Z = 0,
	VX = 0,
	VY = 0,
	VZ = 0,
	AngularSpeed = 0,
	WalkSpeed = 16,
	JumpVelocity = 50,
	Gravity = 196.2,
	State = "Running",
	Seated = false,
	Health = 100,
	Ping = 0.05,
}

local function rayBox(ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz, box)
	-- Like Roblox: a ray that starts inside a part does not hit that part.
	if ox > box.min[1] and ox < box.max[1] and oy > box.min[2] and oy < box.max[2] and oz > box.min[3] and oz < box.max[3] then
		return nil
	end
	local tmin, tmax = 0, 1
	for axis = 1, 3 do
		local o = if axis == 1 then ox elseif axis == 2 then oy else oz
		local d = if axis == 1 then dx elseif axis == 2 then dy else dz
		local lo, hi = box.min[axis], box.max[axis]
		if math.abs(d) < 1e-9 then
			if o < lo or o > hi then
				return nil
			end
		else
			local t1, t2 = (lo - o) / d, (hi - o) / d
			if t1 > t2 then
				t1, t2 = t2, t1
			end
			tmin = math.max(tmin, t1)
			tmax = math.min(tmax, t2)
			if tmin > tmax then
				return nil
			end
		end
	end
	return tmin
end

function Fakes.physics()
	local physics = { Casts = 0, Samples = 0, _boxes = {}, _samples = {} }

	-- Adds a solid box spanning (x1,y1,z1)-(x2,y2,z2).
	function physics.AddBox(_self, x1, y1, z1, x2, y2, z2)
		table.insert(physics._boxes, {
			min = { math.min(x1, x2), math.min(y1, y2), math.min(z1, z2) },
			max = { math.max(x1, x2), math.max(y1, y2), math.max(z1, z2) },
		})
	end

	-- A large floor whose top surface is y = 0.
	function physics.AddFloor(self)
		self:AddBox(-100000, -10, -100000, 100000, 0, 100000)
	end

	-- Sets (merges) the character's next sample. nil fields keep previous values.
	function physics.Set(_self, character, fields)
		local current = physics._samples[character]
		if current == nil then
			current = table.clone(DEFAULT_SAMPLE)
			physics._samples[character] = current
		end
		for key, value in fields do
			current[key] = value
		end
	end

	function physics.Clear(_self, character)
		physics._samples[character] = nil
	end

	-- Hit targets: physics:SetTarget(part, { X, Y, Z, Model?, Player?, Alive?, IsHead? }).
	physics._targets = {}
	function physics.SetTarget(_self, part, info)
		local entry = table.clone(info)
		if entry.Alive == nil then
			entry.Alive = true
		end
		if entry.IsHead == nil then
			entry.IsHead = false
		end
		physics._targets[part] = entry
	end

	function physics.Describe(_self, part)
		local info = physics._targets[part]
		return if info then table.clone(info) else nil
	end

	-- Enforcement correction: moves the scripted character. Records each call.
	physics.Corrections = {}
	function physics.Correct(self, character, x, y, z)
		table.insert(physics.Corrections, { X = x, Y = y, Z = z })
		self:Set(character, { X = x, Y = y, Z = z, VX = 0, VY = 0, VZ = 0 })
	end

	function physics.Cast(_self, ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz, _character)
		physics.Casts += 1
		local length = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)
		local best = nil
		for _, box in physics._boxes do
			local t = rayBox(ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz, box)
			if t and (best == nil or t < best) then
				best = t
			end
		end
		return if best then best * length else nil
	end

	function physics.Sample(self, character, _player)
		local current = physics._samples[character]
		if current == nil then
			return nil
		end
		physics.Samples += 1
		local sample = table.clone(current)
		if sample.GroundClearance == nil then
			local feet = sample.Y - STANDING_HEIGHT
			local distance = self:Cast(sample.X, feet + 0.01, sample.Z, 0, -64, 0)
			sample.GroundClearance = if distance then math.max(0, distance - 0.01) else math.huge
		end
		return sample
	end

	return physics
end

-- ACF-owned remotes (Environment.Remotes) backed by fireable fakes.
function Fakes.remotes()
	local provider = { Created = {} }
	function provider.GetOrCreate(_self, name, className)
		local existing = provider.Created[name]
		if existing then
			return existing
		end
		local remote = if className == "RemoteFunction" then Fakes.remoteFunction(name) else Fakes.remoteEvent(name)
		provider.Created[name] = remote
		return remote
	end
	return provider
end

-- Environment ------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Returns env plus handles: { Env, Players, Clock, Heartbeat, Physics, Remotes, Logs }.
-- Logs captures every emitted entry; nothing is printed.
-- In-memory Environment.Store. `Deferred = true` holds callbacks until Complete(), to
-- model DataStore latency; FailLoads / FailSaves make the next N calls fail.
function Fakes.store()
	local store = {
		Data = {},
		Deferred = false,
		FailLoads = 0,
		FailSaves = 0,
		Loads = 0,
		Saves = 0,
		-- Per DataStore name: { Loads, Saves }.
		ByScope = {},
		_pending = {},
	}
	local function run(fn)
		if store.Deferred then
			table.insert(store._pending, fn)
		else
			fn()
		end
	end
	local function count(scope, field)
		store.ByScope[scope] = store.ByScope[scope] or { Loads = 0, Saves = 0 }
		store.ByScope[scope][field] += 1
	end
	function store.Load(_self, scope, key, callback)
		store.Loads += 1
		count(scope, "Loads")
		run(function()
			if store.FailLoads > 0 then
				store.FailLoads -= 1
				callback(false, nil, "fake load failure")
				return
			end
			local bucket = store.Data[scope]
			callback(true, if bucket then bucket[key] else nil, nil)
		end)
	end
	function store.Save(_self, scope, key, value, callback)
		store.Saves += 1
		count(scope, "Saves")
		run(function()
			if store.FailSaves > 0 then
				store.FailSaves -= 1
				callback(false, "fake save failure")
				return
			end
			store.Data[scope] = store.Data[scope] or {}
			store.Data[scope][key] = value
			callback(true, nil)
		end)
	end
	-- Runs held callbacks (in order). Returns how many ran.
	function store.Complete()
		local pending = store._pending
		store._pending = {}
		for _, fn in pending do
			fn()
		end
		return #pending
	end
	return store
end

-- Environment.Enforcer that records calls. Kick makes the player leave, like Roblox.
function Fakes.enforcer(players)
	local enforcer = { Kicks = {}, Bans = {}, Unbans = {}, FailBans = 0 }
	function enforcer.Kick(_self, player, message)
		table.insert(enforcer.Kicks, { UserId = player.UserId, Message = message })
		players:Leave(player)
	end
	function enforcer.Ban(_self, userId, seconds, displayReason, privateReason, callback)
		table.insert(enforcer.Bans, { UserId = userId, Seconds = seconds, Display = displayReason, Private = privateReason })
		if enforcer.FailBans > 0 then
			enforcer.FailBans -= 1
			callback(false, "fake ban api failure")
		else
			callback(true, nil)
		end
	end
	function enforcer.Unban(_self, userId, callback)
		table.insert(enforcer.Unbans, userId)
		callback(true, nil)
	end
	return enforcer
end

function Fakes.environment(options: { Heartbeat: boolean?, PrintLogs: boolean?, Store: any?, ClockStart: number? }?)
	local opts = options or {}
	local players = Fakes.players()
	local clock = Fakes.clock(opts.ClockStart)
	local heartbeat = if opts.Heartbeat then Signal.new() else nil
	local physics = Fakes.physics()
	local remotes = Fakes.remotes()
	local logs = {}
	local counter = 0
	local store = opts.Store or Fakes.store()
	local hooks = {}
	-- Like BindToClose: every registration runs at shutdown.
	local shutdown = {
		Flush = function()
			for _, hook in hooks do
				hook.flush()
			end
		end,
		Pending = function()
			local total = 0
			for _, hook in hooks do
				total += hook.pending()
			end
			return total
		end,
		Hooks = hooks,
	}
	local enforcer = Fakes.enforcer(players)
	local env = {
		Store = store,
		Enforcer = enforcer,
		OnShutdown = function(flush, pending)
			table.insert(hooks, { flush = flush, pending = pending })
		end,
		Players = players,
		Heartbeat = heartbeat,
		Physics = physics,
		Remotes = remotes,
		Clock = { Now = clock.Now, Wall = clock.Wall },
		GenerateId = function()
			counter += 1
			return string.format("id-%06d", counter)
		end,
		LogSink = function(entry)
			table.insert(logs, entry)
			if opts.PrintLogs then
				print(entry.Level, entry.Scope, entry.Message)
			end
		end,
	}
	return {
		Env = env,
		Players = players,
		Clock = clock,
		Heartbeat = heartbeat,
		Physics = physics,
		Remotes = remotes,
		Logs = logs,
		Store = store,
		-- Flush() runs every shutdown hook; Pending() sums them.
		Shutdown = shutdown,
		Enforcer = enforcer,
	}
end

-- Finds captured log entries whose message contains `text`.
function Fakes.findLogs(logs, text: string, level: string?)
	local found = {}
	for _, entry in logs do
		if string.find(entry.Message, text, 1, true) and (level == nil or entry.Level == level) then
			table.insert(found, entry)
		end
	end
	return found
end

-- Roblox value stand-ins ---------------------------------------------------------------
-- In Roblox (Studio test runs) these return real engine objects. Under Lune they return
-- tables whose metatable __type makes the harness typeof report the engine type name.

local IS_ROBLOX = (Instance :: any) ~= nil

local CLASS_PARENTS = {
	RemoteEvent = "BaseRemoteEvent",
	UnreliableRemoteEvent = "BaseRemoteEvent",
	Model = "PVInstance",
	Part = "BasePart",
	BasePart = "PVInstance",
}

local FakeInstanceMethods = {}
local FakeInstanceMeta = { __type = "Instance", __index = FakeInstanceMethods }

function FakeInstanceMethods:IsA(className)
	local current = self.ClassName
	while current do
		if current == className then
			return true
		end
		current = CLASS_PARENTS[current]
	end
	return className == "Instance"
end

function FakeInstanceMethods:IsDescendantOf(ancestor)
	local node = self.Parent
	while node do
		if node == ancestor then
			return true
		end
		node = node.Parent
	end
	return false
end

function FakeInstanceMethods:GetChildren()
	return table.clone(self._children)
end

function FakeInstanceMethods:GetDescendants()
	local result = {}
	local function walk(node)
		for _, child in node._children do
			table.insert(result, child)
			walk(child)
		end
	end
	walk(self)
	return result
end

function FakeInstanceMethods:FindFirstChild(name)
	for _, child in self._children do
		if child.Name == name then
			return child
		end
	end
	return nil
end

function FakeInstanceMethods:GetAttribute(name)
	return self._attributes[name]
end

function FakeInstanceMethods:SetAttribute(name, value)
	self._attributes[name] = value
end

function FakeInstanceMethods:GetFullName()
	return self.Name
end

-- Creates an Instance (real in Roblox, fake under Lune) parented to `parent`.
function Fakes.instance(className: string, name: string?, parent: any?)
	if IS_ROBLOX then
		local real = Instance.new(className)
		real.Name = name or className
		real.Parent = parent
		return real
	end
	local fake = setmetatable({
		Name = name or className,
		ClassName = className,
		Parent = parent,
		_children = {},
		_attributes = {},
	}, FakeInstanceMeta)
	if className == "RemoteEvent" or className == "UnreliableRemoteEvent" then
		fake.OnServerEvent = Signal.new()
	end
	if parent then
		table.insert(parent._children, fake)
	end
	return fake
end

function Fakes.vector3(x: number, y: number, z: number)
	if IS_ROBLOX then
		return Vector3.new(x, y, z)
	end
	return setmetatable({ X = x, Y = y, Z = z, Magnitude = math.sqrt(x * x + y * y + z * z) }, { __type = "Vector3" })
end

function Fakes.cframe(x: number, y: number, z: number)
	if IS_ROBLOX then
		return CFrame.new(x, y, z)
	end
	return setmetatable({
		Position = Fakes.vector3(x, y, z),
		GetComponents = function()
			return x, y, z, 1, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 1
		end,
	}, { __type = "CFrame" })
end

-- Fireable remotes. Always fakes: a real RemoteEvent's OnServerEvent cannot be fired from
-- the server, so tests simulate client requests through these.
local function fakeRemote(className: string, name: string?)
	local remote = {
		Name = name or className,
		ClassName = className,
		OnServerEvent = if className ~= "RemoteFunction" then Signal.new() else nil,
		OnServerInvoke = nil,
	}
	function remote.IsA(_self, query)
		return FakeInstanceMethods.IsA(remote, query)
	end
	return remote
end

function Fakes.remoteEvent(name: string?)
	return fakeRemote("RemoteEvent", name)
end

function Fakes.unreliableRemoteEvent(name: string?)
	return fakeRemote("UnreliableRemoteEvent", name)
end

function Fakes.remoteFunction(name: string?)
	return fakeRemote("RemoteFunction", name)
end

-- Simulates a client firing a remote event: remote.OnServerEvent fires with (player, ...).
function Fakes.fire(remote, player, ...)
	remote.OnServerEvent:Fire(player, ...)
end

-- Simulates a client invoking a RemoteFunction. Returns what the server callback returns.
-- With no callback set, Roblox would leave the client yielding; we report that as an error.
function Fakes.invoke(remote, player, ...)
	local callback = remote.OnServerInvoke
	if callback == nil then
		error("RemoteFunction has no OnServerInvoke (client would yield forever)")
	end
	return callback(player, ...)
end

-- A minimal valid module definition that records its lifecycle calls.
function Fakes.module(name: string, overrides: { [string]: any }?)
	local calls = {}
	local definition = {
		Name = name,
		Calls = calls,
		Context = nil,
	}
	function definition:Initialize(context)
		table.insert(calls, "Initialize")
		self.Context = context
	end
	function definition:Start()
		table.insert(calls, "Start")
	end
	function definition:Stop()
		table.insert(calls, "Stop")
	end
	function definition:Destroy()
		table.insert(calls, "Destroy")
	end
	for key, value in overrides or {} do
		definition[key] = value
	end
	return definition
end

return Fakes

```

`tests/Specs/Integration/BehavioralHarness.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Shared behavioural test world: the full framework with fakes, N players and a
	deterministic random generator (Park-Miller), so every scenario is reproducible in
	Lune and in Studio.

	Players stand at (0, 3, 10 * i). A target dummy (player 100) stands at (50, 3, 0)
	with an UpperTorso and a Head registered as hit targets, for combat accuracy tests.
]]

local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Framework = require(ACF.Core.Framework)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local Harness = {}

-- Deterministic uniform [0, 1) generator.
function Harness.rng(seed: number)
	local state = seed % 2147483647
	if state <= 0 then
		state += 2147483646
	end
	local function uniform(): number
		state = (state * 16807) % 2147483647
		return (state - 1) / 2147483646
	end
	local rng = {}
	function rng.uniform(): number
		return uniform()
	end
	-- Approximately normal (sum of 12 uniforms).
	function rng.normal(mean: number, std: number): number
		local sum = 0
		for _ = 1, 12 do
			sum += uniform()
		end
		return mean + (sum - 6) * std
	end
	function rng.chance(p: number): boolean
		return uniform() < p
	end
	return rng
end

Harness.RIFLE = { Kind = "Hitscan", Damage = 25, HeadshotMultiplier = 2, FireRate = 10, MaxRange = 300 }

function Harness.world(options)
	local opts = options or {}
	local fake = Fakes.environment()
	fake.Physics:AddFloor()
	local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
	framework:Initialize(opts.Config or {})
	local behavioral = framework:GetModuleState("Behavioral") ~= "Disabled" and framework:GetModule("Behavioral") or nil
	for name, signal in opts.Signals or {} do
		behavioral:DefineSignal(name, signal)
	end
	local combat = framework:GetModuleState("Combat") ~= "Disabled" and framework:GetModule("Combat") or nil
	if combat and opts.Combat then
		combat:Define("Rifle", Harness.RIFLE)
	end
	framework:Start()

	local players = {}
	for i = 1, opts.Players or 6 do
		local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(i, "P" .. i))
		local character = player:Spawn()
		fake.Physics:Set(character, { X = 0, Y = 3, Z = i * 10 })
		if opts.Combat then
			Fakes.instance("Tool", "Rifle", character)
		end
		players[i] = player
	end

	local world = {
		framework = framework,
		fake = fake,
		behavioral = behavioral,
		combat = combat,
		players = players,
	}

	if opts.Combat then
		local dummy = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(100, "Dummy"))
		local dummyCharacter = dummy:Spawn()
		fake.Physics:Set(dummyCharacter, { X = 50, Y = 3, Z = 0 })
		local torso = Fakes.instance("Part", "UpperTorso", dummyCharacter)
		local head = Fakes.instance("Part", "Head", dummyCharacter)
		fake.Physics:SetTarget(torso, { X = 50, Y = 3, Z = 0, Model = dummyCharacter, Player = dummy })
		fake.Physics:SetTarget(head, { X = 50, Y = 4.5, Z = 0, Model = dummyCharacter, Player = dummy, IsHead = true })
		world.dummy = dummy

		-- One rifle shot from player i: "miss", "body" or "head".
		function world.shoot(i: number, outcome: string)
			local claim = { Origin = Fakes.vector3(0, 4.5, i * 10) }
			if outcome == "body" then
				claim.Target = torso
				claim.HitPosition = Fakes.vector3(50, 3, 0)
			elseif outcome == "head" then
				claim.Target = head
				claim.HitPosition = Fakes.vector3(50, 4.5, 0)
			end
			return combat:ValidateAttack(players[i], "Rifle", claim)
		end
	end

	function world.advance(seconds: number)
		fake.Clock.Advance(seconds)
		framework:Step()
	end

	-- Behavioural evidence kinds for player i (or a player object), as a set.
	function world.kinds(target)
		local player = if type(target) == "number" then players[target] else target
		local kinds = {}
		for _, record in framework:GetEvidence(player) do
			if record.Category == "Behavioral" then
				kinds[record.Kind] = record
			end
		end
		return kinds
	end

	-- Indices of players with any behavioural evidence.
	function world.flagged(): { number }
		local list = {}
		for i, player in players do
			if next(world.kinds(player)) ~= nil then
				table.insert(list, i)
			end
		end
		return list
	end

	return world
end

return Harness

```

`tests/Specs/Integration/BehavioralLegit.spec.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Legitimate behaviour: every scenario must produce NO behavioural evidence. This is the
	false-positive guard for Phase 5; a failure here is a detector bug, not a test bug.

	Scenarios cover varied earning patterns (steady, lumpy, bursty, 2x gamepass, AFK, late
	joiners), server-wide income events, small servers, strong shooters, human clicking
	(including deliberately rhythmic play) and occasional invalid actions.
]]

local Harness = require(script.Parent.BehavioralHarness)

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	local function expectClean(world)
		local flagged = world.flagged()
		if #flagged > 0 then
			local parts = {}
			for _, i in flagged do
				local kinds = {}
				for kind in world.kinds(i) do
					table.insert(kinds, kind)
				end
				table.insert(parts, `P{i}: {table.concat(kinds, ",")}`)
			end
			error({ __testFailure = true, message = "unexpected behavioural evidence: " .. table.concat(parts, "; ") }, 0)
		end
	end

	-- Quantises a time to the next 60 Hz frame, as a Roblox server receives events.
	local function frame(time: number): number
		return math.ceil(time * 60 - 1e-9) / 60
	end

	t.describe("Legitimate behaviour", function()
		t.it("an honest economy with steady, lumpy, bursty, gamepass, AFK and late players", function()
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 8, Signals = { Coins = {} } })
			local rng = Harness.rng(11)
			for second = 1, 900 do
				for i = 1, 3 do -- steady earners
					w.behavioral:RecordSignal(w.players[i], "Coins", math.max(0, rng.normal(5, 1.5)))
				end
				if second % 20 == 0 then -- quest rewards in lumps
					w.behavioral:RecordSignal(w.players[4], "Coins", 100)
				end
				if rng.chance(0.3) then -- bursty
					w.behavioral:RecordSignal(w.players[5], "Coins", math.max(0, rng.normal(16, 5)))
				end
				w.behavioral:RecordSignal(w.players[6], "Coins", math.max(0, rng.normal(10, 2))) -- 2x gamepass
				if second == 5 then -- earns once, then AFK
					w.behavioral:RecordSignal(w.players[7], "Coins", 40)
				end
				if second > 450 then -- starts playing late
					w.behavioral:RecordSignal(w.players[8], "Coins", math.max(0, rng.normal(5, 1.5)))
				end
				w.advance(1)
			end
			local baseline = w.behavioral:GetBaseline("Coins")
			expect(baseline ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(baseline.Source).toBe("Population")
			expect(math.abs(baseline.Mean - 5) < 1.5).toBe(true)
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("a server-wide double-coins event does not flag anyone", function()
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 6, Signals = { Coins = {} } })
			local rng = Harness.rng(23)
			for second = 1, 600 do
				local multiplier = if second > 300 then 2.5 else 1
				for i = 1, 6 do
					w.behavioral:RecordSignal(w.players[i], "Coins", math.max(0, rng.normal(5, 1.5)) * multiplier)
				end
				w.advance(1)
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("a small server has no population baseline, so no statistical reports", function()
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 2, Signals = { Coins = {} } })
			for _ = 1, 300 do
				w.behavioral:RecordSignal(w.players[1], "Coins", 5)
				w.behavioral:RecordSignal(w.players[2], "Coins", 60)
				w.advance(1)
			end
			expect(w.behavioral:GetBaseline("Coins")).toBeNil()
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("an income spike shorter than the CUSUM needs is not reported", function()
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 6, Signals = { Coins = {} } })
			for second = 1, 400 do
				for i = 1, 6 do
					local amount = 5
					if i == 1 and second > 200 and second <= 215 then
						amount = 500 -- a big legitimate payout spread over 15 s
					end
					w.behavioral:RecordSignal(w.players[i], "Coins", amount + (i % 3))
				end
				w.advance(1)
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("strong and average shooters are not flagged (5 minutes, 9,000 validated shots)", function()
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 6, Combat = true })
			local rng = Harness.rng(5)
			local accuracy = { 0.3, 0.35, 0.45, 0.5, 0.6, 0.8 }
			local headshots = { 0.15, 0.2, 0.25, 0.3, 0.35, 0.55 }
			for _ = 1, 1500 do
				for i = 1, 6 do
					local outcome = "miss"
					if rng.chance(accuracy[i]) then
						outcome = if rng.chance(headshots[i]) then "head" else "body"
					end
					expect(w.shoot(i, outcome).Allowed).toBe(true)
				end
				w.advance(0.2)
			end
			local profile = w.behavioral:GetProfile(w.players[6])
			expect(profile.Weapons.Rifle.Accuracy > 0.75).toBe(true)
			expect(w.behavioral:GetStats().CombatConnected).toBe(true)
			-- Fire at an exactly regular rate is also not "automation": Combat does not feed
			-- the timing detector.
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("a strong player alone on a server is judged only by the absolute floor", function()
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 1, Combat = true })
			local rng = Harness.rng(9)
			for _ = 1, 1500 do
				local outcome = "miss"
				if rng.chance(0.85) then
					outcome = if rng.chance(0.5) then "head" else "body"
				end
				w.shoot(1, outcome)
				w.advance(0.2)
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("human clicking, including deliberately rhythmic play", function()
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 3 })
			local rng = Harness.rng(77)
			local times = { 0, 0, 0 }
			local now = 0
			-- P1 casual (CV ~15%), P2 rhythmic (CV ~4%), P3 slow and careful (CV ~8%).
			local means = { 0.3, 0.5, 1.5 }
			local cvs = { 0.15, 0.04, 0.08 }
			local nextAt = { 0.3, 0.5, 1.5 }
			for _ = 1, 4000 do
				local who, soonest = 1, math.huge
				for i = 1, 3 do
					if nextAt[i] < soonest then
						who, soonest = i, nextAt[i]
					end
				end
				local at = frame(soonest)
				if at > now then
					w.advance(at - now)
					now = at
				end
				w.behavioral:RecordAction(w.players[who], "Click")
				times[who] += 1
				nextAt[who] = soonest + math.max(0.05, rng.normal(means[who], means[who] * cvs[who]))
			end
			expect(times[1] > 1000).toBe(true)
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("occasional invalid actions (clicking Buy while broke) are not reported", function()
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 1 })
			local rng = Harness.rng(31)
			for minute = 1, 10 do
				for second = 1, 60 do
					local valid = second > 10 or minute % 3 ~= 0
					w.behavioral:RecordAction(w.players[1], "Buy", valid)
					w.advance(frame(math.max(0.1, rng.normal(1, 0.25))))
				end
			end
			-- A frustrated burst: 15 invalid clicks in 5 s, then they stop.
			for _ = 1, 15 do
				w.behavioral:RecordAction(w.players[1], "Buy", false)
				w.advance(frame(math.max(0.1, rng.normal(1 / 3, 0.08))))
			end
			for _ = 1, 120 do
				w.advance(1)
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("works without Combat and reports it is not connected", function()
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 4, Signals = { Coins = {} }, Config = { Modules = { Combat = false } } })
			for _ = 1, 60 do
				for i = 1, 4 do
					w.behavioral:RecordSignal(w.players[i], "Coins", 5)
				end
				w.advance(1)
			end
			expect(w.behavioral:GetStats().CombatConnected).toBe(false)
			expect(w.behavioral:GetProfile(w.players[1]).Signals.Coins.MeanRate > 4).toBe(true)
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("statistics reset when a player leaves and rejoins", function()
			local w = Harness.world({ Players = 1, Signals = { Coins = {} } })
			for _ = 1, 30 do
				w.behavioral:RecordSignal(w.players[1], "Coins", 5)
				w.behavioral:RecordAction(w.players[1], "Click")
				w.advance(1)
			end
			w.fake.Players:Leave(w.players[1])
			w.fake.Players:Join(w.players[1])
			local profile = w.behavioral:GetProfile(w.players[1])
			expect(next(profile.Signals)).toBeNil()
			expect(next(profile.Actions)).toBeNil()
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/Integration/CombatHarness.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Shared combat test arena. A shooter stands at (0,3,0) holding the weapons it is given;
	a victim stands at (x,3,0). Parts are registered as hit targets in the fake physics.
]]

local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Framework = require(ACF.Core.Framework)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local Harness = {}

function Harness.arena(options)
	local opts = options or {}
	local fake = Fakes.environment()
	fake.Physics:AddFloor()
	local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
	framework:Initialize(opts.Config or {})
	local combat = framework:GetModule("Combat")
	for id, definition in opts.Weapons or {} do
		combat:Define(id, definition)
	end
	framework:Start()

	local shooter = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1, "Shooter"))
	local shooterCharacter = shooter:Spawn()
	fake.Physics:Set(shooterCharacter, { X = 0, Y = 3, Z = 0 })
	for id in opts.Weapons or {} do
		if opts.Owned == nil or table.find(opts.Owned, id) then
			Fakes.instance("Tool", id, shooterCharacter)
		end
	end

	local victim = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(2, "Victim"))
	local victimCharacter = victim:Spawn()
	local distance = opts.VictimDistance or 50
	fake.Physics:Set(victimCharacter, { X = distance, Y = 3, Z = 0 })
	local torso = Fakes.instance("Part", "UpperTorso", victimCharacter)
	local head = Fakes.instance("Part", "Head", victimCharacter)
	fake.Physics:SetTarget(torso, { X = distance, Y = 3, Z = 0, Model = victimCharacter, Player = victim })
	fake.Physics:SetTarget(head, { X = distance, Y = 4.5, Z = 0, Model = victimCharacter, Player = victim, IsHead = true })

	local arena = {
		framework = framework,
		fake = fake,
		combat = combat,
		shooter = shooter,
		shooterCharacter = shooterCharacter,
		victim = victim,
		victimCharacter = victimCharacter,
		torso = torso,
		head = head,
		distance = distance,
	}

	-- Moves the victim (and its hit parts) to x.
	function arena.moveVictim(x)
		arena.distance = x
		fake.Physics:Set(victimCharacter, { X = x })
		fake.Physics:SetTarget(torso, { X = x, Y = 3, Z = 0, Model = victimCharacter, Player = victim })
		fake.Physics:SetTarget(head, { X = x, Y = 4.5, Z = 0, Model = victimCharacter, Player = victim, IsHead = true })
	end

	function arena.claim(fields)
		local claim = { Origin = Fakes.vector3(0, 4.5, 0), Target = torso, HitPosition = Fakes.vector3(arena.distance, 3, 0) }
		for k, v in fields or {} do
			if v == Fakes.NONE then
				claim[k] = nil
			else
				claim[k] = v
			end
		end
		return claim
	end

	function arena.advance(seconds)
		fake.Clock.Advance(seconds)
		framework:Step()
	end

	function arena.kinds()
		local kinds = {}
		for _, record in framework:GetEvidence(shooter) do
			if record.Category == "Combat" then
				kinds[record.Kind] = record
			end
		end
		return kinds
	end

	function arena.combatEvidence()
		local list = {}
		for _, record in framework:GetEvidence(shooter) do
			if record.Category == "Combat" then
				table.insert(list, record.Kind)
			end
		end
		return list
	end

	return arena
end

Harness.RIFLE = {
	Kind = "Hitscan",
	Damage = 25,
	HeadshotMultiplier = 2,
	FireRate = 10,
	MaxRange = 300,
	Ammo = { Magazine = 30, Reserve = 90, ReloadSeconds = 2 },
}

Harness.SWORD = { Kind = "Melee", Damage = 30, FireRate = 2, MaxRange = 8 }

Harness.ROCKET = {
	Kind = "Projectile",
	Damage = 80,
	FireRate = 1,
	MaxRange = 500,
	ProjectileSpeed = 100,
	ProjectileLifetime = 6,
}

Harness.FIREBALL = { Kind = "Ability", Damage = 40, Cooldown = 5, MaxRange = 60 }

return Harness

```

`tests/Specs/Integration/CombatLegit.spec.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Legitimate combat: every scenario must be accepted (or refused without blame) and must
	produce no Combat evidence. Covers max-rate fire, reload cycles, laggy shooters,
	moving targets, projectile travel, abilities, kill races and friendly-fire rules.
]]

local Harness = require(script.Parent.CombatHarness)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	local function expectClean(arena)
		local evidence = arena.combatEvidence()
		if #evidence > 0 then
			error({ __testFailure = true, message = "unexpected combat evidence: " .. table.concat(evidence, ", ") }, 0)
		end
	end

	t.describe("Legitimate combat", function()
		t.it("hits are authorized with server-computed damage", function()
```
