```luau
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			local result = a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim())
			expect(result.Allowed).toBe(true)
			expect(result.Damage).toBe(25)
			expect(result.Target.Player).toBe(a.victim)
			a.advance(0.1)
			local headshot = a.combat:ValidateAttack(
				a.shooter,
				"Rifle",
				a.claim({ Target = a.head, HitPosition = Fakes.vector3(50, 4.5, 0) })
			)
			a.advance(0.1)
			expect(headshot.Allowed).toBe(true)
			expect(headshot.Damage).toBe(50)
			expect(headshot.Headshot).toBe(true)
			expectClean(a)
		end)

		t.it("firing a full magazine at the maximum fire rate, then reloading", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			local accepted = 0
			for _ = 1, 30 do
				if a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim()).Allowed then
					accepted += 1
				end
				a.advance(0.1)
			end
			expect(accepted).toBe(30)
			expect((a.combat:GetAmmo(a.shooter, "Rifle"))).toBe(0)
			expect((a.combat:Reload(a.shooter, "Rifle"))).toBe(true)
			a.advance(2)
			local magazine, reserve = a.combat:GetAmmo(a.shooter, "Rifle")
			expect(magazine).toBe(30)
			expect(reserve).toBe(60)
			expect(a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim()).Allowed).toBe(true)
			expectClean(a)
		end)

		t.it("a laggy client whose shots arrive in small bunches", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			-- Two shots delivered together, then a gap: average rate is still 10/s.
			for _ = 1, 10 do
				a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim())
				a.advance(0.09)
				a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim())
				a.advance(0.11)
			end
			expectClean(a)
		end)

		t.it("a shooter with high ping hitting a moving target", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			a.fake.Physics:Set(a.shooterCharacter, { Ping = 0.4 })
			for i = 1, 20 do
				-- The client saw the target ~0.4 s ago: claimed hit lags the server position.
				local claimedX = 50 + (i - 1) * 3
				a.moveVictim(50 + i * 3 + 6)
				a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim({ HitPosition = Fakes.vector3(claimedX, 3, 0) }))
				a.advance(0.15)
			end
			expectClean(a)
		end)

		t.it("a shooter moving while firing (origin follows the character)", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			a.fake.Physics:Set(a.shooterCharacter, { Ping = 0.15 })
			for i = 1, 20 do
				local x = i * 16 * 0.15
				a.fake.Physics:Set(a.shooterCharacter, { X = x })
				-- Client origin is where it was ~ping ago.
				a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim({ Origin = Fakes.vector3(x - 2.4, 4.5, 0) }))
				a.advance(0.15)
			end
			expectClean(a)
		end)

		t.it("kill race: shooting a target that just died is refused without blame", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			a.fake.Physics:SetTarget(a.torso, { X = 50, Y = 3, Z = 0, Model = a.victimCharacter, Player = a.victim, Alive = false })
			for _ = 1, 20 do
				local result = a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim())
				expect(result.Allowed).toBe(false)
				expect(result.Reason).toBe("TargetDead")
				expect(result.ShotAccepted).toBe(true)
				a.advance(0.1)
			end
			expectClean(a)
		end)

		t.it("missed shots (no target) are fine and still consume ammo", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			for _ = 1, 5 do
				local result = a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim({ Target = Fakes.NONE, HitPosition = Fakes.NONE }))
				expect(result.Allowed).toBe(true)
				expect(result.Damage).toBe(0)
				a.advance(0.1)
			end
			expect((a.combat:GetAmmo(a.shooter, "Rifle"))).toBe(25)
			expectClean(a)
		end)

		t.it("melee within reach", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Sword = Harness.SWORD }, VictimDistance = 5 })
			for _ = 1, 10 do
				local result = a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Sword", { Target = a.torso })
				expect(result.Allowed).toBe(true)
				expect(result.Damage).toBe(30)
				a.advance(0.5)
			end
			expectClean(a)
		end)

		t.it("projectile: fire, travel time, impact", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rocket = Harness.ROCKET }, VictimDistance = 100 })
			for shot = 1, 5 do
				local id = "rocket-" .. shot
				local fired = a.combat:FireProjectile(a.shooter, "Rocket", {
					Origin = Fakes.vector3(0, 3, 0),
					Direction = Fakes.vector3(1, 0, 0),
					ShotId = id,
				})
				expect(fired.Allowed).toBe(true)
				a.advance(1.0) -- 100 studs at 100 studs/s
				local hit = a.combat:ValidateProjectileHit(a.shooter, id, { Target = a.torso, HitPosition = Fakes.vector3(100, 3, 0) })
				expect(hit.Allowed).toBe(true)
				expect(hit.Damage).toBe(80)
				a.advance(0.1)
			end
			expectClean(a)
		end)

		t.it("lobbed projectile with gravity", function()
			local weapons = {
				Grenade = {
					Kind = "Projectile",
					Damage = 60,
					FireRate = 1,
					MaxRange = 200,
					ProjectileSpeed = 50,
					ProjectileGravity = 50,
				},
			}
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = weapons, VictimDistance = 50 })
			-- 45 degree throw from (0,3,0): direction (0.7071, 0.7071, 0).
			a.combat:FireProjectile(a.shooter, "Grenade", {
				Origin = Fakes.vector3(0, 3, 0),
				Direction = Fakes.vector3(1, 1, 0),
				ShotId = "g1",
			})
			-- After t seconds: x = 35.36 t, y = 3 + 35.36 t - 25 t^2. At x = 50, t = 1.414.
			local tFlight = 50 / (50 * math.sqrt(0.5))
			a.advance(tFlight)
			local y = 3 + 50 * math.sqrt(0.5) * tFlight - 25 * tFlight * tFlight
			a.fake.Physics:SetTarget(a.torso, { X = 50, Y = y, Z = 0, Model = a.victimCharacter, Player = a.victim })
			local hit = a.combat:ValidateProjectileHit(a.shooter, "g1", { Target = a.torso, HitPosition = Fakes.vector3(50, y, 0) })
			expect(hit.Allowed).toBe(true)
			expectClean(a)
		end)

		t.it("piercing projectile hits two targets", function()
			local weapons = {
				Spear = { Kind = "Projectile", Damage = 30, FireRate = 1, MaxRange = 200, ProjectileSpeed = 200, Pierce = 1 },
			}
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = weapons, VictimDistance = 40 })
			local other = Fakes.instance("Part", "Torso", Fakes.instance("Model", "Npc"))
			a.fake.Physics:SetTarget(other, { X = 60, Y = 3, Z = 0, Model = other.Parent })
			a.combat:FireProjectile(a.shooter, "Spear", { Origin = Fakes.vector3(0, 3, 0), Direction = Fakes.vector3(1, 0, 0), ShotId = "s1" })
			a.advance(0.2)
			expect(a.combat:ValidateProjectileHit(a.shooter, "s1", { Target = a.torso }).Allowed).toBe(true)
			a.advance(0.1)
			expect(a.combat:ValidateProjectileHit(a.shooter, "s1", { Target = other }).Allowed).toBe(true)
			expectClean(a)
		end)

		t.it("ability used exactly on cooldown, including an AoE without a target", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Fireball = Harness.FIREBALL } })
			for _ = 1, 4 do
				local result = a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Fireball", { HitPosition = Fakes.vector3(40, 3, 0) })
				expect(result.Allowed).toBe(true)
				a.advance(4.95) -- within the cooldown tolerance
			end
			expectClean(a)
		end)

		t.it("team rules: friendly fire is refused without heavy blame", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			a.combat:SetProvider("CanDamage", function(_, target)
				return target.Player ~= a.victim
			end)
			for _ = 1, 4 do
				local result = a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim())
				expect(result.Reason).toBe("FriendlyFire")
				a.advance(0.1)
			end
			expectClean(a)
		end)

		t.it("custom damage via DamageCalculator", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			a.combat:SetProvider("DamageCalculator", function(info)
				return info.BaseDamage * 0.5
			end)
			expect(a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim()).Damage).toBe(12.5)
		end)

		t.it("game-managed ammo via SetAmmo", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			a.combat:SetAmmo(a.shooter, "Rifle", 1, 0)
			expect(a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim()).Allowed).toBe(true)
			a.advance(0.1)
			local empty = a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim())
			expect(empty.Reason).toBe("NoAmmo")
			expect((a.combat:Reload(a.shooter, "Rifle"))).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("shooting world parts and NPCs", function()
			local a = Harness.arena({ Weapons = { Rifle = Harness.RIFLE } })
			local crate = Fakes.instance("Part", "Crate")
			a.fake.Physics:SetTarget(crate, { X = 30, Y = 1, Z = 0 })
			local result = a.combat:ValidateAttack(a.shooter, "Rifle", a.claim({ Target = crate, HitPosition = Fakes.vector3(30, 1, 0) }))
			expect(result.Allowed).toBe(true)
			expect(result.Target.Model).toBeNil()
			expectClean(a)
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/Integration/Enforcement.spec.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Graduated enforcement in the full framework: restriction of tagged remotes and combat,
	movement correction, temporary restriction, automatic and approved kicks and bans,
	escalation, shadow mode, overrides, persistence and the audit log.

	Probe modules drive risk with chosen categories; real RemoteSecurity, Combat and
	Movement consult enforcement.
]]

local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Framework = require(ACF.Core.Framework)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local PROBES = {
	MoveProbe = "Movement",
	CombatProbe = "Combat",
	EcoProbe = "Economy",
	AimStat = "Behavioral",
}

local function world(config, envOptions)
	local fake = Fakes.environment(envOptions)
	fake.Physics:AddFloor()
	local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
	local probes = {}
	for name, category in PROBES do
		local probe = Fakes.module(name, { Category = category })
		framework:RegisterModule(probe)
		probes[name] = probe
	end
	local merged = config or {}
	merged.Risk = merged.Risk or {}
	merged.Risk.Groups = merged.Risk.Groups or { AimStat = "Aim" }
	framework:Initialize(merged)
	framework:GetModule("Combat"):Define("Rifle", { Kind = "Hitscan", Damage = 25, FireRate = 10, MaxRange = 300 })
	framework:Start()
	local w = { framework = framework, fake = fake, probes = probes, enforcer = fake.Enforcer }
	function w.join(id)
		local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(id or 1, "P" .. tostring(id or 1)))
		local character = player:Spawn()
		fake.Physics:Set(character, {})
		Fakes.instance("Tool", "Rifle", character)
		return player, character
	end
	function w.report(player, probe, severity, kind)
		probes[probe].Context:Report(player, { Kind = kind or (probe .. "Kind"), Severity = severity, Confidence = 1 })
	end
	function w.advance(seconds, step)
		local dt = step or 1
		local steps = math.max(1, math.ceil(seconds / dt))
		for _ = 1, steps do
			fake.Clock.Advance(seconds / steps)
			framework:Step()
		end
	end
	function w.audit(kind, userId)
		return framework:GetAuditLog({ Type = kind, UserId = userId })
	end
	function w.remote(name, restrict)
		local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent(name)
		local calls = { n = 0 }
		framework:GetModule("RemoteSecurity"):ProtectEvent(remote, {
			Arguments = {},
			Restrict = restrict,
			Handler = function()
				calls.n += 1
			end,
		})
		return remote, calls
	end
	function w.shoot(player)
		return framework:GetModule("Combat"):ValidateAttack(player, "Rifle", { Origin = Fakes.vector3(0, 4.5, 0) })
	end
	return w
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("Enforcement", function()
		t.it("normal players are at level 0 and nothing is restricted", function()
			local w = world()
			local p = w.join()
			local trade, calls = w.remote("Trade", "Economy")
			Fakes.fire(trade, p)
			expect(calls.n).toBe(1)
			expect(w.framework:GetEnforcement(p).Level).toBe(0)
			expect(w.shoot(p).Allowed).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("level 2 rejects only operations tagged with the player's risk groups", function()
			local w = world()
			local p = w.join()
			local trade, tradeCalls = w.remote("Trade", "Economy")
			local emote, emoteCalls = w.remote("Emote", nil)
			local sprint, sprintCalls = w.remote("Sprint", "Movement")
			w.report(p, "EcoProbe", 45) -- Economy 45: Restrict band, tag Economy
			local state = w.framework:GetEnforcement(p)
			expect(state.Level).toBe(2)
			expect(state.Tags).toEqual({ "Economy" })
			for _ = 1, 5 do
				Fakes.fire(trade, p)
			end
			Fakes.fire(emote, p)
			Fakes.fire(sprint, p)
			expect(tradeCalls.n).toBe(0)
			expect(emoteCalls.n).toBe(1)
			expect(sprintCalls.n).toBe(1)
			expect(w.framework:GetModule("RemoteSecurity"):GetStats().Trade.Rejected.restricted).toBe(5)
			-- An enforcement rejection is not player behaviour: no Remote evidence.
			for _, record in w.framework:GetEvidence(p) do
				expect(record.Category ~= "Remote").toBe(true)
			end
			-- Five rejections, one audit entry (summarised).
			expect(#w.audit("Rejected", 1)).toBe(1)
			expect(w.framework:IsRestricted(p, "Economy")).toBe(true)
			expect(w.shoot(p).Allowed).toBe(true) -- combat is not tagged
		end)

		t.it("Aim or Combat restriction refuses shots without scoring them", function()
			local w = world()
			local p = w.join()
			w.report(p, "AimStat", 45)
			local result = w.shoot(p)
			expect(result.Allowed).toBe(false)
			expect(result.Reason).toBe("Restricted")
			expect(result.ShotAccepted).toBe(false)
			expect(w.framework:GetModule("Combat"):GetStats().Rejected.Restricted).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("level 3 corrects a Movement-tagged player back to the last grounded position", function()
			local w = world()
			local p, character = w.join()
			for i = 1, 10 do
				w.fake.Physics:Set(character, { X = i * 2 })
				w.advance(0.2, 0.2)
			end
			w.report(p, "MoveProbe", 65) -- Investigation, tag Movement
			expect(w.framework:GetEnforcement(p).Level).toBe(3)
			local before = #w.fake.Physics.Corrections
			w.fake.Physics:Set(character, { X = 150 }) -- teleport
			w.advance(0.2, 0.2)
			local corrections = w.fake.Physics.Corrections
			expect(#corrections).toBe(before + 1)
			expect(corrections[#corrections].X).toBe(20)
			expect(#w.audit("Corrected", 1)).toBe(1)
			-- Cooldown: a second teleport inside 1 s is not corrected again.
			w.fake.Physics:Set(character, { X = 160 })
			w.advance(0.2, 0.2)
			expect(#w.fake.Physics.Corrections).toBe(before + 1)
		end)

		t.it("no correction without a Movement tag, or beyond MaxDistance", function()
			local w = world()
			local p, character = w.join()
			for i = 1, 5 do
				w.fake.Physics:Set(character, { X = i })
				w.advance(0.2, 0.2)
			end
			w.report(p, "CombatProbe", 65) -- Investigation, tag Combat only
			w.fake.Physics:Set(character, { X = 150 })
			w.advance(0.2, 0.2)
			expect(#w.fake.Physics.Corrections).toBe(0)

			local w2 = world()
			local p2, c2 = w2.join()
			w2.advance(0.4, 0.2)
			w2.report(p2, "MoveProbe", 65)
			w2.fake.Physics:Set(c2, { X = 900 })
			w2.advance(0.2, 0.2)
			expect(#w2.fake.Physics.Corrections).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("level 4 restricts every tag for TemporaryRestrictionSeconds", function()
			local w = world()
			local p = w.join()
			local emote, calls = w.remote("Emote", true)
			w.report(p, "AimStat", 85) -- EnforcementReview, statistical only: no kick
			local state = w.framework:GetEnforcement(p)
			expect(state.Level).toBe(4)
			expect(state.RestrictedFor).toBe(600)
			expect(w.framework:IsRestricted(p, "Movement")).toBe(true)
			Fakes.fire(emote, p)
			expect(calls.n).toBe(0)
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("statistical evidence alone opens a case; staff approval kicks", function()
			local w = world()
			local p = w.join()
			w.report(p, "AimStat", 50)
			w.report(p, "EcoProbe", 40)
			local cases = w.framework:GetCases({ Status = "Open" })
			expect(#cases).toBe(1)
			expect(cases[1].ProposedLevel).toBe(5)
			expect(cases[1].Type).toBe("Action")
			expect(string.find(cases[1].Explanation, "Aim", 1, true) ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(0)
			expect(function()
				w.framework:ApproveCase(cases[1].CaseId, {})
			end).toThrow("options.By")
			w.framework:ApproveCase(cases[1].CaseId, { By = "Mod_Alice", Note = "reviewed footage" })
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(1)
			expect(string.find(w.enforcer.Kicks[1].Message, "ACF-", 1, true) ~= nil).toBe(true)
			local kicked = w.audit("Kicked", 1)[1]
			expect(kicked.By).toBe("Mod_Alice")
			expect(kicked.ApprovedBy).toBe("Mod_Alice")
			expect(w.framework:GetCases({ Status = "Approved" })[1].DecidedBy).toBe("Mod_Alice")
		end)

		t.it("rejecting a case takes no action and is audited", function()
			local w = world()
			local p = w.join()
			w.report(p, "AimStat", 85)
			local case = w.framework:GetCases({ Status = "Open" })[1]
			w.framework:RejectCase(case.CaseId, { By = "Mod_Bob", Note = "pro player, verified" })
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(0)
			expect(w.audit("CaseRejected", 1)[1].By).toBe("Mod_Bob")
			expect(function()
				w.framework:ApproveCase(case.CaseId, { By = "Mod_Bob" })
			end).toThrow("is Rejected")
		end)

		t.it("the Investigation band opens a review case; staff can choose the action", function()
			local w = world()
			local p = w.join()
			w.report(p, "CombatProbe", 65)
			local case = w.framework:GetCases({ Status = "Open" })[1]
			expect(case.Type).toBe("Review")
			expect(case.ProposedLevel).toBeNil()
			expect(function()
				w.framework:ApproveCase(case.CaseId, { By = "Mod" })
			end).toThrow("needs a removal Level")
			w.framework:ApproveCase(case.CaseId, { By = "Mod", Level = 6, Hours = 2 })
			expect(w.enforcer.Bans[1].Seconds).toBe(7200)
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("corroborated evidence kicks automatically, then waits for new evidence after rejoin", function()
			local w = world()
			local p = w.join()
			w.report(p, "MoveProbe", 50)
			w.report(p, "CombatProbe", 35)
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(1)
			local kicked = w.audit("Kicked", 1)[1]
			expect(kicked.By).toBe("ACF (automatic)")
			expect(w.framework:GetEnforcement(1).History[1].Type).toBe("Kick")

			w.join(1) -- rejoins with the same (decaying) risk
			w.advance(5)
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(1)
			expect(w.framework:GetEnforcement(1).Level).toBe(4) -- still restricted

			w.report(1, "MoveProbe", 50, "AnotherKind") -- cheats again
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(2)
			w.join(1)
			w.advance(1)
			w.report(1, "CombatProbe", 50, "AnotherKind")
			-- Two recent kicks: the next step is a temporary ban, which needs approval (AutoBan off).
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(2)
			local case = w.framework:GetCases({ Status = "Open" })[1]
			expect(case.ProposedLevel).toBe(6)
		end)

		t.it("AutoBan escalates to an automatic temporary ban; permanent still needs approval", function()
			local w = world({ Enforcement = { AutoBan = true, Bans = { KicksBeforeTempBan = 1, TempBansBeforePermanentProposal = 1 } } })
			local p = w.join()
			w.report(p, "MoveProbe", 50)
			w.report(p, "CombatProbe", 35)
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(1)
			w.join(1)
			w.advance(1)
			w.report(1, "MoveProbe", 50, "K2")
			expect(#w.enforcer.Bans).toBe(1)
			expect(w.enforcer.Bans[1].Seconds).toBe(24 * 3600)
			w.advance(25 * 3600, 3600) -- ban over
			w.join(1)
			w.report(1, "MoveProbe", 60, "K3")
			w.report(1, "CombatProbe", 50, "K3")
			expect(#w.enforcer.Bans).toBe(1)
			local case = w.framework:GetCases({ Status = "Open" })[1]
			expect(case.ProposedLevel).toBe(7)
		end)

		t.it("shadow mode decides and audits everything but applies nothing", function()
			local w = world({ Enforcement = { ShadowMode = true } })
			local p = w.join()
			local trade, calls = w.remote("Trade", "Economy")
			w.report(p, "EcoProbe", 45)
			Fakes.fire(trade, p)
			expect(calls.n).toBe(1)
			expect(w.audit("Rejected", 1)[1].Shadow).toBe(true)
			w.report(p, "MoveProbe", 50)
			w.report(p, "CombatProbe", 35)
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(0)
			expect(w.audit("ShadowAction", 1)[1].Level).toBe("Kick")
			expect(w.shoot(p).Allowed).toBe(true)
			-- Staff actions still work in shadow mode.
			w.framework:Kick(p, { By = "Mod", Reason = "manual" })
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("shadow cases cannot be approved", function()
			local w = world({ Enforcement = { ShadowMode = true } })
			local p = w.join()
			w.report(p, "AimStat", 85)
			local case = w.framework:GetCases({ Status = "Shadow" })[1]
			expect(case ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(function()
				w.framework:ApproveCase(case.CaseId, { By = "Mod" })
			end).toThrow("is Shadow")
		end)

		t.it("Mode Off only logs; Manual never removes automatically", function()
			local off = world({ Enforcement = { Mode = "Off" } })
			local p = off.join()
			off.report(p, "MoveProbe", 50)
			off.report(p, "CombatProbe", 50)
			expect(off.framework:GetEnforcement(p).Level).toBe(0)
			expect(#off.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(0)

			local manual = world({ Enforcement = { Mode = "Manual" } })
			local q = manual.join()
			manual.report(q, "MoveProbe", 50)
			manual.report(q, "CombatProbe", 50)
			expect(#manual.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(0)
			expect(manual.framework:GetEnforcement(q).Level).toBe(4)
			expect(manual.framework:GetCases({ Status = "Open" })[1].ProposedLevel).toBe(5)
		end)

		t.it("temporary bans are enforced on join and expire", function()
			local w = world()
			local p = w.join()
			local appeal = w.framework:Ban(p, { By = "Admin", Reason = "exploit", Hours = 2 })
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(1)
			expect(w.enforcer.Bans[1].Seconds).toBe(7200)
			expect(string.find(w.enforcer.Kicks[1].Message, appeal, 1, true) ~= nil).toBe(true)
			w.join(1)
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(2)
			expect(w.audit("BanEnforced", 1)[1].AppealRef).toBe(appeal)
			w.advance(7300, 100)
			w.join(1)
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(2)
			expect(#w.audit("BanExpired", 1)).toBe(1)
			local history = w.framework:GetEnforcement(1).History
			expect(history[#history].Type).toBe("Expired")
		end)

		t.it("bans persist across servers through the Store, and unbans lift them", function()
			local store = Fakes.store()
			local first = world(nil, { Store = store })
			first.framework:Ban(42, { By = "Admin", Reason = "offline ban" }) -- player not in this server
			expect(#first.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(0)
			expect(store.Data.ACF_Bans_v1.Bans_42.Active.Level).toBe(7)

			local second = world(nil, { Store = store })
			second.join(42)
			expect(#second.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(1)
			second.framework:Unban(42, { By = "Admin", Reason = "appeal upheld" })
			expect(second.enforcer.Unbans[1]).toBe(42)
			second.join(42)
			expect(#second.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(1)
			local record = store.Data.ACF_Bans_v1.Bans_42
			expect(record.Active).toBeNil()
			expect(record.History[#record.History].Type).toBe("Unban")
		end)

		t.it("a ban takes effect immediately even when the record store is failing", function()
			local store = Fakes.store()
			store.FailLoads = 100
			local w = world(nil, { Store = store })
			local p = w.join()
			w.framework:Ban(p, { By = "Admin", Reason = "x", Hours = 1 })
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(1)
			expect(#w.enforcer.Bans).toBe(1)
			for _ = 1, 6 do
				w.advance(5)
			end
			expect(#w.audit("PersistenceFailed", 1) >= 1).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("a failed Roblox ban API call is logged; ACF's record still enforces the ban", function()
			local w = world()
			w.enforcer.FailBans = 1
			local p = w.join()
			w.framework:Ban(p, { By = "Admin", Hours = 5 })
			expect(#Fakes.findLogs(w.fake.Logs, "Roblox ban API call failed", "Error")).toBe(1)
			w.join(1)
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(2)
		end)

		t.it("overrides: exempt players are never restricted or removed; level overrides expire", function()
			local w = world()
			local p = w.join()
			w.framework:SetEnforcementOverride(p, { Exempt = true, By = "Admin", Reason = "developer testing" })
			w.report(p, "MoveProbe", 50)
			w.report(p, "CombatProbe", 50)
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(0)
			expect(w.framework:IsRestricted(p, "Movement")).toBe(false)
			expect(w.framework:GetEnforcement(p).Level).toBe(0)
			w.framework:ClearEnforcementOverride(p, { By = "Admin" })

			local q = w.join(2)
			w.framework:SetEnforcementOverride(q, { Level = 2, Seconds = 60, By = "Mod", Reason = "watch" })
			expect(w.framework:GetEnforcement(q).Level).toBe(2)
			expect(function()
				w.framework:SetEnforcementOverride(q, { Level = 5, By = "Mod" })
			end).toThrow("use Kick/Ban")
			w.advance(61)
			expect(w.framework:GetEnforcement(q).Override).toBeNil()
			expect(#w.audit("OverrideCleared", 2)).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("every staff action needs By; the audit log is bounded and published", function()
			local w = world({ Enforcement = { Audit = { MaxEntries = 10 } } })
			local p = w.join()
			expect(function()
				w.framework:Kick(p, {})
			end).toThrow("options.By")
			expect(function()
				w.framework:Ban(p, { By = "x", Hours = -1 })
			end).toThrow("positive number")
			local published = 0
			w.framework:Subscribe("ACF.EnforcementAudit", function()
				published += 1
			end)
			for i = 1, 15 do
				w.framework:SetEnforcementOverride(p, { Level = i % 3, By = "Mod" })
			end
			expect(#w.framework:GetAuditLog() <= 10).toBe(true)
			expect(published >= 15).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("a long restriction is sent for review (mitigated cheaters reach staff)", function()
			local w = world()
			local p = w.join()
			-- Severity 20 each minute settles around 53: inside Restrict, never higher.
			for minute = 1, 15 do
				w.report(p, "EcoProbe", 20)
				w.advance(60)
				if minute == 5 then
					expect(w.framework:GetEnforcement(p).Level).toBe(2)
					expect(#w.framework:GetCases()).toBe(0)
				end
			end
			local cases = w.framework:GetCases({ Status = "Open" })
			expect(#cases).toBe(1)
			expect(cases[1].Type).toBe("Review")
			expect(string.find(cases[1].Reason, "restricted for", 1, true) ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("departed players are forgotten after ForgetAfterSeconds; bans still apply on return", function()
			local store = Fakes.store()
			local w = world(nil, { Store = store })
			local p = w.join(5)
			w.framework:Ban(p, { By = "Admin", Hours = 48 })
			for id = 10, 30 do
				local q = w.join(id)
				w.fake.Players:Leave(q)
			end
			w.advance(3700, 100)
			local stats = w.framework:GetStatus().Enforcement
			expect(stats.Users).toBe(0)
			expect(stats.Records).toBe(0)
			w.join(5)
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(2) -- the ban, reloaded from the Store
		end)

		t.it("a forgotten player's decaying risk does not re-create enforcement state", function()
			-- Forgotten after 60 s, while risk (90) is still decaying through the bands.
			local w = world({ Enforcement = { Mode = "Manual", ForgetAfterSeconds = 60 } })
			local p = w.join(9)
			w.report(p, "MoveProbe", 90) -- EnforcementReview; Manual, so no kick
			w.fake.Players:Leave(p)
			local case = w.framework:GetCases({ Status = "Open" })[1]
			w.framework:RejectCase(case.CaseId, { By = "Mod" })
			w.advance(120, 5)
			expect(w.framework:GetStatus().Enforcement.Users).toBe(0)
			expect(w.framework:GetRisk(9).Band ~= "Normal").toBe(true) -- still decaying
			w.advance(3600, 5) -- through Investigation, Restrict, Increased, Normal
			expect(w.framework:GetStatus().Enforcement.Users).toBe(0)
			expect(#w.framework:GetCases({ Status = "Open" })).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("a case can still be approved after its player has left and been forgotten", function()
			local w = world({ Enforcement = { Mode = "Manual" } })
			local p = w.join(4)
			w.report(p, "MoveProbe", 90)
			local case = w.framework:GetCases({ Status = "Open" })[1]
			w.fake.Players:Leave(p)
			w.advance(3700, 100)
			expect(w.framework:GetStatus().Enforcement.Users).toBe(0)
			w.framework:ApproveCase(case.CaseId, { By = "Mod", Level = 6, Hours = 24 })
			w.join(4)
			expect(#w.enforcer.Kicks).toBe(1) -- the approved ban applies on return
		end)

		t.it("without a Store, ban records are never forgotten", function()
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			fake.Env.Store = nil
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			framework:Initialize({})
			framework:Start()
			-- Banned while in the server: kicked, so they leave (LeftAt set) and are later forgotten.
			fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(77))
			framework:Ban(77, { By = "Admin" })
			expect(#fake.Enforcer.Kicks).toBe(1)
			for _ = 1, 80 do
				fake.Clock.Advance(60)
				framework:Step()
			end
			expect(framework:GetStatus().Enforcement.Users).toBe(0)
			fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(77))
			expect(#fake.Enforcer.Kicks).toBe(2)
		end)

		t.it("pending enforcement writes are flushed at shutdown", function()
			local store = Fakes.store()
			local w = world(nil, { Store = store })
			store.Deferred = true
			w.framework:Ban(7, { By = "Admin" })
			expect(w.fake.Shutdown.Pending() >= 1).toBe(true)
			for _ = 1, 3 do
				store.Complete()
				w.fake.Shutdown.Flush()
			end
			store.Complete()
			expect(w.fake.Shutdown.Pending()).toBe(0)
			expect(store.Data.ACF_Bans_v1.Bans_7.Active.Level).toBe(7)
		end)
	end)
end
```

`tests/Specs/Integration/ExampleDetector.spec.luau`:

```luau
local ServerStorage = game:GetService("ServerStorage")
local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Framework = require(ACF.Core.Framework)
local Configuration = require(ACF.Core.Configuration)
local Detector = require(ServerStorage.ACFExamples.LeaderstatRateDetector)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local function withCoins(player, initial)
	local coins = { Value = initial }
	local stats = {
		FindFirstChild = function(_, name)
			return if name == "Coins" then coins else nil
		end,
	}
	player:SetChild("leaderstats", stats)
	return coins
end

local function boot(settings)
	local fake = Fakes.environment()
	local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
	framework:Initialize({ ModuleSettings = { LeaderstatRate = settings or {} } })
	framework:RegisterModule(Detector.new(Configuration.Schema))
	framework:Start()
	return framework, fake
end

local function tick(framework, fake, seconds)
	fake.Clock.Advance(seconds)
	framework:Step()
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("Example: LeaderstatRateDetector", function()
		t.it("does not flag legitimate income", function()
			local framework, fake = boot({ MaxGainPerSecond = 10, SampleIntervalSeconds = 1 })
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			local coins = withCoins(player, 0)
			for _ = 1, 10 do
				tick(framework, fake, 1)
				coins.Value += 8
			end
			tick(framework, fake, 1)
			expect(#framework:GetEvidence(player)).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("flags a gain rate above the limit with an explanation", function()
			local framework, fake = boot({ MaxGainPerSecond = 10, SampleIntervalSeconds = 1 })
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			local coins = withCoins(player, 0)
			tick(framework, fake, 1)
			coins.Value += 1000
			tick(framework, fake, 1)
			local records = framework:GetEvidence(player)
			expect(#records).toBe(1)
			expect(records[1].Kind).toBe("StatGainRateExceeded")
			expect(records[1].Category).toBe("Economy")
			expect(records[1].Details.Rate).toBe(1000)
			expect(records[1].Confidence).toBe(0.9)
		end)

		t.it("ignores spending", function()
			local framework, fake = boot({ MaxGainPerSecond = 10, SampleIntervalSeconds = 1 })
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			local coins = withCoins(player, 5000)
			tick(framework, fake, 1)
			coins.Value = 0
			tick(framework, fake, 1)
			expect(#framework:GetEvidence(player)).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("ignores players without the stat", function()
			local framework, fake = boot({ SampleIntervalSeconds = 1 })
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			tick(framework, fake, 1)
			tick(framework, fake, 1)
			expect(#framework:GetEvidence(player)).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("rejects invalid detector settings at registration", function()
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			framework:Initialize({ ModuleSettings = { LeaderstatRate = { SampleIntervalSeconds = 0 } } })
			expect(function()
				framework:RegisterModule(Detector.new(Configuration.Schema))
			end).toThrow("SampleIntervalSeconds")
		end)
	end)
end
```

`tests/Specs/Integration/Framework.spec.luau`:

```luau
local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Framework = require(ACF.Core.Framework)
local Topics = require(ACF.Core.Topics)
local Configuration = require(ACF.Core.Configuration)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local S = Configuration.Schema

local function boot(config, modules, options)
	local fake = Fakes.environment(options)
	local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
	framework:Initialize(config)
	for _, def in modules or {} do
		framework:RegisterModule(def)
	end
	return framework, fake
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("Framework lifecycle", function()
		t.it("moves through Created, Initialized, Running, Stopped, Destroyed", function()
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			local seen = {}
			expect(framework:GetState()).toBe("Created")
			framework:Initialize({})
			framework:Subscribe(Topics.FrameworkStateChanged, function(p)
				table.insert(seen, p.To)
			end)
			framework:Start()
			framework:Stop()
			framework:Start()
			framework:Destroy()
			expect(seen).toEqual({ "Running", "Stopped", "Running", "Stopped" })
			expect(framework:GetState()).toBe("Destroyed")
		end)

		t.it("rejects calls in the wrong state", function()
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			expect(function()
				framework:Start()
			end).toThrow("requires state Initialized")
			framework:Initialize({})
			expect(function()
				framework:Initialize({})
			end).toThrow("only be called once")
		end)

		t.it("raises a readable error on invalid configuration and allows a retry", function()
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			expect(function()
				framework:Initialize({ LogLevel = "Loud", Evidence = { MaxRecordsPerPlayer = -1 } })
			end).toThrow("ACF configuration is invalid")
			expect(framework:GetState()).toBe("Created")
			framework:Initialize({})
			expect(framework:GetState()).toBe("Initialized")
		end)

		t.it("validates the environment", function()
			expect(function()
				Framework.new({} :: any)
			end).toThrow("Environment.Players")
		end)

		t.it("queues modules registered before Initialize", function()
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			local def = Fakes.module("Early")
			framework:RegisterModule(def)
			framework:Initialize({})
			framework:Start()
			expect(def.Calls).toEqual({ "Initialize", "Start" })
		end)

		t.it("refuses module registration after the first Start", function()
			local framework = boot({})
			framework:Start()
			expect(function()
				framework:RegisterModule(Fakes.module("Late"))
			end).toThrow("before the first Start")
		end)

		t.it("honours the Modules enable map and EnabledByDefault", function()
			local on = Fakes.module("On")
			local off = Fakes.module("Off")
			local optIn = Fakes.module("OptIn", { EnabledByDefault = false })
			local framework = boot({ Modules = { Off = false } }, { on, off, optIn })
			local status = framework:Start()
			expect(status.Modules.On.State).toBe("Running")
			expect(status.Modules.Off.State).toBe("Disabled")
			expect(status.Modules.OptIn.State).toBe("Disabled")
		end)

		t.it("warns about configured modules that are not registered", function()
			-- Economy was announced for Phase 5 and shipped as Behavioral signals instead.
			local framework, fake = boot({ Modules = { Economy = true } })
			framework:Start()
			local warnings = Fakes.findLogs(fake.Logs, "Configured module is not registered", "Warn")
			expect(#warnings).toBe(1)
			expect(warnings[1].Fields.Module).toBe("Economy")
			expect(#Fakes.findLogs(fake.Logs, "not available in this ACF version", "Warn")).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("fails Start when a Critical module cannot start, and stops the others", function()
			local healthy = Fakes.module("Healthy")
			local critical = Fakes.module("Core1", {
				Critical = true,
				Start = function()
					error("hard dependency missing")
				end,
			})
			local framework = boot({}, { healthy, critical })
			expect(function()
				framework:Start()
			end).toThrow("critical modules failed to start")
			expect(framework:GetState()).toBe("Initialized")
			expect(framework:GetStatus().Modules.Healthy.State).toBe("Stopped")
		end)

		t.it("survives a non-critical module failing", function()
			local broken = Fakes.module("Broken", {
				Initialize = function()
					error("bug")
				end,
			})
			local framework = boot({}, { broken, Fakes.module("Fine") })
			local status = framework:Start()
			expect(status.State).toBe("Running")
			expect(status.Modules.Broken.State).toBe("Failed")
			expect(status.Modules.Fine.State).toBe("Running")
		end)

		t.it("validates module configuration at registration", function()
			local def = Fakes.module("Speed", { ConfigSchema = S.Table({ Max = S.Number(16, { Min = 0 }) }) })
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			framework:Initialize({ ModuleSettings = { Speed = { Max = -5 } } })
			expect(function()
				framework:RegisterModule(def)
			end).toThrow("ModuleSettings.Speed.Max")
		end)

		t.it("delivers validated module configuration through the context", function()
			local def = Fakes.module("Speed", { ConfigSchema = S.Table({ Max = S.Number(16) }) })
			local framework = boot({ ModuleSettings = { Speed = { Max = 20 } } }, { def })
			framework:Start()
			expect(def.Context.Config.Max).toBe(20)
			expect(table.isfrozen(def.Context.Config)).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("rejects settings for modules that never registered", function()
			local framework = boot({ ModuleSettings = { Sped = { Max = 1 } } })
			expect(function()
				framework:Start()
			end).toThrow("ModuleSettings.Sped")
		end)

		t.it("drives the scheduler from the heartbeat when one is provided", function()
			local runs = 0
			local def = Fakes.module("Ticker", {
				Start = function(self)
					self.Context.Scheduler:Every("tick", 1, function()
						runs += 1
					end)
				end,
			})
			local framework, fake = boot({}, { def }, { Heartbeat = true })
			framework:Start()
			fake.Clock.Advance(1)
			fake.Heartbeat:Fire()
			expect(runs).toBe(1)
			framework:Stop()
			expect(fake.Heartbeat:ConnectionCount()).toBe(0)
			fake.Clock.Advance(1)
			fake.Heartbeat:Fire()
			expect(runs).toBe(1)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Module context", function()
		t.it("auto-releases subscriptions and tasks made during Start when stopped", function()
			local events, ticks = 0, 0
			local def = Fakes.module("M", {
				Start = function(self)
					self.Context.Events:Subscribe(Topics.PlayerSessionStarted, function()
						events += 1
					end)
					self.Context.Scheduler:Every("tick", 1, function()
						ticks += 1
					end)
				end,
			})
			local framework, fake = boot({}, { def })
			framework:Start()
			framework:Stop()
			fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			fake.Clock.Advance(5)
			framework:Step()
			expect(events).toBe(0)
			expect(ticks).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("re-establishes Start resources after a restart", function()
			local events = 0
			local def = Fakes.module("M", {
				Start = function(self)
					self.Context.Events:Subscribe(Topics.PlayerSessionStarted, function()
						events += 1
					end)
				end,
			})
			local framework, fake = boot({}, { def })
			framework:Start()
			framework:Stop()
			framework:Start()
			fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			expect(events).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("namespaces module topics and forbids publishing core topics", function()
			local received = nil
			local def = Fakes.module("Speed", {
				Start = function(self)
					local topic = self.Context.Events:Declare("Sample")
					self.Context.Events:Subscribe(topic, function(p)
						received = p
					end)
					self.Context.Events:Publish(topic, 42)
				end,
			})
			local framework = boot({}, { def })
			framework:Start()
			expect(received).toBe(42)
			expect(function()
				def.Context.Events:Publish(Topics.DetectionReported, {})
			end).toThrow("may only publish its own topics")
		end)

		t.it("only exposes declared dependencies through GetModule", function()
			local base = Fakes.module("Base", { Answer = 42 })
			local user = Fakes.module("User", { Dependencies = { "Base" } })
			local framework = boot({}, { base, user, Fakes.module("Other") })
			framework:Start()
			expect(user.Context:GetModule("Base").Answer).toBe(42)
			expect(function()
				user.Context:GetModule("Other")
			end).toThrow("did not declare")
		end)

		t.it("gives each module a scoped logger", function()
			local def = Fakes.module("Speed", {
				Start = function(self)
					self.Context.Logger:Info("hello")
				end,
			})
			local framework, fake = boot({}, { def })
			framework:Start()
			local entry = Fakes.findLogs(fake.Logs, "hello")[1]
			expect(entry.Scope).toBe("Speed")
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Player sessions", function()
		t.it("creates sessions for joining and pre-existing players", function()
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			fake.Players:Preload(Fakes.player(1))
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			framework:Initialize({})
			framework:Start()
			fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(2))
			expect(#framework:GetSessions()).toBe(2)
		end)

		t.it("lets modules observe players who were present before Start", function()
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			fake.Players:Preload(Fakes.player(7))
			local seen = {}
			local def = Fakes.module("Watcher", {
				Start = function(self)
					self.Context.Events:Subscribe(Topics.PlayerSessionStarted, function(p)
						table.insert(seen, p.Session.UserId)
					end)
				end,
			})
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			framework:Initialize({})
			framework:RegisterModule(def)
			framework:Start()
			expect(seen).toEqual({ 7 })
		end)

		t.it("ends sessions on leave, runs session cleanup, and issues new ids on rejoin", function()
			local framework, fake = boot({})
			framework:Start()
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			local first = framework:GetSession(player)
			local cleaned = false
			first.Cleanup:Add(function()
				cleaned = true
			end)
			first:GetState("x", function()
				return { 1 }
			end)
			fake.Players:Leave(player)
			expect(framework:GetSession(player)).toBeNil()
			expect(first.Active).toBe(false)
			expect(cleaned).toBe(true)
			expect(first:GetState("x")).toBeNil()

			local again = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			local second = framework:GetSession(again)
			expect(second.SessionId ~= first.SessionId).toBe(true)
			expect(framework:GetSession(first)).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("tracks character spawns and generations", function()
			local framework, fake = boot({})
			local spawns = {}
			framework:Subscribe(Topics.CharacterSpawned, function(p)
				table.insert(spawns, p.Generation)
			end)
			framework:Start()
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			player:Spawn()
			player:Spawn()
			local session = framework:GetSession(player)
			expect(spawns).toEqual({ 1, 2 })
			expect(session.CharacterGeneration).toBe(2)
			player:Despawn()
			expect(session.Character).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("treats an already-spawned character as a spawn when the session starts", function()
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			local player = Fakes.player(1)
			player:Spawn()
			fake.Players:Preload(player)
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			framework:Initialize({})
			framework:Start()
			expect(framework:GetSession(player).CharacterGeneration).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("ignores duplicate PlayerAdded for the same player", function()
			local framework, fake = boot({})
			framework:Start()
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			local session = framework:GetSession(player)
			fake.Players.PlayerAdded:Fire(player)
			expect(framework:GetSession(player)).toBe(session)
			expect(framework:GetStatus().Players.Started).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("disconnects player connections when stopped", function()
			local framework, fake = boot({})
			framework:Start()
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			framework:Stop()
			expect(fake.Players.PlayerAdded:ConnectionCount()).toBe(0)
			expect(player.CharacterAdded:ConnectionCount()).toBe(0)
			expect(#framework:GetSessions()).toBe(0)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Detection pipeline", function()
		local function detector(overrides)
			return Fakes.module("Speed", overrides or { Category = "Movement" })
		end

		t.it("records a detection as evidence and publishes it", function()
			local def = detector()
			local framework, fake = boot({}, { def })
			local published = {}
			framework:OnDetection(function(p)
				table.insert(published, p)
			end)
			framework:Start()
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			local ok, id = def.Context:Report(player, {
				Kind = "SpeedExceeded",
				Severity = 20,
				Confidence = 0.7,
				Details = { Speed = 80, Limit = 16 },
			})
			expect(ok).toBe(true)
			expect(#published).toBe(1)
			local event = published[1].Event
			expect(event.Id).toBe(id)
			expect(event.Detector).toBe("Speed")
			expect(event.Category).toBe("Movement")
			expect(event.UserId).toBe(1)
			expect(event.Details.Speed).toBe(80)
			expect(table.isfrozen(event)).toBe(true)
			local evidence = framework:GetEvidence(player)
			expect(#evidence).toBe(1)
			expect(evidence[1].Kind).toBe("SpeedExceeded")
		end)

		t.it("accepts a UserId or Session as the target", function()
			local def = detector()
			local framework, fake = boot({}, { def })
			framework:Start()
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(5))
			expect((def.Context:Report(5, { Kind = "A", Severity = 1, Confidence = 1 }))).toBe(true)
			expect((def.Context:Report(framework:GetSession(player), { Kind = "B", Severity = 1, Confidence = 1 }))).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("rejects reports for unknown players", function()
			local def = detector()
			local framework = boot({}, { def })
			framework:Start()
			local ok, reason = def.Context:Report(Fakes.player(99), { Kind = "A", Severity = 1, Confidence = 1 })
			expect(ok).toBe(false)
			expect(reason).toBe("no_session")
		end)

		t.it("rejects reports from a module that is not running", function()
			local def = detector()
			local framework, fake = boot({}, { def })
			framework:Start()
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			framework:Stop()
			fake.Players:Join(player)
			local ok, reason = def.Context:Report(player, { Kind = "A", Severity = 1, Confidence = 1 })
			expect(ok).toBe(false)
			expect(reason).toBe("module_not_running")
		end)

		t.it("validates report input", function()
			local def = detector()
			local framework, fake = boot({}, { def })
			framework:Start()
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			local cases = {
				{ Kind = "", Severity = 1, Confidence = 1 },
				{ Kind = "A", Severity = 101, Confidence = 1 },
				{ Kind = "A", Severity = 1, Confidence = 1.5 },
				{ Kind = "A", Severity = 0 / 0, Confidence = 1 },
				{ Kind = "A", Severity = 1, Confidence = 1, Details = "text" },
				{ Kind = string.rep("x", 65), Severity = 1, Confidence = 1 },
			}
			for _, input in cases do
				local ok, reason = def.Context:Report(player, input)
				expect(ok).toBe(false)
				expect(reason).toContain("invalid_input")
			end
			expect(#framework:GetEvidence(player)).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("keeps evidence after the player leaves", function()
			local def = detector()
			local framework, fake = boot({}, { def })
			framework:Start()
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			def.Context:Report(player, { Kind = "A", Severity = 5, Confidence = 1 })
			fake.Players:Leave(player)
			expect(#framework:GetEvidence(1)).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("prunes expired evidence on the scheduler", function()
			local def = detector()
			local framework, fake = boot({ Evidence = { RetentionSeconds = 10, PruneIntervalSeconds = 5 } }, { def })
			framework:Start()
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			def.Context:Report(player, { Kind = "A", Severity = 5, Confidence = 1 })
			fake.Clock.Advance(15)
			framework:Step()
			expect(#framework:GetEvidence(1)).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("isolates a failing detection subscriber from the reporting module", function()
			local def = detector()
			local framework, fake = boot({}, { def })
			framework:OnDetection(function()
				error("subscriber bug")
			end)
			framework:Start()
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			local ok = def.Context:Report(player, { Kind = "A", Severity = 5, Confidence = 1 })
			expect(ok).toBe(true)
			expect(#framework:GetEvidence(player)).toBe(1)
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/Integration/Latency.spec.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Phase 8 latency sweep: honest populations at increasing ping, with jittery delivery
	(stalls at >= 0.25 s), lag spikes, jumps, combat against moving targets, economy and
	remotes. Up to the compensation limit (Movement/Combat MaxPingCompensationSeconds 0.5)
	there must be no evidence at all; beyond it, no enforcement.
]]

local Simulation = require(script.Parent.Parent.Simulation)

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	local function honestRun(seed, ping)
		local sim = Simulation.new({ Seed = seed, Ping = ping, Config = { Enforcement = { ShadowMode = true } } })
		for _ = 1, 15 do
			sim.add("Honest")
		end
		sim.run(180)
		return sim, sim.honestReport()
	end

	local function describe(report)
		local kinds = {}
		for kind, count in report.Kinds do
			table.insert(kinds, `{kind} x{count}`)
		end
		return table.concat(kinds, ", ")
	end

	t.describe("Latency", function()
		for _, case in { { 0.02, 0.08 }, { 0.15, 0.25 }, { 0.3, 0.4 }, { 0.45, 0.5 } } do
			t.it(`no evidence for honest players at {case[1]}-{case[2]} s ping`, function()
				local _, report = honestRun(21, case)
				if report.WithEvidence > 0 then
					error({ __testFailure = true, message = "honest evidence: " .. describe(report) }, 0)
				end
			end)
		end

		t.it("beyond the compensation limit (0.6-0.9 s ping): no enforcement and no restriction", function()
			local sim, report = honestRun(22, { 0.6, 0.9 })
			expect(report.Restricted).toBe(0)
			expect(report.Kicked).toBe(0)
			for _, agent in sim.agents do
				expect(#sim.framework:GetAuditLog({ UserId = agent.id, Type = "ShadowAction" })).toBe(0)
			end
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/Integration/MovementLegit.spec.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	False-positive guard: legitimate play that must NEVER produce movement or physics
	evidence. Every scenario runs through the full framework with a fake world (floor at
	y = 0, standing root height 3) and 0.2 s server samples.
]]

local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Framework = require(ACF.Core.Framework)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local DT = 0.2

local function setup(config)
	local fake = Fakes.environment()
	fake.Physics:AddFloor()
	local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
	framework:Initialize(config or {})
	framework:Start()
	local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
	local character = player:Spawn()
	fake.Physics:Set(character, {})
	local world = { framework = framework, fake = fake, player = player, character = character, t = 0 }
	-- Advances one sample: applies fields, moves the clock, runs scheduled work.
	function world.step(fields)
		world.t += DT
		if fields then
			fake.Physics:Set(world.character, fields)
		end
		fake.Clock.Advance(DT)
		framework:Step()
	end
	function world.evidence()
		local movementOrPhysics = {}
		for _, record in framework:GetEvidence(player) do
			if record.Category == "Movement" or record.Category == "Physics" then
				table.insert(movementOrPhysics, record)
			end
		end
		return movementOrPhysics
	end
	return world
end

local function describeEvidence(records)
	local kinds = {}
	for _, r in records do
		table.insert(kinds, r.Kind)
	end
	return table.concat(kinds, ", ")
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	local function expectClean(world)
		local records = world.evidence()
		if #records > 0 then
			error({ __testFailure = true, message = "unexpected evidence: " .. describeEvidence(records) }, 0)
		end
		expect(world.framework:GetModule("Movement"):GetStats().Samples > 0).toBe(true)
	end

	t.describe("Legitimate movement produces no evidence", function()
		t.it("walking at WalkSpeed for 20 seconds, changing direction", function()
			local w = setup()
			local x, z = 0, 0
			for i = 1, 100 do
				local dirX = if (i // 10) % 2 == 0 then 1 else 0
				local dirZ = 1 - dirX
				x += 16 * DT * dirX
				z += 16 * DT * dirZ
				w.step({ X = x, Z = z, VX = 16 * dirX, VZ = 16 * dirZ })
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("sprinting after the server raises WalkSpeed", function()
			local w = setup()
			local x = 0
			for _ = 1, 60 do
				x += 32 * DT
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 32, WalkSpeed = 32 })
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("lag: a 1.5 s freeze followed by a catch-up burst", function()
			local w = setup()
			local x = 0
			for i = 1, 60 do
				x += 16 * DT
				local frozen = i >= 20 and i < 28
				if not frozen then
					w.step({ X = x, VX = 16 })
				else
					w.step(nil)
				end
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("lag: a 3 s freeze longer than the speed window", function()
			local w = setup()
			local x = 0
			for i = 1, 80 do
				x += 16 * DT
				if i >= 20 and i < 35 then
					w.step(nil)
				else
					w.step({ X = x, VX = 16 })
				end
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("high ping with jittery delivery", function()
			local w = setup()
			local x = 0
			for i = 1, 80 do
				x += 16 * DT
				-- Alternate stalls and double-steps as packets bunch up.
				if i % 3 == 0 then
					w.step({ Ping = 0.4 })
				else
					w.step({ X = x, VX = 16, Ping = 0.4 })
				end
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("lag: a 2.8 s freeze in mid-air, then the catch-up landing (found by the Phase 8 simulation)", function()
			local w = setup()
			local x = 0
			for _ = 1, 10 do
				x += 14 * DT
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 14 })
			end
			-- Jump: rising for two samples, then the client stops sending updates. The
			-- server keeps the last position and the last (non-zero) velocity.
			w.step({ X = x + 2.8, Y = 11, VX = 14, VY = 30, State = "Jumping" })
			w.step({ X = x + 5.6, Y = 13.5, VX = 14, VY = 11, State = "Jumping" })
			for _ = 1, 14 do
				w.step()
			end
			-- Updates resume: the character has long since landed further on.
			w.step({ X = x + 40, Y = 3, VX = 14, VY = 0, State = "Running" })
			for i = 1, 10 do
				w.step({ X = x + 40 + i * 2.8, VX = 14 })
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("repeated normal jumps while walking", function()
			local w = setup()
			local x, airT = 0, nil
			for i = 1, 100 do
				x += 16 * DT
				if i % 6 == 0 then
					airT = 0
				end
				local y = 3
				if airT then
					airT += DT
					y = 3 + 50 * airT - 98.1 * airT * airT
					if y <= 3 then
						y, airT = 3, nil
					end
				end
				w.step({ X = x, Y = y, VX = 16, VY = 0 })
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("falling off a high ledge", function()
			local w = setup()
			w.fake.Physics:AddBox(-10, -10, -10, 10, 600, 10) -- tower, top at y = 600
			local x, y, vy = 0, 603, 0
			for i = 1, 60 do
				x += 16 * DT
				if x > 10 then
					vy -= 196.2 * DT
					y = math.max(3, y + vy * DT)
				end
				w.step({ X = x, Y = y, VX = 16, VY = vy })
				if y <= 3 then
					vy = 0
				end
				local _ = i
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("respawning far away", function()
			local w = setup()
			for i = 1, 10 do
				w.step({ X = i })
			end
			w.character = w.player:Spawn()
			w.fake.Physics:Set(w.character, { X = 5000, Z = -3000 })
			for i = 1, 20 do
				w.step({ X = 5000 + i })
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("server-authorized teleport", function()
			local w = setup()
			for i = 1, 10 do
				w.step({ X = i })
			end
			w.framework:GetModule("Movement"):AuthorizeTeleport(w.player)
			w.step({ X = 9000, Y = 503 })
			w.fake.Physics:AddBox(8000, 490, -1000, 10000, 500, 1000)
			for i = 1, 30 do
				w.step({ X = 9000 + i })
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("driving a vehicle, then getting out", function()
			local w = setup()
			local x = 0
			for _ = 1, 30 do
				x += 120 * DT
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 120, Seated = true, State = "Seated" })
			end
			for _ = 1, 20 do
				x += 16 * DT
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 16, Seated = false, State = "Running" })
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("climbing a tall ladder", function()
			local w = setup()
			local y = 3
			for _ = 1, 50 do
				y += 8 * DT
				w.step({ Y = y, VY = 8, State = "Climbing" })
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("swimming at the surface", function()
			local w = setup()
			local x = 0
			for _ = 1, 50 do
				x += 12 * DT
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 12, Y = 40, GroundClearance = 37, State = "Swimming" })
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("dying (ragdoll flung away) and respawning", function()
			local w = setup()
			w.step({ X = 1 })
			for i = 1, 10 do
				w.step({ Health = 0, State = "Dead", X = i * 50, AngularSpeed = 400 })
			end
			w.character = w.player:Spawn()
			w.fake.Physics:Set(w.character, {})
			for i = 1, 10 do
				w.step({ X = i })
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("a game dash granted with AllowMovement Speed", function()
			local w = setup()
			local x = 0
			for _ = 1, 10 do
				x += 16 * DT
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 16 })
			end
			w.framework:GetModule("Movement"):Allow(w.player, "Speed", 1, { Multiplier = 4 })
			for _ = 1, 5 do
				x += 60 * DT
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 60 })
			end
			for _ = 1, 30 do
				x += 16 * DT
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 16 })
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("knockback granted with AllowMovement Impulse", function()
			local w = setup()
			w.step({ X = 0 })
			w.framework:GetModule("Movement"):Allow(w.player, "Impulse", 1)
			local x = 0
			for _ = 1, 4 do
				x += 250 * DT
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 250, VY = 120, AngularSpeed = 90 })
			end
			for i = 1, 30 do
				w.step({ X = x + i * 0.5, VX = 2, VY = 0, AngularSpeed = 0 })
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("walking alongside a wall and through an open doorway", function()
			local w = setup()
			-- Wall at x = 20 with a doorway gap between z = -3 and z = 3.
			w.fake.Physics:AddBox(19, 0, -100, 21, 20, -3)
			w.fake.Physics:AddBox(19, 0, 3, 21, 20, 100)
			local x = 0
			for _ = 1, 40 do
				x += 16 * DT
				w.step({ X = x, Z = 0, VX = 16 })
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("standing still for a long time, then walking", function()
			local w = setup()
			for _ = 1, 100 do
				w.step(nil)
			end
			local x = 0
			for _ = 1, 30 do
				x += 16 * DT
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 16 })
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("riding a moving train while walking along it", function()
			local w = setup()
			local x = 0
			for _ = 1, 60 do
				x += (60 + 16) * DT
				w.step({ X = x, VX = 76, GroundSpeed = 60 })
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)

		t.it("double jump allowed by configuration", function()
			local w = setup({ ModuleSettings = { Movement = { MaxAirJumps = 1 } } })
			local samples = { 3, 9, 13, 14, 12, 18, 21, 21, 18, 12, 4, 3, 3 }
			for _ = 1, 3 do
				for _, y in samples do
					w.step({ Y = y })
				end
				for _ = 1, 10 do
					w.step(nil)
				end
			end
			expectClean(w)
		end)
	end)
end
```
