`tests/Specs/Integration/Population.spec.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Phase 8 population run (short version of tests/lune/evaluate.luau): honest players and
	one of each cheat in one server, full stack, enforcement in shadow mode.

	Honest players must produce no evidence, never leave the Normal band and never be the
	subject of an enforcement decision. Every cheat must be caught.
]]

local Simulation = require(script.Parent.Parent.Simulation)

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("Population", function()
		t.it("15 honest players and 9 cheaters for 4 minutes: every cheat caught, no honest evidence", function()
			local sim = Simulation.new({ Seed = 11, Config = { Enforcement = { ShadowMode = true } } })
			for _ = 1, 15 do
				sim.add("Honest")
			end
			local cheaters = {}
			for _, cheat in Simulation.CHEATS do
				cheaters[cheat] = sim.add(cheat, 30)
			end
			sim.run(240)

			local honest = sim.honestReport()
			local kinds = {}
			for kind, count in honest.Kinds do
				table.insert(kinds, `{kind} x{count}`)
			end
			if honest.WithEvidence > 0 then
				error({ __testFailure = true, message = "honest evidence: " .. table.concat(kinds, ", ") }, 0)
			end
			expect(honest.MaxBand).toBe("Normal")
			for _, agent in sim.agents do
				if agent.role == "Honest" then
					expect(#sim.framework:GetAuditLog({ UserId = agent.id, Type = "ShadowAction" })).toBe(0)
					expect(#sim.framework:GetAuditLog({ UserId = agent.id, Type = "Rejected" })).toBe(0)
				end
			end

			local missed = {}
			for cheat, agent in cheaters do
				if agent.firstEvidence == nil then
					table.insert(missed, cheat)
				end
			end
			table.sort(missed)
			expect(missed).toEqual({})
		end)

		t.it("live enforcement neutralises hard-evidence cheaters and never touches honest players", function()
			-- Each cheater must end up removed, or held at Correction or above with a staff
			-- case open. A flyer rubber-banded every second stops gaining airtime, so its
			-- risk can plateau below EnforcementReview: mitigated, and staff decide.
			local sim = Simulation.new({ Seed = 12 })
			for _ = 1, 15 do
				sim.add("Honest")
			end
			local cheaters = { sim.add("Fly", 20), sim.add("Teleport", 20), sim.add("Noclip", 20), sim.add("Speed", 20) }
			sim.run(240)
			for _, agent in cheaters do
				if not agent.kicked then
					local state = sim.framework:GetEnforcement(agent.id)
					if state.Level < 3 or state.OpenCase == nil then
						error({ __testFailure = true, message = `{agent.role} not neutralised: level {state.LevelName}, case {tostring(state.OpenCase)}` }, 0)
					end
				end
			end
			for _, agent in sim.agents do
				if agent.role == "Honest" then
					expect(agent.kicked).toBe(false)
					expect(sim.framework:GetEnforcement(agent.id).Level).toBe(0)
				end
			end
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/Integration/RemoteSecurity.spec.luau`:

```luau
local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Framework = require(ACF.Core.Framework)
local V = require(ACF.Detection.RemoteSecurity.Validators)
local Policies = require(ACF.Detection.RemoteSecurity.Policies)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local function boot(config)
	local fake = Fakes.environment()
	local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
	framework:Initialize(config or {})
	local security = framework:GetModule("RemoteSecurity")
	return framework, fake, security
end

local function start(config)
	local framework, fake, security = boot(config)
	framework:Start()
	local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
	return framework, fake, security, player
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("RemoteSecurity registration", function()
		t.it("is a built-in module that runs by default", function()
			local framework = start()
			expect(framework:GetStatus().Modules.RemoteSecurity.State).toBe("Running")
		end)

		t.it("can be disabled by configuration", function()
			local framework = boot({ Modules = { RemoteSecurity = false } })
			framework:Start()
			expect(framework:GetStatus().Modules.RemoteSecurity.State).toBe("Disabled")
		end)

		t.it("validates its configuration", function()
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			expect(function()
				framework:Initialize({ ModuleSettings = { RemoteSecurity = { Suspicion = { HalfLifeSeconds = 0 } } } })
			end).toThrow("ModuleSettings.RemoteSecurity.Suspicion.HalfLifeSeconds")
		end)

		t.it("rejects invalid protection specs with clear messages", function()
			local _, _, security = boot()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Buy")
			local function handler() end
			local cases = {
				{ nil, "spec must be a table" },
				{ { Arguments = {} }, "Handler function is required" },
				{ { Handler = handler }, "Arguments must be an array" },
				{ { Handler = handler, Arguments = { "string" } }, "Arguments[1] is not a validator" },
				{ { Handler = handler, Arguments = {}, Cooldwn = 1 }, "unknown spec field 'Cooldwn'" },
				{ { Handler = handler, Arguments = {}, RateLimit = { Burst = 0, PerSecond = 1 } }, "Burst >= 1" },
				{ { Handler = handler, Arguments = {}, Idempotency = { Argument = 1 } }, "must index one of the Arguments" },
				{ { Handler = handler, Arguments = { V.boolean() }, Idempotency = { Argument = 1 } }, "string or number" },
				{ { Handler = handler, Arguments = {}, RejectResponse = false }, "only applies to RemoteFunctions" },
				{ { Handler = handler, Arguments = {}, SuspicionWeights = { Typo = 1 } }, "unknown SuspicionWeights key" },
			}
			for _, case in cases do
				expect(function()
					security:ProtectEvent(remote, case[1])
				end).toThrow(case[2])
			end
		end)

		t.it("rejects the wrong remote class and double protection", function()
			local _, _, security = boot()
			local spec = { Arguments = {}, Handler = function() end }
			expect(function()
				security:ProtectEvent(Fakes.remoteFunction(), spec)
			end).toThrow("expects a RemoteEvent")
			expect(function()
				security:ProtectFunction(Fakes.remoteEvent(), spec)
			end).toThrow("expects a RemoteFunction")
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent()
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, spec)
			expect(function()
				security:ProtectEvent(remote, spec)
			end).toThrow("already protected")
		end)

		t.it("accepts UnreliableRemoteEvents", function()
			local _, _, security, player = start()
			local remote = Fakes.unreliableRemoteEvent("Aim")
			local calls = 0
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = { V.vector3() },
				Handler = function()
					calls += 1
				end,
			})
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, Fakes.vector3(1, 2, 3))
			expect(calls).toBe(1)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("RemoteSecurity pipeline", function()
		t.it("passes validated arguments to the handler", function()
			local _, _, security, player = start()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Buy")
			local received
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = { V.string(), V.integer({ Min = 1, Max = 99 }) },
				Handler = function(p, item, qty)
					received = { p, item, qty }
				end,
			})
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, "Sword", 3)
			expect(received[1]).toBe(player)
			expect(received[2]).toBe("Sword")
			expect(received[3]).toBe(3)
		end)

		t.it("rejects invalid arguments and extra arguments without calling the handler", function()
			local _, _, security, player = start()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Buy")
			local calls = 0
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = { V.integer({ Min = 1, Max = 99 }) },
				Handler = function()
					calls += 1
				end,
			})
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, 1000)
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, "1")
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, 1, "extra")
			expect(calls).toBe(0)
			expect(security:GetStats().Buy.Rejected.invalid_arguments).toBe(3)
		end)

		t.it("lets optional trailing arguments be omitted", function()
			local _, _, security, player = start()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Chat")
			local got
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = { V.string(), V.optional(V.string()) },
				Handler = function(_, a, b)
					got = { a, b }
				end,
			})
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, "hi")
			expect(got[1]).toBe("hi")
			expect(got[2]).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("rate limits per player and per remote", function()
			local _, fake, security, player = start()
			local other = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(2))
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Click")
			local calls = 0
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = {},
				RateLimit = { Burst = 3, PerSecond = 1 },
				Handler = function()
					calls += 1
				end,
			})
			for _ = 1, 10 do
				Fakes.fire(remote, player)
			end
			expect(calls).toBe(3)
			Fakes.fire(remote, other)
			expect(calls).toBe(4)
			fake.Clock.Advance(1)
			Fakes.fire(remote, player)
			expect(calls).toBe(5)
		end)

		t.it("uses the configured default rate limit", function()
			local _, _, security, player = start({ ModuleSettings = { RemoteSecurity = { DefaultRateLimit = { Burst = 2, PerSecond = 1 } } } })
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Click")
			local calls = 0
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = {},
				Handler = function()
					calls += 1
				end,
			})
			for _ = 1, 5 do
				Fakes.fire(remote, player)
			end
			expect(calls).toBe(2)
		end)

		t.it("enforces cooldown between accepted requests", function()
			local _, fake, security, player = start()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Swing")
			local calls = 0
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = {},
				Cooldown = 1,
				Handler = function()
					calls += 1
				end,
			})
			Fakes.fire(remote, player)
			Fakes.fire(remote, player)
			fake.Clock.Advance(1)
			Fakes.fire(remote, player)
			expect(calls).toBe(2)
			expect(security:GetStats().Swing.Rejected.cooldown).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("applies permission and ownership checks", function()
			local _, _, security, player = start()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("EditPlot")
			local calls = 0
			local mine = Fakes.instance("Model", "Mine")
			mine:SetAttribute("OwnerUserId", 1)
			local theirs = Fakes.instance("Model", "Theirs")
			theirs:SetAttribute("OwnerUserId", 2)
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = { V.instance("Model") },
				Permission = Policies.Permissions.userIds({ 1 }),
				Ownership = Policies.Ownership.attribute(1, "OwnerUserId"),
				Handler = function()
					calls += 1
				end,
			})
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, mine)
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, theirs)
			expect(calls).toBe(1)
			expect(security:GetStats().EditPlot.Rejected.ownership_denied).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("denies the request when a policy check errors", function()
			local _, fake, security, player = start()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Admin")
			local calls = 0
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = {},
				Permission = function()
					error("datastore down")
				end,
				Handler = function()
					calls += 1
				end,
			})
			Fakes.fire(remote, player)
			expect(calls).toBe(0)
			expect(#Fakes.findLogs(fake.Logs, "policy check errored", "Error")).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("rejects duplicate transaction ids inside the window", function()
			local _, fake, security, player = start()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Purchase")
			local calls = 0
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = { V.string({ MaxLength = 36 }) },
				Idempotency = { Argument = 1, WindowSeconds = 10 },
				Handler = function()
					calls += 1
				end,
			})
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, "txn-1")
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, "txn-1")
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, "txn-2")
			expect(calls).toBe(2)
			fake.Clock.Advance(11)
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, "txn-1")
			expect(calls).toBe(3)
		end)

		t.it("keeps transaction memory bounded", function()
			local framework, _, security, player = start()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Purchase")
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = { V.integer() },
				RateLimit = { Burst = 10000, PerSecond = 1000 },
				Idempotency = { Argument = 1, WindowSeconds = 60, MaxEntries = 8 },
				Handler = function() end,
			})
			for i = 1, 500 do
				Fakes.fire(remote, player, i)
			end
			expect(security:GetStats().Purchase.Accepted).toBe(500)
			local state = framework:GetSession(player):GetState("RemoteSecurity.State").Purchase
			expect(#state.idOrder <= 8).toBe(true)
			local stored = 0
			for _ in state.seenIds do
				stored += 1
			end
			expect(stored <= 8).toBe(true)
			-- The most recent id is still remembered and rejected as a duplicate.
			Fakes.fire(remote, player, 500)
			expect(security:GetStats().Purchase.Rejected.duplicate_transaction).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("requires a character when asked", function()
			local _, _, security, player = start()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Jump")
			local calls = 0
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = {},
				RequireCharacter = true,
				Handler = function()
					calls += 1
				end,
			})
			Fakes.fire(remote, player)
			player:Spawn()
			Fakes.fire(remote, player)
			expect(calls).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("ignores requests from players without a session", function()
			local _, _, security = start()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Buy")
			local calls = 0
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = {},
				Handler = function()
					calls += 1
				end,
			})
			Fakes.fire(remote, Fakes.player(99))
			expect(calls).toBe(0)
			expect(security:GetStats().Buy.Rejected.no_session).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("isolates handler errors", function()
			local _, fake, security, player = start()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Buggy")
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = {},
				Handler = function()
					error("game bug")
				end,
			})
			expect(function()
				Fakes.fire(remote, player)
			end).never.toThrow()
			expect(security:GetStats().Buggy.HandlerErrors).toBe(1)
			expect(#Fakes.findLogs(fake.Logs, "Remote handler errored", "Error")).toBe(1)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("RemoteSecurity functions", function()
		t.it("returns handler results for valid calls and RejectResponse otherwise", function()
			local _, _, security, player = start()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteFunction("GetPrice")
			security:ProtectFunction(remote, {
				Arguments = { V.string({ MaxLength = 16 }) },
				RejectResponse = false,
				Handler = function(_, item)
					return item == "Sword", 100
				end,
			})
			local ok, price = Fakes.invoke(remote, player, "Sword")
			expect(ok).toBe(true)
			expect(price).toBe(100)
			expect(Fakes.invoke(remote, player, 42)).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("returns RejectResponse when the handler errors", function()
			local _, _, security, player = start()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteFunction("Broken")
			security:ProtectFunction(remote, {
				Arguments = {},
				RejectResponse = "error",
				Handler = function()
					error("bug")
				end,
			})
			expect(Fakes.invoke(remote, player)).toBe("error")
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("RemoteSecurity lifecycle", function()
		t.it("protections made before Start activate on Start", function()
			local framework, fake, security = boot()
			local remote = Fakes.remoteEvent("Early")
			local calls = 0
			security:ProtectEvent(remote, {
				Arguments = {},
				Handler = function()
					calls += 1
				end,
			})
			framework:Start()
			local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			Fakes.fire(remote, player)
			expect(calls).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("fails closed while stopped and resumes on restart", function()
			local framework, fake, security, player = start()
			local event = Fakes.remoteEvent("E")
			local fn = Fakes.remoteFunction("F")
			local calls = 0
			security:ProtectEvent(event, {
				Arguments = {},
				Handler = function()
					calls += 1
				end,
			})
			security:ProtectFunction(fn, {
				Arguments = {},
				RejectResponse = "closed",
				Handler = function()
					return "open"
				end,
			})
			framework:Stop()
			Fakes.fire(event, player)
			expect(calls).toBe(0)
			expect(Fakes.invoke(fn, player)).toBe("closed")
			framework:Start()
			fake.Players:Join(player)
			Fakes.fire(event, player)
			expect(calls).toBe(1)
			expect(Fakes.invoke(fn, player)).toBe("open")
		end)

		t.it("unprotect removes the handler", function()
			local _, _, security, player = start()
			local event = Fakes.remoteEvent("E")
			local calls = 0
			local handle = security:ProtectEvent(event, {
				Arguments = {},
				Handler = function()
					calls += 1
				end,
			})
			handle.Unprotect()
			Fakes.fire(event, player)
			expect(calls).toBe(0)
			expect(security:IsProtected(event)).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("finds unprotected remotes", function()
			local _, _, security = start()
			local root = Fakes.instance("Folder", "Remotes")
			local protectedRemote = Fakes.instance("RemoteEvent", "Safe", root)
			local open = Fakes.instance("RemoteEvent", "Open", root)
			local openFn = Fakes.instance("RemoteFunction", "OpenFn", root)
			Fakes.instance("Folder", "NotARemote", root)
			security:ProtectEvent(protectedRemote, { Arguments = {}, Handler = function() end })
			local found = security:FindUnprotected(root)
			expect(#found).toBe(2)
			expect(table.find(found, open) ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(table.find(found, openFn) ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/Integration/Risk.spec.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Centralised risk inside the full framework. Probe modules report detections with
	chosen categories, severities and confidences; a real Movement run checks the
	end-to-end path.
]]

local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Framework = require(ACF.Core.Framework)
local Topics = require(ACF.Core.Topics)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local PROBES = {
	MoveProbe = "Movement",
	PhysProbe = "Physics", -- the "Physics" category is grouped with Movement by default
	AimProbe = "Combat",
	EcoProbe = "Economy",
	NetProbe = "Remote",
}

local function world(config, envOptions)
	local fake = Fakes.environment(envOptions)
	local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
	local probes = {}
	for name, category in PROBES do
		local probe = Fakes.module(name, { Category = category })
		framework:RegisterModule(probe)
		probes[name] = probe
	end
	framework:Initialize(config or {})
	framework:Start()
	local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1, "Suspect"))
	local w = { framework = framework, fake = fake, player = player, probes = probes, changes = {} }
	framework:Subscribe(Topics.RiskChanged, function(payload)
		table.insert(w.changes, payload)
	end)
	function w.report(probe, kind, severity, confidence, target)
		return probes[probe].Context:Report(target or player, { Kind = kind, Severity = severity, Confidence = confidence })
	end
	function w.advance(seconds)
		local steps = math.max(1, math.ceil(seconds))
		for _ = 1, steps do
			fake.Clock.Advance(seconds / steps)
			framework:Step()
		end
	end
	function w.risk()
		return framework:GetRisk(player)
	end
	return w
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("Risk", function()
		t.it("one detection contributes severity x confidence", function()
			local w = world()
			w.report("MoveProbe", "TeleportDetected", 30, 0.8)
			local r = w.risk()
			expect(r.Risk).toBeCloseTo(24, 6)
			expect(r.Band).toBe("Increased")
			expect(r.Groups[1].Group).toBe("Movement")
			expect(r.Groups[1].Kinds[1].Kind).toBe("TeleportDetected")
		end)

		t.it("follows R = sum(w c exp(-lambda t)) across independent kinds", function()
			local w = world()
			local lambda = math.log(2) / 600
			w.report("MoveProbe", "TeleportDetected", 30, 1)
			w.advance(200)
			w.report("AimProbe", "CombatFireRate", 20, 0.5)
			w.advance(100)
			local expected = 30 * math.exp(-lambda * 300) + 20 * 0.5 * math.exp(-lambda * 100)
			expect(w.risk().Risk).toBeCloseTo(expected, 4)
		end)

		t.it("decays over time and publishes the lowered band", function()
			local w = world()
			w.report("MoveProbe", "TeleportDetected", 50, 1)
			expect(w.risk().Band).toBe("Restrict")
			w.advance(700)
			expect(w.risk().Band).toBe("Increased")
			local last = w.changes[#w.changes]
			expect(last.PreviousBand).toBe("Restrict")
			expect(last.Band).toBe("Increased")
			w.advance(3600)
			expect(w.risk().Band).toBe("Normal")
		end)

		t.it("suppresses duplicates: a burst of one kind counts once", function()
			local w = world()
			for _ = 1, 20 do
				w.report("NetProbe", "RemoteRateLimited", 20, 1)
			end
			local r = w.risk()
			expect(r.Risk).toBeCloseTo(20, 6)
			expect(r.Groups[1].Kinds[1].Suppressed).toBe(19)
			expect(w.framework:GetStatus().Risk.Suppressed).toBe(19)
		end)

		t.it("one kind repeating forever saturates at severity x confidence x RepeatCap", function()
			-- RepeatCap 3 (calibrated in Phase 8): sustained severe evidence escalates,
			-- low-severity evidence stays below EnforcementReview however long it lasts.
			-- Enforcement is off so the player is not removed part-way.
			local w = world({ Enforcement = { Mode = "Off" } })
			for _ = 1, 120 do -- 20 minutes of a report every 10 s
				w.report("MoveProbe", "Flight", 30, 0.9)
				w.report("NetProbe", "RemoteCooldownViolation", 10, 0.9)
				w.advance(10)
			end
			local r = w.risk()
			local movement, remote
			for _, group in r.Groups do
				if group.Group == "Movement" then
					movement = group.Score
				elseif group.Group == "Remote" then
					remote = group.Score
				end
			end
			expect(movement <= 81 + 1e-6).toBe(true)
			expect(movement > 78).toBe(true)
			expect(remote <= 27 + 1e-6).toBe(true)
			local single = world({ Enforcement = { Mode = "Off" } })
			for _ = 1, 120 do
				single.report("NetProbe", "RemoteCooldownViolation", 15, 0.9)
				single.advance(10)
			end
			-- 15 x 0.9 x 3 = 40.5 at most; decay between reports keeps it just below.
			expect(single.risk().Risk <= 40.5).toBe(true)
			expect(single.risk().Risk > 38).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("correlated kinds are not double-counted; independent groups add", function()
			local correlated = world()
			correlated.report("MoveProbe", "SpeedExceeded", 30, 1)
			correlated.report("PhysProbe", "VelocityMismatch", 30, 1)
			expect(correlated.risk().Risk).toBeCloseTo(37.5, 6) -- 30 + 0.25 x 30
			expect(#correlated.risk().Groups).toBe(1)

			local independent = world()
			independent.report("MoveProbe", "SpeedExceeded", 30, 1)
			independent.report("AimProbe", "CombatNoLineOfSight", 30, 1)
			expect(independent.risk().Risk).toBeCloseTo(60, 6)
			expect(independent.risk().Band).toBe("Investigation")
		end)

		t.it("several independent groups reach EnforcementReview with an explanation", function()
			local w = world()
			w.report("MoveProbe", "TeleportDetected", 30, 0.9)
			w.report("AimProbe", "CombatOutOfRange", 30, 0.9)
			w.report("EcoProbe", "SignalAnomaly", 20, 0.8)
			w.report("NetProbe", "RemoteInvalidArguments", 15, 0.8)
			local r = w.risk()
			expect(r.Band).toBe("EnforcementReview")
			expect(r.Confidence > 0.99).toBe(true)
			expect(string.find(r.Explanation, "TeleportDetected", 1, true) ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(string.find(r.Explanation, "CombatOutOfRange", 1, true) ~= nil).toBe(true)
			local bands = {}
			for _, change in w.changes do
				table.insert(bands, change.Band)
			end
			-- 27 -> 54 -> 70 -> 82
			expect(bands).toEqual({ "Increased", "Restrict", "Investigation", "EnforcementReview" })
			expect(w.changes[4].Session ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("Groups configuration merges over the defaults", function()
			local w = world({ Risk = { Groups = { AimProbe = "Movement" } } })
			w.report("MoveProbe", "SpeedExceeded", 40, 1)
			w.report("AimProbe", "CombatFireRate", 40, 1)
			expect(w.risk().Risk).toBeCloseTo(50, 6)
			w.report("PhysProbe", "FlingDetected", 40, 1) -- default Physics -> Movement still applies
			expect(#w.risk().Groups).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("leaving and rejoining does not reset risk", function()
			local w = world()
			w.report("MoveProbe", "TeleportDetected", 50, 1)
			w.fake.Players:Leave(w.player)
			w.advance(10)
			w.fake.Players:Join(w.player)
			expect(w.risk().Risk > 49).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("history records detections and band changes; Reset clears and is recorded", function()
			local w = world()
			w.report("MoveProbe", "TeleportDetected", 50, 1)
			w.report("AimProbe", "CombatFireRate", 10, 1)
			local history = w.framework:GetRiskHistory(w.player)
			local types = {}
			for _, entry in history do
				table.insert(types, entry.Type)
			end
			-- 50 enters Restrict; +10 reaches Investigation.
			expect(types).toEqual({ "Band", "Detection", "Band", "Detection" })
			expect(history[2].Contribution).toBe(50)
			w.framework:ResetRisk(w.player, { Reason = "false positive, ticket 12" })
			expect(w.risk().Risk).toBe(0)
			local after = w.framework:GetRiskHistory(w.player)
			expect(after[#after].Type).toBe("Reset")
			expect(after[#after].Note).toBe("false positive, ticket 12")
			expect(w.changes[#w.changes].Band).toBe("Normal")
		end)

		t.it("per-user memory is bounded (keys and history)", function()
			local w = world()
			for i = 1, 150 do
				w.report("NetProbe", "Kind" .. i, 1, 0.5)
				w.advance(0.2) -- stay under the per-detector report rate limit
			end
			local r = w.risk()
			local kinds = 0
			for _, group in r.Groups do
				kinds += #group.Kinds
			end
			expect(kinds).toBe(64)
			expect(w.framework:GetStatus().Risk.Evicted).toBe(86)
			expect(#w.framework:GetRiskHistory(w.player) <= 50).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("departed users are forgotten once their risk has decayed", function()
			local w = world()
			w.report("MoveProbe", "TeleportDetected", 10, 1)
			w.fake.Players:Leave(w.player)
			expect(w.framework:GetStatus().Risk.Tracked).toBe(1)
			w.advance(7200)
			expect(w.framework:GetStatus().Risk.Tracked).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("invalid band order fails Initialize with a clear message", function()
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			expect(function()
				framework:Initialize({ Risk = { Bands = { Restrict = 70 } } })
			end).toThrow("Risk.Bands.Investigation must be greater than Risk.Bands.Restrict")
			expect(framework:GetState()).toBe("Created")
		end)

		t.it("Risk.Enabled = false turns the engine off", function()
			local w = world({ Risk = { Enabled = false } })
			w.report("MoveProbe", "TeleportDetected", 50, 1)
			expect(w.framework:GetStatus().Risk).toBeNil()
			expect(function()
				w.framework:GetRisk(w.player)
			end).toThrow("risk assessment is disabled")
		end)

		t.it("end to end: a minute of hovering raises risk; walking does not", function()
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			fake.Physics:AddFloor()
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			framework:Initialize({})
			framework:Start()
			local cheater = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
			local honest = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(2))
			local c1, c2 = cheater:Spawn(), honest:Spawn()
			fake.Physics:Set(c1, {})
			fake.Physics:Set(c2, {})
			for i = 1, 300 do -- one minute
				fake.Physics:Set(c1, { Y = 60, VY = 0 })
				fake.Physics:Set(c2, { X = i * 0.2 * 14 })
				fake.Clock.Advance(0.2)
				framework:Step()
			end
			local r = framework:GetRisk(cheater)
			expect(r.Band ~= "Normal").toBe(true)
			expect(string.find(r.Explanation, "Flight", 1, true) ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(framework:GetRisk(honest).Risk).toBe(0)
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/Integration/RiskPersistence.spec.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Persistent risk: saving at session end (only when justified), seeding the next
	server, batching, retries, DataStore failures, latency races and shutdown flushing.
	A shared fake store stands in for the DataStore across "servers".
]]

local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Framework = require(ACF.Core.Framework)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local SCOPE = "ACF_Risk_v1"
local ON = { Risk = { Persistence = { Enabled = true } } }

local function server(store, config, clockStart)
	local fake = Fakes.environment({ Store = store, ClockStart = clockStart })
	local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
	local probe = Fakes.module("Probe", { Category = "Movement" })
	local probe2 = Fakes.module("Probe2", { Category = "Combat" })
	framework:RegisterModule(probe)
	framework:RegisterModule(probe2)
	framework:Initialize(config or ON)
	framework:Start()
	local s = { framework = framework, fake = fake, store = store }
	function s.join(id)
		return fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(id or 1))
	end
	function s.report(player, severity, which)
		local module = if which == 2 then probe2 else probe
		module.Context:Report(player, { Kind = "TeleportDetected", Severity = severity, Confidence = 1 })
	end
	function s.advance(seconds)
		local steps = math.max(1, math.ceil(seconds))
		for _ = 1, steps do
			fake.Clock.Advance(seconds / steps)
			framework:Step()
		end
	end
	return s
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("Risk persistence", function()
		t.it("is off by default: no risk DataStore traffic", function()
			local store = Fakes.store()
			local s = server(store, {})
			local p = s.join()
			s.report(p, 60)
			s.fake.Players:Leave(p)
			s.advance(60)
			s.framework:Stop()
			-- The ban check on join (Enforcement) reads its own store; the risk store is untouched.
			expect(store.ByScope[SCOPE]).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("saves only sessions ending at or above MinRiskToSave, in the flush batch", function()
			local store = Fakes.store()
			local s = server(store)
			local high, low = s.join(1), s.join(2)
			s.report(high, 55)
			s.report(low, 30)
			s.fake.Players:Leave(high)
			s.fake.Players:Leave(low)
			local function saves()
				return if store.ByScope[SCOPE] then store.ByScope[SCOPE].Saves else 0
			end
			expect(saves()).toBe(0) -- batched, not written on leave
			s.advance(31)
			expect(saves()).toBe(1)
			local record = store.Data[SCOPE].Risk_1
			expect(record.Version).toBe(1)
			expect(record.Risk >= 40).toBe(true)
			expect(#record.Sessions).toBe(1)
			expect(record.Sessions[1].Top[1].Group).toBe("Movement")
			expect(store.Data[SCOPE].Risk_2).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("seeds the next server with weighted, decayed, capped history", function()
			local store = Fakes.store()
			local first = server(store)
			local p = first.join()
			first.report(p, 60)
			first.fake.Players:Leave(p)
			first.framework:Stop() -- flushes the queue
			local saved = store.Data[SCOPE].Risk_1.Risk

			-- Same player, another server, two hours later.
			local second = server(store, nil, 1000 + 7200)
			local again = second.join()
			local r = second.framework:GetRisk(again)
			local expected = math.min(35, saved * 0.5 * 0.5 ^ (2 / 24))
			expect(r.Persistent).toBeCloseTo(expected, 1)
			expect(r.Band).toBe("Increased")
			expect(string.find(r.Explanation, "History", 1, true) ~= nil).toBe(true)
			local history = second.framework:GetRiskHistory(again)
			expect(history[#history].Type).toBe("History")
		end)

		t.it("history alone is capped below the Restrict band", function()
			local store = Fakes.store()
			store.Data[SCOPE] = { Risk_1 = { Version = 1, Risk = 100, WallTime = 1700000000 + 1000, Sessions = {} } }
			local s = server(store, nil, 1000 + 60)
			local p = s.join()
			local r = s.framework:GetRisk(p)
			expect(r.Persistent).toBe(35)
			expect(r.Band).toBe("Increased")
		end)

		t.it("old history fades out (a week later adds nothing)", function()
			local store = Fakes.store()
			store.Data[SCOPE] = { Risk_1 = { Version = 1, Risk = 90, WallTime = 1700000000 + 1000, Sessions = {} } }
			local s = server(store, nil, 1000 + 7 * 86400)
			local p = s.join()
			expect(s.framework:GetRisk(p).Persistent).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("a failed load means no history, never an error or added risk", function()
			local store = Fakes.store()
			store.Data[SCOPE] = { Risk_1 = { Version = 1, Risk = 90, WallTime = 1700000000 + 1000, Sessions = {} } }
			store.FailLoads = 1
			local s = server(store, nil, 1060)
			local p = s.join()
			expect(s.framework:GetRisk(p).Risk).toBe(0)
			expect(s.framework:GetStatus().Risk.LoadFailed).toBe(1)
			expect(#Fakes.findLogs(s.fake.Logs, "could not be loaded", "Warn")).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("corrupt or foreign records are ignored", function()
			for _, bad in { "text", { Version = 99, Risk = 90, WallTime = 0 }, { Version = 1, Risk = "90", WallTime = 0 } } do
				local store = Fakes.store()
				store.Data[SCOPE] = { Risk_1 = bad }
				local s = server(store)
				local p = s.join()
				expect(s.framework:GetRisk(p).Risk).toBe(0)
			end
		end)

		t.it("a load that completes after the player left is discarded", function()
			local store = Fakes.store()
			store.Data[SCOPE] = { Risk_1 = { Version = 1, Risk = 90, WallTime = 1700000000 + 1000, Sessions = {} } }
			store.Deferred = true
			local s = server(store, nil, 1060)
			local p = s.join()
			s.fake.Players:Leave(p)
			store.Complete()
			expect(s.framework:GetRisk(p).Persistent).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("failed saves are retried, then given up after MaxAttempts", function()
			local store = Fakes.store()
			local s = server(store)
			local p1, p2 = s.join(1), s.join(2)
			s.report(p1, 60)
			s.report(p2, 60)
			store.FailSaves = 1 -- first write fails once
			s.fake.Players:Leave(p1)
			s.advance(31)
			s.advance(31)
			expect(store.Data[SCOPE].Risk_1 ~= nil).toBe(true)
			local stats = s.framework:GetStatus().Risk
			expect(stats.SaveRetries).toBe(1)
			expect(stats.Saved).toBe(1)

			store.FailSaves = 10
			s.fake.Players:Leave(p2)
			for _ = 1, 4 do
				s.advance(31)
			end
			stats = s.framework:GetStatus().Risk
			expect(stats.SaveFailed).toBe(1)
			expect(stats.Queued).toBe(0)
			expect(#Fakes.findLogs(s.fake.Logs, "giving up", "Error")).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("writes are batched to MaxWritesPerFlush", function()
			local store = Fakes.store()
			local s = server(store, { Risk = { Persistence = { Enabled = true, MaxWritesPerFlush = 3 } } })
			local players = {}
			for id = 1, 7 do
				players[id] = s.join(id)
				s.report(players[id], 60)
			end
			for id = 1, 7 do
				s.fake.Players:Leave(players[id])
			end
			local function saves()
				return if store.ByScope[SCOPE] then store.ByScope[SCOPE].Saves else 0
			end
			s.advance(31)
			expect(saves()).toBe(3)
			s.advance(30)
			expect(saves()).toBe(6)
			s.advance(30)
			expect(saves()).toBe(7)
		end)

		t.it("shutdown flushes everything and reports pending writes until they finish", function()
			local store = Fakes.store()
			local s = server(store)
			local p = s.join()
			s.report(p, 60)
			s.fake.Players:Leave(p)
			store.Deferred = true
			expect(s.fake.Shutdown.Pending()).toBe(1)
			s.fake.Shutdown.Flush()
			expect(s.fake.Shutdown.Pending()).toBe(1) -- in flight
			store.Complete()
			expect(s.fake.Shutdown.Pending()).toBe(0)
			expect(store.Data[SCOPE].Risk_1 ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("keeps the last MaxSessions session summaries across servers", function()
			local store = Fakes.store()
			for i = 1, 7 do
				local s = server(store, nil, 1000 + i * 3600)
				local p = s.join()
				s.report(p, 60)
				s.report(p, 30, 2)
				s.fake.Players:Leave(p)
				s.framework:Stop()
			end
			local record = store.Data[SCOPE].Risk_1
			expect(#record.Sessions).toBe(5)
			expect(record.Sessions[5].WallTime > record.Sessions[1].WallTime).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("ResetRisk with Persistent clears the saved risk but keeps the audit trail", function()
			local store = Fakes.store()
			local s = server(store)
			local p = s.join()
			s.report(p, 60)
			s.fake.Players:Leave(p)
			s.framework:Stop()
			local s2 = server(store, nil, 1000 + 3600)
			local again = s2.join()
			expect(s2.framework:GetRisk(again).Persistent > 0).toBe(true)
			s2.framework:ResetRisk(again, { Persistent = true, Reason = "appeal 7 upheld" })
			s2.advance(31)
			local record = store.Data[SCOPE].Risk_1
			expect(record.Risk).toBe(0)
			expect(record.ResetReason).toBe("appeal 7 upheld")
			expect(#record.Sessions).toBe(1)
			expect(s2.framework:GetRisk(again).Risk).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("enabled without a Store warns and runs without persistence", function()
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			fake.Env.Store = nil
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			framework:Initialize(ON)
			framework:Start()
			expect(framework:GetStatus().Risk.Persistence).toBe(false)
			expect(#Fakes.findLogs(fake.Logs, "no Store", "Warn")).toBe(1)
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/Integration/Telemetry.spec.luau`:

```luau
local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Framework = require(ACF.Core.Framework)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local function setup(config)
	local fake = Fakes.environment()
	local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
	framework:Initialize(config or { Modules = { Telemetry = true } })
	framework:Start()
	local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
	local remote = fake.Remotes.Created.Telemetry
	local world = { framework = framework, fake = fake, player = player, remote = remote }
	function world.advance(seconds)
		local steps = math.ceil(seconds / 1)
		for _ = 1, steps do
			fake.Clock.Advance(seconds / steps)
			framework:Step()
		end
	end
	function world.kinds()
		local kinds = {}
		for _, r in framework:GetEvidence(player) do
			kinds[r.Kind] = r
		end
		return kinds
	end
	return world
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("Telemetry", function()
		t.it("is disabled by default and creates no remote", function()
			local w = setup({})
			expect(w.framework:GetStatus().Modules.Telemetry.State).toBe("Disabled")
			expect(w.remote).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("creates and protects its remote when enabled", function()
			local w = setup()
			expect(w.remote ~= nil).toBe(true)
			local security = w.framework:GetModule("RemoteSecurity")
			expect(security:IsProtected(w.remote)).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("records heartbeats and FPS", function()
			local w = setup()
			Fakes.fire(w.remote, w.player, 1, 60)
			expect(w.framework:GetModule("Telemetry"):GetFps(w.player)).toBe(60)
		end)

		t.it("does not report players who send heartbeats", function()
			local w = setup()
			local sequence = 0
			for _ = 1, 60 do
				w.advance(5)
				sequence += 1
				Fakes.fire(w.remote, w.player, sequence, 60)
			end
			expect(w.kinds().TelemetryMissing).toBeNil()
			expect(w.kinds().TelemetrySequence).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("does not report a short outage", function()
			local w = setup()
			local sequence = 0
			for _ = 1, 10 do
				w.advance(5)
				sequence += 1
				Fakes.fire(w.remote, w.player, sequence, 60)
			end
			w.advance(40) -- lag spike / brief disconnect
			sequence += 1
			Fakes.fire(w.remote, w.player, sequence, 60)
			for _ = 1, 20 do
				w.advance(5)
				sequence += 1
				Fakes.fire(w.remote, w.player, sequence, 60)
			end
			expect(w.kinds().TelemetryMissing).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("reports a sustained missing heartbeat at low confidence", function()
			local w = setup()
			Fakes.fire(w.remote, w.player, 1, 60)
			w.advance(240)
			local record = w.kinds().TelemetryMissing
			expect(record ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(record.Category).toBe("Integrity")
			expect(record.Confidence <= 0.5).toBe(true)
			expect(record.Details.EverReceived).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("respects the startup grace period", function()
			local w = setup({ Modules = { Telemetry = true }, ModuleSettings = { Telemetry = { StartupGraceSeconds = 600 } } })
			w.advance(300)
			expect(w.kinds().TelemetryMissing).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("reports replayed or backwards sequences", function()
			local w = setup()
			Fakes.fire(w.remote, w.player, 10, 60)
			for _ = 1, 8 do
				Fakes.fire(w.remote, w.player, 10, 60)
				w.advance(5)
			end
			expect(w.kinds().TelemetrySequence ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("rejects malformed heartbeats through RemoteSecurity", function()
			local w = setup()
			Fakes.fire(w.remote, w.player, "1", 60)
			Fakes.fire(w.remote, w.player, 1, 99999)
			Fakes.fire(w.remote, w.player, 0 / 0, 60)
			local stats = w.framework:GetModule("RemoteSecurity"):GetStats()["ACF.Telemetry"]
			expect(stats.Rejected.invalid_arguments).toBe(3)
			expect(stats.Accepted).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("fails cleanly without a remote provider", function()
			local fake = Fakes.environment()
			fake.Env.Remotes = nil
			local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
			framework:Initialize({ Modules = { Telemetry = true } })
			local status = framework:Start()
			expect(status.Modules.Telemetry.State).toBe("Failed")
			expect(status.State).toBe("Running")
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/Runner.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Runs every "*.spec" ModuleScript under this folder. Shared by the Lune entry point
	(tests/lune/run.luau) and Studio:

		require(game:GetService("ServerStorage").ACFTests.Runner).run()
]]

local TestKit = require(script.Parent.TestKit)

local Runner = {}

function Runner.run(options: { Filter: string?, Print: ((string) -> ())? }?)
	local specs = {}
	for _, descendant in script.Parent:GetDescendants() do
		local name = descendant.Name
		if descendant:IsA("ModuleScript") and string.sub(name, -5) == ".spec" then
			table.insert(specs, {
				name = `{descendant.Parent.Name}/{string.sub(name, 1, -6)}`,
				spec = require(descendant),
			})
		end
	end
	table.sort(specs, function(a, b)
		return a.name < b.name
	end)
	return TestKit.run(specs, options)
end

return Runner

```

`tests/Specs/Simulation.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Population simulator for Phase 8: honest players and cheaters running together
	through the full framework (detection, risk, enforcement) with fakes. Deterministic
	(seeded Park-Miller RNG), so a run is reproducible in Lune and in Studio.

	Used by Integration/Population.spec (a short run in the normal suite) and by
	tests/lune/evaluate.luau (long runs that measure accuracy, false positives, CPU and
	memory; see docs/EVALUATION.md).

	World: floor at y = 0. Honest players stay in |x|, |z| <= 120. A wall stands at
	x = 150..152, z = -60..60, y = 0..20, used only by the noclip cheater. Server samples
	every 0.2 s.

	Honest behaviour, randomised per player:
	  - walking below WalkSpeed with direction changes, idling, jumping;
	  - sprinting granted by the server (WalkSpeed 24);
	  - lag spikes (0.6-3 s freezes, then a catch-up);
	  - jittery delivery at high ping;
	  - respawns, and server teleports with AuthorizeTeleport;
	  - shooting at other players with a personal accuracy / headshot rate;
	  - earning coins steadily, in lumps, in bursts, or with a 2x gamepass;
	  - clicking in human rhythm (CV 8-30 %), at 60 Hz frame times;
	  - a protected shop remote, with the odd purchase they can't afford.

	Cheats (start at StartAt, honest before):
	  Speed (2.5x), Teleport (80 studs every 3 s), Fly (hover at y 40), Noclip (walks through
	  the wall), Aimbot (99 % hits, 90 % heads), RapidFire (15 shots/s on a 10/s rifle),
	  Duper (10x coins), AutoClicker (1 s period, 3 ms jitter), RemoteSpam (invalid args).
]]

local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Framework = require(ACF.Core.Framework)
local Validators = require(ACF.Detection.RemoteSecurity.Validators)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Fakes)

local DT = 0.2
local GRAVITY = 196.2
local STAND = 3
local BOUNDS = 120

local Simulation = {}

Simulation.CHEATS = table.freeze({
	"Speed",
	"Teleport",
	"Fly",
	"Noclip",
	"Aimbot",
	"RapidFire",
	"Duper",
	"AutoClicker",
	"RemoteSpam",
})

-- Evidence categories that count as catching each cheat.
Simulation.EXPECTED = table.freeze({
	Speed = { Movement = true, Physics = true },
	Teleport = { Movement = true, Physics = true },
	Fly = { Movement = true },
	Noclip = { Movement = true },
	Aimbot = { Behavioral = true },
	RapidFire = { Combat = true },
	Duper = { Behavioral = true },
	AutoClicker = { Behavioral = true },
	RemoteSpam = { Remote = true },
})

local BAND_INDEX = { Normal = 1, Increased = 2, Restrict = 3, Investigation = 4, EnforcementReview = 5 }

local function rng(seed: number)
	local state = seed % 2147483647
	if state <= 0 then
		state += 2147483646
	end
	local r = {}
	function r.u(): number
		state = (state * 16807) % 2147483647
		return (state - 1) / 2147483646
	end
	function r.range(a: number, b: number): number
		return a + (b - a) * r.u()
	end
	function r.normal(mean: number, std: number): number
		local sum = 0
		for _ = 1, 12 do
			sum += r.u()
		end
		return mean + (sum - 6) * std
	end
	function r.chance(p: number): boolean
		return r.u() < p
	end
	return r
end

local function frame(t: number): number
	return math.ceil(t * 60 - 1e-9) / 60
end

function Simulation.new(options)
	local opts = options or {}
	local seed = opts.Seed or 1
	local r = rng(seed)
	local fake = Fakes.environment({ Store = opts.Store })
	fake.Physics:AddFloor()
	fake.Physics:AddBox(150, 0, -60, 152, 20, 60)
	local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
	for _, module in opts.Modules or {} do
		framework:RegisterModule(module)
	end
	local config = opts.Config or {}
	framework:Initialize(config)
	local combat = framework:GetModule("Combat")
	combat:Define("Rifle", { Kind = "Hitscan", Damage = 20, HeadshotMultiplier = 2, FireRate = 10, MaxRange = 300 })
	local behavioral = framework:GetModule("Behavioral")
	behavioral:DefineSignal("Coins")
	framework:Start()
	local movement = framework:GetModule("Movement")
	local buy = Fakes.remoteEvent("Buy")
	framework:GetModule("RemoteSecurity"):ProtectEvent(buy, {
		Arguments = { Validators.string({ MaxLength = 32 }), Validators.integer({ Min = 1, Max = 99 }) },
		RateLimit = { Burst = 5, PerSecond = 2 },
		Restrict = "Economy",
		Handler = function() end,
	})

	local sim = {
		framework = framework,
		fake = fake,
		agents = {},
		now = 0,
		acfSeconds = 0,
		stepSeconds = 0,
		steps = 0,
		events = {},
		nextId = 1,
		-- Per-player base ping is drawn from [pingMin, pingMax] seconds.
		pingMin = if opts.Ping then opts.Ping[1] else 0.03,
		pingMax = if opts.Ping then opts.Ping[2] else 0.45,
	}

	local function timed(fn, ...)
		local started = os.clock()
		local a, b = fn(...)
		sim.acfSeconds += os.clock() - started
		return a, b
	end

	local function schedule(at: number, fn)
		table.insert(sim.events, { at = at, fn = fn })
	end

	local function placeTargets(agent)
		fake.Physics:SetTarget(agent.torso, { X = agent.sx, Y = agent.sy, Z = agent.sz, Model = agent.character, Player = agent.player })
		fake.Physics:SetTarget(agent.head, { X = agent.sx, Y = agent.sy + 1.5, Z = agent.sz, Model = agent.character, Player = agent.player, IsHead = true })
	end

	local function spawn(agent, x: number, z: number)
		agent.character = agent.player:Spawn()
		agent.torso = Fakes.instance("Part", "UpperTorso", agent.character)
		agent.head = Fakes.instance("Part", "Head", agent.character)
		Fakes.instance("Tool", "Rifle", agent.character)
		agent.x, agent.y, agent.z, agent.vy = x, STAND, z, 0
		agent.sx, agent.sy, agent.sz = x, STAND, z
		fake.Physics:Set(agent.character, {
			X = x, Y = STAND, Z = z, VX = 0, VY = 0, VZ = 0,
			WalkSpeed = agent.walkSpeed, State = "Running", Ping = agent.ping,
		})
		placeTargets(agent)
	end

	-- Adds a player. role = "Honest" or one of Simulation.CHEATS.
	function sim.add(role: string, startAt: number?)
		local id = sim.nextId
		sim.nextId += 1
		local agent = {
			id = id,
			role = role,
			startAt = startAt or 60,
			player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(id, `{role}{id}`)),
			walkSpeed = if r.chance(0.25) then 24 else 16,
			ping = r.range(sim.pingMin, sim.pingMax),
			skill = r.range(0.2, 0.7),
			headRate = r.range(0.1, 0.4),
			earn = ({ "steady", "lumpy", "bursty", "gamepass", "none" })[1 + math.floor(r.u() * 5)],
			clicker = r.chance(0.4),
			clickMean = r.range(0.3, 1.5),
			clickCv = r.range(0.08, 0.3),
			heading = r.range(0, 2 * math.pi),
			speed = 0,
			nextDecision = 0,
			freeze = 0,
			firstEvidence = nil,
			firstRestrict = nil,
			maxBand = "Normal",
			kicked = false,
		}
		spawn(agent, r.range(-100, 100), r.range(-100, 100))
		if role == "Noclip" then
			spawn(agent, 125, r.range(-40, 40))
		end
		table.insert(sim.agents, agent)
		if agent.clicker or role == "AutoClicker" then
			sim.scheduleClicks(agent, r.range(1, 10))
		end
		return agent
	end

	local function cheating(agent): boolean
		return agent.role ~= "Honest" and sim.now >= agent.startAt
	end

	-- Clicks are events at exact (60 Hz frame) times, not tick-aligned.
	function sim.scheduleClicks(agent, at: number)
		schedule(at, function()
			if agent.kicked then
				return
			end
			timed(behavioral.RecordAction, behavioral, agent.player, "Click")
			local nextAt
			if agent.role == "AutoClicker" and cheating(agent) then
				nextAt = frame(at + 1 + r.normal(0, 0.003))
			elseif r.chance(0.03) then
				nextAt = frame(at + r.range(5, 30)) -- pause between bursts
			else
				nextAt = frame(at + math.max(0.08, r.normal(agent.clickMean, agent.clickMean * agent.clickCv)))
			end
			sim.scheduleClicks(agent, math.max(nextAt, at + 1 / 60))
		end)
	end

	local function nearestTarget(agent)
		local best, bestDistance = nil, math.huge
		for _, other in sim.agents do
			if other ~= agent and not other.kicked and math.abs(other.sx) <= BOUNDS + 5 and math.abs(other.sz) <= BOUNDS + 5 then
				local d = math.sqrt((other.sx - agent.sx) ^ 2 + (other.sz - agent.sz) ^ 2)
				if d < bestDistance and d < 250 then
					best, bestDistance = other, d
				end
			end
		end
		return best
	end

	local function shoot(agent, hitChance: number, headChance: number)
		local origin = Fakes.vector3(agent.sx, agent.sy + 1.5, agent.sz)
		local claim = { Origin = origin }
		local target = nearestTarget(agent)
		if target and r.chance(hitChance) then
			if r.chance(headChance) then
				claim.Target = target.head
				claim.HitPosition = Fakes.vector3(target.sx, target.sy + 1.5, target.sz)
			else
				claim.Target = target.torso
				claim.HitPosition = Fakes.vector3(target.sx, target.sy, target.sz)
			end
		end
		timed(combat.ValidateAttack, combat, agent.player, "Rifle", claim)
	end

	local function move(agent)
		local cheat = cheating(agent) and agent.role or nil
		if agent.role == "Noclip" then
			-- Paces along x in front of the wall (x 150..152); once cheating, through it.
			local far = if cheat then 166 else 146
			agent.heading = if agent.x >= far then math.pi elseif agent.x <= 120 then 0 else agent.heading
			if agent.heading ~= 0 and agent.heading ~= math.pi then
				agent.heading = 0
			end
			agent.speed = 14
		elseif sim.now >= agent.nextDecision then
			agent.nextDecision = sim.now + r.range(1, 4)
			agent.heading = r.range(0, 2 * math.pi)
			agent.speed = if r.chance(0.2) then 0 else agent.walkSpeed * r.range(0.6, 0.95)
		end
		if agent.role ~= "Noclip" and (math.abs(agent.x) > BOUNDS or math.abs(agent.z) > BOUNDS) then
			agent.heading = math.atan2(-agent.z, -agent.x)
		end
		local speed = agent.speed
		if cheat == "Speed" then
			speed = agent.walkSpeed * 2.5
		end
		local vx, vz = math.cos(agent.heading) * speed, math.sin(agent.heading) * speed
		agent.x += vx * DT
		agent.z += vz * DT

		if cheat == "Teleport" and math.floor(sim.now / DT) % 15 == 0 then
			local angle = math.atan2(-agent.z, -agent.x) + r.range(-0.5, 0.5)
			agent.x += math.cos(angle) * 80
			agent.z += math.sin(angle) * 80
		end

		if cheat == "Fly" then
			agent.y, agent.vy = 40, 0
		else
			if agent.y <= STAND + 1e-6 and r.chance(0.03) then
				agent.vy = 50
			end
			agent.y += agent.vy * DT - 0.5 * GRAVITY * DT * DT
			agent.vy -= GRAVITY * DT
			if agent.y <= STAND then
				agent.y, agent.vy = STAND, 0
			end
		end

		-- Network effects (honest and cheaters alike).
		if agent.freeze > 0 then
			agent.freeze -= 1
			return
		end
		if r.chance(0.002) then
			agent.freeze = math.floor(r.range(3, 15))
			return
		end
		if agent.ping >= 0.25 and r.chance(0.25) then
			return
		end
		local airborne = agent.y > STAND + 0.01
		agent.sx, agent.sy, agent.sz = agent.x, agent.y, agent.z
		fake.Physics:Set(agent.character, {
			X = agent.x, Y = agent.y, Z = agent.z,
			VX = vx, VY = agent.vy, VZ = vz,
			State = if airborne then (if agent.vy > 0 then "Jumping" else "Freefall") else "Running",
			Ping = math.max(0.02, agent.ping + r.normal(0, 0.03)),
		})
		placeTargets(agent)
	end

	local function act(agent)
		local cheat = cheating(agent) and agent.role or nil
		-- Server events: respawn, authorised teleport.
		if r.chance(0.0005) then
			if agent.role == "Noclip" then
				-- Respawn in front of the wall (z within its extent), or it has nothing to pass through.
				spawn(agent, 140, r.range(-40, 40))
			else
				spawn(agent, r.range(-100, 100), r.range(-100, 100))
			end
			return
		end
		if r.chance(0.0005) and agent.role ~= "Noclip" then
			timed(movement.AuthorizeTeleport, movement, agent.player)
			agent.x, agent.z = r.range(-100, 100), r.range(-100, 100)
		end

		-- Combat.
		if cheat == "Aimbot" then
			if r.chance(0.4) then
				shoot(agent, 0.99, 0.9)
			end
		elseif cheat == "RapidFire" then
			for _ = 1, 3 do
				shoot(agent, 1, 0.2)
			end
		elseif r.chance(0.3) then
			shoot(agent, agent.skill, agent.headRate)
		end

		-- Economy.
		local amount = 0
		if cheat == "Duper" then
			amount = 50 * DT
		elseif agent.earn == "steady" then
			amount = math.max(0, r.normal(5, 1.5)) * DT
		elseif agent.earn == "gamepass" then
			amount = math.max(0, r.normal(10, 3)) * DT
		elseif agent.earn == "lumpy" then
			amount = if math.floor(sim.now / DT) % 100 == agent.id % 100 then 100 else 0
		elseif agent.earn == "bursty" then
			amount = if r.chance(0.3) then math.max(0, r.normal(16, 5)) * DT else 0
		end
		if amount > 0 then
			timed(behavioral.RecordSignal, behavioral, agent.player, "Coins", amount)
		end

		-- Shop remote.
		if cheat == "RemoteSpam" then
			for _ = 1, 5 do
				timed(Fakes.fire, buy, agent.player, string.rep("x", 100), -1)
			end
		elseif r.chance(0.04) then
			timed(Fakes.fire, buy, agent.player, "Sword", 1)
			timed(behavioral.RecordAction, behavioral, agent.player, "Buy", not r.chance(0.05))
		end
	end

	local function observe(agent)
		if agent.firstEvidence == nil then
			local expected = Simulation.EXPECTED[agent.role]
			for _, record in framework:GetEvidence(agent.player) do
				if expected == nil or expected[record.Category] then
					agent.firstEvidence = sim.now
					agent.firstKind = record.Kind
					break
				end
			end
		end
		local risk = framework:GetRisk(agent.player)
		if BAND_INDEX[risk.Band] > BAND_INDEX[agent.maxBand] then
			agent.maxBand = risk.Band
		end
		if agent.firstRestrict == nil and BAND_INDEX[risk.Band] >= BAND_INDEX.Restrict then
			agent.firstRestrict = sim.now
		end
	end

	-- Advances the world by `seconds`.
	function sim.run(seconds: number)
		local finish = sim.now + seconds
		while sim.now < finish - 1e-9 do
			local tickEnd = sim.now + DT
			table.sort(sim.events, function(a, b)
				return a.at < b.at
			end)
			while #sim.events > 0 and sim.events[1].at < tickEnd do
				local event = table.remove(sim.events, 1)
				if event.at > sim.now then
					fake.Clock.Advance(event.at - sim.now)
					sim.now = event.at
				end
				event.fn()
			end
			fake.Clock.Advance(tickEnd - sim.now)
			sim.now = tickEnd
			for _, agent in sim.agents do
				if not agent.kicked then
					move(agent)
					act(agent)
				end
			end
			local started = os.clock()
			framework:Step()
			local elapsed = os.clock() - started
			sim.stepSeconds += elapsed
			sim.acfSeconds += elapsed
			sim.steps += 1
			if sim.steps % 5 == 0 then
				for _, agent in sim.agents do
					if not agent.kicked then
						observe(agent)
					end
				end
			end
			for _, agent in sim.agents do
				if not agent.kicked and fake.Players:GetPlayers() and not table.find(fake.Players:GetPlayers(), agent.player) then
					agent.kicked = true
					agent.kickedAt = sim.now
				end
			end
		end
	end

	-- Honest players with any evidence / any enforcement, plus totals.
	function sim.honestReport()
		local report = { Players = 0, WithEvidence = 0, Records = 0, Kinds = {}, MaxBand = "Normal", Kicked = 0, Restricted = 0 }
		for _, agent in sim.agents do
			if agent.role == "Honest" then
				report.Players += 1
				local records = framework:GetEvidence(agent.player)
				if #records > 0 then
					report.WithEvidence += 1
				end
				for _, record in records do
					report.Records += 1
					report.Kinds[record.Kind] = (report.Kinds[record.Kind] or 0) + record.Count
				end
				if BAND_INDEX[agent.maxBand] > BAND_INDEX[report.MaxBand] then
					report.MaxBand = agent.maxBand
				end
				if agent.kicked then
					report.Kicked += 1
				end
				if BAND_INDEX[agent.maxBand] >= BAND_INDEX.Restrict then
					report.Restricted += 1
				end
			end
		end
		return report
	end

	return sim
end

return Simulation

```

`tests/Specs/TestKit.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Minimal dependency-free test framework that runs identically in Lune and Roblox.

	Spec modules return function(t) and use:
		t.describe(name, fn)   t.it(name, fn)   t.beforeEach(fn)   t.afterEach(fn)
		t.expect(value).toBe(x) / toEqual(x) / toBeNil() / toBeTruthy() / toBeFalsy()
		  / toBeGreaterThan(n) / toBeLessThan(n) / toBeCloseTo(n, eps?) / toContain(x)
		  / toHaveLength(n) / toThrow(substring?) / never.toBe(x) / never.toThrow()
]]

local TestKit = {}

local function render(value: any, depth: number?): string
	local level = depth or 0
	if type(value) == "string" then
		return string.format("%q", value)
	end
	if type(value) ~= "table" or level > 2 then
		return tostring(value)
	end
	local parts = {}
	local count = 0
	for k, v in value do
		count += 1
		if count > 8 then
			table.insert(parts, "...")
			break
		end
		table.insert(parts, `{tostring(k)}={render(v, level + 1)}`)
	end
	return "{" .. table.concat(parts, ", ") .. "}"
end

local function deepEqual(a: any, b: any, seen: { [any]: any }?): boolean
	if a == b then
		return true
	end
	if type(a) ~= "table" or type(b) ~= "table" then
		return false
	end
	local visited = seen or {}
	if visited[a] == b then
		return true
	end
	visited[a] = b
	for k, v in a do
		if not deepEqual(v, b[k], visited) then
			return false
		end
	end
	for k in b do
		if a[k] == nil then
			return false
		end
	end
	return true
end

local function fail(message: string)
	error({ __testFailure = true, message = message }, 0)
end

local function makeExpect(actual: any, negate: boolean)
	local function check(passed: boolean, message: string)
		if negate then
			passed = not passed
			message = "NOT: " .. message
		end
		if not passed then
			fail(message)
		end
	end

	local matchers = {}

	function matchers.toBe(expected: any)
		check(actual == expected, `expected {render(actual)} to be {render(expected)}`)
	end

	function matchers.toEqual(expected: any)
		check(deepEqual(actual, expected), `expected {render(actual)} to deep-equal {render(expected)}`)
	end

	function matchers.toBeNil()
		check(actual == nil, `expected {render(actual)} to be nil`)
	end

	function matchers.toBeTruthy()
		check(not not actual, `expected {render(actual)} to be truthy`)
	end

	function matchers.toBeFalsy()
		check(not actual, `expected {render(actual)} to be falsy`)
	end

	function matchers.toBeGreaterThan(n: number)
		check(type(actual) == "number" and actual > n, `expected {render(actual)} > {n}`)
	end

	function matchers.toBeLessThan(n: number)
		check(type(actual) == "number" and actual < n, `expected {render(actual)} < {n}`)
	end

	function matchers.toBeCloseTo(n: number, epsilon: number?)
		local eps = epsilon or 1e-6
		check(type(actual) == "number" and math.abs(actual - n) <= eps, `expected {render(actual)} to be within {eps} of {n}`)
	end

	function matchers.toContain(item: any)
		local found = false
		if type(actual) == "string" then
			found = string.find(actual, tostring(item), 1, true) ~= nil
		elseif type(actual) == "table" then
			for _, v in actual do
				if v == item then
					found = true
					break
				end
			end
		end
		check(found, `expected {render(actual)} to contain {render(item)}`)
	end

	function matchers.toHaveLength(n: number)
		local length = if type(actual) == "table" or type(actual) == "string" then #actual else -1
		check(length == n, `expected length {n}, got {length}`)
	end

	function matchers.toThrow(substring: string?)
		if type(actual) ~= "function" then
			fail("toThrow expects a function")
		end
		local ok, err = pcall(actual)
		local message = if type(err) == "table" and err.message then err.message else tostring(err)
		if negate then
			check(not ok, `expected function to throw, but it raised: {message}`)
			return
		end
		if ok then
			fail("expected function to throw, but it did not")
		end
		if substring and not string.find(message, substring, 1, true) then
			fail(`expected error containing {render(substring)}, got {render(message)}`)
		end
	end

	return matchers
end

function TestKit.expect(actual: any)
	local matchers = makeExpect(actual, false)
	matchers.never = makeExpect(actual, true)
	return matchers
end

type TestNode = {
	name: string,
	fn: (() -> ())?,
	children: { TestNode },
	beforeEach: { () -> () },
	afterEach: { () -> () },
}

-- Collects tests from spec functions, then runs them. Returns a results table.
function TestKit.run(specs: { { name: string, spec: (any) -> () } }, options: { Filter: string?, Print: ((string) -> ())? }?)
	local opts = options or {}
	local out = opts.Print or print
	local results = { Total = 0, Passed = 0, Failed = 0, Failures = {} }

	for _, item in specs do
		local root: TestNode = { name = item.name, children = {}, beforeEach = {}, afterEach = {} }
		local stack = { root }
		local t = {
			expect = TestKit.expect,
			describe = function(name: string, fn: () -> ())
				local node: TestNode = { name = name, children = {}, beforeEach = {}, afterEach = {} }
				table.insert(stack[#stack].children, node)
				table.insert(stack, node)
				fn()
				table.remove(stack)
			end,
			it = function(name: string, fn: () -> ())
				table.insert(stack[#stack].children, { name = name, fn = fn, children = {}, beforeEach = {}, afterEach = {} })
			end,
			beforeEach = function(fn: () -> ())
				table.insert(stack[#stack].beforeEach, fn)
			end,
			afterEach = function(fn: () -> ())
				table.insert(stack[#stack].afterEach, fn)
			end,
		}

		local collected, collectErr = pcall(item.spec, t)
		if not collected then
			results.Total += 1
			results.Failed += 1
			table.insert(results.Failures, { Name = item.name .. " (collect)", Message = tostring(collectErr) })
		else
			local function walk(node: TestNode, path: { string }, befores: { () -> () }, afters: { () -> () })
				local myBefores = table.clone(befores)
				for _, fn in node.beforeEach do
					table.insert(myBefores, fn)
				end
				local myAfters = table.clone(node.afterEach)
				for _, fn in afters do
					table.insert(myAfters, fn)
				end
				for _, child in node.children do
					local childPath = table.clone(path)
					table.insert(childPath, child.name)
					local testFn = child.fn
					if testFn then
						local fullName = table.concat(childPath, " > ")
						if opts.Filter == nil or string.find(fullName, opts.Filter, 1, true) then
							results.Total += 1
							local ok, err = xpcall(function()
								for _, before in myBefores do
									before()
								end
								testFn()
							end, function(e)
								if type(e) == "table" and e.__testFailure then
									return e.message
								end
								return debug.traceback(tostring(e), 2)
							end)
							for _, after in myAfters do
								local afterOk, afterErr = pcall(after)
								if ok and not afterOk then
									ok, err = false, "afterEach: " .. tostring(afterErr)
								end
							end
							if ok then
								results.Passed += 1
							else
								results.Failed += 1
								table.insert(results.Failures, { Name = fullName, Message = tostring(err) })
							end
						end
					else
						walk(child, childPath, myBefores, myAfters)
					end
				end
			end
			walk(root, { root.name }, {}, {})
		end
	end

	for _, failure in results.Failures do
		out(`FAIL  {failure.Name}\n      {string.gsub(failure.Message, "\n", "\n      ")}`)
	end
	out(`\n{results.Passed}/{results.Total} passed, {results.Failed} failed`)
	return results
end

return TestKit

```

`tests/Specs/Unit/BehavioralDetectors.spec.luau`:

```luau
local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local BehavioralDetectors = require(ACF.Detection.Behavioral.BehavioralDetectors)

local SIGNALS = {
	BucketSeconds = 10,
	SelfAlpha = 0.2,
	MinPlayerBuckets = 3,
	ActiveWithinBuckets = 6,
	MinContributors = 4,
	MinRelativeStdDev = 0.1,
	ZClamp = 3,
	CusumK = 0.5,
	CusumH = 6,
	MinRatio = 2,
	HardLimitTolerance = 0.1,
}

local ACCURACY = {
	CheckAccuracy = true,
	CheckHeadshots = true,
	ConfidenceZ = 3.09,
	MinShots = 40,
	AccuracyMargin = 0.35,
	AccuracyFloor = 0.9,
	AccuracyFloorWithoutBaseline = 0.95,
	MinPopulationShots = 200,
	MinHits = 20,
	HeadshotMargin = 0.4,
	HeadshotFloor = 0.75,
	HeadshotFloorWithoutBaseline = 0.9,
	MinPopulationHits = 100,
}

local ACTIONS = {
	CheckTiming = true,
	WindowSize = 30,
	MaxCV = 0.02,
	MinMeanIntervalSeconds = 0.1,
	MaxMeanIntervalSeconds = 10,
	MaxGapSeconds = 30,
	InvalidHalfLifeSeconds = 60,
	MinInvalid = 20,
	MaxInvalidRatio = 0.8,
	InvalidConfidenceZ = 2.33,
}

local COINS = { Name = "Coins", MaxPerSecond = nil, Baseline = nil, MinStdDev = 0, Statistical = true }

-- A player whose EWMA has been fed `rates` (one per bucket, starting at t = 10).
local function playerWith(rates)
	local p = BehavioralDetectors.newSignalPlayer()
	BehavioralDetectors.closeBucket(p, 0, SIGNALS) -- discards the partial first bucket
	for i, rate in rates do
		BehavioralDetectors.recordSignal(p, rate * 10)
		BehavioralDetectors.closeBucket(p, i * 10, SIGNALS)
	end
	return p
end

local function kinds(violations)
	local list = {}
	for _, v in violations do
		table.insert(list, v.Kind)
	end
	return list
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("BehavioralDetectors: signals", function()
		t.it("discards the first partial bucket and counts empty buckets afterwards", function()
			local p = BehavioralDetectors.newSignalPlayer()
			BehavioralDetectors.recordSignal(p, 500)
			expect(BehavioralDetectors.closeBucket(p, 3, SIGNALS)).toBeNil()
			BehavioralDetectors.recordSignal(p, 50)
			expect(BehavioralDetectors.closeBucket(p, 13, SIGNALS)).toBe(5)
			expect(BehavioralDetectors.closeBucket(p, 23, SIGNALS)).toBe(0)
			expect(p.ActiveBuckets).toBe(1)
			expect(p.BucketsSinceActive).toBe(1)
			expect(p.Self.Count).toBe(2)
		end)

		t.it("keeps accumulating when a bucket is shorter than half the bucket length", function()
			local p = BehavioralDetectors.newSignalPlayer()
			BehavioralDetectors.closeBucket(p, 0, SIGNALS)
			BehavioralDetectors.recordSignal(p, 40)
			expect(BehavioralDetectors.closeBucket(p, 4, SIGNALS)).toBeNil()
			expect(BehavioralDetectors.closeBucket(p, 8, SIGNALS)).toBe(5)
		end)

		t.it("lumpy and steady earners at the same average have the same mean", function()
			local steady = playerWith(table.create(40, 5))
			local lumps = {}
			for i = 1, 40 do
				lumps[i] = if i % 2 == 0 then 10 else 0
			end
			local lumpy = playerWith(lumps)
			expect(math.abs(steady.Self.Mean - lumpy.Self.Mean)).toBeLessThan(1.2)
		end)

		t.it("population baseline is the median player and ignores one extreme player", function()
			local players = {
				playerWith({ 5, 5, 5, 5 }),
				playerWith({ 6, 6, 6, 6 }),
				playerWith({ 4, 4, 4, 4 }),
				playerWith({ 5, 5, 5, 5 }),
				playerWith({ 500, 500, 500, 500 }),
			}
			local baseline = BehavioralDetectors.populationBaseline(players, SIGNALS, 0)
			expect(baseline ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(baseline.Mean).toBe(5)
			expect(baseline.Players).toBe(5)
			expect(baseline.Scale < 2).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("no population baseline below MinContributors or for inactive players", function()
			local few = { playerWith({ 5, 5, 5 }), playerWith({ 5, 5, 5 }), playerWith({ 5, 5, 5 }) }
			expect(BehavioralDetectors.populationBaseline(few, SIGNALS, 0)).toBeNil()
			local idle = playerWith({ 5, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0 })
			expect(BehavioralDetectors.recentlyActive(idle, SIGNALS)).toBe(false)
			table.insert(few, idle)
			expect(BehavioralDetectors.populationBaseline(few, SIGNALS, 0)).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("a single extreme bucket cannot raise a SignalAnomaly", function()
			local p = playerWith({ 5, 5, 5 })
			local baseline = { Mean = 5, Scale = 1, Players = 5, Source = "Population" }
			local violations = BehavioralDetectors.evaluateSignal(COINS, p, 1e6, baseline, SIGNALS)
			expect(#violations).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("a sustained excess raises SignalAnomaly with an explanation", function()
			local p = playerWith({ 5, 5, 5 })
			local baseline = { Mean = 5, Scale = 1, Players = 5, Source = "Population" }
			local found = nil
			for i = 1, 6 do
				BehavioralDetectors.recordSignal(p, 500)
				local rate = BehavioralDetectors.closeBucket(p, 30 + i * 10, SIGNALS)
				local violations = BehavioralDetectors.evaluateSignal(COINS, p, rate, baseline, SIGNALS)
				if #violations > 0 then
					found = violations[1]
					expect(i >= 3).toBe(true)
					break
				end
			end
			expect(found ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(found.Kind).toBe("SignalAnomaly")
			expect(found.Details.Signal).toBe("Coins")
			expect(found.Details.Ratio > 2).toBe(true)
			expect(found.Details.BaselineSource).toBe("Population")
		end)

		t.it("a statistically unusual but practically small excess is not reported (MinRatio)", function()
			local p = playerWith({ 5, 5, 5 })
			-- Tiny scale makes every bucket z = 3, but 7/5 is below MinRatio 2.
			local baseline = { Mean = 5, Scale = 0.01, Players = 5, Source = "Population" }
			for i = 1, 20 do
				BehavioralDetectors.recordSignal(p, 70)
				local rate = BehavioralDetectors.closeBucket(p, 30 + i * 10, SIGNALS)
				expect(#BehavioralDetectors.evaluateSignal(COINS, p, rate, baseline, SIGNALS)).toBe(0)
			end
		end)

		t.it("hard limit reports with weight scaled by the excess, and Statistical = false skips CUSUM", function()
			local definition = { Name = "Gems", MaxPerSecond = 2, Baseline = nil, MinStdDev = 0, Statistical = false }
			local p = playerWith({ 1 })
			expect(#BehavioralDetectors.evaluateSignal(definition, p, 2.1, nil, SIGNALS)).toBe(0) -- within tolerance
			local v = BehavioralDetectors.evaluateSignal(definition, p, 3, nil, SIGNALS)
			expect(kinds(v)).toEqual({ "SignalRateLimit" })
			expect(v[1].Weight).toBe(1)
			expect(BehavioralDetectors.evaluateSignal(definition, p, 100, nil, SIGNALS)[1].Weight).toBe(2)
			local baseline = { Mean = 0.1, Scale = 0.01, Players = 5, Source = "Population" }
			for _ = 1, 10 do
				expect(kinds(BehavioralDetectors.evaluateSignal(definition, p, 1.5, baseline, SIGNALS))).toEqual({})
			end
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("BehavioralDetectors: accuracy", function()
		local function counts(shots, hits, heads)
			return { Shots = shots, Hits = hits, Heads = heads, Updated = 0 }
		end

		t.it("needs MinShots before judging accuracy", function()
			local v = BehavioralDetectors.evaluateAccuracy("Rifle", counts(39, 39, 0), counts(1000, 300, 30), ACCURACY)
			expect(#v).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("flags near-perfect accuracy over many shots against the server's ratio", function()
			local player = counts(300, 295, 0)
			local population = counts(300 + 2000, 295 + 700, 0)
			local v = BehavioralDetectors.evaluateAccuracy("Rifle", player, population, ACCURACY)
			expect(kinds(v)).toEqual({ "AccuracyAnomaly" })
			expect(v[1].Details.Basis).toBe("Population")
			expect(v[1].Details.PopulationRatio).toBe(0.35) -- the player's own shots are excluded
		end)

		t.it("does not flag a strong player (80% over 500 shots)", function()
			local v = BehavioralDetectors.evaluateAccuracy("Rifle", counts(500, 400, 0), counts(3000, 1300, 0), ACCURACY)
			expect(#v).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("does not flag a short perfect streak", function()
			local v = BehavioralDetectors.evaluateAccuracy("Rifle", counts(60, 60, 0), counts(3000, 1100, 0), ACCURACY)
			expect(#v).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("uses the absolute floor without enough population shots", function()
			local v = BehavioralDetectors.evaluateAccuracy("Rifle", counts(300, 280, 0), counts(350, 290, 0), ACCURACY)
			expect(#v).toBe(0) -- 93% is below the 95% no-baseline floor
			local v2 = BehavioralDetectors.evaluateAccuracy("Rifle", counts(400, 400, 0), counts(450, 410, 0), ACCURACY)
			expect(kinds(v2)).toEqual({ "AccuracyAnomaly" })
			expect(v2[1].Details.Basis).toBe("Absolute")
		end)

		t.it("flags headshot ratios far above the server's", function()
			local v = BehavioralDetectors.evaluateAccuracy("Rifle", counts(200, 120, 115), counts(2200, 920, 235), ACCURACY)
			expect(kinds(v)).toEqual({ "HeadshotAnomaly" })
			expect(v[1].Details.Headshots).toBe(115)
		end)

		t.it("headshot-heavy but plausible play is not flagged (55% of 200 hits)", function()
			local v = BehavioralDetectors.evaluateAccuracy("Rifle", counts(400, 200, 110), counts(3000, 1200, 300), ACCURACY)
			expect(#v).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("ageing halves counts per half-life", function()
			local c = counts(100, 50, 10)
			BehavioralDetectors.ageCounts(c, 600, 600)
			expect(c.Shots).toBeCloseTo(50, 6)
			expect(c.Hits).toBeCloseTo(25, 6)
			expect(c.Updated).toBe(600)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("BehavioralDetectors: actions", function()
		local function run(intervals, valid)
			local state = BehavioralDetectors.newActionState(0)
			local now = 0
			local found = {}
			BehavioralDetectors.recordAction(state, "Click", valid ~= false, now, ACTIONS)
			for _, interval in intervals do
				now += interval
				local v = BehavioralDetectors.recordAction(state, "Click", valid ~= false, now, ACTIONS)
				if v then
					table.insert(found, v)
				end
			end
			return found, state
		end

		t.it("flags machine-regular timing once per full window", function()
			local intervals = {}
			for i = 1, 60 do
				intervals[i] = 0.5 + (if i % 2 == 0 then 0.002 else -0.002)
			end
			local found = run(intervals)
			expect(#found).toBe(2)
			expect(found[1].Kind).toBe("RegularTiming")
			expect(found[1].Details.CV < 0.02).toBe(true)
			expect(found[1].Details.Samples).toBe(30)
		end)

		t.it("does not flag rhythmic human timing (CV 5%)", function()
			local intervals = {}
			for i = 1, 90 do
				intervals[i] = 0.5 * (1 + 0.05 * math.sin(i * 1.7) * 1.4)
			end
			local found = run(intervals)
			expect(#found).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("a long gap starts a new run", function()
			local intervals = table.create(20, 1)
			table.insert(intervals, 60)
			for _ = 1, 20 do
				table.insert(intervals, 1)
			end
			local found = run(intervals)
			expect(#found).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("ignores very fast and very slow regular actions outside the configured range", function()
			expect(#run(table.create(60, 0.05))).toBe(0) -- 20 per second: below MinMeanInterval
			expect(#run(table.create(60, 20))).toBe(0) -- every 20 s: above MaxMeanInterval
		end)

		t.it("invalid ratio needs volume and a high lower bound", function()
			local _, state = run(table.create(10, 1), false) -- 11 invalid
			expect(BehavioralDetectors.evaluateInvalid(state, "Buy", 10, ACTIONS)).toBeNil()
			local _, flood = run(table.create(40, 0.5), false)
			local v = BehavioralDetectors.evaluateInvalid(flood, "Buy", 20, ACTIONS)
			expect(v ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(v.Kind).toBe("RepeatedInvalidActions")
			-- Mostly valid traffic with some invalid actions is fine.
			local mixed = BehavioralDetectors.newActionState(0)
			for i = 1, 100 do
				BehavioralDetectors.recordAction(mixed, "Buy", i % 3 ~= 0, i * 0.3, ACTIONS)
			end
			expect(BehavioralDetectors.evaluateInvalid(mixed, "Buy", 30, ACTIONS)).toBeNil()
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/Unit/CombatRules.spec.luau`:

```luau
local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local CombatRules = require(ACF.Detection.Combat.CombatRules)

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("CombatRules", function()
		t.it("normalizes a hitscan definition with defaults", function()
			local def = CombatRules.normalize("Rifle", { Kind = "Hitscan", Damage = 25, FireRate = 10, MaxRange = 300 })
			expect(def.HeadshotMultiplier).toBe(1)
			expect(def.Burst).toBe(1)
			expect(def.RequireLineOfSight).toBe(true)
			expect(def.RequireTarget).toBe(false)
			expect(def.Ammo).toBeNil()
			expect(table.isfrozen(def)).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("rejects invalid definitions with precise messages", function()
			local cases = {
				{ { Kind = "Laser", MaxRange = 1 }, "Kind must be" },
				{ { Kind = "Hitscan", MaxRange = 100 }, "FireRate is required" },
				{ { Kind = "Hitscan", FireRate = 5 }, "MaxRange is required" },
				{ { Kind = "Projectile", FireRate = 1, MaxRange = 100 }, "ProjectileSpeed is required" },
				{ { Kind = "Hitscan", FireRate = 1, MaxRange = 100, ProjectileSpeed = 5 }, "only applies" },
				{ { Kind = "Hitscan", FireRate = 1, MaxRange = 100, Damage = -1 }, "Damage must be" },
				{ { Kind = "Hitscan", FireRate = 1, MaxRange = 100, Dmg = 5 }, "unknown field 'Dmg'" },
				{ { Kind = "Hitscan", FireRate = 1, MaxRange = 100, Ammo = { Magazine = 0 } }, "Magazine" },
				{ { Kind = "Hitscan", FireRate = 1, MaxRange = 100, Falloff = { Start = 50, End = 10, MinMultiplier = 0.5 } }, "Falloff" },
				{ { Kind = "Hitscan", FireRate = 0 / 0, MaxRange = 100 }, "FireRate must be" },
			}
			for _, case in cases do
				expect(function()
					CombatRules.normalize("W", case[1])
				end).toThrow(case[2])
			end
		end)

		t.it("computes falloff and headshot damage", function()
			local def = CombatRules.normalize("Rifle", {
				Kind = "Hitscan",
				Damage = 40,
				FireRate = 5,
				MaxRange = 500,
				HeadshotMultiplier = 2,
				Falloff = { Start = 100, End = 300, MinMultiplier = 0.5 },
			})
			expect(CombatRules.damage(def, 50, false)).toBe(40)
			expect(CombatRules.damage(def, 200, false)).toBeCloseTo(30, 1e-9)
			expect(CombatRules.damage(def, 400, false)).toBe(20)
			expect(CombatRules.damage(def, 50, true)).toBe(80)
		end)

		t.it("models projectile motion with gravity", function()
			local x, y, z = CombatRules.projectilePosition(0, 10, 0, 1, 0, 0, 100, 20, 0.5)
			expect(x).toBe(50)
			expect(y).toBeCloseTo(10 - 0.5 * 20 * 0.25, 1e-9)
			expect(z).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("reads only finite Vector3 claims", function()
			local Fakes = require(game:GetService("ServerStorage").ACFTests.Fakes)
			expect((CombatRules.vector(Fakes.vector3(1, 2, 3)))).toBe(1)
			expect((CombatRules.vector(Fakes.vector3(0 / 0, 2, 3)))).toBeNil()
			expect((CombatRules.vector({ X = 1, Y = 2, Z = 3 }))).toBeNil()
		end)
	end)
end
```
