`tests/Specs/Unit/Configuration.spec.luau`:

```luau
local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Configuration = require(ACF.Core.Configuration)
local DefaultConfig = require(ACF.Core.DefaultConfig)

local S = Configuration.Schema

local function load(overrides)
	local config = Configuration.new(DefaultConfig())
	local ok, errors = config:Load(overrides)
	return config, ok, errors
end

local function joined(errors)
	return table.concat(errors, "\n")
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("Configuration", function()
		t.it("loads defaults with no overrides", function()
			local config, ok = load(nil)
			expect(ok).toBe(true)
			expect(config:Get("LogLevel")).toBe("Info")
			expect(config:Get("Evidence.MaxRecordsPerPlayer")).toBe(64)
			expect(config:Get("Enforcement.AutoBan")).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("applies valid overrides", function()
			local config, ok = load({ Debug = true, Evidence = { MaxRecordsPerPlayer = 10 }, Modules = { Movement = false } })
			expect(ok).toBe(true)
			expect(config:Get("Debug")).toBe(true)
			expect(config:Get("Evidence.MaxRecordsPerPlayer")).toBe(10)
			expect(config:Get("Evidence.RetentionSeconds")).toBe(1800)
			expect(config:Get("Modules").Movement).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("accepts the documented illustrative configuration", function()
			local _, ok, errors = load({
				Debug = false,
				Enforcement = { Mode = "Hybrid", AutoKick = true, AutoBan = false, ShadowMode = false },
				Modules = { Movement = true, Physics = true, RemoteSecurity = true, Combat = true, Behavioral = true },
			})
			expect(joined(errors)).toBe("")
			expect(ok).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("reports unknown keys with a suggestion", function()
			local _, ok, errors = load({ Enforcment = {} })
			expect(ok).toBe(false)
			expect(joined(errors)).toContain("Enforcment: unknown configuration key (did you mean 'Enforcement'?)")
		end)

		t.it("collects every error instead of stopping at the first", function()
			local _, ok, errors = load({
				LogLevel = "Verbose",
				Evidence = { MaxRecordsPerPlayer = 0, RetentionSeconds = "long" },
				Enforcement = { Mode = "hybrid" },
			})
			expect(ok).toBe(false)
			expect(#errors).toBe(4)
			local text = joined(errors)
			expect(text).toContain("LogLevel: expected one of")
			expect(text).toContain("Evidence.MaxRecordsPerPlayer: 0 is below the minimum of 1")
			expect(text).toContain("Evidence.RetentionSeconds: expected a finite number")
			expect(text).toContain("(did you mean 'Hybrid'?)")
		end)

		t.it("rejects NaN, infinity and non-integers where integers are required", function()
			local _, ok, errors = load({
				Evidence = { RetentionSeconds = 0 / 0, DuplicateWindowSeconds = math.huge, MaxRecordsPerPlayer = 2.5 },
			})
			expect(ok).toBe(false)
			expect(#errors).toBe(3)
		end)

		t.it("rejects a non-table configuration", function()
			local _, ok, errors = load("yes" :: any)
			expect(ok).toBe(false)
			expect(joined(errors)).toContain("must be a table")
		end)

		t.it("freezes resolved values", function()
			local config = load({})
			local evidence = config:Get("Evidence")
			expect(table.isfrozen(evidence)).toBe(true)
			expect(function()
				evidence.MaxRecordsPerPlayer = 1
			end).toThrow()
		end)

		t.it("is not mutated by later changes to the caller's table", function()
			local overrides = { ModuleSettings = { Mine = { Limit = 5 } } }
			local config = load(overrides)
			overrides.ModuleSettings.Mine.Limit = 999
			config:Extend("ModuleSettings", "Mine", S.Table({ Limit = S.Number(1) }))
			expect(config:Get("ModuleSettings.Mine.Limit")).toBe(5)
		end)

		t.it("validates module sections when the module extends them", function()
			local config, ok = load({ ModuleSettings = { Speed = { MaxSpeed = -1 } } })
			expect(ok).toBe(true)
			local extended, errors = config:Extend("ModuleSettings", "Speed", S.Table({ MaxSpeed = S.Number(16, { Min = 0 }) }))
			expect(extended).toBe(false)
			expect(joined(errors)).toContain("ModuleSettings.Speed.MaxSpeed")
		end)

		t.it("provides defaults for modules without user settings", function()
			local config = load({})
			local ok = config:Extend("ModuleSettings", "Speed", S.Table({ MaxSpeed = S.Number(16) }))
			expect(ok).toBe(true)
			expect(config:Get("ModuleSettings.Speed.MaxSpeed")).toBe(16)
			expect(config:Get("ModuleSettings").Speed.MaxSpeed).toBe(16)
		end)

		t.it("reports module settings no module claimed", function()
			local config = load({ ModuleSettings = { Sped = { MaxSpeed = 1 } } })
			expect(config:UnclaimedKeys()).toEqual({ "ModuleSettings.Sped" })
		end)

		t.it("refuses to load twice or claim a key twice", function()
			local config = load({})
			expect(function()
				config:Load({})
			end).toThrow("already been loaded")
			config:Extend("ModuleSettings", "A", S.Table({}))
			expect(function()
				config:Extend("ModuleSettings", "A", S.Table({}))
			end).toThrow("already been claimed")
		end)

		t.it("validates schema defaults at construction", function()
			expect(function()
				S.Number(5, { Max = 1 })
			end).toThrow("above Max")
			expect(function()
				S.Enum({ "A" }, "B")
			end).toThrow("one of its values")
		end)

		t.it("merges map entries over map defaults", function()
			local config = Configuration.new(S.Table({ Flags = S.Map(S.Boolean(false), { A = true }) }))
			local ok = config:Load({ Flags = { B = true } })
			expect(ok).toBe(true)
			expect(config:Get("Flags")).toEqual({ A = true, B = true })
		end)

		t.it("rejects cyclic tables in open sections without hanging", function()
			local cyclic = {}
			cyclic.self = cyclic
			local _, ok, errors = load({ ModuleSettings = { X = cyclic } })
			expect(ok).toBe(false)
			expect(joined(errors)).toContain("too deep")
		end)
	end)
end
```

`tests/Specs/Unit/CorePrimitives.spec.luau`:

```luau
local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Cleanup = require(ACF.Core.Cleanup)
local EventBus = require(ACF.Core.EventBus)
local Logger = require(ACF.Core.Logger)
local RateLimiter = require(ACF.Core.RateLimiter)
local Scheduler = require(ACF.Core.Scheduler)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local function quietLogger(clock)
	local entries = {}
	local logger = Logger.new({
		Level = "Debug",
		Now = clock.Now,
		Wall = clock.Wall,
		Sink = function(entry)
			table.insert(entries, entry)
		end,
	})
	return logger, entries
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("RateLimiter", function()
		t.it("allows a burst then refills over time", function()
			local clock = Fakes.clock()
			local limiter = RateLimiter.new(3, 1, clock.Now)
			expect(limiter:Allow("a")).toBe(true)
			expect(limiter:Allow("a")).toBe(true)
			expect(limiter:Allow("a")).toBe(true)
			expect(limiter:Allow("a")).toBe(false)
			clock.Advance(1)
			expect(limiter:Allow("a")).toBe(true)
			expect(limiter:Allow("a")).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("tracks keys independently", function()
			local clock = Fakes.clock()
			local limiter = RateLimiter.new(1, 1, clock.Now)
			expect(limiter:Allow("a")).toBe(true)
			expect(limiter:Allow("b")).toBe(true)
			expect(limiter:Allow("a")).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("keeps key storage bounded", function()
			local clock = Fakes.clock()
			local limiter = RateLimiter.new(2, 1, clock.Now, 8)
			for i = 1, 100 do
				limiter:Allow("k" .. i)
				limiter:Allow("k" .. i)
			end
			expect(limiter:GetStats().Keys <= 8).toBe(true)
			expect(limiter:GetStats().Overflows).toBeGreaterThan(0)
		end)

		t.it("prunes fully refilled buckets before overflowing", function()
			local clock = Fakes.clock()
			local limiter = RateLimiter.new(1, 10, clock.Now, 4)
			for i = 1, 4 do
				limiter:Allow("k" .. i)
			end
			clock.Advance(1)
			limiter:Allow("new")
			expect(limiter:GetStats().Overflows).toBe(0)
			expect(limiter:GetStats().Keys).toBe(1)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Cleanup", function()
		t.it("runs tasks in reverse order", function()
			local order = {}
			local bag = Cleanup.new()
			bag:Add(function()
				table.insert(order, 1)
			end)
			bag:Add(function()
				table.insert(order, 2)
			end)
			bag:Clean()
			expect(order).toEqual({ 2, 1 })
			expect(bag:Count()).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("disconnects connection-like and destroys destroyable tables", function()
			local signal = Fakes.Signal.new()
			local bag = Cleanup.new()
			bag:Add(signal:Connect(function() end))
			local destroyed = false
			bag:Add({
				Destroy = function()
					destroyed = true
				end,
			})
			bag:Clean()
			expect(signal:ConnectionCount()).toBe(0)
			expect(destroyed).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("continues after a failing task and reports it", function()
			local errors = {}
			local ran = false
			local bag = Cleanup.new(function(message)
				table.insert(errors, message)
			end)
			bag:Add(function()
				ran = true
			end)
			bag:Add(function()
				error("boom")
			end)
			expect(bag:Clean()).toBe(1)
			expect(ran).toBe(true)
			expect(#errors).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("rejects unsupported values", function()
			expect(function()
				Cleanup.new():Add(42)
			end).toThrow("unsupported")
		end)

		t.it("cancels pending threads", function()
			local bag = Cleanup.new()
			local thread = coroutine.create(function()
				coroutine.yield()
			end)
			coroutine.resume(thread)
			bag:Add(thread)
			expect(bag:Clean()).toBe(0)
			expect(coroutine.status(thread)).toBe("dead")
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Logger", function()
		t.it("filters by level", function()
			local clock = Fakes.clock()
			local logger, entries = quietLogger(clock)
			logger:SetLevel("Warn")
			logger:Info("hidden")
			logger:Warn("shown")
			expect(#entries).toBe(1)
			expect(entries[1].Message).toBe("shown")
		end)

		t.it("scopes entries and freezes fields", function()
			local clock = Fakes.clock()
			local logger, entries = quietLogger(clock)
			local fields = { A = 1 }
			logger:Scope("Movement"):Info("sample", fields)
			fields.A = 2
			expect(entries[1].Scope).toBe("Movement")
			expect(entries[1].Fields.A).toBe(1)
			expect(table.isfrozen(entries[1].Fields)).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("rate limits identical messages and reports the suppressed count", function()
			local clock = Fakes.clock()
			local entries = {}
			local logger = Logger.new({
				Now = clock.Now,
				RateLimitBurst = 2,
				RateLimitWindowSeconds = 10,
				Sink = function(entry)
					table.insert(entries, entry)
				end,
			})
			for _ = 1, 50 do
				logger:Warn("flood")
			end
			expect(#entries).toBe(2)
			expect(logger:GetStats().Suppressed).toBe(48)
			clock.Advance(10)
			logger:Warn("flood")
			expect(entries[3].Suppressed).toBe(48)
		end)

		t.it("keeps a bounded ring buffer readable oldest-first", function()
			local clock = Fakes.clock()
			local logger = Logger.new({ Now = clock.Now, BufferSize = 16, RateLimitBurst = 1000, Sink = function() end })
			for i = 1, 40 do
				logger:Info("entry " .. i)
			end
			local recent = logger:GetRecent()
			expect(#recent).toBe(16)
			expect(recent[1].Message).toBe("entry 25")
			expect(recent[16].Message).toBe("entry 40")
			local lastThree = logger:GetRecent(3)
			expect(lastThree[1].Message).toBe("entry 38")
		end)

		t.it("survives a failing sink", function()
			local clock = Fakes.clock()
			local logger = Logger.new({
				Now = clock.Now,
				Sink = function()
					error("sink down")
				end,
			})
			expect(function()
				logger:Error("still fine")
			end).never.toThrow()
			expect(logger:GetStats().SinkFailures).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("formats entries deterministically", function()
			local line = Logger.Format({
				Time = 0,
				WallTime = 0,
				Level = "Warn",
				Scope = "Core",
				Message = "msg",
				Fields = { B = 2, A = "x" },
			})
			expect(line).toBe('[ACF][Warn][Core] msg A="x" B=2')
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("EventBus", function()
		local clock, bus, entries
		t.beforeEach(function()
			clock = Fakes.clock()
			local logger
			logger, entries = quietLogger(clock)
			bus = EventBus.new({ Logger = logger, MaxHandlerFailures = 3, MaxDispatchDepth = 4 })
			bus:RegisterTopic("T")
		end)

		t.it("delivers to subscribers in order", function()
			local seen = {}
			bus:Subscribe("T", function(p)
				table.insert(seen, "a" .. p)
			end)
			bus:Subscribe("T", function(p)
				table.insert(seen, "b" .. p)
			end)
			expect(bus:Publish("T", 1)).toBe(2)
			expect(seen).toEqual({ "a1", "b1" })
		end)

		t.it("rejects unknown topics in strict mode", function()
			expect(function()
				bus:Publish("Nope", nil)
			end).toThrow("unknown topic")
			expect(function()
				bus:Subscribe("Nope", function() end)
			end).toThrow("unknown topic")
		end)

		t.it("isolates handler errors", function()
			local reached = false
			bus:Subscribe("T", function()
				error("bad handler")
			end)
			bus:Subscribe("T", function()
				reached = true
			end)
			expect(bus:Publish("T")).toBe(1)
			expect(reached).toBe(true)
			expect(#Fakes.findLogs(entries, "Event handler failed", "Error")).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("disconnects a handler after repeated consecutive failures", function()
			local sub = bus:Subscribe("T", function()
				error("always")
			end, "Broken")
			for _ = 1, 3 do
				bus:Publish("T")
			end
			expect(sub.Connected).toBe(false)
			expect(bus:SubscriberCount("T")).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("supports disconnecting during dispatch", function()
			local secondCalls = 0
			local second
			bus:Subscribe("T", function()
				second:Disconnect()
			end)
			second = bus:Subscribe("T", function()
				secondCalls += 1
			end)
			bus:Publish("T")
			bus:Publish("T")
			expect(secondCalls).toBe(0)
			expect(bus:SubscriberCount("T")).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("stops runaway recursive publishing at the depth limit", function()
			local calls = 0
			bus:Subscribe("T", function()
				calls += 1
				bus:Publish("T")
			end)
			bus:Publish("T")
			expect(calls).toBe(4)
			expect(#Fakes.findLogs(entries, "Dispatch depth exceeded")).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("delivers nothing after Destroy", function()
			local calls = 0
			bus:Subscribe("T", function()
				calls += 1
			end)
			bus:Destroy()
			expect(bus:Publish("T")).toBe(0)
			expect(calls).toBe(0)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Scheduler", function()
		local clock, scheduler, entries
		t.beforeEach(function()
			clock = Fakes.clock()
			local logger
			logger, entries = quietLogger(clock)
			scheduler = Scheduler.new({ Now = clock.Now, Logger = logger, MaxConsecutiveFailures = 3 })
		end)

		t.it("runs interval tasks when due and passes dt", function()
			local runs, lastDt = 0, nil
			scheduler:Every("tick", 1, function(_, dt)
				runs += 1
				lastDt = dt
			end)
			scheduler:Step()
			expect(runs).toBe(0)
			clock.Advance(1)
			scheduler:Step()
			expect(runs).toBe(1)
			expect(lastDt).toBe(1)
			clock.Advance(0.5)
			scheduler:Step()
			expect(runs).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("skips missed runs after a stall instead of bursting", function()
			local runs = 0
			scheduler:Every("tick", 1, function()
				runs += 1
			end)
			clock.Advance(10)
			scheduler:Step()
			scheduler:Step()
			expect(runs).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("runs one-shot tasks once", function()
			local runs = 0
			scheduler:After("once", 2, function()
				runs += 1
			end)
			clock.Advance(2)
			scheduler:Step()
			clock.Advance(2)
			scheduler:Step()
			expect(runs).toBe(1)
			expect(scheduler:ActiveCount()).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("cancels tasks via handle", function()
			local runs = 0
			local handle = scheduler:Every("tick", 1, function()
				runs += 1
			end)
			handle:Cancel()
			clock.Advance(5)
			scheduler:Step()
			expect(runs).toBe(0)
			expect(handle:IsActive()).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("disables a task after consecutive failures and keeps others running", function()
			local healthy = 0
			scheduler:Every("broken", 1, function()
				error("fail")
			end)
			scheduler:Every("healthy", 1, function()
				healthy += 1
			end)
			for _ = 1, 5 do
				clock.Advance(1)
				scheduler:Step()
			end
			local stats = scheduler:GetStats()
			expect(stats.broken.Failures).toBe(3)
			expect(stats.broken.Disabled).toBe(1)
			expect(healthy).toBe(5)
			expect(#Fakes.findLogs(entries, "disabled after repeated failures")).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("validates arguments", function()
			expect(function()
				scheduler:Every("x", 0, function() end)
			end).toThrow("positive")
			expect(function()
				scheduler:Every("x", 0 / 0, function() end)
			end).toThrow("positive")
			expect(function()
				scheduler:After("", 1, function() end)
			end).toThrow("name")
		end)

		t.it("binds to a heartbeat signal", function()
			local heartbeat = Fakes.Signal.new()
			local runs = 0
			scheduler:Every("tick", 1, function()
				runs += 1
			end)
			scheduler:Bind(heartbeat)
			clock.Advance(1)
			heartbeat:Fire()
			expect(runs).toBe(1)
			scheduler:Unbind()
			expect(heartbeat:ConnectionCount()).toBe(0)
		end)
	end)
end
```

`tests/Specs/Unit/EnforcementPolicy.spec.luau`:

```luau
local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Configuration = require(ACF.Core.Configuration)
local DefaultConfig = require(ACF.Core.DefaultConfig)
local EnforcementPolicy = require(ACF.Enforcement.EnforcementPolicy)
local BanRecords = require(ACF.Enforcement.BanRecords)

-- The resolved Enforcement configuration, with overrides.
local function settings(overrides)
	local config = Configuration.new(DefaultConfig())
	local ok, errors = config:Load({ Enforcement = overrides or {} })
	assert(ok, errors and table.concat(errors, "\n"))
	return config:Get("Enforcement")
end

local function input(band, groups, confidence, kicks, tempBans)
	local list = {}
	for group, score in groups or {} do
		table.insert(list, { Group = group, Score = score })
	end
	table.sort(list, function(a, b)
		return a.Score > b.Score
	end)
	return {
		Band = band,
		Risk = 0,
		Confidence = confidence or 0.95,
		Groups = list,
		RecentKicks = kicks or 0,
		TemporaryBans = tempBans or 0,
	}
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("EnforcementPolicy", function()
		t.it("maps bands to levels 0-4", function()
			local cfg = settings()
			expect(EnforcementPolicy.decide(input("Normal"), cfg).Level).toBe(0)
			expect(EnforcementPolicy.decide(input("Increased"), cfg).Level).toBe(1)
			expect(EnforcementPolicy.decide(input("Restrict"), cfg).Level).toBe(2)
			expect(EnforcementPolicy.decide(input("Investigation"), cfg).Level).toBe(3)
			local review = EnforcementPolicy.decide(input("EnforcementReview", { Combat = 10 }, 0.5), cfg)
			expect(review.Level).toBe(4)
			expect(review.Proposed).toBe(5)
		end)

		t.it("tags are groups at or above TagMinScore", function()
			local d = EnforcementPolicy.decide(input("Restrict", { Economy = 30, Movement = 14.9, Aim = 15 }), settings())
			table.sort(d.Tags)
			expect(d.Tags).toEqual({ "Aim", "Economy" })
		end)

		t.it("Mode Off logs only", function()
			local d = EnforcementPolicy.decide(input("EnforcementReview", { Movement = 90 }), settings({ Mode = "Off" }))
			expect(d.Level).toBe(0)
			expect(d.Proposed).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("kicks automatically on strong non-statistical evidence (Hybrid, AutoKick)", function()
			local d = EnforcementPolicy.decide(input("EnforcementReview", { Movement = 45, Combat = 40 }), settings())
			expect(d.Level).toBe(5)
			expect(d.Corroborated).toBe(true)
			expect(d.Proposed).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("two independent groups corroborate when at least one is non-statistical", function()
			local ok = EnforcementPolicy.corroborated(input("EnforcementReview", { Movement = 20, Aim = 30 }), settings())
			expect(ok).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("statistical evidence alone never corroborates", function()
			local d = EnforcementPolicy.decide(input("EnforcementReview", { Aim = 50, Economy = 40, Automation = 30 }, 0.99), settings())
			expect(d.Corroborated).toBe(false)
			expect(d.Level).toBe(4)
			expect(d.Proposed).toBe(5)
			expect(string.find(d.Reason, "only statistical", 1, true) ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("low confidence, AutoKick = false and Manual mode all become proposals", function()
			local strong = { Movement = 60, Combat = 30 }
			expect(EnforcementPolicy.decide(input("EnforcementReview", strong, 0.8), settings()).Proposed).toBe(5)
			expect(EnforcementPolicy.decide(input("EnforcementReview", strong), settings({ AutoKick = false })).Proposed).toBe(5)
			local manual = EnforcementPolicy.decide(input("EnforcementReview", strong), settings({ Mode = "Manual" }))
			expect(manual.Level).toBe(4)
			expect(manual.Proposed).toBe(5)
		end)

		t.it("escalates: repeated kicks -> temporary ban; temporary bans -> permanent proposal", function()
			local strong = { Movement = 60, Combat = 30 }
			local d = EnforcementPolicy.decide(input("EnforcementReview", strong, 0.95, 2, 0), settings())
			expect(d.Proposed).toBe(6) -- AutoBan is off by default
			local auto = EnforcementPolicy.decide(input("EnforcementReview", strong, 0.95, 2, 0), settings({ AutoBan = true }))
			expect(auto.Level).toBe(6)
			local perm = EnforcementPolicy.decide(input("EnforcementReview", strong, 0.95, 0, 2), settings({ AutoBan = true }))
			expect(perm.Level).toBe(4)
			expect(perm.Proposed).toBe(7) -- permanent bans need approval by default
			local unattended = EnforcementPolicy.decide(
				input("EnforcementReview", strong, 0.95, 0, 2),
				settings({ AutoBan = true, Bans = { RequireApprovalForPermanentBan = false } })
			)
			expect(unattended.Level).toBe(7)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("BanRecords", function()
		t.it("accepts valid stored records and rejects foreign ones", function()
			expect(BanRecords.fromStored(1, "x")).toBeNil()
			expect(BanRecords.fromStored(1, { Version = 2 })).toBeNil()
			local record = BanRecords.fromStored(1, {
				Version = 1,
				Active = { Level = 6, IssuedAt = 100, ExpiresAt = 200, AppealRef = "ACF-1", Reason = string.rep("x", 1000) },
				History = { { Type = "Kick", At = 50 }, { Type = 5, At = 1 }, "junk" },
			})
			expect(record.Active.Level).toBe(6)
			expect(#record.Active.Reason).toBe(400)
			expect(#record.History).toBe(1)
			local badLevel = BanRecords.fromStored(1, { Version = 1, Active = { Level = 9, IssuedAt = 1 }, History = {} })
			expect(badLevel.Active).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("temporary bans expire into history; permanent bans do not", function()
			local record = BanRecords.new(1)
			record.Active = { Level = 6, AppealRef = "ACF-A", Reason = "", By = "x", IssuedAt = 0, ExpiresAt = 100 }
			expect(BanRecords.activeBan(record, 99) ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(BanRecords.activeBan(record, 100)).toBeNil()
			expect(BanRecords.expire(record, 150, 20)).toBe(true)
			expect(record.Active).toBeNil()
			expect(record.History[1].Type).toBe("Expired")
			record.Active = { Level = 7, AppealRef = "ACF-B", Reason = "", By = "x", IssuedAt = 0, ExpiresAt = nil }
			expect(BanRecords.expire(record, 1e12, 20)).toBe(false)
			expect(BanRecords.activeBan(record, 1e12) ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("temporary ban length escalates and is capped", function()
			local record = BanRecords.new(1)
			expect(BanRecords.tempBanHours(record, 24, 2, 720)).toBe(24)
			BanRecords.addHistory(record, { Type = "TemporaryBan", At = 1 }, 20)
			BanRecords.addHistory(record, { Type = "TemporaryBan", At = 2 }, 20)
			expect(BanRecords.tempBanHours(record, 24, 2, 720)).toBe(96)
			for i = 1, 10 do
				BanRecords.addHistory(record, { Type = "TemporaryBan", At = 2 + i }, 20)
			end
			expect(BanRecords.tempBanHours(record, 24, 2, 720)).toBe(720)
		end)

		t.it("history is bounded and messages are filled in", function()
			local record = BanRecords.new(1)
			for i = 1, 30 do
				BanRecords.addHistory(record, { Type = "Kick", At = i }, 20)
			end
			expect(#record.History).toBe(20)
			expect(record.History[1].At).toBe(11)
			expect(BanRecords.countSince(record, "Kick", 25)).toBe(6)
			local text = BanRecords.format("until {until}, ref {appeal}", "ACF-XYZ", 0)
			expect(text).toBe("until 1970-01-01 00:00 UTC, ref ACF-XYZ")
			expect(BanRecords.format("{until}", "r", nil)).toBe("further notice")
		end)
	end)
end
```

`tests/Specs/Unit/EvidenceManager.spec.luau`:

```luau
local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local EvidenceManager = require(ACF.Intelligence.EvidenceManager)
local Logger = require(ACF.Core.Logger)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local function setup(settings)
	local clock = Fakes.clock()
	local counter = 0
	local manager = EvidenceManager.new({
		Clock = { Now = clock.Now, Wall = clock.Wall },
		Logger = Logger.new({ Now = clock.Now, Sink = function() end }),
		GenerateId = function()
			counter += 1
			return "rec-" .. counter
		end,
		Settings = settings or { MaxRecordsPerPlayer = 4, RetentionSeconds = 60, DuplicateWindowSeconds = 5 },
	})
	local eventCount = 0
	local function event(fields)
		eventCount += 1
		local base = {
			Id = "evt-" .. eventCount,
			Detector = "Speed",
			Category = "Movement",
			Kind = "SpeedExceeded",
			UserId = 1,
			SessionId = "s1",
			Severity = 10,
			Confidence = 0.5,
			Time = clock.Now(),
			WallTime = clock.Wall(),
			Details = table.freeze({}),
		}
		for k, v in fields or {} do
			base[k] = v
		end
		return table.freeze(base)
	end
	return manager, clock, event
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("EvidenceManager", function()
		t.it("creates a record per new detection", function()
			local manager, _, event = setup()
			local record, isNew = manager:Record(event())
			expect(isNew).toBe(true)
			expect(record.Count).toBe(1)
			expect(record.FirstEventId).toBe("evt-1")
			expect(table.isfrozen(record)).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("merges duplicates inside the window keeping maxima", function()
			local manager, clock, event = setup()
			manager:Record(event({ Severity = 10, Confidence = 0.9 }))
			clock.Advance(2)
			local record, isNew = manager:Record(event({ Severity = 30, Confidence = 0.4, Details = table.freeze({ N = 2 }) }))
			expect(isNew).toBe(false)
			expect(record.Count).toBe(2)
			expect(record.Severity).toBe(30)
			expect(record.Confidence).toBe(0.9)
			expect(record.Details.N).toBe(2)
			expect(record.LastSeen - record.FirstSeen).toBe(2)
			expect(#manager:Get(1)).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("starts a new record once the duplicate window has passed", function()
			local manager, clock, event = setup()
			manager:Record(event())
			clock.Advance(6)
			local _, isNew = manager:Record(event())
			expect(isNew).toBe(true)
			expect(#manager:Get(1)).toBe(2)
		end)

		t.it("keeps different kinds separate", function()
			local manager, _, event = setup()
			manager:Record(event({ Kind = "A" }))
			manager:Record(event({ Kind = "B" }))
			expect(#manager:Get(1)).toBe(2)
		end)

		t.it("evicts the least recently seen record beyond the cap", function()
			local manager, clock, event = setup()
			for i = 1, 5 do
				manager:Record(event({ Kind = "K" .. i }))
				clock.Advance(1)
			end
			local records = manager:Get(1)
			expect(#records).toBe(4)
			expect(records[1].Kind).toBe("K2")
			expect(manager:Summary(1).Evicted).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("prunes expired records and empty stores", function()
			local manager, clock, event = setup()
			manager:Record(event())
			clock.Advance(61)
			expect(manager:Prune()).toBe(1)
			expect(manager:GetStats().Users).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("filters by detector, category, and time", function()
			local manager, clock, event = setup()
			manager:Record(event({ Detector = "Speed", Kind = "A" }))
			clock.Advance(10)
			manager:Record(event({ Detector = "Fly", Category = "Physics", Kind = "B" }))
			expect(#manager:Get(1, { Detector = "Fly" })).toBe(1)
			expect(#manager:Get(1, { Category = "Movement" })).toBe(1)
			expect(#manager:Get(1, { Since = clock.Now() - 1 })).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("summarises counts by category", function()
			local manager, _, event = setup()
			manager:Record(event())
			manager:Record(event())
			manager:Record(event({ Kind = "Other", Category = "Physics" }))
			local summary = manager:Summary(1)
			expect(summary.TotalDetections).toBe(3)
			expect(summary.Merged).toBe(1)
			expect(summary.ByCategory.Movement).toBe(2)
			expect(summary.ByCategory.Physics).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("returns snapshots that do not change after later merges", function()
			local manager, _, event = setup()
			local first = manager:Record(event())
			manager:Record(event())
			expect(first.Count).toBe(1)
		end)
	end)
end
```

`tests/Specs/Unit/ModuleLoader.spec.luau`:

```luau
local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local EventBus = require(ACF.Core.EventBus)
local Logger = require(ACF.Core.Logger)
local ModuleLoader = require(ACF.Core.ModuleLoader)
local Topics = require(ACF.Core.Topics)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local function setup()
	local clock = Fakes.clock()
	local entries = {}
	local logger = Logger.new({
		Level = "Debug",
		Now = clock.Now,
		RateLimitBurst = 1000,
		Sink = function(e)
			table.insert(entries, e)
		end,
	})
	local bus = EventBus.new({ Logger = logger })
	for _, topic in Topics do
		bus:RegisterTopic(topic)
	end
	local transitions = {}
	bus:Subscribe(Topics.ModuleStateChanged, function(p)
		table.insert(transitions, `{p.Name}:{p.From}->{p.To}`)
	end)
	local loader = ModuleLoader.new({
		Logger = logger,
		Bus = bus,
		MakeContext = function(entry)
			return { Name = entry.Name }
		end,
	})
	return loader, transitions, entries
end

local function enabled()
	return true
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("ModuleLoader validation", function()
		t.it("accepts a complete definition", function()
			expect(ModuleLoader.Validate(Fakes.module("Speed"))).toBeNil()
		end)

		t.it("requires all four lifecycle methods", function()
			local def = Fakes.module("Speed")
			def.Destroy = nil
			expect(ModuleLoader.Validate(def)).toContain("Destroy")
		end)

		t.it("rejects bad names, categories, and dependency lists", function()
			expect(ModuleLoader.Validate(Fakes.module("1bad"))).toContain("identifier")
			expect(ModuleLoader.Validate(Fakes.module("A", { Category = "Cheats" }))).toContain("Category")
			expect(ModuleLoader.Validate(Fakes.module("A", { Dependencies = { "A" } }))).toContain("itself")
			expect(ModuleLoader.Validate(Fakes.module("A", { Dependencies = { "B", "B" } }))).toContain("twice")
			expect(ModuleLoader.Validate(Fakes.module("A", { ConfigSchema = {} }))).toContain("Schema.Table")
			expect(ModuleLoader.Validate("nope")).toContain("table")
		end)

		t.it("rejects duplicate registration", function()
			local loader = setup()
			loader:Register(Fakes.module("A"))
			expect(function()
				loader:Register(Fakes.module("A"))
			end).toThrow("already registered")
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("ModuleLoader lifecycle", function()
		t.it("initializes and starts in dependency order, stops and destroys in reverse", function()
			local loader = setup()
			local log = {}
			local function tracked(name, deps)
				return Fakes.module(name, {
					Dependencies = deps,
					Initialize = function()
						table.insert(log, "init " .. name)
					end,
					Start = function()
						table.insert(log, "start " .. name)
					end,
					Stop = function()
						table.insert(log, "stop " .. name)
					end,
					Destroy = function()
						table.insert(log, "destroy " .. name)
					end,
				})
			end
			loader:Register(tracked("C", { "B" }))
			loader:Register(tracked("A", nil))
			loader:Register(tracked("B", { "A" }))
			loader:Resolve(enabled)
			expect(loader:GetOrder()).toEqual({ "A", "B", "C" })
			loader:InitializeAll()
			loader:StartAll()
			loader:DestroyAll()
			expect(log).toEqual({
				"init A", "init B", "init C",
				"start A", "start B", "start C",
				"stop C", "stop B", "stop A",
				"destroy C", "destroy B", "destroy A",
			})
		end)

		t.it("publishes every state transition", function()
			local loader, transitions = setup()
			loader:Register(Fakes.module("A"))
			loader:Resolve(enabled)
			loader:InitializeAll()
			loader:StartAll()
			loader:StopAll()
			expect(transitions).toEqual({
				"A:Registered->Initializing",
				"A:Initializing->Initialized",
				"A:Initialized->Starting",
				"A:Starting->Running",
				"A:Running->Stopping",
				"A:Stopping->Stopped",
			})
		end)

		t.it("marks a throwing module Failed and blocks its dependents only", function()
			local loader, _, entries = setup()
			loader:Register(Fakes.module("Broken", {
				Initialize = function()
					error("cannot init")
				end,
			}))
			loader:Register(Fakes.module("Dependent", { Dependencies = { "Broken" } }))
			loader:Register(Fakes.module("Independent"))
			loader:Resolve(enabled)
			loader:InitializeAll()
			loader:StartAll()
			local states = loader:GetStates()
			expect(states.Broken.State).toBe("Failed")
			expect(states.Broken.Error).toContain("cannot init")
			expect(states.Dependent.State).toBe("Blocked")
			expect(states.Independent.State).toBe("Running")
			expect(#Fakes.findLogs(entries, "Module failed to initialize", "Error")).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("blocks modules with missing or disabled dependencies", function()
			local loader = setup()
			loader:Register(Fakes.module("NeedsGhost", { Dependencies = { "Ghost" } }))
			loader:Register(Fakes.module("Off"))
			loader:Register(Fakes.module("NeedsOff", { Dependencies = { "Off" } }))
			loader:Register(Fakes.module("Chain", { Dependencies = { "NeedsOff" } }))
			loader:Resolve(function(entry)
				return entry.Name ~= "Off"
			end)
			local states = loader:GetStates()
			expect(states.NeedsGhost.State).toBe("Blocked")
			expect(states.NeedsGhost.Error).toContain("missing dependency 'Ghost'")
			expect(states.Off.State).toBe("Disabled")
			expect(states.NeedsOff.Error).toContain("disabled")
			expect(states.Chain.State).toBe("Blocked")
		end)

		t.it("detects dependency cycles", function()
			local loader = setup()
			loader:Register(Fakes.module("A", { Dependencies = { "B" } }))
			loader:Register(Fakes.module("B", { Dependencies = { "A" } }))
			loader:Register(Fakes.module("Free"))
			loader:Resolve(enabled)
			local states = loader:GetStates()
			expect(states.A.State).toBe("Failed")
			expect(states.A.Error).toContain("cycle")
			expect(states.Free.State).toBe("Registered")
		end)

		t.it("releases Start resources at Stop and Initialize resources at Destroy", function()
			local loader = setup()
			local released = {}
			local entry
			loader:Register(Fakes.module("A", {
				Initialize = function()
					entry.ActiveCleanup:Add(function()
						table.insert(released, "init-resource")
					end)
				end,
				Start = function()
					entry.ActiveCleanup:Add(function()
						table.insert(released, "start-resource")
					end)
				end,
			}))
			entry = loader:Get("A")
			loader:Resolve(enabled)
			loader:InitializeAll()
			loader:StartAll()
			loader:StopAll()
			expect(released).toEqual({ "start-resource" })
			loader:DestroyAll()
			expect(released).toEqual({ "start-resource", "init-resource" })
		end)

		t.it("can restart stopped modules", function()
			local loader = setup()
			local def = Fakes.module("A")
			loader:Register(def)
			loader:Resolve(enabled)
			loader:InitializeAll()
			loader:StartAll()
			loader:StopAll()
			loader:StartAll()
			expect(loader:GetStates().A.State).toBe("Running")
			expect(def.Calls).toEqual({ "Initialize", "Start", "Stop", "Start" })
		end)

		t.it("still destroys a module whose Start failed", function()
			local loader = setup()
			local def = Fakes.module("A", {
				Start = function()
					error("nope")
				end,
			})
			local destroyed = false
			def.Destroy = function()
				destroyed = true
			end
			loader:Register(def)
			loader:Resolve(enabled)
			loader:InitializeAll()
			loader:StartAll()
			loader:DestroyAll()
			expect(destroyed).toBe(true)
			expect(loader:GetStates().A.State).toBe("Destroyed")
		end)

		t.it("refuses registration after resolve", function()
			local loader = setup()
			loader:Resolve(enabled)
			expect(function()
				loader:Register(Fakes.module("Late"))
			end).toThrow("after modules have been resolved")
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/Unit/MovementDetectors.spec.luau`:

```luau
local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local MovementDetectors = require(ACF.Detection.Movement.MovementDetectors)
local ViolationTracker = require(ACF.Intelligence.ViolationTracker)

local SETTINGS = {
	MinAllowedSpeed = 8,
	SpeedTolerance = 1.25,
	SpeedSlackStuds = 4,
	MinWindowSeconds = 0.5,
	MaxWindowSeconds = 2,
	MaxPingCompensationSeconds = 0.5,
	TeleportSlackStuds = 10,
	TeleportMinDistance = 20,
	VerticalSlackStuds = 8,
	MaxVerticalPlatformSpeed = 25,
	AirborneClearance = 1.5,
	MaxAirtimeExtraSeconds = 1,
	MinFallStudsPerSecond = 4,
	MaxStallCreditSeconds = 3,
	MaxTimeCreditSeconds = 3,
	StallMinVelocity = 1,
	MaxAirJumps = 0,
	AirJumpRiseStuds = 3,
	MaxHorizontalAcceleration = 600,
	NoclipMinDistance = 1,
	NoclipMinThickness = 0.5,
	Detectors = { Speed = true, Teleport = true, Flight = true, InfiniteJump = true, Acceleration = true, Noclip = true },
}

local NO_ALLOWANCES = { Teleport = false, Flight = false, Noclip = false, Impulse = false, SpeedMultiplier = 1 }

local function sample(fields)
	local s = {
		X = 0, Y = 3, Z = 0, VX = 0, VY = 0, VZ = 0, AngularSpeed = 0, WalkSpeed = 16,
		JumpVelocity = 50, Gravity = 196.2, GroundClearance = 0, State = "Running",
		Seated = false, Health = 100, Ping = 0,
	}
	for k, v in fields do
		s[k] = v
	end
	return s
end

local function kinds(result)
	local list = {}
	for _, v in result.Violations do
		table.insert(list, v.Kind)
	end
	return list
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("MovementDetectors", function()
		t.it("allowed speed follows server WalkSpeed with a floor", function()
			expect(MovementDetectors.allowedSpeed(sample({ WalkSpeed = 16 }), SETTINGS, NO_ALLOWANCES)).toBe(20)
			expect(MovementDetectors.allowedSpeed(sample({ WalkSpeed = 0 }), SETTINGS, NO_ALLOWANCES)).toBe(10)
			local boosted = table.clone(NO_ALLOWANCES)
			boosted.SpeedMultiplier = 2
			expect(MovementDetectors.allowedSpeed(sample({ WalkSpeed = 16 }), SETTINGS, boosted)).toBe(40)
		end)

		t.it("gives time credit after a freeze (no speed or teleport on catch-up)", function()
			local state = MovementDetectors.newState(1)
			MovementDetectors.evaluate(state, sample({ X = 0 }), 0, SETTINGS, NO_ALLOWANCES)
			-- Unchanged position for 3 s keeps the t = 0 timestamp.
			for i = 1, 15 do
				MovementDetectors.evaluate(state, sample({ X = 0 }), i * 0.2, SETTINGS, NO_ALLOWANCES)
			end
			local result = MovementDetectors.evaluate(state, sample({ X = 50 }), 3.2, SETTINGS, NO_ALLOWANCES)
			expect(#result.Violations).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("weights speed violations by how far they exceed the limit", function()
			local state = MovementDetectors.newState(1)
			for i = 0, 10 do
				MovementDetectors.evaluate(state, sample({ X = i * 0.2 * 40 }), i * 0.2, SETTINGS, NO_ALLOWANCES)
			end
			local result = MovementDetectors.evaluate(state, sample({ X = 11 * 0.2 * 40 }), 2.2, SETTINGS, NO_ALLOWANCES)
			expect(kinds(result)).toEqual({ "SpeedExceeded" })
			local v = result.Violations[1]
			expect(v.Weight > 1 and v.Weight <= 4).toBe(true)
			expect(v.Details.ObservedSpeed).toBeCloseTo(40, 0.5)
		end)

		t.it("re-anchors the path after a teleport so it counts once", function()
			local state = MovementDetectors.newState(1)
			MovementDetectors.evaluate(state, sample({ X = 0 }), 0, SETTINGS, NO_ALLOWANCES)
			MovementDetectors.evaluate(state, sample({ X = 1 }), 0.2, SETTINGS, NO_ALLOWANCES)
			local jump = MovementDetectors.evaluate(state, sample({ X = 500 }), 0.4, SETTINGS, NO_ALLOWANCES)
			expect(kinds(jump)).toEqual({ "TeleportDetected" })
			local after = MovementDetectors.evaluate(state, sample({ X = 501 }), 1.0, SETTINGS, NO_ALLOWANCES)
			expect(#after.Violations).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("does not flag a normal jump arc", function()
			local state = MovementDetectors.newState(1)
			local any = 0
			for i = 0, 6 do
				local airT = i * 0.1
				local y = math.max(3, 3 + 50 * airT - 98.1 * airT * airT)
				local result = MovementDetectors.evaluate(
					state,
					sample({ Y = y, GroundClearance = y - 3 }),
					airT,
					SETTINGS,
					NO_ALLOWANCES
				)
				any += #result.Violations
			end
			expect(any).toBe(0)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("ViolationTracker", function()
		local settings = { HalfLifeSeconds = 10, ReportThreshold = 5, ReportIntervalSeconds = 10, ConfidenceScale = 8 }

		t.it("never reports a single sub-threshold violation", function()
			local tracker = ViolationTracker.new(settings)
			local report = tracker:Record("Speed", 4, 0)
			expect(report).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("reports sustained violations and throttles repeats", function()
			local tracker = ViolationTracker.new(settings)
			expect((tracker:Record("Speed", 3, 0))).toBe(false)
			expect((tracker:Record("Speed", 3, 0.2))).toBe(true)
			expect((tracker:Record("Speed", 3, 0.4))).toBe(false)
			expect((tracker:Record("Speed", 3, 11))).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("decays by half each half-life", function()
			local tracker = ViolationTracker.new(settings)
			tracker:Record("Speed", 4, 0)
			expect(tracker:Score("Speed", 10)).toBeCloseTo(2, 1e-9)
			expect(tracker:Score("Speed", 20)).toBeCloseTo(1, 1e-9)
		end)

		t.it("caps confidence", function()
			local tracker = ViolationTracker.new({
				HalfLifeSeconds = 10,
				ReportThreshold = 1,
				ReportIntervalSeconds = 0,
				ConfidenceScale = 1,
				MaxConfidence = 0.5,
			})
			local _, _, confidence = tracker:Record("X", 100, 0)
			expect(confidence).toBe(0.5)
		end)

		t.it("keeps keys independent", function()
			local tracker = ViolationTracker.new(settings)
			tracker:Record("A", 4, 0)
			expect(tracker:Score("B", 0)).toBe(0)
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/Unit/RiskModel.spec.luau`:

```luau
local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local RiskModel = require(ACF.Intelligence.RiskModel)

local BANDS = { Increased = 20, Restrict = 40, Investigation = 60, EnforcementReview = 80 }

local function key(group, score, confidence, detector, kind)
	return {
		Detector = detector or "D",
		Kind = kind or "K",
		Category = "Custom",
		Group = group,
		Score = score or 0,
		Updated = 0,
		Severity = 0,
		Confidence = confidence or 0,
		Count = 0,
		Suppressed = 0,
		LastAccepted = -math.huge,
	}
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("RiskModel", function()
		t.it("the first detection adds exactly severity x confidence", function()
			local k = key("G")
			expect(RiskModel.add(k, 30, 0.8, 2)).toBeCloseTo(24, 9)
			expect(k.Score).toBeCloseTo(24, 9)
			expect(k.Count).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("decays with lambda = ln 2 / half-life, i.e. exp(-lambda t)", function()
			local lambda = math.log(2) / 600
			expect(RiskModel.decay(50, 600, 600)).toBeCloseTo(25, 9)
			expect(RiskModel.decay(50, 250, 600)).toBeCloseTo(50 * math.exp(-lambda * 250), 9)
			expect(RiskModel.decay(50, -10, 600)).toBe(50)
		end)

		t.it("repeats of one kind saturate at severity x confidence x RepeatCap", function()
			local k = key("G")
			for _ = 1, 500 do
				RiskModel.add(k, 30, 0.9, 2)
			end
			expect(k.Score <= 54 + 1e-9).toBe(true)
			expect(k.Score > 53).toBe(true)
			-- Second detection adds half as much as the first (diminishing returns).
			local fresh = key("G")
			local first = RiskModel.add(fresh, 20, 1, 2)
			local second = RiskModel.add(fresh, 20, 1, 2)
			expect(first).toBe(20)
			expect(second).toBe(10)
		end)

		t.it("a zero-confidence or zero-severity detection adds nothing", function()
			local k = key("G")
			expect(RiskModel.add(k, 30, 0, 2)).toBe(0)
			expect(RiskModel.add(k, 0, 1, 2)).toBe(0)
			expect(k.Count).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("resolves groups by Detector.Kind, then Detector, then Category", function()
			local groups = { ["Behavioral.AccuracyAnomaly"] = "Aim", Physics = "Movement", Remote = "Net" }
			expect(RiskModel.groupFor("Behavioral", "AccuracyAnomaly", "Behavioral", groups)).toBe("Aim")
			expect(RiskModel.groupFor("Physics", "FlingDetected", "Physics", groups)).toBe("Movement")
			expect(RiskModel.groupFor("RemoteSecurity", "X", "Remote", groups)).toBe("Net")
			expect(RiskModel.groupFor("Combat", "CombatFireRate", "Combat", groups)).toBe("Combat")
		end)

		t.it("correlated keys: the strongest counts fully, the rest at SecondaryWeight", function()
			local groups = RiskModel.summarize({
				key("Movement", 30, 0.9, "Movement", "TeleportDetected"),
				key("Movement", 20, 0.5, "Physics", "VelocityMismatch"),
				key("Combat", 10, 0.7, "Combat", "CombatFireRate"),
			}, 0.25)
			expect(#groups).toBe(2)
			expect(groups[1].Group).toBe("Movement")
			expect(groups[1].Score).toBeCloseTo(35, 9)
			expect(groups[1].Confidence).toBe(0.9)
			expect(groups[1].Keys[1].Kind).toBe("TeleportDetected")
			expect(groups[2].Score).toBe(10)
		end)

		t.it("combines groups additively, capped at 100, with a noisy-OR confidence", function()
			local groups = RiskModel.summarize({ key("A", 30, 0.5), key("B", 40, 0.5) }, 0.25)
			local risk, confidence = RiskModel.combine(groups, 5)
			expect(risk).toBe(75)
			expect(confidence).toBeCloseTo(0.75, 9)
			local capped = RiskModel.combine(RiskModel.summarize({ key("A", 90), key("B", 90) }, 0.25), 0)
			expect(capped).toBe(100)
		end)

		t.it("bands follow the configured lower bounds", function()
			expect(RiskModel.band(0, BANDS)).toBe("Normal")
			expect(RiskModel.band(19.99, BANDS)).toBe("Normal")
			expect(RiskModel.band(20, BANDS)).toBe("Increased")
			expect(RiskModel.band(40, BANDS)).toBe("Restrict")
			expect(RiskModel.band(60, BANDS)).toBe("Investigation")
			expect(RiskModel.band(79.9, BANDS)).toBe("Investigation")
			expect(RiskModel.band(80, BANDS)).toBe("EnforcementReview")
			expect(RiskModel.band(100, BANDS)).toBe("EnforcementReview")
		end)

		t.it("explanations name each group and its strongest kinds", function()
			local k1 = key("Movement", 30, 0.9, "Movement", "TeleportDetected")
			k1.Count = 3
			k1.Severity = 30
			local groups = RiskModel.summarize({ k1 }, 0.25)
			local text = RiskModel.explain(42.5, "Restrict", groups, 10)
			expect(string.find(text, "Risk 42.5 (Restrict)", 1, true) ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(string.find(text, "TeleportDetected x3", 1, true) ~= nil).toBe(true)
			expect(string.find(text, "History 10", 1, true) ~= nil).toBe(true)
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/Unit/Statistics.spec.luau`:

```luau
local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local Statistics = require(ACF.Intelligence.Statistics)

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("Statistics", function()
		t.it("EWMA starts at the first value and converges to a constant input", function()
			local e = Statistics.newEwma()
			Statistics.updateEwma(e, 10, 0.2)
			expect(e.Mean).toBe(10)
			expect(e.Variance).toBe(0)
			for _ = 1, 200 do
				Statistics.updateEwma(e, 20, 0.2)
			end
			expect(e.Mean).toBeCloseTo(20, 4)
			expect(Statistics.stdDev(e)).toBeLessThan(0.01)
			expect(e.Count).toBe(201)
		end)

		t.it("EWMA variance approximates the variance of an alternating input", function()
			local e = Statistics.newEwma()
			for i = 1, 2000 do
				Statistics.updateEwma(e, if i % 2 == 0 then 0 else 10, 0.05)
			end
			expect(math.abs(e.Mean - 5)).toBeLessThan(0.5)
			-- Population variance of {0, 10} is 25; the EW estimate is close to it.
			expect(math.abs(e.Variance - 25)).toBeLessThan(3)
		end)

		t.it("CUSUM ignores values at or below the allowance", function()
			local c = Statistics.newCusum()
			for _ = 1, 1000 do
				local alarm = Statistics.cusumStep(c, 0.5, 0.5, 6)
				expect(alarm).toBe(false)
			end
			expect(c.S).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("CUSUM with clamped z needs several steps to alarm, then resets", function()
			local c = Statistics.newCusum()
			local z = Statistics.zScore(1e9, 1, 1, 0, 0, 3) -- clamped to 3
			expect(z).toBe(3)
			expect((Statistics.cusumStep(c, z, 0.5, 6))).toBe(false) -- 2.5
			expect((Statistics.cusumStep(c, z, 0.5, 6))).toBe(false) -- 5.0
			local alarm, statistic, steps = Statistics.cusumStep(c, z, 0.5, 6) -- 7.5
			expect(alarm).toBe(true)
			expect(statistic).toBeCloseTo(7.5, 6)
			expect(steps).toBe(3)
			expect(c.S).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("CUSUM drifts back to zero when values return to normal", function()
			local c = Statistics.newCusum()
			Statistics.cusumStep(c, 3, 0.5, 6)
			Statistics.cusumStep(c, 3, 0.5, 6)
			Statistics.cusumStep(c, -3, 0.5, 6)
			Statistics.cusumStep(c, -3, 0.5, 6)
			expect(c.S).toBe(0)
			expect(c.Steps).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("z-score floors the scale so near-zero variance cannot explode", function()
			expect(Statistics.zScore(10.1, 10, 0, 0, 0.1, 3)).toBeCloseTo(0.1, 6) -- scale = 1
			expect(Statistics.zScore(12, 10, 0.001, 2, 0, 10)).toBeCloseTo(1, 6)
			expect(Statistics.zScore(5, 0, 0, 0, 0, 3)).toBe(0) -- no scale at all
			expect(Statistics.zScore(-100, 0, 1, 0, 0, 3)).toBe(-3)
		end)

		t.it("Wilson lower bound reflects sample size", function()
			-- 10/10 is weak evidence; 300/300 is strong.
			expect(Statistics.wilsonLower(10, 10, 3.09)).toBeLessThan(0.55)
			expect(Statistics.wilsonLower(300, 300, 3.09)).toBeGreaterThan(0.96)
			-- A known value: 50/100 at z = 1.96 is about 0.4038.
			expect(Statistics.wilsonLower(50, 100, 1.96)).toBeCloseTo(0.4038, 3)
			expect(Statistics.wilsonLower(0, 0, 3)).toBe(0)
			expect(Statistics.wilsonLower(0, 50, 3)).toBe(0)
		end)

		t.it("decay factor halves per half-life and never grows", function()
			expect(Statistics.decayFactor(10, 10)).toBeCloseTo(0.5, 9)
			expect(Statistics.decayFactor(30, 10)).toBeCloseTo(0.125, 9)
			expect(Statistics.decayFactor(-5, 10)).toBe(1)
		end)

		t.it("mean and sample standard deviation", function()
			local mean, std = Statistics.meanStd({ 2, 4, 4, 4, 5, 5, 7, 9 })
			expect(mean).toBe(5)
			expect(std).toBeCloseTo(2.138, 3)
			expect(select(2, Statistics.meanStd({ 3 }))).toBe(0)
			expect((Statistics.meanStd({}))).toBe(0)
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/Specs/Unit/Validators.spec.luau`:

```luau
local ACF = game:GetService("ServerScriptService").ACF
local V = require(ACF.Detection.RemoteSecurity.Validators)
local Policies = require(ACF.Detection.RemoteSecurity.Policies)
local Fakes = require(script.Parent.Parent.Fakes)

local function check(validator, value)
	return validator.Check(value, "arg")
end

return function(t)
	local expect = t.expect

	t.describe("Validators: scalars", function()
		t.it("number enforces type, finiteness, range and integer", function()
			local n = V.number({ Min = 0, Max = 10 })
			expect((check(n, 5))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(n, "5"))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(n, 0 / 0))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(n, math.huge))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(n, -1))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(n, 11))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(V.integer(), 1.5))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(V.integer(), 2))).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("string defaults to 256 bytes and valid UTF-8", function()
			local s = V.string()
			expect((check(s, "hello"))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(s, string.rep("a", 257)))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(s, "\255\254"))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(V.string({ AllowInvalidUtf8 = true }), "\255"))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(V.string({ MinLength = 2 }), "a"))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(V.string({ Pattern = "^%a+$" }), "abc1"))).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("boolean, literal and enum", function()
			expect((check(V.boolean(), false))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(V.boolean(), 0))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(V.literal("buy"), "buy"))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(V.literal("buy"), "sell"))).toBe(false)
			local e = V.enum({ "Red", "Blue", 3 })
			expect((check(e, "Red"))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(e, 3))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(e, "Green"))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(e, {}))).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("optional and union", function()
			local o = V.optional(V.number())
			expect((check(o, nil))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(o, "x"))).toBe(false)
			local u = V.union(V.number(), V.string())
			expect((check(u, 1))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(u, "a"))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(u, true))).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("custom checks are isolated from errors", function()
			local c = V.custom("even", function(x)
				return x % 2 == 0, "must be even"
			end)
			expect((check(c, 4))).toBe(true)
			local ok, err = check(c, 3)
			expect(ok).toBe(false)
			expect(err).toContain("must be even")
			local ok2, err2 = check(c, "not a number")
			expect(ok2).toBe(false)
			expect(err2).toContain("errored")
		end)

		t.it("rejects non-validators at construction", function()
			expect(function()
				V.optional({} :: any)
			end).toThrow("must be a validator")
			expect(function()
				V.array(5 :: any)
			end).toThrow("must be a validator")
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Validators: tables", function()
		t.it("array accepts sequences and rejects holes, extra keys and oversize", function()
			local a = V.array(V.number(), { MaxLength = 3 })
			expect((check(a, { 1, 2, 3 }))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(a, {}))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(a, { 1, 2, 3, 4 }))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(a, { 1, nil, 3 }))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(a, { 1, x = 2 }))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(a, { 1, "2" }))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(a, { [1.5] = 1 }))).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("reports element paths", function()
			local _, err = check(V.array(V.number()), { 1, "x" })
			expect(err).toBe("arg[2]: expected number, got string")
		end)

		t.it("struct requires declared fields and rejects unknown keys", function()
			local s = V.struct({ Id = V.string(), Count = V.optional(V.integer()) })
			expect((check(s, { Id = "a" }))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(s, { Id = "a", Count = 2 }))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(s, { Count = 2 }))).toBe(false)
			local ok, err = check(s, { Id = "a", Admin = true })
			expect(ok).toBe(false)
			expect(err).toBe("arg: unexpected key")
			expect((check(s, { Id = "a", [1] = true }))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(V.struct({ Id = V.string() }, { AllowExtra = true }), { Id = "a", Other = 1 }))).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("map bounds entries and validates keys and values", function()
			local m = V.map(V.string({ MaxLength = 8 }), V.number(), { MaxEntries = 2 })
			expect((check(m, { a = 1, b = 2 }))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(m, { a = 1, b = 2, c = 3 }))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(m, { a = "x" }))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(m, { [5] = 1 }))).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("never echoes attacker-supplied strings in errors", function()
			local secret = "<<INJECTED-LOG-LINE>>"
			local validators = {
				V.number(),
				V.enum({ "A" }),
				V.literal("x"),
				V.struct({ A = V.number() }),
				V.string({ MaxLength = 3 }),
				V.string({ Pattern = "^%d+$" }),
			}
			for _, validator in validators do
				local _, err = check(validator, secret)
				expect(string.find(err, secret, 1, true)).toBeNil()
			end
			local _, err = check(V.struct({ A = V.number() }), { [secret] = 1 })
			expect(string.find(err, secret, 1, true)).toBeNil()
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Validators: Roblox types", function()
		t.it("instance checks class and container", function()
			local folder = Fakes.instance("Folder", "Plots")
			local part = Fakes.instance("Part", "Wall", folder)
			local loose = Fakes.instance("Part", "Loose")
			expect((check(V.instance(), part))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(V.instance("BasePart"), part))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(V.instance("Model"), part))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(V.instance(nil, { DescendantOf = folder }), part))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(V.instance(nil, { DescendantOf = folder }), loose))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(V.instance(), { Name = "fake" }))).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("vector3 rejects non-finite components and enforces magnitude", function()
			expect((check(V.vector3(), Fakes.vector3(1, 2, 3)))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(V.vector3(), Fakes.vector3(0 / 0, 0, 0)))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(V.vector3(), Fakes.vector3(math.huge, 0, 0)))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(V.vector3({ MaxMagnitude = 10 }), Fakes.vector3(100, 0, 0)))).toBe(false)
			expect((check(V.vector3(), { X = 1, Y = 2, Z = 3 }))).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("cframe rejects non-finite components", function()
			expect((check(V.cframe(), Fakes.cframe(1, 2, 3)))).toBe(true)
			expect((check(V.cframe(), Fakes.cframe(0 / 0, 2, 3)))).toBe(false)
		end)
	end)

	t.describe("Policies", function()
		t.it("userIds and combinators", function()
			local admins = Policies.Permissions.userIds({ 1, 2 })
			expect((admins(Fakes.player(1)))).toBe(true)
			expect((admins(Fakes.player(3)))).toBe(false)
			local always = function()
				return true
			end
			expect((Policies.Permissions.all(admins, always)(Fakes.player(3)))).toBe(false)
			expect((Policies.Permissions.any(admins, always)(Fakes.player(3)))).toBe(true)
		end)

		t.it("groupRank fails closed when the lookup errors", function()
			local player = Fakes.player(1)
			player.GetRankInGroup = function()
				error("http down")
			end
			expect((Policies.Permissions.groupRank(5, 1)(player))).toBe(false)
			player.GetRankInGroup = function()
				return 200
			end
			expect((Policies.Permissions.groupRank(5, 100)(player))).toBe(true)
			expect((Policies.Permissions.groupRank(5, 255)(player))).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("descendantOfCharacter", function()
			local player = Fakes.player(1)
			local check1 = Policies.Ownership.descendantOfCharacter(1)
			local part = Fakes.instance("Part")
			expect((check1(player, part))).toBe(false)
			local character = player:Spawn()
			local tool = Fakes.instance("Part", "Handle", character)
			expect((check1(player, tool))).toBe(true)
			expect((check1(player, part))).toBe(false)
		end)

		t.it("attribute ownership walks ancestors", function()
			local player = Fakes.player(42)
			local plot = Fakes.instance("Model", "Plot")
			plot:SetAttribute("OwnerUserId", 42)
			local wall = Fakes.instance("Part", "Wall", plot)
			local other = Fakes.instance("Model", "Other")
			other:SetAttribute("OwnerUserId", 7)
			local otherWall = Fakes.instance("Part", "Wall", other)
			local check1 = Policies.Ownership.attribute(1, "OwnerUserId")
			expect((check1(player, wall))).toBe(true)
			expect((check1(player, otherWall))).toBe(false)
			expect((check1(player, Fakes.instance("Part")))).toBe(false)
		end)
	end)
end

```

`tests/lune/VirtualDataModel.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Lune-only: builds a virtual Roblox instance tree from a Rojo project file and
	implements script-relative `require(instance)`, so ACF's unmodified source runs
	outside Roblox.

	Supported: $className, $path (directories, init.luau, *.luau, *.server.luau,
	*.client.luau), nested tree nodes. Nodes expose Name, ClassName, Parent,
	FindFirstChild, WaitForChild, GetChildren, GetDescendants, GetFullName, IsA and
	child access by indexing. The DataModel node supports GetService for services that
	exist in the tree.

	This is a test harness, not a Roblox emulator: there is no physics, replication or
	real Players service. Anything platform-specific is supplied by fakes through
	ACF's Environment.
]]

local fs = require("@lune/fs")
local luau = require("@lune/luau")
local serde = require("@lune/serde")
local task = require("@lune/task")

local VirtualDataModel = {}

-- Roblox's typeof reports "Instance", "Vector3", ... for engine types. Test fakes mark
-- themselves with a metatable __type field; this typeof honours it so validators see
-- the same type names they would in Roblox.
local nativeTypeof = typeof
local function harnessTypeof(value: any): string
	if type(value) == "table" then
		local mt = getmetatable(value)
		if type(mt) == "table" and type(rawget(mt, "__type")) == "string" then
			return rawget(mt, "__type")
		end
	end
	return nativeTypeof(value)
end

local Node = {}
local NodeMethods = {}

local function newNode(name: string, className: string, parent: any?)
	local node = setmetatable({
		Name = name,
		ClassName = className,
		Parent = parent,
		_children = {},
		_order = {},
		_source = nil,
		_sourcePath = nil,
	}, Node)
	if parent then
		rawget(parent, "_children")[name] = node
		table.insert(rawget(parent, "_order"), node)
	end
	return node
end

Node.__index = function(self, key)
	local method = NodeMethods[key]
	if method then
		return method
	end
	local child = rawget(self, "_children")[key]
	if child then
		return child
	end
	error(`{key} is not a valid member of {rawget(self, "ClassName")} "{NodeMethods.GetFullName(self)}"`, 2)
end

Node.__tostring = function(self)
	return rawget(self, "Name")
end

function NodeMethods.FindFirstChild(self, name: string)
	return rawget(self, "_children")[name]
end

function NodeMethods.WaitForChild(self, name: string)
	local child = rawget(self, "_children")[name]
	if child == nil then
		error(`WaitForChild: '{name}' does not exist under '{NodeMethods.GetFullName(self)}' (virtual tree never yields)`, 2)
	end
	return child
end

function NodeMethods.GetChildren(self)
	return table.clone(rawget(self, "_order"))
end

function NodeMethods.GetDescendants(self)
	local result = {}
	local function walk(node)
		for _, child in rawget(node, "_order") do
			table.insert(result, child)
			walk(child)
		end
	end
	walk(self)
	return result
end

function NodeMethods.IsA(self, className: string)
	local mine = rawget(self, "ClassName")
	if mine == className then
		return true
	end
	if className == "LuaSourceContainer" then
		return mine == "ModuleScript" or mine == "Script" or mine == "LocalScript"
	end
	return className == "Instance"
end

function NodeMethods.GetFullName(self)
	local parts = {}
	local node = self
	while node ~= nil and rawget(node, "ClassName") ~= "DataModel" do
		table.insert(parts, 1, rawget(node, "Name"))
		node = rawget(node, "Parent")
	end
	return table.concat(parts, ".")
end

function NodeMethods.GetService(self, name: string)
	local child = rawget(self, "_children")[name]
	if child == nil then
		error(`GetService: service '{name}' is not available in the virtual DataModel`, 2)
	end
	return child
end

local SCRIPT_SUFFIXES = {
	{ ".server.luau", "Script" },
	{ ".client.luau", "LocalScript" },
	{ ".luau", "ModuleScript" },
	{ ".server.lua", "Script" },
	{ ".client.lua", "LocalScript" },
	{ ".lua", "ModuleScript" },
}

local function classifyFile(fileName: string): (string?, string?)
	for _, pair in SCRIPT_SUFFIXES do
		local suffix, className = pair[1], pair[2]
		if string.sub(fileName, -#suffix) == suffix then
			return string.sub(fileName, 1, #fileName - #suffix), className
		end
	end
	return nil, nil
end

local function joinPath(a: string, b: string): string
	return a .. "/" .. b
end

local function mountPath(path: string, name: string, parent: any)
	if fs.isFile(path) then
		local _, className = classifyFile(path)
		local node = newNode(name, className or "StringValue", parent)
		rawset(node, "_source", fs.readFile(path))
		rawset(node, "_sourcePath", path)
		return node
	end
	if not fs.isDir(path) then
		error(`VirtualDataModel: path '{path}' does not exist`)
	end
	local entries = fs.readDir(path)
	table.sort(entries)
	local initFile, initClass = nil, nil
	for _, entry in entries do
		local base, className = classifyFile(entry)
		if base == "init" then
			initFile, initClass = joinPath(path, entry), className
		end
	end
	local node = newNode(name, initClass or "Folder", parent)
	if initFile then
		rawset(node, "_source", fs.readFile(initFile))
		rawset(node, "_sourcePath", initFile)
	end
	for _, entry in entries do
		local full = joinPath(path, entry)
		if fs.isDir(full) then
			mountPath(full, entry, node)
		else
			local base, className = classifyFile(entry)
			if base and base ~= "init" and className then
				local child = newNode(base, className, node)
				rawset(child, "_source", fs.readFile(full))
				rawset(child, "_sourcePath", full)
			end
		end
	end
	return node
end

local function buildTree(definition: { [string]: any }, name: string, parent: any?, root: string)
	local node
	if definition["$path"] then
		node = mountPath(joinPath(root, definition["$path"]), name, parent)
	else
		node = newNode(name, definition["$className"] or "Folder", parent)
	end
	for key, value in definition do
		if string.sub(key, 1, 1) ~= "$" and type(value) == "table" then
			buildTree(value, key, node, root)
		end
	end
	return node
end

-- Loads a Rojo project. Returns `game` (the DataModel node) and a `require` that
-- executes ModuleScript nodes with Roblox-style globals.
function VirtualDataModel.load(projectFile: string, root: string)
	local project = serde.decode("json", fs.readFile(projectFile))
	local game = buildTree(project.tree, "Game", nil, root)
	rawset(game, "ClassName", "DataModel")

	local cache = {}
	local loading = {}

	local requireNode
	requireNode = function(node)
		if type(node) ~= "table" or getmetatable(node) ~= Node then
			error("require expects a ModuleScript instance (virtual tree)", 2)
		end
		if rawget(node, "ClassName") ~= "ModuleScript" then
			error(`require: '{NodeMethods.GetFullName(node)}' is a {rawget(node, "ClassName")}, not a ModuleScript`, 2)
		end
		local cached = cache[node]
		if cached ~= nil then
			return cached.value
		end
		if loading[node] then
			error(`require: cyclic dependency involving '{NodeMethods.GetFullName(node)}'`, 2)
		end
		loading[node] = true
		local chunk = luau.load(rawget(node, "_source"), {
			debugName = rawget(node, "_sourcePath") or NodeMethods.GetFullName(node),
			environment = {
				script = node,
				require = requireNode,
				game = game,
				task = task,
				typeof = harnessTypeof,
			},
		})
		local results = table.pack(chunk())
		loading[node] = nil
		if results.n ~= 1 then
			error(`Module '{NodeMethods.GetFullName(node)}' must return exactly one value, returned {results.n}`, 2)
		end
		cache[node] = { value = results[1] }
		return results[1]
	end

	return game, requireNode
end

return VirtualDataModel

```

`tests/lune/bench.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Micro-benchmarks for hot paths (Lune). Numbers are indicative only: Lune's Luau VM
	is not Roblox's server, but relative costs and regressions show up reliably.

		./.bin/lune.exe run tests/lune/bench.luau
]]

local process = require("@lune/process")
local VirtualDataModel = require("./VirtualDataModel")

local root = process.cwd
local game, requireModule = VirtualDataModel.load(root .. "/test.project.json", root)
local ACF = game.ServerScriptService.ACF
local Framework = requireModule(ACF.Core.Framework)
local V = requireModule(ACF.Detection.RemoteSecurity.Validators)
local Fakes = requireModule(game.ServerStorage.ACFTests.Fakes)

local function bench(name: string, iterations: number, fn: () -> ())
	for _ = 1, math.min(1000, iterations) do
		fn()
	end
	local started = os.clock()
	for _ = 1, iterations do
		fn()
	end
	local elapsed = os.clock() - started
	print(string.format("%-46s %9.2f us/op  (%d ops)", name, elapsed / iterations * 1e6, iterations))
end

local fake = Fakes.environment()
local framework = Framework.new(fake.Env)
framework:Initialize({})
framework:Start()
local security = framework:GetModule("RemoteSecurity")
local player = fake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))

local buy = Fakes.remoteEvent("Buy")
security:ProtectEvent(buy, {
	Arguments = { V.string({ MaxLength = 32 }), V.integer({ Min = 1, Max = 99 }) },
	RateLimit = { Burst = 1e9, PerSecond = 1e9 },
	Handler = function() end,
})

local inventory = Fakes.remoteEvent("Inventory")
security:ProtectEvent(inventory, {
	Arguments = { V.array(V.struct({ Id = V.string({ MaxLength = 16 }), Count = V.integer() }), { MaxLength = 32 }) },
	RateLimit = { Burst = 1e9, PerSecond = 1e9 },
	Handler = function() end,
})
local items = {}
for i = 1, 32 do
	items[i] = { Id = "item" .. i, Count = i }
end

local flooded = Fakes.remoteEvent("Flooded")
security:ProtectEvent(flooded, {
	Arguments = {},
	RateLimit = { Burst = 1, PerSecond = 0.001 },
	Handler = function() end,
})

local huge = table.create(100000, 1)

bench("accepted request (2 scalar args)", 200000, function()
	Fakes.fire(buy, player, "Sword", 3)
end)
bench("accepted request (array of 32 structs)", 50000, function()
	Fakes.fire(inventory, player, items)
end)
bench("rejected: invalid argument", 200000, function()
	Fakes.fire(buy, player, "Sword", -1)
end)
bench("rejected: rate limited (flood)", 200000, function()
	Fakes.fire(flooded, player)
end)
bench("rejected: 100k-element table (bounded walk)", 20000, function()
	Fakes.fire(inventory, player, huge)
end)

local evidence = framework:GetEvidenceSummary(player)
print(string.format("\nevidence records after flood: %d (merged %d)", evidence.Records, evidence.Merged))

-- Movement + Physics: one scheduler tick sampling 50 walking players (fake probe/world).
local mfake = Fakes.environment()
mfake.Physics:AddFloor()
mfake.Physics:AddBox(500, 0, -1000, 502, 20, 1000) -- a wall, so noclip casts do work
local mframework = Framework.new(mfake.Env)
mframework:Initialize({ ModuleSettings = { Movement = { SampleIntervalSeconds = 0.2 } } })
mframework:Start()
local characters = {}
for i = 1, 50 do
	local p = mfake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1000 + i))
	local c = p:Spawn()
	mfake.Physics:Set(c, { X = 0, Z = i * 5 })
	characters[i] = c
end
local step = 0
bench("movement tick, 50 players (per tick)", 2000, function()
	step += 1
	for i, c in characters do
		mfake.Physics:Set(c, { X = step * 3.2, Z = i * 5, VX = 16 })
	end
	mfake.Clock.Advance(0.2)
	mframework:Step()
end)
print(string.format("  -> %d fake probe samples, %d ray casts", mfake.Physics.Samples, mfake.Physics.Casts))

-- Combat: one validated hitscan hit (shot + hit checks + line-of-sight cast).
local cfake = Fakes.environment()
cfake.Physics:AddFloor()
local cframework = Framework.new(cfake.Env)
cframework:Initialize({})
local combat = cframework:GetModule("Combat")
combat:Define("Rifle", { Kind = "Hitscan", Damage = 25, FireRate = 1e6, Burst = 1e6, MaxRange = 500 })
combat:Define("Rocket", { Kind = "Projectile", Damage = 50, FireRate = 1e6, Burst = 1e6, MaxRange = 500, ProjectileSpeed = 200, ProjectileGravity = 30 })
cframework:Start()
local shooter = cfake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(1))
local shooterCharacter = shooter:Spawn()
cfake.Physics:Set(shooterCharacter, {})
Fakes.instance("Tool", "Rifle", shooterCharacter)
Fakes.instance("Tool", "Rocket", shooterCharacter)
local victim = cfake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(2))
local victimCharacter = victim:Spawn()
local torso = Fakes.instance("Part", "Torso", victimCharacter)
cfake.Physics:SetTarget(torso, { X = 100, Y = 3, Z = 0, Model = victimCharacter, Player = victim })
local claim = { Origin = Fakes.vector3(0, 4.5, 0), Target = torso, HitPosition = Fakes.vector3(100, 3, 0) }
bench("combat: validated hitscan hit", 100000, function()
	combat:ValidateAttack(shooter, "Rifle", claim)
end)
local shot = 0
bench("combat: projectile fire + arcing hit validation", 20000, function()
	shot += 1
	local id = "b" .. shot
	combat:FireProjectile(shooter, "Rocket", { Origin = Fakes.vector3(0, 3, 0), Direction = Fakes.vector3(1, 0, 0), ShotId = id })
	cfake.Clock.Advance(0.6)
	combat:ValidateProjectileHit(shooter, id, { Target = torso })
end)

-- Behavioural: 50 players, 4 signals, actions and combat shots ---------------------
local bfake = Fakes.environment()
local bframework = Framework.new(bfake.Env)
bframework:Initialize({ Modules = { Movement = false, Physics = false } })
local behavioral = bframework:GetModule("Behavioral")
for _, name in { "Coins", "Gems", "XP", "Kills" } do
	behavioral:DefineSignal(name, { MaxPerSecond = 1e6 })
end
bframework:Start()
local bplayers = {}
for i = 1, 50 do
	bplayers[i] = bfake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(i))
end
-- Warm up history so baselines exist.
for _ = 1, 30 do
	for i = 1, 50 do
		for _, name in { "Coins", "Gems", "XP", "Kills" } do
			behavioral:RecordSignal(bplayers[i], name, 5 + i % 7)
		end
	end
	bfake.Clock.Advance(10)
	bframework:Step()
end
local bi = 0
bench("behavioral: RecordSignal", 200000, function()
	bi = bi % 50 + 1
	behavioral:RecordSignal(bplayers[bi], "Coins", 5)
end)
bench("behavioral: RecordAction (timing + validity)", 200000, function()
	bi = bi % 50 + 1
	bfake.Clock.Advance(0.001)
	behavioral:RecordAction(bplayers[bi], "Click", true)
end)
bench("behavioral: bucket close, 50 players x 4 signals", 2000, function()
	for i = 1, 50 do
		behavioral:RecordSignal(bplayers[i], "Coins", 5)
	end
	bfake.Clock.Advance(10)
	bframework:Step()
end)

-- Risk: detection -> risk update, and the periodic recompute ------------------------
local rfake = Fakes.environment()
local rframework = Framework.new(rfake.Env)
local probe = Fakes.module("BenchProbe", { Category = "Movement" })
rframework:RegisterModule(probe)
rframework:Initialize({ Modules = { Movement = false, Physics = false }, Detection = { ReportsPerSecond = 1000, ReportBurst = 10000 } })
rframework:Start()
local rplayers = {}
for i = 1, 50 do
	rplayers[i] = rfake.Players:Join(Fakes.player(i))
	for k = 1, 6 do
		probe.Context:Report(rplayers[i], { Kind = "Kind" .. k, Severity = 10, Confidence = 0.5 })
	end
end
local ri, rk = 0, 0
bench("risk: detection -> risk update (6 keys/user)", 100000, function()
	ri = ri % 50 + 1
	rk = rk % 6 + 1
	rfake.Clock.Advance(0.01) -- past duplicate suppression for most calls
	probe.Context:Report(rplayers[ri], { Kind = "Kind" .. rk, Severity = 10, Confidence = 0.5 })
end)
local riskEngine = (rframework :: any)._services.Risk
bench("risk: recompute, 50 users x 6 keys", 5000, function()
	riskEngine:Recompute()
end)
```
