`tests/lune/evaluate.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Phase 8 evaluation (Lune). Runs the population simulator and measures:

	  detection   per cheat: caught or not, time to first evidence, time to the Restrict
	              band, highest band, what enforcement would do (shadow mode)
	  false pos.  honest players with any evidence, records per player-hour, bands reached,
	              enforcement actions against honest players
	  cpu         ACF time per server tick at 10 / 50 / 100 players (full stack)
	  memory      Lua heap over a churn soak (players joining and leaving for 2 simulated h)
	  logging     log calls per second through the rate-limited logger
	  stability   errors, disabled tasks and disconnected handlers, with a faulty module

		./.bin/lune.exe run tests/lune/evaluate.luau [quick|full] [--write]

	--write stores the report in docs/EVALUATION.md. Detection and false-positive numbers
	are deterministic (seeded); CPU and memory figures depend on the machine.
]]

local fs = require("@lune/fs")
local process = require("@lune/process")
local VirtualDataModel = require("./VirtualDataModel")

local root = process.cwd
local game, requireModule = VirtualDataModel.load(root .. "/test.project.json", root)
local Simulation = requireModule(game.ServerStorage.ACFTests.Simulation)
local Fakes = requireModule(game.ServerStorage.ACFTests.Fakes)
local Framework = requireModule(game.ServerScriptService.ACF.Core.Framework)
local Logger = requireModule(game.ServerScriptService.ACF.Core.Logger)

local mode = process.args[1] or "quick"
local write = table.find(process.args, "--write") ~= nil
local FULL = mode == "full"

local SEEDS = if FULL then 10 else 3
local HONEST = if FULL then 30 else 20
local MINUTES = if FULL then 10 else 5
local SHADOW = { Enforcement = { ShadowMode = true } }

local out = {}
local function line(text: string?)
	table.insert(out, text or "")
	print(text or "")
end

local function fmt(value: number?, places: number?): string
	if value == nil then
		return "—"
	end
	return string.format(`%.{places or 1}f`, value)
end

local function median(list: { number }): number?
	if #list == 0 then
		return nil
	end
	table.sort(list)
	local middle = (#list + 1) // 2
	return if #list % 2 == 1 then list[middle] else (list[middle] + list[middle + 1]) / 2
end

line("# ACF Evaluation")
line()
line(`Generated by \`tests/lune/evaluate.luau {mode}\` (Lune {"0.10.5"}). Seeds 1–{SEEDS}; {HONEST} honest players`)
line(`and one of each cheat per seed; {MINUTES} simulated minutes per seed; cheats start at 60 s.`)
line("Enforcement runs in shadow mode so cheaters stay in the server to be measured; the")
line("\"would do\" column is what enforcement decided.")
line()

-- 1. Detection and false positives -----------------------------------------------------

local perCheat = {}
for _, cheat in Simulation.CHEATS do
	perCheat[cheat] = { Caught = 0, Runs = 0, Detect = {}, Restrict = {}, Bands = {}, Actions = {} }
end
local honestTotals = { Players = 0, WithEvidence = 0, Records = 0, Kinds = {}, Restricted = 0, MaxBand = "Normal", ShadowActions = 0, Rejected = 0 }
local BAND_INDEX = { Normal = 1, Increased = 2, Restrict = 3, Investigation = 4, EnforcementReview = 5 }

for seed = 1, SEEDS do
	local sim = Simulation.new({ Seed = seed, Config = SHADOW })
	for _ = 1, HONEST do
		sim.add("Honest")
	end
	local cheaters = {}
	for _, cheat in Simulation.CHEATS do
		cheaters[cheat] = sim.add(cheat, 60)
	end
	sim.run(MINUTES * 60)

	for cheat, agent in cheaters do
		local entry = perCheat[cheat]
		entry.Runs += 1
		if agent.firstEvidence then
			entry.Caught += 1
			table.insert(entry.Detect, agent.firstEvidence - agent.startAt)
		end
		if agent.firstRestrict then
			table.insert(entry.Restrict, agent.firstRestrict - agent.startAt)
		end
		entry.Bands[agent.maxBand] = (entry.Bands[agent.maxBand] or 0) + 1
		local wouldRemove = #sim.framework:GetAuditLog({ UserId = agent.id, Type = "ShadowAction" }) > 0
		if wouldRemove then
			entry.Actions["would remove"] = (entry.Actions["would remove"] or 0) + 1
		end
		local state = sim.framework:GetEnforcement(agent.player)
		entry.Actions[state.LevelName] = (entry.Actions[state.LevelName] or 0) + 1
	end

	local honest = sim.honestReport()
	honestTotals.Players += honest.Players
	honestTotals.WithEvidence += honest.WithEvidence
	honestTotals.Records += honest.Records
	honestTotals.Restricted += honest.Restricted
	for kind, count in honest.Kinds do
		honestTotals.Kinds[kind] = (honestTotals.Kinds[kind] or 0) + count
	end
	if BAND_INDEX[honest.MaxBand] > BAND_INDEX[honestTotals.MaxBand] then
		honestTotals.MaxBand = honest.MaxBand
	end
	for _, agent in sim.agents do
		if agent.role == "Honest" then
			honestTotals.ShadowActions += #sim.framework:GetAuditLog({ UserId = agent.id, Type = "ShadowAction" })
			honestTotals.Rejected += #sim.framework:GetAuditLog({ UserId = agent.id, Type = "Rejected" })
		end
	end
	print(`  seed {seed} done`)
end

line("## Detection")
line()
line("| Cheat | Caught | Median time to evidence (s) | Median time to Restrict band (s) | Highest band (runs) | Enforcement level at end; runs where it would remove |")
line("|-------|--------|------------------------------|-----------------------------------|---------------------|------------------------------------------------------|")
for _, cheat in Simulation.CHEATS do
	local entry = perCheat[cheat]
	local bands = {}
	for band, count in entry.Bands do
		table.insert(bands, `{band} {count}`)
	end
	table.sort(bands)
	local actions = {}
	for action, count in entry.Actions do
		table.insert(actions, `{action} ×{count}`)
	end
	table.sort(actions)
	line(
		`| {cheat} | {entry.Caught}/{entry.Runs} | {fmt(median(entry.Detect))} | {fmt(median(entry.Restrict))} | {table.concat(bands, ", ")} | {table.concat(actions, ", ")} |`
	)
end
line()

local playerHours = honestTotals.Players * MINUTES / 60
line("## False positives (honest players)")
line()
line(`| Measure | Result |`)
line(`|---------|--------|`)
line(`| Honest players simulated | {honestTotals.Players} ({fmt(playerHours)} player-hours) |`)
line(`| Players with any evidence | {honestTotals.WithEvidence} |`)
line(`| Evidence records per player-hour | {fmt(honestTotals.Records / playerHours, 3)} |`)
local kinds = {}
for kind, count in honestTotals.Kinds do
	table.insert(kinds, `{kind} ×{count}`)
end
table.sort(kinds)
line(`| Kinds seen | {if #kinds > 0 then table.concat(kinds, ", ") else "none"} |`)
line(`| Highest risk band reached | {honestTotals.MaxBand} |`)
line(`| Players ever at Restrict or above | {honestTotals.Restricted} |`)
line(`| Enforcement removals that would have happened | {honestTotals.ShadowActions} |`)
line(`| Restriction refusals that would have happened | {honestTotals.Rejected} |`)
line()

-- 1b. Live enforcement (shadow mode off) ------------------------------------------------

line("## Enforcement, live (shadow mode off, default Hybrid policy)")
line()
line(`Seed 1 again, {MINUTES} minutes, with enforcement acting. Corrections count summarised`)
line("audit entries (one per minute of repeated corrections, with a count).")
line()
line("| Player | Removed | Removed after (s) | Corrections | Cases opened (proposed level) | Final level |")
line("|--------|---------|-------------------|-------------|-------------------------------|-------------|")
do
	local live = Simulation.new({ Seed = 1 })
	for _ = 1, HONEST do
		live.add("Honest")
	end
	local cheaters = {}
	for _, cheat in Simulation.CHEATS do
		cheaters[cheat] = live.add(cheat, 60)
	end
	live.run(MINUTES * 60)
	local function corrections(userId)
		local total = 0
		for _, entry in live.framework:GetAuditLog({ UserId = userId, Type = "Corrected" }) do
			total += entry.Count or 1
		end
		return total
	end
	local function cases(userId)
		local list = {}
		for _, case in live.framework:GetCases({ UserId = userId }) do
			table.insert(list, `{case.Type}{if case.ProposedLevel then " " .. case.ProposedLevel else ""}`)
		end
		return if #list > 0 then table.concat(list, ", ") else "none"
	end
	for _, cheat in Simulation.CHEATS do
		local agent = cheaters[cheat]
		line(
			`| {cheat} | {if agent.kicked then "yes" else "no"} | {if agent.kickedAt then fmt(agent.kickedAt - agent.startAt) else "—"} | {corrections(agent.id)} | {cases(agent.id)} | {live.framework:GetEnforcement(agent.id).LevelName} |`
		)
	end
	local honestKicked, honestCorrections, honestCases, honestRejected = 0, 0, 0, 0
	for _, agent in live.agents do
		if agent.role == "Honest" then
			honestKicked += if agent.kicked then 1 else 0
			honestCorrections += corrections(agent.id)
			honestCases += #live.framework:GetCases({ UserId = agent.id })
			honestRejected += #live.framework:GetAuditLog({ UserId = agent.id, Type = "Rejected" })
		end
	end
	line(`| **{HONEST} honest players** | {honestKicked} | — | {honestCorrections} | {honestCases} | rejections: {honestRejected} |`)
end
line()

-- 2. CPU ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------

line("## CPU (ACF time per 0.2 s server tick, full stack)")
line()
line("| Players | Mean per tick (ms) | Share of one core at 5 Hz |")
line("|---------|--------------------|---------------------------|")
for _, count in { 10, 50, 100 } do
	local sim = Simulation.new({ Seed = 99, Config = SHADOW })
	for i = 1, count do
		sim.add(if i % 10 == 0 then Simulation.CHEATS[1 + (i // 10) % #Simulation.CHEATS] else "Honest", 30)
	end
	sim.run(30) -- warm-up
	sim.acfSeconds, sim.steps = 0, 0
	sim.run(if FULL then 120 else 60)
	local perTick = sim.acfSeconds / sim.steps * 1000
	line(`| {count} | {fmt(perTick, 2)} | {fmt(perTick / 200 * 100, 2)} % |`)
end
line()
line("Includes every ACF call the simulation makes (movement sampling, combat validation,")
line("signals, actions, remote checks, risk and enforcement), not the simulation's own work.")
line("Lune's VM is not a Roblox server; compare runs, don't treat these as server numbers.")
line()

-- 3. Memory and stability soak -------------------------------------------------------

line("## Memory and stability soak")
line()
local faulty = Fakes.module("Faulty", {
	Category = "Custom",
	Start = function(self)
		self.Context.Scheduler:Every("Explode", 1, function()
			error("faulty module task")
		end)
		self.Context.Events:Subscribe("ACF.DetectionReported", function()
			error("faulty module handler")
		end)
	end,
})
local sim = Simulation.new({ Seed = 7, Config = SHADOW, Modules = { faulty } })
local active = {}
for _ = 1, 40 do
	table.insert(active, sim.add("Honest"))
end
for i = 1, 5 do
	table.insert(active, sim.add(Simulation.CHEATS[i], 0))
end
local samples = {}
local soakMinutes = if FULL then 120 else 40
local baseline = collectgarbage("count")
for minute = 1, soakMinutes do
	-- Churn: two players leave and two join every minute.
	for _ = 1, 2 do
		local leaving = table.remove(active, 1)
		sim.fake.Players:Leave(leaving.player)
		sim.fake.Physics:Clear(leaving.character) -- the simulator's own fake state
		leaving.kicked = true
		table.insert(active, sim.add(if minute % 3 == 0 then Simulation.CHEATS[1 + minute % #Simulation.CHEATS] else "Honest", 0))
	end
	sim.run(60)
	if minute % 10 == 0 then
		local st = sim.framework:GetStatus()
		table.insert(samples, {
			minute,
			collectgarbage("count"),
			st.Evidence.Records,
			st.Evidence.Users,
			st.Risk.Tracked,
			st.Enforcement.Users,
			st.Enforcement.Records,
			st.Enforcement.Audit.Entries,
		})
	end
end
local status = sim.framework:GetStatus()
line(`{soakMinutes} simulated minutes, 45 players at a time, 2 leaving and 2 joining every minute`)
line(`({#sim.agents} distinct players in total). A faulty module throws from a scheduled task`)
line("and from an event handler.")
line()
line("Heap is sampled with `collectgarbage(\"count\")` (Lune cannot force a collection), so")
line("individual samples include uncollected garbage; look at the trend, not one value.")
line()
line("ACF's own per-user structures are counted directly; they are what must stay bounded.")
line()
line("| Minute | Lua heap (KB) | Evidence records | Evidence users | Risk users | Enforcement users | Enforcement records | Audit entries |")
line("|--------|---------------|------------------|----------------|------------|-------------------|---------------------|---------------|")
line(`| 0 | {fmt(baseline, 0)} | | | | | | |`)
for _, sample in samples do
	line(`| {sample[1]} | {fmt(sample[2], 0)} | {sample[3]} | {sample[4]} | {sample[5]} | {sample[6]} | {sample[7]} | {sample[8]} |`)
end
line()
local tracked = status.Risk and status.Risk.Tracked or 0
line("| Stability measure | Result |")
line("|-------------------|--------|")
line(`| Framework state | {status.State} |`)
line(`| Active sessions | {status.Players.Active or status.Players.Count or "?"} |`)
line(`| Evidence records held | {status.Evidence.Records or "?"} |`)
line(`| Risk users tracked | {tracked} |`)
line(`| Event handler errors (isolated) | {status.EventBus.HandlerErrors} |`)
local disabled, failures = {}, 0
for name, task in status.Scheduler do
	failures += task.Failures
	if task.Disabled > 0 then
		table.insert(disabled, name)
	end
end
line(`| Scheduled task failures (isolated) | {failures} |`)
line(`| Tasks disabled after repeated failures | {if #disabled > 0 then table.concat(disabled, ", ") else "none"} |`)
line(`| Modules still Running | {#sim.framework._services.Loader:InState("Running")} |`)
line()

-- 4. Logging -------------------------------------------------------------------------------

line("## Logging")
line()
local sinkCalls = 0
local logger = Logger.new({
	Level = "Info",
	BufferSize = 500,
	RateLimitBurst = 20,
	RateLimitWindowSeconds = 10,
	Now = os.clock,
	Wall = os.time,
	Sink = function()
		sinkCalls += 1
	end,
})
local count = 200000
local started = os.clock()
for i = 1, count do
	logger:Warn("flood", { I = i })
end
local floodSeconds = os.clock() - started
started = os.clock()
for i = 1, count do
	logger:Debug("filtered", { I = i })
end
local filteredSeconds = os.clock() - started
line("| Case | Cost per call | Reached the sink |")
line("|------|---------------|------------------|")
line(`| Warn flood ({count} calls, rate-limited) | {fmt(floodSeconds / count * 1e6, 2)} µs | {sinkCalls} |`)
line(`| Debug below level (filtered) | {fmt(filteredSeconds / count * 1e6, 2)} µs | 0 |`)
line()

if write then
	fs.writeFile(root .. "/docs/EVALUATION.md", table.concat(out, "\n") .. "\n")
	print("wrote docs/EVALUATION.md")
end

```

`tests/lune/run.luau`:

```luau
--[[
	Offline test entry point (Lune).

		./.bin/lune.exe run tests/lune/run.luau [filter]

	Builds the virtual DataModel from test.project.json and runs the same Runner that
	Studio uses (ServerStorage.ACFTests.Runner). Exits non-zero on any failure.
]]

local process = require("@lune/process")
local VirtualDataModel = require("./VirtualDataModel")

local root = process.cwd
local game, requireModule = VirtualDataModel.load(root .. "/test.project.json", root)

local filter = process.args[1]
local Runner = requireModule(game.ServerStorage.ACFTests.Runner)
local results = Runner.run({ Filter = filter })

process.exit(if results.Failed == 0 and results.Total > 0 then 0 else 1)

```

`tools/bootstrap-tools.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Downloads the pinned toolchain into .bin/ (Windows x64). Rojo and Lune are fetched from
# their GitHub releases; luau-lsp provides type checking.
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
mkdir -p .bin && cd .bin
fetch() { curl -fsSL -o "$1" "$2"; }
tmp=$(mktemp -d)
fetch "$tmp/rojo.zip" https://github.com/rojo-rbx/rojo/releases/download/v7.7.0/rojo-7.7.0-windows-x86_64.zip
fetch "$tmp/lune.zip" https://github.com/lune-org/lune/releases/download/v0.10.5/lune-0.10.5-windows-x86_64.zip
fetch "$tmp/lsp.zip" https://github.com/JohnnyMorganz/luau-lsp/releases/download/1.70.1/luau-lsp-win64.zip
for z in "$tmp"/*.zip; do unzip -o -q "$z" -d .; done
fetch globalTypes.d.luau https://raw.githubusercontent.com/JohnnyMorganz/luau-lsp/main/scripts/globalTypes.d.luau
rm -rf "$tmp"
ls -1

```

`tools/build.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Builds distributable models:
#   build/ACF.rbxm        library only (ReplicatedStorage.ACFShared + ServerScriptService.ACF)
#   build/ACF-Tests.rbxl  place with library, tests and examples for running the suite in Studio
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
mkdir -p build
./.bin/rojo.exe build default.project.json -o build/ACF.rbxl
./.bin/rojo.exe build test.project.json -o build/ACF-Tests.rbxl
echo "built build/ACF.rbxl and build/ACF-Tests.rbxl"

```

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Strict type-check of src/ and examples/ with luau-lsp, Roblox definitions and a Rojo sourcemap.
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
./.bin/rojo.exe sourcemap test.project.json -o sourcemap.json
./.bin/luau-lsp.exe analyze --sourcemap=sourcemap.json --definitions=.bin/globalTypes.d.luau --platform=roblox src examples
echo "type check passed"

```

`tools/release.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Release builds for the canary and stable channels (see RELEASING.md).
#
#   tools/release.sh canary   tag v<VERSION>-rc.<N> and build release artifacts
#   tools/release.sh stable   tag v<VERSION>; only for a commit already released as a canary
#
# Requires a clean working tree, a passing tools/verify.sh and a CHANGELOG entry for
# VERSION. Stamps the channel and release version into a staged copy of src/ (the
# repository itself always says "development"), then builds:
#   build/release/ACF-<release>.rbxl   place with ACF installed
#   build/release/ACF-<release>.rbxm   model: drag its folders into the matching services
#   build/release/ACF-<release>.json   manifest (version, channel, commit, tests, checksums)
# Tags are created locally; push them with: git push origin <tag>
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."

channel="${1:-}"
if [[ "$channel" != "canary" && "$channel" != "stable" ]]; then
	echo "usage: tools/release.sh canary|stable" >&2
	exit 2
fi

if [[ -n "$(git status --porcelain)" ]]; then
	echo "release: working tree is not clean; commit or stash first" >&2
	exit 1
fi

version="$(grep -o 'VERSION = "[^"]*"' src/Shared/Constants.luau | cut -d'"' -f2)"
if ! grep -q "^## \[$version\]" CHANGELOG.md; then
	echo "release: CHANGELOG.md has no '## [$version]' entry" >&2
	exit 1
fi

commit="$(git rev-parse HEAD)"
if [[ "$channel" == "canary" ]]; then
	count="$(git tag --list "v$version-rc.*" | wc -l | tr -d ' ')"
	release="$version-rc.$((count + 1))"
else
	if git rev-parse -q --verify "refs/tags/v$version" >/dev/null; then
		echo "release: v$version already exists" >&2
		exit 1
	fi
	if ! git tag --points-at HEAD | grep -q "^v$version-rc\."; then
		echo "release: stable must be a commit already released as a canary (v$version-rc.N on HEAD)" >&2
		exit 1
	fi
	release="$version"
fi
tag="v$release"

verify_log="$(mktemp)"
./tools/verify.sh | tee "$verify_log"
tests="$(grep -o '[0-9]*/[0-9]* passed' "$verify_log" | tail -1)"
rm -f "$verify_log"

stage="build/release/stage-$release"
rm -rf "$stage"
mkdir -p "$stage"
cp -r src "$stage/src"
sed -i "s/VERSION = \"[^\"]*\"/VERSION = \"$release\"/; s/CHANNEL = \"[^\"]*\"/CHANNEL = \"$channel\"/" "$stage/src/Shared/Constants.luau"
cp default.project.json "$stage/place.project.json"
cat >"$stage/model.project.json" <<JSON
{
  "name": "ACF-$release",
  "tree": {
    "\$className": "Folder",
    "ServerScriptService": { "\$className": "Folder", "ACF": { "\$path": "src/Server" } },
    "ReplicatedStorage": { "\$className": "Folder", "ACFShared": { "\$path": "src/Shared" } },
    "StarterPlayerScripts": { "\$className": "Folder", "ACFTelemetry": { "\$path": "src/Client/ClientTelemetry.client.luau" } }
  }
}
JSON

out="build/release/ACF-$release"
./.bin/rojo.exe build "$stage/place.project.json" -o "$out.rbxl"
./.bin/rojo.exe build "$stage/model.project.json" -o "$out.rbxm"
place_sha="$(sha256sum "$out.rbxl" | cut -d' ' -f1)"
model_sha="$(sha256sum "$out.rbxm" | cut -d' ' -f1)"
cat >"$out.json" <<JSON
{
  "version": "$release",
  "channel": "$channel",
  "commit": "$commit",
  "date": "$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)",
  "tests": "$tests",
  "place": { "file": "ACF-$release.rbxl", "sha256": "$place_sha" },
  "model": { "file": "ACF-$release.rbxm", "sha256": "$model_sha" }
}
JSON
rm -rf "$stage"

git tag -a "$tag" -m "ACF $release ($channel)"
echo "released $release ($channel): $out.rbxl, $out.rbxm, $out.json"
echo "tag $tag created locally; push with: git push origin $tag"

```

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Pre-commit gate: tests, strict type check and build. Exits non-zero on the first failure.

./tools/test.sh
./tools/check.sh
